中文摘要翻译: 建筑空间智能——即识别和推断建筑空间的能力——是机器人导航、具身交互、3D场景理解与生成等任务的基础。尽管已有广泛研究评估了视觉语言模型（VLM）的基本空间技能，如相对方向、距离比较和物体计数，但这些任务仅覆盖了空间认知的最基础层次，在很大程度上忽略了建筑空间的更高层认知，包括布局理解、流线模式和功能分区。在这项工作中，我们提出了ArchSIBench——一个基于建筑学、认知科学和心理学视角的建筑空间智能基准。ArchSIBench涵盖五个核心维度：感知、推理、导航、变换和配置，包含17个细粒度子任务。通过具有建筑背景的专家精心人工标注，我们构建了3000个问答对，以实现对建筑空间智能的全面评估。基于ArchSIBench，我们评估了多种VLM，发现大多数模型的建筑空间智能与人类基线存在显著差异；此外，模型在不同能力维度上表现出较大的差异性。部分最先进的模型可以接近未经建筑训练的人类评估者的水平。然而，与经过建筑训练的人类评估者相比，仍存在明显差距，尤其是在空间变换和配置推理方面。我们相信，ArchSIBench将为测量和推进VLM的建筑空间智能提供重要见解和系统资源。数据集和代码可在https://huggingface.co/datasets/ArchSIBench/ArchSIBench获取。

</details>


### [68] [Map-Mono-Ego: Map-Grounded Global Human Pose Estimation from Monocular Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20889)
*Hiroyuki Deguchi,Ryosuke Hori,Kotaro Amaya,Tsubasa Maruyama,Mitsunori Tada,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MapMonoEgo框架，利用预扫描的3D点云从单目相机实现全局一致的人体姿态估计，无需专用多传感器硬件；同时发布AIST-Living数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单目自我中心人体姿态估计方法仅关注相对运动，无法获取用户在环境中的绝对位置，且单目视觉的尺度模糊性导致严重的平移漂移，限制了长期跟踪能力。

Method: 提出MapMonoEgo框架，仅使用单目相机并结合预扫描的3D点云，实现全局一致的人体姿态估计。

Result: 在AIST-Living数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著优于当前最先进的基线方法，验证了其在无专用硬件条件下的实用性。

Conclusion: MapMonoEgo能够仅用单目相机实现全局一致的人体姿态估计，有效解决平移漂移问题，适用于实际监控任务。

Original Abstract: Monocular egocentric human pose estimation is essential for ubiquitous activity monitoring. However, understanding the user's absolute location within the environment remains a challenge. Existing methods primarily focus on relative motion from an initial position, and tend not to account for the wearer's absolute location within an environment. Furthermore, inherent scale ambiguity in monocular vision leads to severe translational drift, limiting long-term tracking without specialized multi-sensor hardware. To address this, we propose MapMonoEgo, a novel framework achieving globally consistent human pose estimation solely from a monocular camera by leveraging a pre-scanned 3D point cloud. We also introduce AIST-Living dataset, a new dataset pairing egocentric video with ground-truth motion in a scanned environment. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline, proving its utility for practical monitoring tasks without specialized hardware.

中文摘要翻译: 单目自我中心人体姿态估计对于普适性活动监测至关重要。然而，理解用户在环境中的绝对位置仍然是一个挑战。现有方法主要关注从初始位置开始的相对运动，且通常不考虑穿戴者在环境中的绝对位置。此外，单目视觉固有的尺度模糊性会导致严重的平移漂移，限制了在没有专用多传感器硬件情况下的长期跟踪。为解决这一问题，我们提出了MapMonoEgo，一种新颖的框架，通过利用预扫描的三维点云，仅从单目相机即可实现全局一致的人体姿态估计。我们还引入了AIST-Living数据集，这是一个将自我中心视频与扫描环境中的真实运动进行配对的新数据集。实验表明，我们的方法显著优于最先进的基线方法，证明了其在无需专用硬件的实际监测任务中的实用性。

</details>


### [69] [HDMoE: A Hierarchical Decoupling-Fusion Mixture-of-Experts Framework for Multimodal Cancer Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20891)
*Huayi Wang,Haochao Ying,Yuyang Xu,Qiyao Zheng,jun wang,Cheng Zhang,Ying Sun,Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化解耦-融合混合专家（HDMoE）框架，用于多模态生存预测。该框架通过两级MoE和随机特征重组模块，有效去除冗余信息、提取细粒度模态内和模态间特征，并在多个数据集上取得了优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于特征解耦-融合范式的多模态生存预测方法存在两个不足：(1) 解耦前未能减少模态特征的冗余信息，负面影响解耦与融合效果；(2) 缺乏对特征间细粒度关系的建模能力，无法捕获模态内和模态间的局部信息交互。

Method: 提出HDMoE框架，包含两级MoE和两个随机特征重组（RFR）模块。第一级MoE通过共享专家和路由专家去除冗余信息并提取细粒度的模态内特定特征；第二级MoE实现细粒度的模态间特征解耦。每个MoE层后接一个RFR模块，用于精细融合模态内和模态间特征，捕获更细粒度的跨模态关系。

Result: 在私有肝癌（LC）数据集和三个TCGA公开数据集上的大量实验结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的HDMoE框架通过层次化解耦-融合策略，有效解决了多模态生存预测中特征冗余和细粒度交互建模不足的问题，在多个数据集上表现优异。

Original Abstract: Multimodal survival prediction, a crucial yet challenging task, demands the integration of multimodal medical data (\eg Whole Slide Images (WSIs) and Genomic Profiles) to achieve accurate prognostic modeling. Given the inherent heterogeneity across modalities, the feature decoupling-fusion paradigm has emerged as a dominant approach. However, these methods have the following shortcomings: (1) fail to reduce the redundant information of modality features before decoupling, which negatively affects the feature decoupling and fusion effect;(2) lack the ability to model the fine-grained relationships of the features and capture the local information interactions between intra- and inter-modality features. To address these issues, we propose a \underline{H}ierarchical \underline{D}ecoupling-Fusion \underline{M}ixture-\underline{o}f-\underline{E}xperts (HDMoE) framework with two levels of MoE and \underline{R}andom \underline{F}eature \underline{R}eorganization (RFR) modules.In the first-level MoE, shared experts and routed experts are employed to remove redundant information and extract fine-grained specific features within each modality, while the second-level MoE facilitates fine-grained inter-modality feature decoupling. Besides, we design two RFR modules following each level of MoE to finely fuse intra- and inter-modality features, which can help the model capture more fine-grained relationships between modalities. Extensive experimental results on our private Liver Cancer (LC) and three TCGA public datasets confirm the effectiveness of our proposed method. Codes are available at https://github.com/ZJUMAI/HDMoE.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态生存预测是一项关键但具有挑战性的任务，它要求整合多模态医学数据（如全切片图像和基因组图谱）以实现准确的预后建模。由于模态间存在固有的异质性，特征解耦-融合范式已成为主流方法。然而，这些方法存在以下不足：(1) 在解耦之前未能减少模态特征的冗余信息，这对特征解耦和融合效果产生了负面影响；(2) 缺乏对特征间细粒度关系的建模能力，无法捕获模态内和模态间特征的局部信息交互。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一个层次化解耦-融合混合专家（HDMoE）框架，该框架包含两级MoE和随机特征重组（RFR）模块。在第一级MoE中，共享专家和路由专家被用于去除冗余信息并提取每个模态内的细粒度特定特征，而第二级MoE则促进细粒度的模态间特征解耦。此外，我们在每一级MoE之后设计了两个RFR模块，用于精细融合模态内和模态间特征，这有助于模型捕获模态之间更细粒度的关系。在我们私有的肝癌（LC）数据集和三个TCGA公开数据集上的大量实验结果确认了我们所提出方法的有效性。代码已开源在 https://github.com/ZJUMAI/HDMoE。

</details>


### [70] [VISTA: Technical Report for the Ego4D Short-Term Object Interaction Anticipation at EgoVis 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20901)
*Qiaohui Chu,Haoyu Zhang,Yisen Feng,Meng Liu,Weili Guan,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个结合V-JEPA与StillFast设计的短时程物体交互预测框架，在EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA挑战赛中取得第一名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 针对Ego4D短时程物体交互预测任务，现有方法难以同时有效利用高分辨率空间信息与视频时序上下文，VISTA旨在通过StillFast双分支设计融合两者优势。

Method: 采用StillFast结构：1）空间分支使用COCO预训练的Faster R-CNN ResNet-50 FPN从最后一帧高分辨率图像生成目标提议；2）时间分支使用冻结的V-JEPA 2.1提取视频片段级自我中心时序特征；3）通过特征调制与ROI级上下文融合将时序表示注入检测分支；4）融合后的提议特征输入多头STA预测器，进行框精炼、名词分类、动词分类、接触时间回归与交互置信度估计；5）集成多个互补预测结果提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA挑战赛官方平台上取得第一名。

Conclusion: VISTA通过StillFast双分支设计与V-JEPA时序编码的有效融合，在自我中心视频短时程交互预测任务上达到了当前最优性能。

Original Abstract: We propose VISTA, a V-JEPA Integrated StillFast Temporal Anticipator for the Ego4D Short-Term Object Interaction Anticipation (STA) Challenge at EgoVis 2026. Given an egocentric video timestamp, the task requires anticipating the next human-object interaction, including the future active object's bounding box, noun category, verb category, time-to-contact, and confidence score. VISTA follows a StillFast-style design that combines object-centric spatial detection with short-horizon temporal context. Specifically, a COCO-pretrained Faster R-CNN ResNet-50 FPN detector generates object proposals from the last observed high-resolution frame, while a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 temporal branch extracts clip-level egocentric context from the observed video. The temporal representation is injected into the detection pathway through feature modulation and ROI-level context fusion. The fused proposal features are then passed to multi-head STA predictors for box refinement, noun classification, verb classification, time-to-contact regression, and interaction confidence estimation. For the final submission, we further ensemble complementary predictions to improve robustness. Experimental results on the official challenge server show that VISTA achieves first place in the EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA Challenge. Our code will be released at https://github.com/CorrineQiu/VISTA.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出VISTA——一种融合V-JEPA与StillFast的时序预测器，用于EgoVis 2026的Ego4D短时程物体交互预测挑战赛。给定一个自我中心视频的时间戳，该任务要求预测下一个人类-物体交互，包括未来活动物体的边界框、名词类别、动词类别、接触时间以及置信度分数。VISTA采用StillFast风格的设计，将目标中心的空间检测与短时程时序上下文相结合。具体而言，一个经COCO预训练的Faster R-CNN ResNet-50 FPN检测器从最后一帧高分辨率图像中生成目标提议，同时一个冻结的V-JEPA 2.1时序分支从观测视频中提取片段级别的自我中心上下文。时序表示通过特征调制和ROI级上下文融合注入检测通路。融合后的提议特征随后被送入多头STA预测器，进行边界框精炼、名词分类、动词分类、接触时间回归以及交互置信度估计。在最终提交中，我们进一步集成互补的预测结果以提升鲁棒性。在官方挑战服务器上的实验结果表明，VISTA在EgoVis 2026 Ego4D STA挑战赛中取得第一名。我们的代码将在https://github.com/CorrineQiu/VISTA开源。

</details>


### [71] [JFAA: Technical Report for the EPIC-KITCHENS-100 Action Anticipation Challenge at EgoVis 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20904)
*Qiaohui Chu,Haoyu Zhang,Yisen Feng,Meng Liu,Weili Guan,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JFAA是一种基于JEPA的未来动作预测方法，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100动作预测任务中取得第一名。它使用冻结的编码器/预测器提取特征，并通过轻量级注意力探针和场感知集成策略提升预测鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 受V-JEPA 2.1在表示学习和未来预测方面的能力启发，旨在利用其冻结的编码器和预测器，结合轻量级可训练模块，高效解决厨房场景下的细粒度动作预测问题。

Method: 1) 使用V-JEPA 2.1的冻结编码器和预测器提取观察上下文特征和近未来潜变量；2) 设计轻量级注意力探针，通过独立的任务查询分别预测动词、名词和动作logits；3) 构建场感知集成策略，基于选择的epoch级预测，让每个输出字段受益于最可靠的候选结果。

Result: 在EgoVis 2026 EK-100动作预测挑战赛官方榜单上取得第一名。

Conclusion: JFAA验证了V-JEPA在动作预测任务中的有效性，冻结特征提取结合轻量级探针与场感知集成的方法能够在动作预测任务上取得最优性能。

Original Abstract: We propose JFAA, a JEPA-based Future Action Anticipation method for the EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (EK-100) Action Anticipation task. Inspired by the representation learning and future prediction ability of V-JEPA 2.1, JFAA uses a frozen encoder and predictor to extract observed context features and near-future latent tokens. A lightweight attentive probe is then trained to predict verb, noun, and action logits with separate task queries. To improve robustness, we further build a field-aware ensemble over selected epoch-level predictions, allowing each output field to benefit from its most reliable candidates. Experimental results on the official challenge server show that JFAA achieves first place in the EgoVis 2026 EK-100 Action Anticipation Challenge. Our code will be released at https://github.com/CorrineQiu/JFAA.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出JFAA，一种基于JEPA的未来动作预测方法，用于EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (EK-100) 动作预测任务。受V-JEPA 2.1在表示学习和未来预测能力方面的启发，JFAA使用冻结的编码器和预测器来提取观察到的上下文特征和近未来潜变量。然后训练一个轻量级注意力探针，通过独立的任务查询来预测动词、名词和动作logits。为提升鲁棒性，我们进一步在选定的epoch级预测上构建场感知集成策略，使每个输出字段能从其最可靠的候选中获益。在官方挑战服务器上的实验结果表明，JFAA在EgoVis 2026 EK-100动作预测挑战赛中取得了第一名。我们的代码将在https://github.com/CorrineQiu/JFAA发布。

</details>


### [72] [FlowLong: Inference-time Long Video Generation via Manifold-constrained Tweedie Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20910)
*Jangho Park,Geon Yeong Park,Gihyun Kwon,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的长视频生成推理方法，通过重叠滑动窗口、Tweedie匹配和随机早期采样实现跨窗口的一致性，支持比原生窗口长数倍的视频生成，适用于多种架构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无训练方法分为双向模型扩展（与特定架构紧密耦合且长程质量退化）和自回归模型（暴露偏差导致漂移误差和重复运动模式），需一种架构无关、无需额外训练的长视频生成方法。

Method: 1) 重叠滑动窗口生成长视频；2) 相邻窗口预测的干净样本通过Tweedie匹配混合，以强制流形约束和时间一致性；3) 随机早期采样在Tweedie匹配修正后注入新噪声同步轨迹，然后切换至确定性ODE采样保持视觉保真度。

Result: 生成的视频比原生窗口长度长数倍，在时间一致性和视觉质量上优于无训练基线和自回归基线，并扩展到音视频联合生成和文本到3DGS且无需微调。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种简单、架构无关且无需额外训练的推理时方法，能有效解决长视频生成中的质量退化和漂移问题，具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Extending the generation horizon of video diffusion models to long sequences remains a long-standing and important challenge. Existing training-free approaches fall into two categories: extensions of bidirectional models, which are tightly coupled to specific architectures and suffer from quality degradation over long horizons, and autoregressive models, which accumulate drift errors due to exposure bias and tend to produce repetitive motion patterns. To address these issues, we propose a novel but simple inference-time approach for long video generation that is architecture-agnostic and requires no additional training. Our method generates long videos via overlapping sliding windows, where predicted clean samples from adjacent windows are blended via \emph{Tweedie matching} to enforce both \textbf{manifold constraint and temporal consistency} across overlap regions. \emph{Stochastic early-phase sampling} then synchronizes per-window trajectories by injecting fresh noise after each Tweedie matching correction in the high-noise phase, before transitioning to deterministic ODE sampling to preserve fine-grained visual fidelity. Applied to various video generation models, our method generates videos several times longer than the native window length while outperforming both training-free and autoregressive baselines in temporal consistency and visual quality, and further extends to audio-video joint generation and text-to-3DGS without any fine-tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 将视频扩散模型的生成范围扩展到长序列仍然是一个长期且重要的挑战。现有的无训练方法分为两类：双向模型的扩展，这类方法与特定架构紧密耦合且在长范围内出现质量退化；自回归模型，则因暴露偏差累积漂移误差并倾向于产生重复的运动模式。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新颖但简单的推理时方法，用于长视频生成，该方法与架构无关且无需额外训练。我们的方法通过重叠滑动窗口生成长视频，相邻窗口预测的干净样本通过Tweedie匹配进行混合，以在重叠区域强制施加流形约束和时间一致性。随机早期采样则在高噪声阶段每次Tweedie匹配修正后注入新噪声，以同步各窗口的轨迹，随后过渡到确定性ODE采样以保持细粒度的视觉保真度。应用于多种视频生成模型，我们的方法生成的视频比原生窗口长度长数倍，同时在时间一致性和视觉质量上优于无训练基线和自回归基线，并进一步扩展到音视频联合生成和文本到3DGS，且无需任何微调。

</details>


### [73] [PaintCopilot: Modeling Painting as Autonomous Artistic Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20941)
*Yunge Wen,Yuancheng Shen,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaintCopilot 是一个协同式神经绘画助手，将绘画建模为基于画布状态和笔触历史的自回归艺术行为，无需目标图像即可预测未来笔触，支持四种交互式工作流，实现人机交替控制的共创绘画。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经绘画方法将绘画视为对预定义参考图像的像素重建，缺乏对艺术创作过程中开放性、渐进式行为的建模能力，无法支持真正的协同创作。

Method: 提出三个互补模型：ViT-based Target Predictor（从部分画布推断艺术家意图）、自回归 Next Stroke Predictor（通过流匹配生成时序连贯笔触）、VAE-based Region Sampler（按需合成语义局部笔触序列）；基于三种可微分笔触表示（Hard Round、Brush Tip、2D Gaussian）支持四种交互工作流。

Result: 通过专业艺术家的案例研究，证明 PaintCopilot 能够实现流畅的协同绘画工作流，艺术家与AI在创作过程中持续交替控制。

Conclusion: PaintCopilot 将绘画从像素重建范式转变为开放式的自回归艺术行为建模，为AI辅助艺术创作提供了新的协同范式。

Original Abstract: We present PaintCopilot, a co-creative neural painting assistant that models painting as an open-ended autoregressive artistic behavior conditioned on evolving canvas states and prior brushstroke history, without requiring a target image. Unlike existing neural painting methods that frame painting as pixel reconstruction toward a predefined reference, PaintCopilot predicts future strokes directly from learned artistic dynamics, analogous to how large language models continue text sequences from prior context.
  The framework proposes three complementary models: a ViT-based Target Predictor that infers artist intent from partial canvas observations, an autoregressive Next Stroke Predictor that generates temporally coherent brushstrokes via flow matching, and a VAE-based Region Sampler that synthesizes semantically localized stroke sequences on demand. Built on three differentiable brush representations (Hard Round, Brush Tip, and 2D Gaussian), the system supports four interactive workflows: Optimize History, Stroke Completion, Region Inpainting, and Dynamic Brush. Through case studies with professional artists, we demonstrate that PaintCopilot enables fluid co-creative painting workflows in which artists and AI continuously alternate control throughout the creative process.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了PaintCopilot，一个协同式神经绘画助手，它将绘画建模为一种开放式的自回归艺术行为，该行为以不断演变的画布状态和先前的笔触历史为条件，无需目标图像。与现有将绘画框架化为对预定义参考图像进行像素重建的神经绘画方法不同，PaintCopilot直接从学习到的艺术动态中预测未来笔触，类似于大语言模型如何根据先前上下文延续文本序列。该框架提出了三个互补模型：基于ViT的目标预测器（Target Predictor），从部分画布观测中推断艺术家意图；自回归的下一笔触预测器（Next Stroke Predictor），通过流匹配（flow matching）生成时序连贯的笔触；以及基于VAE的区域采样器（Region Sampler），按需合成语义局部化的笔触序列。基于三种可微分笔触表示（硬圆刷、刷尖和二维高斯），该系统支持四种交互式工作流：历史优化（Optimize History）、笔触补全（Stroke Completion）、区域修复（Region Inpainting）和动态笔刷（Dynamic Brush）。通过与专业艺术家的案例研究，我们证明了PaintCopilot能够实现流畅的协同绘画工作流，使艺术家和AI在创作过程中持续交替控制。

</details>


### [74] [Bridging Structure and Language: Graph-Based Visual Reasoning for Autonomous Road Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20942)
*Lena Wild,Katie Z Luo,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRS框架，将几何道路结构与开放词汇语义融合为统一图表示，通过递归图查询自动生成问答对，以小模型（2-4B参数）和少量数据（20-80场景）显著提升VLM的道路结构化推理能力，揭示当前瓶颈在于缺乏结构化监督而非模型规模。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）在道路理解中缺乏几何与关系基础，而传统模块化系统（如HD地图）虽精度高但语义僵化，两者在结构化道路推理上均存在不足。

Method: 提出Combined Road Substrate (CRS)框架，构建图结构表示统一几何道路结构与开放词汇语义；通过递归图查询自动生成组合复杂、语言多样的问答对；引入"grounding for free"机制确保逻辑可追溯至具体地图元素；程序化提取链式思维（chain-of-thought）监督轨迹。

Result: 最先进的VLM（包括大型闭源模型）在结构化道路推理上表现困难；仅用20-80个CRS增强场景训练2-4B参数的小模型，即可在组合推理任务上获得稳定收益；行为分析表明基线模型失败于关系场景理解，而CRS训练后失败模式降级为属性识别。

Conclusion: 道路理解的主要瓶颈并非模型规模，而是缺乏结构化监督；CRS框架能有效弥补这一鸿沟。

Original Abstract: Structured road understanding of lane geometry, topology, and traffic element relationships is foundational to safe autonomous driving. While vision-language models (VLMs) offer promising semantic flexibility, they lack the geometric and relational grounding required for precise road reasoning. Conversely, traditional modular systems, e.g., HD maps and topological road graphs, provide structural precision but remain semantically rigid. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Combined Road Substrate (CRS), a graph-grounded framework that makes geometric road structure and open-vocabulary semantics jointly executable in a single representation. CRS enables the automatic generation of compositionally complex and linguistically varied question-answer pairs via recursive graph queries, augmented with a "grounding for free" mechanism that ensures logical traceability to specific map elements, and procedurally extracted chain-of-thought supervision traces. We demonstrate that state-of-the-art VLMs - including large, closed-source models - struggle significantly with structured road reasoning, yet training a small 2- or 4-billion-parameter model with as few as 20 to 80 CRS-enriched scenes yields stable gains in compositional reasoning tasks of varying depth. Analysis of model behavior via verifiable reasoning traces reveals a systematic shift in failure modes: whereas baseline models fail at relational scene understanding, CRS-trained models reduce failures to attribute recognition, suggesting that the primary bottleneck in road understanding is not model scale, but the absence of structured supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 结构化道路理解——包括车道几何、拓扑结构以及交通元素关系——是安全自动驾驶的基础。视觉-语言模型（VLM）虽然在语义上具有灵活性，但在精确的道路推理中缺乏必要的几何和关系基础。相反，传统模块化系统（如高清地图和拓扑道路图）提供结构精度，但语义上较为僵化。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了Combined Road Substrate (CRS)，一个基于图结构的框架，使得几何道路结构与开放词汇语义能够在单一表示中联合执行。CRS通过递归图查询自动生成组合复杂且语言多样的问答对，并配备"grounding for free"机制，确保逻辑可追溯至特定地图元素，同时程序化提取链式思维监督轨迹。我们证明，最先进的VLM（包括大型闭源模型）在结构化道路推理上表现显著困难，而仅需使用20至80个CRS增强场景训练一个20亿或40亿参数的小模型，便能在不同深度的组合推理任务中取得稳定提升。通过可验证的推理轨迹进行模型行为分析，揭示了失败模式的系统性转变：基线模型在关系场景理解上失败，而CRS训练的模型将失败降级至属性识别层面，这表明道路理解的主要瓶颈并非模型规模，而是缺乏结构化监督。

</details>


### [75] [Focus-then-Context: Subject-Centric Progressive Visual Token Reduction for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20950)
*Yulin Zhao,Yun Wang,Dehua Zheng,Borui jiang,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPpruner，一种基于主体-上下文的渐进式视觉token缩减范式，模拟人类视觉感知的"聚焦-上下文"机制，通过显式建模视觉显著性与语义相关性来挖掘视觉主体谱系，并聚合相邻区域的上下文线索以恢复全局关系依赖，从而在保持高保真度表示的同时大幅加速VLM推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉token缩减方法虽然减轻了VLM推理的计算负担，但无意中保留了与用户查询严格对齐的孤立视觉主体，未能充分探索显著主体及其上下文关系。

Method: 首先构建焦点识别模块，显式建模视觉显著性与语义相关性的相互作用，挖掘全面的视觉主体谱系；然后开发上下文感知的结构扫描模块，从相邻区域聚合上下文线索，恢复全局关系依赖以维护所保留主体的结构完整性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL上仅保留22.2%的视觉token即可实现最高2.53倍加速；在LLaVA上实现67%的FLOPs缩减，准确率仅下降0.6%，持续优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的主体渐进式缩减范式能有效平衡VLM推理效率和表示保真度，通过模拟人类视觉感知机制实现显著的加速效果与可忽略的性能损失。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) face a bottleneck of prohibitive computational costs arising from massive visual token sequences during inference. Existing vision token reduction methods alleviate this burden, but they unintentionally preserve the isolated visual subject strictly aligned with the user's query, which fails to substantially explore salient subjects and their contextual relationships. In this paper, we propose SPpruner, a subject-centric progressive reduction paradigm that emulates the \textit{Focus-then-Context} mechanism of the human visual perception system. Specifically, we first construct a focus identification module to explicitly model the interplay between visual saliency and semantic relevance. Herein, it can excavate the comprehensive visual subject spectrum to ensure a high-fidelity representation of visual input. Subsequently, a context-aware structural scanning module is developed to aggregate contextual cues from neighboring regions. As such, it can effectively restore global relational dependencies to uphold the structural integrity of the preserved subjects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm consistently outperforms SOTA methods, achieving up to 2.53 times speedup with only 22.2% of visual tokens retained in Qwen2.5-VL and a 67% FLOPs reduction on LLaVA with a negligible 0.6% accuracy drop.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在推理过程中面临着由海量视觉token序列引发的计算成本瓶颈。现有视觉token缩减方法虽然缓解了这一负担，但它们无意中保留了与用户查询严格对齐的孤立视觉主体，未能充分探索显著主体及其上下文关系。本文提出SPpruner，一种主体渐进式缩减范式，模拟人类视觉感知系统的"聚焦-上下文"机制。具体而言，我们首先构建焦点识别模块，显式建模视觉显著性与语义相关性的相互作用。该模块能够挖掘全面的视觉主体谱系，以确保视觉输入的高保真度表示。随后，我们开发了上下文感知的结构扫描模块，从相邻区域聚合上下文线索。这样，它可以有效恢复全局关系依赖，从而维护所保留主体的结构完整性。大量实验表明，我们的范式持续优于SOTA方法，在Qwen2.5-VL上仅保留22.2%的视觉token即可实现最高2.53倍加速，在LLaVA上实现67%的FLOPs缩减，准确率仅下降0.6%。

</details>


### [76] [DrawMotion: Generating 3D Human Motions by Freehand Drawing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20955)
*Tao Wang,Lei Jin,Zhihua Wu,Qiaozhi He,Jiaming Chu,Yu Cheng,Junliang Xing,Jian Zhao,Shuicheng Yan,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DrawMotion，一种基于扩散模型的文本到动作生成框架，通过引入手绘草图条件实现多条件融合，使用户能够更直观地控制生成动作，实验表明手绘方式可减少约46.7%的用户时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成方法依赖纯文本输入，而用户往往难以通过文字精确表达其意图动作，存在表达能力不足的问题。

Method: 1) 提出自动生成手绘火柴人草图的算法，用于捕捉用户意图；2) 设计多条件模块(MCM)集成到扩散过程中，实现文本条件与手绘条件的融合并降低计算复杂度；3) 利用MCM中间特征的连续空间特性，通过无训练的分类器引导梯度更新特征，使生成动作与用户意图对齐。

Result: 手绘草图方法相比纯文本输入减少了约46.7%的用户时间，定量实验和用户研究均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DrawMotion通过引入手绘草图条件，有效解决了文本表达动作意图不充分的问题，在多条件融合和动作生成质量上均取得了显著提升。

Original Abstract: Text-to-motion generation, which translates textual descriptions into human motions, faces the challenge that users often struggle to precisely convey their intended motions through text alone. To address this issue, this paper introduces DrawMotion, an efficient diffusion-based framework designed for multi-condition scenarios. DrawMotion generates motions based on both a conventional text condition and a novel hand-drawing condition, which provide semantic and spatial control over the generated motions, respectively. Specifically, we tackle the fine-grained motion generation task from three perspectives: 1) freehand drawing condition. To accurately capture users' intended motions without requiring tedious textual input, we develop an algorithm to automatically generate hand-drawn stickman sketches across different dataset formats; 2) multi-condition fusion. We propose a Multi-Condition Module (MCM) that is integrated into the diffusion process, enabling the model to exploit all possible condition combinations while reducing computational complexity compared to conventional approaches; and 3) training-free guidance. Notably, the MCM in DrawMotion ensures that its intermediate features lie in a continuous space, allowing classifier-guidance gradients to update the features and thereby aligning the generated motions with user intentions while preserving fidelity. Quantitative experiments and user studies demonstrate that the freehand drawing approach reduces user time by approximately 46.7% when generating motions aligned with their imagination. The code, demos, and relevant data are publicly available at https://github.com/InvertedForest/DrawMotion.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到动作生成旨在将文本描述转化为人体运动，其面临的一个挑战是用户往往难以仅通过文本精确表达其意图动作。为了解决这一问题，本文提出了DrawMotion，一个高效的多条件扩散框架。DrawMotion基于常规文本条件和一种新颖的手绘条件生成动作，这两种条件分别提供语义控制和空间控制。具体而言，我们从三个角度解决细粒度动作生成任务：1）手绘条件。为了准确捕捉用户意图动作而无需繁琐的文字输入，我们开发了一种算法，能够跨不同数据集格式自动生成手绘火柴人草图；2）多条件融合。我们提出了一个集成到扩散过程中的多条件模块（MCM），使模型能够利用所有可能的条件组合，同时相比传统方法降低计算复杂度；3）无训练引导。值得注意的是，DrawMotion中的MCM确保其中间特征位于连续空间内，这使得分类器引导梯度能够更新这些特征，从而在保持保真度的同时使生成动作与用户意图对齐。定量实验和用户研究表明，手绘草图方法在生成与用户想象一致的动作时，用户时间减少了约46.7%。代码、演示和相关数据已在https://github.com/InvertedForest/DrawMotion公开。

</details>


### [77] [Finding the Correct Visual Evidence Without Forgetting: Mitigating Hallucination in LVLMs via Inter-Layer Visual Attention Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20965)
*Yutong Xie,Zhenglin Hua,Ran Wang,Wing W. Y. Ng,Xizhao Wang,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现LVLMs在生成过程中对正确视觉证据关注不足且逐渐遗忘，并提出一种基于层间视觉注意力差异（ILVAD）的无训练、即插即用的幻觉缓解方法，通过在生成时增强视觉证据的注意力并强调与视觉证据强相关的文本token，在多个模型上一致地减轻了幻觉。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LVLMs在视觉-语言任务中仍存在幻觉问题，即生成与视觉内容不一致的响应。作者发现这一问题源于模型对正确视觉证据关注不足，且在生成过程中逐渐遗忘该证据。

Method: 提出ILVAD方法：1）从早期生成token到视觉token的跨层注意力权重中，识别被重复激活的视觉token形成显著性图；2）通过显著性图在生成过程中增强对视觉证据的注意力以减少视觉遗忘；3）利用显著性图获取生成文本对视觉证据的注意力分数，选择和强调与视觉证据强对齐的文本token。该方法无需训练，即插即用。

Result: 在五个最近发布的、不同架构的LVLMs上进行的多项基准评估表明，该方法能够一致地缓解各种LVLMs中的幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 基于层间视觉注意力差异（ILVAD）提出的无训练幻觉缓解方法能有效提升LVLMs对视觉证据的关注，减少视觉遗忘，从而在多种模型架构上一致地抑制幻觉生成。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown remarkable performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks. Despite this progress, they are still prone to hallucination, generating responses that are inconsistent with visual content. In this work, we find that LVLMs tend to hallucinate when they pay insufficient attention to the correct visual evidence and gradually forget it during the generation process. We empirically find that although LVLMs overall attend insufficiently to visual evidence, they exhibit sensitivity to the correct visual evidence in specific layers, with notable inter-layer discrepancy. Motivated by this observation, we propose a novel hallucination mitigation method that enhances visual evidence based on Inter-Layer Visual Attention Discrepancy (ILVAD). Specifically, we obtain the attention weights from early generated tokens to visual tokens across layers and identify the tokens that are repeatedly activated as visual evidence, forming a saliency map. We then enhance attention to visual evidence during generation through the saliency map to reduce visual forgetting. In addition, we leverage the saliency map to obtain attention scores of generated text to visual evidence, in order to select and emphasize text tokens that are strongly grounded in visual evidence. Our method is training-free and plug-and-play. Multiple benchmark evaluations conducted on five recently released models show that our method can consistently mitigate hallucinations in different LVLMs over various architectures. Code is available at https://github.com/ytx-ML/ILVAD.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在广泛的视觉-语言任务中展现了卓越的性能。尽管取得了这些进展，它们仍然容易产生幻觉，即生成与视觉内容不一致的响应。在这项工作中，我们发现LVLMs在对正确的视觉证据关注不足并在生成过程中逐渐遗忘它时，倾向于产生幻觉。我们通过实验发现，尽管LVLMs整体上对视觉证据关注不足，但在特定层中它们对正确视觉证据表现出敏感性，且存在显著的层间差异。受此观察启发，我们提出了一种基于层间视觉注意力差异（ILVAD）的新型幻觉缓解方法，该方法增强了视觉证据。具体来说，我们获取早期生成token到视觉token的跨层注意力权重，识别被重复激活的token作为视觉证据，形成显著性图。然后，我们在生成过程中通过显著性图增强对视觉证据的注意力，以减少视觉遗忘。此外，我们利用显著性图获取生成文本对视觉证据的注意力分数，以选择和强调那些与视觉证据紧密关联的文本token。我们的方法无需训练且即插即用。在五个最近发布的模型上进行的多项基准评估表明，我们的方法能够一致地缓解不同架构LVLMs中的幻觉现象。代码可在 https://github.com/ytx-ML/ILVAD 获取。

</details>


### [78] [Comparative Evaluation of Deep Learning Models for Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20971)
*Akhitha Pakala,Mohammed Mahir Rahman,Shahzad Memon,Tauseef Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了VGG16、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0和XceptionNet四种预训练CNN在假图像检测任务上的性能。VGG16以91%的准确率最高，但所有模型均存在数据集不平衡、过拟合和可解释性差等局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于GAN的图像伪造技术日益复杂，对数字取证构成了重大挑战，因此需要研究和比较不同CNN架构在伪造图像检测中的表现。

Method: 采用统一的预处理和训练流程，对真实和经过篡改的图像数据集进行尺寸调整、归一化和数据增强，使用VGG16、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0和XceptionNet四种预训练CNN架构进行分类。

Result: VGG16准确率最高（91%），XceptionNet、ResNet50和EfficientNetB0均为90%。EfficientNetB0对伪造图像更敏感，但对真实样本的可靠性较低，反映了不平衡偏差的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可复现的基线，强调了平衡数据集、先进数据增强和公平性感知训练对于开发可靠的伪造图像检测系统的必要性。

Original Abstract: The growing sophistication of GAN-based image manipulation presents significant challenges for digital forensics. This study compares the performance of four pretrained CNN architectures including VGG16, ResNet50, EfficientNetB0, and XceptionNet for fake image detection using a unified preprocessing and training pipeline. A dataset of real and manipulated images was processed through resizing, normalization, and augmentation to address class imbalance and improve generalization. Models were evaluated using Accuracy, Precision, Recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. VGG16 achieved the highest accuracy at 91%, with XceptionNet, ResNet50, and EfficientNetB0 each reaching 90%. EfficientNetB0 showed stronger sensitivity to fake images but reduced reliability on real samples, reflecting imbalance-driven bias. Limitations include dataset imbalance, overfitting, and limited interpretability, which affect cross-domain robustness. The study provides a reproducible baseline and underscores the need for balanced datasets, advanced augmentation, and fairness-aware training to develop reliable fake image detection systems.

中文摘要翻译: 基于GAN的图像伪造技术日益复杂，给数字取证带来了重大挑战。本研究采用统一的预处理和训练流程，比较了四种预训练CNN架构（包括VGG16、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0和XceptionNet）在伪造图像检测中的性能。通过对真实图像和经篡改图像的数据集进行尺寸调整、归一化和数据增强处理，以解决类别不平衡问题并提升泛化能力。模型使用准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数和ROC-AUC进行评估。VGG16取得了最高准确率91%，XceptionNet、ResNet50和EfficientNetB0均达到90%。EfficientNetB0对伪造图像表现出更强的敏感性，但对真实样本的可靠性较低，反映出不平衡偏差的影响。局限性包括数据集不平衡、过拟合以及可解释性有限，这些问题影响了跨领域的鲁棒性。本研究提供了可复现的基线，并强调了需要平衡数据集、先进的数据增强以及公平性感知训练，以开发可靠的伪造图像检测系统。

</details>


### [79] [Towards Integrated Rock Support Visualisation in 3D Point Cloud of Underground Mines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20973)
*Dibyayan Patra,Simit Raval,Pasindu Ranasinghe,Bikram Banerjee,Ismet Canbulat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于3D点云的自动化框架，将结构映射、锚杆识别、不连续面拟合和锚杆方向估计整合为统一流程，实现地下矿山支护可视化与几何有效性评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究中，不连续面表征和锚杆识别通常作为独立任务处理，限制了它们对集成支护评估的价值，因此需要一种自动化的集成框架来直接评估支护与围岩结构的空间相互作用。

Method: 提出自动化框架，包含结构映射、锚杆识别、不连续面拟合、锚杆方向估计四个模块，整合为统一工作流；生成集成3D可视化（拟合不连续面与锚杆向量）及立体投影分析；同时可视化锚杆安装质量指标（外露长度、偏离顶板法线的偏差）。

Result: 在真实地下金属矿山扫描数据上验证，该框架在中等规模点云上实现了准确的结构映射和锚杆识别结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动化的集成岩土支护评估提供了实用方案，无需人工测量或额外现场数据采集即可评估锚杆支护有效性。

Original Abstract: The effectiveness of rock support in underground mines depends on the interaction between installed rock bolts and the structural fabric of the surrounding rock mass. However, discontinuity characterisation and rock bolt identification are commonly treated as separate tasks, limiting their value for integrated support assessment. This study presents an automated framework for integrated rock support visualisation using 3D point clouds of underground mine excavations. The framework integrates structure mapping, rock bolt identification, discontinuity plane fitting, and bolt orientation estimation into a unified workflow optimised for accuracy and computational efficiency. The outputs are used to generate an integrated 3D visualisation of fitted discontinuity planes and bolt vectors, enabling direct assessment of their spatial intersections and geometric relationships. A complementary stereographic analysis of discontinuity poles and bolt orientations is also performed to evaluate overall bolting geometric effectiveness relative to the mapped structural fabric. Additionally, bolt-level quality metrics, including exposed protrusion length and deviation from the local roof normal, are visualised to support assessment of installation quality. The proposed framework is demonstrated on real underground metal mine scans, producing accurate structure mapping and rock bolt identification results in medium-scale point clouds. Overall, the study provides a practical step towards automated, integrated geotechnical assessment of rock support effectiveness without requiring manual measurements or additional in-situ data acquisition.

中文摘要翻译: 地下矿山锚杆支护的有效性取决于安装锚杆与围岩结构面之间的相互作用。然而，不连续面表征和锚杆识别通常被视为独立的任务，限制了其对集成支护评估的价值。本研究提出了一种自动化框架，利用地下矿山开挖工程的3D点云实现集成的支护可视化。该框架将结构映射、锚杆识别、不连续面拟合和锚杆方向估计整合为一个统一工作流，针对准确性和计算效率进行了优化。输出结果用于生成拟合不连续面与锚杆向量的集成3D可视化，从而直接评估它们的空间相交关系与几何关系。同时进行了不连续面极点与锚杆方向的互补立体投影分析，以评估相对于已映射结构面的整体锚固几何有效性。此外，还可视化了锚杆级别的质量指标，包括外露长度和偏离局部顶板法线的偏差，以支持安装质量评估。该框架在真实地下金属矿山扫描数据上进行了验证，在中等规模点云上产生了准确的结构映射和锚杆识别结果。总体而言，本研究为实现自动化、集成的岩土支护有效性评估提供了实用步骤，无需人工测量或额外的现场数据采集。

</details>


### [80] [CHOIR: Contact-aware 4D Hand-Object Interaction Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20992)
*Hao Xu,Yilin Liu,Yinqiao Wang,Chi-Wing Fu,Niloy J. Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHOIR是一个从日常单目视频中重建手-物交互（HOI）的4D重建框架，通过将接触作为显式耦合信号，联合优化手部运动、物体形状与6D位姿及接触时空信息，在复杂场景下显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常假设已知物体或受控场景，且分别估计手和物体在杂波、遮挡和未见物体几何下容易产生对齐偏差，缺乏从开放世界单目视频中可规模化挖掘真实交互的4D重建能力。

Method: 1) 利用开放世界视觉先验初始化粗粒度的、与接触无关的4D HOI序列；2) 引入生成式HOI空间校正模块，预测射线深度校正并修正手与物体的相对位置，进而推导初始逐帧接触对应关系；3) 采用接触感知的联合优化，动态更新接触约束，强制执行几何、时序和接触一致性。

Result: 在受控和挑战性视频上的实验表明，CHOIR在物体重建质量、物理合理性和时间一致性方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将接触作为手与物体之间的显式耦合信号，CHOIR能够从开放世界的日常单目视频中生成可复用的4D交互基元，支持场景感知合成与规划等下游应用。

Original Abstract: We ask whether everyday open-world monocular videos can be turned into reusable 4D interaction primitives: articulated hand motion, object shape with 6D pose over time, and the when/where of contact. Such a capability would enable scalable mining of real interactions and, beyond reconstruction, support scene-aware synthesis and planning. However, reconstructing hand-object interaction (HOI) from challenging monocular videos remains difficult: methods often assume known objects or curated scenes, and separately estimated hands and objects easily become misaligned under clutter, occlusion, and unseen object geometries. Targeting this setting, we present CHOIR, a Contact-aware HOI Reconstruction framework for a monocular camera, using contact as an explicit coupling signal between hands and objects. CHOIR first initializes a coarse, contact-agnostic 4D HOI sequence from open-world visual priors. It then introduces a generative HOI spatial rectification module to predict ray-depth corrections and rectify hand-object relative placement, then derive initial per-frame contact correspondences on the rectified geometry. Last, a contact-aware joint optimization with dynamically updated contact constraints enforces geometric, temporal, and contact consistency. Experiments on controlled and challenging videos show that CHOIR improves object reconstruction, physical plausibility, and temporal consistency over state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 我们探究是否可以将日常开放世界的单目视频转化为可复用的4D交互基元：即手部关节运动、随时间变化的物体形状与6D位姿，以及接触的时空位置。这种能力将支持对真实交互的可规模化挖掘，并且在重建之外，还能支持场景感知的合成与规划。然而，从具有挑战性的单目视频中重建手-物交互（HOI）仍然困难重重：现有方法通常假设已知物体或受控场景，且分别估计的手和物体在杂波、遮挡及未见物体几何下容易产生对齐偏差。针对这一场景，我们提出了CHOIR——一种面向单目相机的接触感知手-物交互重建框架，将接触作为手与物体之间的显式耦合信号。CHOIR首先利用开放世界视觉先验初始化一个粗粒度的、与接触无关的4D HOI序列；接着引入一个生成式HOI空间校正模块，预测射线深度校正并修正手与物体的相对位置，然后在校正后的几何上推导出初始的逐帧接触对应关系；最后，采用接触感知的联合优化，通过动态更新的接触约束强制执行几何、时序和接触一致性。在受控和具有挑战性的视频上的实验表明，CHOIR在物体重建质量、物理合理性和时间一致性方面均优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [81] [Hybrid Machine Learning Model for Forest Height Estimation from TanDEM-X and Landsat Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20997)
*Islam Mansour,Ronny Haensch,Irena Hajnsek,Konstantinos Papathanassiou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过融合光学Landsat数据扩展特征空间，改进原有基于物理模型的混合机器学习方法，以解决森林高度反演中的模糊性问题，并在加蓬Lopé国家公园的TanDEM-X数据上验证了有效性，RMSE降低13.5%，MAE降低16.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始混合模型（ML+物理模型）在从TanDEM-X干涉相干数据估计森林高度时，由于特征空间有限，无法解决高度/结构与基线/地形坡度之间的模糊性，需要引入补充信息来提升反演精度。

Method: 在原有仅使用TanDEM-X干涉相干特征的基础上，扩展特征空间，引入光学Landsat数据作为补充信息，提供关于森林类型或结构的额外约束，构建扩展的混合模型。

Result: 在加蓬Lopé国家公园多个TanDEM-X数据集上验证，与原始混合模型相比，扩展模型RMSE降低13.5%，MAE降低16.6%。

Conclusion: 多光谱输入（光学Landsat数据）能够有效补充干涉相干特征的不足，提升混合模型反演森林高度的精度，证实了多源遥感数据融合的附加价值。

Original Abstract: Integrating machine learning (ML) with physical models (PM) has emerged as a promising way of retrieving geophysical parameters from remote sensing data. In this context, a ML model for estimating forest height from TanDEM-X interferometric coherence measurements has recently been proposed, that constrains the learning process through a PM. While the features used for training and inversion where selected to ensure the physical consistency of the solutions, they could not resolve all height / structure and baseline / terrain slope ambiguities in the data. To improve this, the extension of the feature space with optical Landsat data is proposed able to provide complementary information on forest type or structure. The extended model is applied and validated on several TanDEM-X acquisitions over the Gabonese Lopé national park site and assessed against airborne LiDAR measurements. Results show a 13.5% reduction in RMSE and a 16.6% reduction in MAE compared to the original hybrid model, confirming the added value of multispectral inputs.

中文摘要翻译: 将机器学习与物理模型相结合，已发展成为一种从遥感数据中反演地球物理参数的有效途径。在此背景下，近期有一项研究提出了一种基于TanDEM-X干涉相干测量值的森林高度估计模型，该模型通过物理模型约束学习过程。虽然用于训练和反演的特征已经过筛选以确保解的物理一致性，但无法解决数据中所有的高度/结构与基线/地形坡度模糊性问题。为改进这一点，本文提出扩展特征空间，引入光学Landsat数据，以提供关于森林类型或结构的补充信息。扩展后的模型在加蓬Lopé国家公园的多个TanDEM-X数据上进行了应用和验证，并以机载LiDAR测量结果为基准进行评估。结果表明，与原始混合模型相比，均方根误差降低13.5%，平均绝对误差降低16.6%，证实了多光谱输入的附加价值。

</details>


### [82] [DySink: Dynamic Frame Sinks for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21028)
*Bo Ye,Xinyu Cui,Jian Zhao,Tong Wei,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DySink提出了一种基于检索的动态帧沉落机制，通过紧凑记忆库选择与当前视觉状态相关的历史帧作为动态锚点，并引入沉落异常门（Sink Anomaly Gate）检测多头注意力过度一致性问题，从而避免长视频自回归生成中的早期帧偏见和沉落崩溃现象，在分钟级视频生成中提升了动态质量和时序一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归长视频生成采用有界内存流式策略，通常使用局部窗口维持短期连续性，并依赖早期帧作为静态长程锚点。但这种固定分配方式导致：当当前视觉状态与早期帧显著偏离时，早期帧仍被保留在缓存中，而更相关的中期历史帧却被丢弃。这导致保留的长程上下文缺乏自适应性，生成偏向过时线索；严重时，RoPE引起的相位重对齐会导致多头注意力同质化，引发沉落崩溃（sink collapse），内容向沉落帧退化。

Method: 提出DySink框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）检索型动态帧沉落机制，维护一个紧凑记忆库，从中选择与当前视觉状态最相关的历史帧作为动态锚点，替代固定的早期帧缓存；（2）沉落异常门（Sink Anomaly Gate），检测检索到的上下文中多头注意力之间的过度一致性，当检测到崩溃倾向时抑制该上下文，避免生成退化。

Result: 在分钟级长视频生成实验上，DySink在动态度（dynamic degree）指标上持续优于强基线方法，同时实现了更高的时序质量。

Conclusion: DySink通过动态检索相关历史帧替代静态早期帧缓存，并引入注意力异常检测门控机制，有效解决了自回归长视频生成中的长程上下文自适应问题与沉落崩溃问题，在保持时序质量的同时提升了生成视频的动态性。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive long video generation often adopts bounded-memory streaming for efficiency, typically combining local windows for short-term continuity with static early-frame sinks as long-range anchors. However, this fixed allocation keeps early frames cached even when the current visual state has substantially diverged from them, while discarding potentially more relevant intermediate history. As a result, the retained long-range context may become less adaptive and bias generation toward outdated cues; in severe cases, RoPE-induced phase re-alignment can homogenize inter-head attention and cause sink collapse, where content regresses toward sink frames. We propose DySink, a retrieval-based framework that maintains a compact memory bank and selects visually relevant historical frames as dynamic frame sinks. DySink couples adaptive retrieval with a sink anomaly gate, which detects excessive inter-head consensus over retrieved context and suppresses collapse-prone context. Experiments on minute-long videos show that DySink consistently improves dynamic degree over strong baselines while also achieving higher temporal quality. The code and model weights will be released at https://github.com/yebo0216best/DySink.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归长视频生成通常采用有界内存流式策略以提高效率，典型做法是将局部窗口用于短期连续性，并将静态的早期帧沉落（early-frame sink）作为长程锚点。然而，这种固定分配方式使得早期帧即使在当前视觉状态与它们显著偏离时仍被保留在缓存中，而可能更相关的中间历史帧却被丢弃。结果，保留的长程上下文变得缺乏自适应性，生成偏向过时的线索；严重情况下，RoPE引起的相位重对齐会导致多头注意力同质化，引发沉落崩溃（sink collapse），内容向沉落帧退化。我们提出DySink，一种基于检索的框架，维护一个紧凑的记忆库，并选择与当前视觉相关的历史帧作为动态帧沉落（dynamic frame sink）。DySink将自适应检索与沉落异常门（sink anomaly gate）相结合，该门控机制检测检索到的上下文上多头注意力之间的过度一致性，并抑制易崩溃的上下文。在分钟级视频上的实验表明，DySink在动态度（dynamic degree）指标上持续优于强基线方法，同时实现了更高的时序质量。代码和模型权重将在https://github.com/yebo0216best/DySink发布。

</details>


### [83] [Towards Physically Consistent 4D Scene Reconstruction for Closed-loop Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21032)
*Bowyn Tan,Yutong Xie,Bai Huang,Fan Luo,Xiao Li,Naizheng Wang,Yang Guan,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有3DGS方法在街景重建中无法同时实现新视角合成与时序建模的根源——时空参数的信用分配困境，并提出正交投影梯度（OPG）层次化训练方法与时序正则化策略，有效解耦时空参数，在保持NVS能力的同时提升时序动态建模性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3DGS及其4D扩展方法无法同时实现高保真街景重建所需的新视角合成与时变信息建模两个关键能力，其根本原因尚未被揭示。本文旨在诊断该局限性的内在机理并提出解决方案。

Method: (1) 建立信息几何诊断框架，揭示时空参数间的信用分配困境源于单源观测中视点与时间的确定性耦合导致的低秩结构与零空间歧义；(2) 提出正交投影梯度（OPG）层次化训练方法：先锁定空间表征完整性，再限制时序更新于空间零空间内，实现主动信用分配；(3) 提出时序正则化策略，基于外观一致演化的物理先验施加平滑约束，优化时序解空间。

Result: 实验表明，该方法不仅保持了稳定的新视角合成能力，而且在传统观测复现指标上表现出更优性能，间接反映了时序动态建模能力的提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了时空参数耦合导致的信用分配问题，在街景重建中同时实现了高质量新视角合成与时序动态建模，为端到端自动驾驶闭环仿真提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: High-fidelity street scene reconstruction is pivotal for end-to-end autonomous driving simulation, where novel-view synthesis (NVS) and time-varying information modeling are two fundamental capabilities to facilitate closed-loop training. However, existing 3DGS methods and their 4D extensions fail to simultaneously achieve both. To bridge this gap, we establish an information-geometric diagnostic framework, revealing that this limitation stems from a credit assignment dilemma between spatial and temporal parameters. Specifically, the deterministic coupling between viewpoint and time in single-source observation creates a low-rank structure that induces massive null-space ambiguity between static view-dependent and dynamic time-varying components. Temporal information overshadows spatial cues, causing the estimation variance of spatial parameters to diverge. To address this issue, we propose Orthogonal Projected Gradient (OPG), a hierarchical training method designed to restore spatial identifiability. OPG prioritizes the integrity of spatial representations by securing them in an initial stage, then restricts temporal updates to the spatial null space, enabling proactive credit assignment. While OPG isolates temporal updates algebraically, Temporal Regularization Strategy is proposed to further refine the temporal solution space by imposing a smoothness constraint based on the physical prior of consistent appearance evolution, ensuring that the reconstructed scene remains physically consistent in closed-loop simulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method not only maintains stable NVS capabilities but also demonstrates superior performance in traditional observation-reproducing metrics, which indirectly reflect the capability of modeling temporal dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 高保真街景重建对于端到端自动驾驶仿真至关重要，其中新视角合成与时变信息建模是支撑闭环训练的两个基础能力。然而，现有3DGS方法及其4D扩展无法同时实现这两者。为弥合这一差距，我们建立了一个信息几何诊断框架，揭示该局限源于空间参数与时间参数之间的信用分配困境。具体而言，单源观测中视点与时间的确定性耦合形成了低秩结构，导致静态视角相关成分与动态时变成分之间存在大量的零空间歧义。时间信息压制了空间线索，使得空间参数的估计方差发散。为解决这一问题，我们提出正交投影梯度——一种层次化训练方法，旨在恢复空间可辨识性。OPG通过在初始阶段锁定空间表征的完整性来优先保障空间表征，然后将时序更新限制在空间零空间中，从而实现主动信用分配。在OPG代数层面隔离时序更新的基础上，我们进一步提出时序正则化策略，基于外观一致演化的物理先验施加平滑约束来优化时序解空间，确保重建场景在闭环仿真中保持物理一致性。大量实验表明，我们的方法不仅保持了稳定的新视角合成能力，还在传统观测复现指标上展现出更优性能，这间接反映了其时序动态建模能力的提升。

</details>


### [84] [Dynamic Video Generation: Shaping Video Generation Across Time and Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21042)
*Shikang Zheng,Jingkai Huang,Jiacheng Liu,Guantao Chen,Lixuan,Yuqi Lin,Peiliang Cai,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DVG是一种动态视频生成框架，通过联合分配时间和空间上的计算资源，自动选择内容感知的加速策略，在不重新训练的情况下实现近无损加速，最高可达7倍（与蒸馏结合可达18倍）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型在视频生成中迭代去噪过程计算开销大，虽然渐进分辨率采样方法通过降低早期阶段潜在分辨率来加速，但将其扩展到视频生成面临挑战：时间维度的引入带来了多样化的时空需求，仅压缩单一维度会导致加速有限或质量下降。

Method: 提出DVG（Dynamic Video Generation）框架，该框架联合分配时间与空间上的计算资源，自动选择内容感知的加速策略，无需手动调参或重新训练。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo和HunyuanVideo-1.5上实现最高7倍加速，与蒸馏方法结合可达18倍加速，且实现近无损加速。

Conclusion: DVG作为一种无需手动调优或重训练的动态加速框架，能够高效联合优化时空计算资源，在多种模型和任务上实现近无损加速，有望成为大规模高效视频生成系统的关键组件。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved impressive performance in video generation, but their iterative denoising process remains computationally expensive due to the large number of tokens processed at each timestep. Recently, progressive resolution sampling has emerged as a promising acceleration approach by reducing latent resolution in early stages. However, scaling this idea to video generation remains challenging, as the additional temporal dimension introduces diverse spatio-temporal demands across different videos, and compressing only a single dimension often leads to limited acceleration or degraded quality. Therefore, we propose DVG, a Dynamic Video Generation framework that jointly allocates computation across time and space, automatically selecting content-aware acceleration strategies without manual tuning or retraining. DVG achieves near-lossless acceleration across models and tasks, reaching up to 7 times speedup on HunyuanVideo and HunyuanVideo-1.5, and 18 times when combined with distillation, demonstrating its potential as a key component in today's large-scale efficient video generation systems. Our code is in supplementary material and will be released on Github.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型在视频生成领域取得了令人瞩目的性能，但由于每个时间步需要处理大量token，其迭代去噪过程仍然计算开销巨大。近年来，渐进分辨率采样作为一种有前景的加速方法，通过降低早期阶段的潜在分辨率来提升效率。然而，将这一思路扩展到视频生成仍面临挑战：额外的时间维度引入了不同视频间多样化的时空需求，仅压缩单一维度往往导致加速效果有限或生成质量下降。为此，我们提出了DVG（Dynamic Video Generation）——一种动态视频生成框架，该框架联合分配时间与空间上的计算资源，自动选择内容感知的加速策略，无需手动调参或重新训练。DVG在多种模型和任务上实现了近无损加速，在HunyuanVideo和HunyuanVideo-1.5上最高可达7倍加速，与蒸馏方法结合时可达18倍加速，展现出其作为当今大规模高效视频生成系统中关键组件的潜力。我们的代码见补充材料，并将在Github上开源。

</details>


### [85] [Multimodal LLMs under Pairwise Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21059)
*Yan Li,Yunlong Deng,Yuewen Sun,Gongxu Luo,Kun Zhang,Guangyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用成对模态数据来训练多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的表示学习框架，通过两阶段方法（潜在表示对齐和跨模态重组合）实现无需完整多路对齐数据的跨模态学习，并在新增3D点云和触觉模态时展现出强跨模态性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM训练依赖人工精心构建的多路对齐多模态数据，成本高昂且难以扩展到新领域。本文旨在探索仅利用成对模态数据作为完整联合多模态分布的替代，降低数据构建成本，提升跨域可扩展性。

Method: 提出两阶段表示学习框架。阶段一：潜在表示对齐，通过自模态重构和成对对比学习学习共享潜在空间，并在对比学习中引入部分对齐和最小潜在规范化的归纳偏置。阶段二：跨模态重组合，将新引入模态的编码器与预训练模态的解码器集成，实现跨模态迁移和生成。

Result: 在预训练MLLM中新增3D点云和触觉两种模态（共三个模态对）进行评估，结果表明通过学习对齐的潜在表示空间，模型取得了强跨模态性能。

Conclusion: 仅利用成对模态数据即可有效对齐多模态潜在表示，无需完整的多路对齐数据，为MLLM的跨模态扩展提供了一种低成本、可扩展的方案。

Original Abstract: Despite the impressive results achieved by multimodal large language models (MLLMs), their training typically relies on jointly curated multimodal data, requiring substantial human effort to construct multi-way aligned datasets and thereby limiting scalability across domains. In this work, we explore training MLLMs by only leveraging multiple paired modalities as a surrogate for the full joint multimodal distribution. Specifically, we first provide a theoretical analysis of the conditions under which the representations are identifiable with only observing pairwise modalities. Building on this analysis, we propose a representation learning framework for aligning latent representations across modalities using only pairwise data. The framework consists of two stages: latent representation alignment and cross-modal recomposition. Specifically, in the first stage, we learn the shared latent space across modalities by both self-modal reconstruction and pair-wise contrastive learning. We also incorporate an inductive bias in the contrastive learning process by partially aligning and minimal latent specification. In stage two, we integrate the encoder of newly introduced modalities with the decoders of the pre-trained modalities to facilitate cross-modal transfer and generation. We evaluate our method by newly adding 3D point clouds and tactile modalities into pre-trained MLLMs with three modality pairs and show that, by learning an aligned latent representation space, our model achieves strong cross-modal performance.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）取得了令人瞩目的成果，但其训练通常依赖于联合整理的多模态数据，需要大量人工来构建多路对齐的数据集，从而限制了跨领域的可扩展性。本文探索仅利用多个成对模态数据作为完整联合多模态分布的替代来训练MLLMs。具体而言，我们首先对仅观察成对模态时表示可识别的条件进行了理论分析。基于此分析，我们提出了一种仅使用成对数据来对齐跨模态潜在表示的表示学习框架。该框架包含两个阶段：潜在表示对齐和跨模态重组合。在第一阶段，我们通过自模态重构和成对对比学习来学习跨模态的共享潜在空间。我们还在对比学习过程中引入了部分对齐和最小潜在规范化的归纳偏置。在第二阶段，我们将新引入模态的编码器与预训练模态的解码器集成，以促进跨模态迁移和生成。我们通过向预训练MLLMs中新增3D点云和触觉两种模态（共三个模态对）来评估我们的方法，结果表明，通过学习对齐的潜在表示空间，我们的模型实现了强大的跨模态性能。

</details>


### [86] [Q-ARVD: Quantizing Autoregressive Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21072)
*Siao Tang,Xinyin Ma,Gongfan Fang,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-ARVD框架，针对自回归视频扩散模型（ARVDs）的量化难题，解决了帧间量化敏感度高度不平衡和权重中异常值模式异质两大挑战，通过最终质量感知的帧加权机制和异常值感知的自适应双尺度量化，实现了高效且准确的模型量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型（ARVDs）推理成本高昂，阻碍了实际部署。模型量化是提升效率的自然方向，但现有为标准扩散Transformer设计的量化方案直接应用于ARVDs效果不佳，因其量化行为与双向扩散模型存在显著差异。因此需要专门针对ARVDs的量化方法。

Method: (S1) 最终质量感知的帧加权机制：将帧级量化敏感度纳入量化目标，解决帧间敏感度高度不平衡问题；(S2) 异常值感知的自适应双尺度量化：自动检测各层异常值通道的存在与否及数量，将异常值通道隔离，保护正常通道的量化精度。

Result: 实验证明Q-ARVD在自回归视频扩散模型量化任务上显著优于直接应用现有量化方案的基线方法。

Conclusion: Q-ARVD框架能够有效克服ARVDs量化中的两大挑战（帧间敏感度不平衡和权重异常值异质），实现更优的量化性能，为ARVDs的实际部署提供了高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models (ARVDs) have emerged as a promising architecture for streaming video generation, paving the way for real-time interactive video generation and world modeling. Despite their potential, the substantial inference cost of ARVDs remains a major obstacle to practical deployment, making model quantization a natural direction for improving efficiency. However, quantization for ARVDs remains largely unexplored. Our empirical analysis shows that directly applying existing quantization schemes developed for standard diffusion transformers to ARVDs leads to suboptimal performance, revealing quantization behaviors that differ from those observed in bidirectional diffusion models. In this paper, we identify two critical challenges in quantizing ARVDs: (C1) Highly unbalanced frame-wise quantization sensitivity. Error accumulation during autoregressive generation can induce severely skewed quantization sensitivity across frames, following an exponential-like decay pattern. (C2) Prominent and heterogeneous outlier patterns in weights. Weight distributions exhibit pronounced outlier channels, whose patterns vary substantially across layer types and block depths. To address these issues, we propose Q-ARVD, a novel framework for accurate ARVD quantization. (S1) To tackle the highly unbalanced frame-wise sensitivity, Q-ARVD incorporates a final-quality aware frame-weighting mechanism into the quantization objective. (S2) To prevent heterogeneous outliers from degrading performance, Q-ARVD introduces an outlier-aware adaptive dual-scale quantization, which automatically detects the presence and quantity of outlier channels for an arbitrary layer, and isolates them to protect normal channels. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of Q-ARVD.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归视频扩散模型（ARVDs）已成为流式视频生成的一种有前景的架构，为实时交互式视频生成和世界建模铺平了道路。尽管潜力巨大，ARVDs高昂的推理成本仍是实际部署的主要障碍，使得模型量化成为提升效率的自然方向。然而，ARVDs的量化问题尚未得到充分探索。我们的实证分析表明，将现有为标准扩散Transformer设计的量化方案直接应用于ARVDs会导致性能次优，揭示了与双向扩散模型不同的量化行为。在本文中，我们识别了量化ARVDs的两个关键挑战：(C1) 高度不平衡的帧级量化敏感度。自回归生成过程中的误差累积会导致帧间量化敏感度严重偏斜，呈现类似指数衰减的模式。(C2) 权重中突出且异质的异常值模式。权重分布呈现出显著的异常值通道，且其模式在不同层类型和块深度之间差异显著。为解决这些问题，我们提出Q-ARVD，一个用于精确ARVD量化的新颖框架。(S1) 为解决高度不平衡的帧级敏感度，Q-ARVD在量化目标中引入了最终质量感知的帧加权机制。(S2) 为防止异质异常值降低性能，Q-ARVD引入了异常值感知的自适应双尺度量化，该方法能够自动检测任意层中异常值通道的存在与否及其数量，并将其隔离以保护正常通道。大量实验证明了Q-ARVD的优越性。

</details>


### [87] [SpectralEarth-FM: Bringing Hyperspectral Imagery into Multimodal Earth Observation Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21075)
*Nassim Ait Ali Braham,Aaron Banze,Conrad M. Albrecht,Julien Mairal,Jocelyn Chanussot,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpectralEarth-FM，一种层次化Transformer模型，用于多传感器地球观测数据（包括高光谱、多光谱、SAR等）的联合预训练与融合，并构建了配套的大规模数据集SpectralEarth-MM，在多个下游任务上取得最优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有地球观测基础模型多训练于多光谱、SAR等数据，高光谱影像（HSI）被严重低估；而现有的高光谱基础模型仅单独使用HSI训练，未探索HSI与其他遥感传感器的联合预训练与融合。

Method: 提出层次化Transformer架构，包含光谱标记化（spectral tokenization）、传感器专用编码器、跨传感器融合模块和共享层次编码器；构建SpectralEarth-MM数据集（含EnMAP、EMIT、DESIS等星载HSI与Sentinel-2、Landsat-8/9、LST、Sentinel-1 SAR的共定位数据）；采用JEPA风格的自监督预训练目标，匹配全局视图与单传感器局部视图的表征。

Result: 在高光谱下游任务和标准EO基准（PANGAEA协议）上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过多传感器联合预训练，SpectralEarth-FM有效融合了高光谱与其他遥感数据，显著提升了多种遥感任务的性能。

Original Abstract: Earth observation (EO) foundation models (FMs) are increasingly trained on multisensor data, spanning multispectral imagery (MSI), synthetic aperture radar (SAR), and derived geospatial layers, but hyperspectral imagery (HSI) remains underrepresented. Conversely, existing hyperspectral FMs are trained on HSI alone, leaving joint pretraining and fusion of HSI with co-located EO sensors unexplored. We introduce SpectralEarth-FM, a hierarchical transformer for multisensor EO input with heterogeneous spectral dimensionality. The architecture combines spectral tokenization for hyperspectral inputs, sensor-specific encoders, a cross-sensor fusion module, and a shared hierarchical encoder, enabling joint processing of HSI and lower-channel observations. To pretrain SpectralEarth-FM, we curate SpectralEarth-MM, a dataset that co-locates HSI from three spaceborne sensors (EnMAP, EMIT, DESIS) with Sentinel-2, Landsat-8/9 optical imagery, Landsat land surface temperature (LST), and Sentinel-1 SAR, over common geographic footprints. It comprises approximately 2M globally distributed locations, 25M georeferenced patches, and over 40TB of data. Pretraining uses a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA)-style objective that matches representations between global views and single-sensor local views from the same location. We evaluate SpectralEarth-FM on hyperspectral downstream tasks and standard EO benchmarks following the PANGAEA protocol, achieving state-of-the-art results across both evaluation settings.

中文摘要翻译: 地球观测基础模型越来越多地使用多传感器数据进行训练，涵盖多光谱影像、合成孔径雷达以及衍生地理空间图层，但高光谱影像仍未被充分纳入。反之，现有的高光谱基础模型仅使用高光谱数据单独训练，未探索高光谱与其他共定位遥感传感器的联合预训练与融合。我们提出SpectralEarth-FM，一种面向具有异质光谱维度的多传感器地球观测输入的层次化Transformer。该架构结合了高光谱输入的谱标记化、传感器专用编码器、跨传感器融合模块以及共享层次编码器，实现了高光谱与低通道观测数据的联合处理。为预训练SpectralEarth-FM，我们构建了SpectralEarth-MM数据集，该数据集将来自三个星载传感器（EnMAP、EMIT、DESIS）的高光谱影像与Sentinel-2、Landsat-8/9光学影像、Landsat地表温度以及Sentinel-1 SAR数据在共同地理足迹上进行共定位。该数据集包含约200万个全球分布的位置点、2500万个地理参考图块以及超过40TB的数据。预训练采用联合嵌入预测架构风格的目标函数，匹配同一位置的全局视图与单传感器局部视图之间的表征。我们在高光谱下游任务和遵循PANGAEA协议的标准EO基准上评估SpectralEarth-FM，在两个评估设置下均取得了最先进的结果。

</details>


### [88] [VDFP: Video Deflickering with Flicker-banding Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21079)
*Zhiyi Zhou,Libo Zhu,Zihan Zhou,Yulun Zhang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VDFP框架，通过滚动快门机制退化场建模和时空连续先验感知，结合生成式先验，有效消除手机拍摄屏幕时的频闪条纹伪影，在保真度和时序一致性上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 手机拍摄数字屏幕时因硬件同步不匹配产生严重频闪条纹（banding），现有视频恢复方法难以处理这类结构化周期性亮度波动，常产生残留伪影或过度平滑，且缺乏真实场景数据集。

Method: 首先构建真实世界数据集DeViD；然后提出VDFP框架，包含：(1) 基于滚动快门机制的退化场建模（DFM），可合成复杂多条纹场景；(2) 时空连续先验感知模块（CPP），通过Flicker-Aware均方误差（FA-MSE）优化以捕捉亮度过渡，而非传统二值分割；(3) 零初始化增强输入层，保留预训练生成先验和时空先验感知。

Result: 大量实验表明VDFP显著优于其他方法，能消除复杂频闪条纹，同时保持高保真空间细节和时序一致性。

Conclusion: VDFP通过结合物理退化建模与感知引导生成框架，有效解决了屏幕拍摄视频的频闪去除难题，数据集和代码将开源。

Original Abstract: Capturing digital screens with smartphones frequently induces severe banding due to hardware synchronization mismatches. Existing video restoration methods struggle with these structured, periodic luminance fluctuations, often resulting in residual artifacts or over-smoothed textures. We firstly construct DeViD, a real-world dataset in various scenes to deal with the lack of available datasets.Then we propose VDFP (Video Deflickering with Flicker-banding Priors), a novel perception-guided generation framework. First, we introduce a Degradation Field Modeling Based on Rolling Shutter Mechanism (DFM) capable of synthesizing complex multi-banding scenarios. Second, we present a spatial-temporal continuous prior perception (CPP). Unlike traditional binary segmentation, this module is optimized via a Flicker-Aware Mean Squared Error (FA-MSE) to capture the luminance transitions. By zero-initializing an augmented input layer, our model preserves pre-trained generative priors as well as spatial-temporal prior perception. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VDFP significantly outperforms other methods, eliminating complex banding with high-fidelity spatial details and temporal consistency. Our dataset and code will be released at~ https://github.com/ZhiyiZZhou/VDFP.

中文摘要翻译: 使用智能手机拍摄数字屏幕时，由于硬件同步不匹配，经常会产生严重的频闪条纹。现有的视频恢复方法难以处理这些结构化的周期性亮度波动，通常会导致残留伪影或过度平滑的纹理。我们首先构建了DeViD，一个涵盖多种场景的真实世界数据集，以应对可用数据集的匮乏。随后，我们提出了VDFP（基于频闪条纹先验的视频去闪烁框架），一种新颖的感知引导生成框架。首先，我们引入了基于滚动快门机制的退化场建模（DFM），能够合成复杂的多条纹场景。其次，我们提出了一种时空连续先验感知（CPP）模块。与传统二值分割不同，该模块通过Flicker-Aware均方误差（FA-MSE）进行优化，以捕捉亮度过渡。通过零初始化增强输入层，我们的模型保留了预训练生成先验以及时空先验感知。大量实验表明，VDFP显著优于其他方法，能够在消除复杂频闪条纹的同时，保持高保真的空间细节和时序一致性。我们的数据集和代码将在https://github.com/ZhiyiZZhou/VDFP公开发布。

</details>


### [89] [TextSculptor: Training and Benchmarking Scene Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21090)
*Yiheng Lin,Siyu Jiao,Xiaohan Lan,Wei Zhou,Qi She,Fei Yu,Heyun Chen,Zhengwei Wang,Jinghuan Chen,Moran Li,Yingchen Yu,Zijian Feng,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei,Yujie Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextSculptor是一个针对场景文本编辑的全面框架，包含自动化数据构建流水线、大规模数据集TextSculpt-Data（320万样本）和标准化基准TextSculpt-Bench，显著提升了开源模型在场景文本编辑上的性能，缩小了与商业模型的差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前场景文本编辑面临两大挑战：一是高质量训练数据稀缺，二是缺乏针对文本编辑的标准化基准，导致开源模型性能远落后于商业系统。

Method: 提出TextSculptor框架：1）构建自动化数据流水线，结合文本感知图像合成与程序化文本渲染合成；2）基于此流水线构建TextSculpt-Data数据集（320万训练样本，含120万OCR验证的文本到图像样本和200万配对文本编辑样本）；3）提出TextSculpt-Bench基准，覆盖文本添加、替换、删除和混合编辑四种任务，并通过OCR文本对齐、多模态评判和背景区域相似度进行综合评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，TextSculptor显著提升了开源模型的文本编辑性能，缩小了与商业专有模型的差距。

Conclusion: TextSculptor通过提供高质量数据和标准化评估基准，有效推动了场景文本编辑领域的发展，数据与基准已开源。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and diffusion-based generative models have substantially improved prompt-driven image editing. However, scene text editing remains challenging, as it requires models to precisely modify textual content while preserving visual realism and non-target regions. Current open-source models still lag behind proprietary systems, largely due to the scarcity of high-quality training data and the lack of standardized benchmarks tailored to text editing. To address these challenges, we present TextSculptor, a comprehensive framework for data construction and evaluation of scene text editing. We first develop an automated data construction pipeline that combines text-aware image synthesis with programmatic text rendering and compositing. Based on this pipeline, we build TextSculpt-Data, a large-scale dataset containing 3.2M training samples, including 1.2M OCR-verified text-to-image samples and 2M paired text editing samples with naturally aligned source-target images and strong background consistency. We further introduce TextSculpt-Bench, a benchmark covering four fundamental text editing tasks: text addition, text replacement, text removal, and hybrid editing. To support reliable evaluation, we design a tailored protocol that measures text accuracy, visual quality, and background preservation through OCR-based text alignment, multimodal judgment, and background-region similarity. Extensive experiments show that TextSculptor improves open-source text editing performance and narrows the gap to proprietary models. The data and benchmark are available at https://github.com/linyiheng123/TextSculptor.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型和基于扩散的生成模型的最新进展显著提升了基于提示的图像编辑能力。然而，场景文本编辑仍然具有挑战性，因为它要求模型在精确修改文本内容的同时保持视觉真实感和非目标区域不变。当前的开源模型仍落后于商业系统，这主要是由于缺乏高质量的训练数据和针对文本编辑的标准化基准。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了TextSculptor——一个用于场景文本编辑数据构建和评估的全面框架。我们首先开发了一个自动化数据构建流水线，将文本感知图像合成与程序化文本渲染合成相结合。基于该流水线，我们构建了TextSculpt-Data，一个包含320万训练样本的大规模数据集，其中包括120万经OCR验证的文本到图像样本和200万配对的文本编辑样本，具有自然的源-目标图像对齐和强背景一致性。我们进一步引入了TextSculpt-Bench，一个涵盖四种基础文本编辑任务（文本添加、文本替换、文本删除和混合编辑）的基准。为支持可靠的评估，我们设计了一套定制化评估协议，通过基于OCR的文本对齐、多模态评判和背景区域相似度来度量文本准确性、视觉质量和背景保持能力。大量实验表明，TextSculptor提升了开源文本编辑性能，缩小了与商业模型的差距。数据和基准已在https://github.com/linyiheng123/TextSculptor公开。

</details>


### [90] [RCGDet3D: Rethinking 4D Radar-Camera Fusion-based 3D Object Detection with Enhanced Radar Feature Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21112)
*Weiyi Xiong,Bing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对4D汽车雷达点云稀疏性问题，本文发现增强雷达特征提取比复杂多模态融合更有效，并提出RCGDet3D，通过射线中心的高斯编码器（R-PGE）和语义注入（SI）模块，在保持实时性的同时实现了优于现有方法的检测精度和速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有4D雷达-相机融合方法侧重复杂融合策略，在密集特征图上计算量大，拖慢推理速度，限制了实时部署；而雷达稀疏点云的特征提取虽计算量小但未被充分探索。本文旨在探索能否通过增强雷达特征提取本身来替代复杂的融合模块，实现性能与速度的双赢。

Method: 提出RCGDet3D框架，核心改进包括：(1) 射线中心的高斯编码器（R-PGE），在射线对齐坐标系中预测高斯属性，再统一投影到鸟瞰图（BEV）空间，将坐标变换与表示学习解耦，提升几何一致性并降低学习难度；(2) 语义注入（SI）模块，融合图像视觉线索，生成几何更精确、语义更丰富的雷达特征。整体简化了多模态融合，侧重雷达特征编码。

Result: 在View-of-Delft (VoD) 和 TJ4DRadSet 数据集上，RCGDet3D在检测精度和推理速度上均优于当前最先进方法，为实时部署设立了新的基准。

Conclusion: 仅增强雷达特征提取即可达到甚至超越复杂融合模块的性能，同时保持实时性；所提的R-PGE和SI模块有效提升了雷达特征的几何一致性与语义丰富度，验证了简化融合、强化主干特征的思路在4D雷达目标检测中的潜力。

Original Abstract: 4D automotive radar is indispensable for autonomous driving due to its low cost and robustness, yet its point cloud sparsity challenges 3D object detection. Existing 4D radar-camera fusion methods focus on complex fusion strategies, trading inference speed for marginal gains. This trade-off hinders real-time deployment due to heavy computation on dense feature maps. In contrast, feature extraction from sparse radar points is less time-consuming but remains under-explored. This work uncovers that simply enhancing radar feature extraction can achieve comparable or even higher performance than elaborate fusion modules, while maintaining real-time performance. Based on this finding, we propose RCGDet3D, which centers on radar feature encoding and simplifies multi-modal fusion. Its encoder inherits from the efficient Gaussian Splatting-based Point Gaussian Encoder (PGE) in RadarGaussianDet3D with two key improvements. First, the Ray-centric PGE (R-PGE) predicts Gaussian attributes in ray-aligned coordinate systems before unifying them to Bird's-Eye View (BEV) space, significantly improving geometric consistency and reducing learning difficulty by decoupling the coordinate transformation from representation learning. Second, a Semantic Injection (SI) module incorporates visual cues from images, producing more geometrically accurate and semantically enriched radar features. Experiments on View-of-Delft (VoD) and TJ4DRadSet show that RCGDet3D outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and speed, setting a new benchmark for real-time deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 4D汽车雷达因其低成本和高鲁棒性在自动驾驶中不可或缺，但其点云稀疏性给3D目标检测带来了挑战。现有的4D雷达-相机融合方法侧重于复杂的融合策略，以牺牲推理速度换取微小的性能提升。这种权衡因密集特征图上的大量计算而阻碍了实时部署。相比之下，从稀疏雷达点中提取特征计算量较小，但尚未被充分探索。本文发现，仅增强雷达特征提取就能达到甚至超过精巧融合模块的性能，同时保持实时性能。基于这一发现，我们提出了RCGDet3D，该方法以雷达特征编码为核心，简化了多模态融合。其编码器继承自RadarGaussianDet3D中高效的基于高斯泼溅的点高斯编码器（PGE），并做了两项关键改进。首先，射线中心PGE（R-PGE）在射线对齐坐标系中预测高斯属性，再将其统一到鸟瞰图（BEV）空间，通过将坐标变换与表示学习解耦，显著提升了几何一致性并降低了学习难度。其次，语义注入（SI）模块融合了图像的视觉线索，生成几何更精确、语义更丰富的雷达特征。在View-of-Delft（VoD）和TJ4DRadSet上的实验表明，RCGDet3D在精度和速度上均优于现有最先进方法，为实时部署设立了新的基准。

</details>


### [91] [ROAR-3D: Routing Arbitrary Views for High-Fidelity 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21121)
*Hanxiao Sun,Mingxin Yang,Shuhui Yang,Zebin He,Xintong Han,Hongbo Fu,Chunchao Guo,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROAR-3D 是一种轻量级方法，通过解耦视角控制与几何传输、设计双流注意力机制和视角路由策略，将预训练的单视图3D生成模型升级为可接受任意数量未配准多视图图像的模型，在极小参数量下实现多视图3D生成SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单视图3D生成存在不可见区域模糊性，多视图条件可减少歧义，但现有方法要么需要固定规范视角，要么依赖外部重建模块导致训练成本高、生成质量受限。作者观察到预训练单视图模型已具备强2D到3D基础能力，但其条件机制将视角控制与几何传输纠缠在一起，当来自不同视角的图像被简单组合时两者相互冲突。

Method: 提出ROAR-3D，核心包含三个组件：(1) 逐token视角路由器，将每个3D潜变量token分配给最相关的视图，隐式建立2D-3D对应关系无需显式位姿输入；(2) 双流注意力设计，保持预训练主视角行为的同时，通过独立路径处理辅助视图以专门进行几何强化；(3) 方向扰动策略，确保辅助路径学习与视角无关的几何传输。

Result: ROAR-3D在极小可训练参数量和相比于单视图基线可忽略的推理开销下，实现了多视图3D生成质量的最优结果，支持测试时视图数量从1到12+的扩展且性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 通过轻量化改造预训练单视图模型即可高效实现多视图条件3D生成，避免复杂的外部重建模块，验证了利用预训练模型已有2D到3D基础能力的可行性。

Original Abstract: Single-image-to-3D generative models can now produce high-quality geometry, yet conditioning on a single view inevitably introduces ambiguity about unseen regions. Multi-view conditioning can reduce this ambiguity, but existing methods either require fixed canonical viewpoints or rely on external reconstruction modules that impose heavy training costs and limit generation quality. We observe that pretrained single-view models already possess strong 2D-to-3D grounding that can be reused for multi-view conditioning. However, a closer analysis reveals that their conditioning mechanism entangles orientation control with geometry transfer, two functions that conflict when images from different viewpoints are naively combined. Based on this analysis, we propose ROAR-3D, a lightweight method that upgrades a pretrained single-view model to accept an arbitrary number of unposed images. A token-wise view router assigns each 3D latent token to its most relevant view, implicitly establishing 2D-to-3D correspondences without explicit pose input. A dual-stream attention design preserves the pretrained primary-view behavior while routing auxiliary views through a separate path dedicated to geometric enrichment. An orientation perturbation strategy ensures the auxiliary path learns orientation-independent geometry transfer. These components introduce minimal trainable parameters and add negligible inference overhead relative to the single-view baseline. ROAR-3D achieves state-of-the-art multi-view 3D generation quality and supports test-time view scaling from 1 to 12+ views with consistent improvements.

中文摘要翻译: 单图像到3D生成模型如今可以产生高质量的几何结构，但基于单视图的条件输入不可避免地引入了关于未见区域的模糊性。多视图条件输入可以减少这种歧义，但现有方法要么需要固定的规范视角，要么依赖外部重建模块，这带来了沉重的训练成本并限制了生成质量。我们观察到，预训练的单视图模型已经具备可复用于多视图条件输入的强大2D到3D基础能力。然而，进一步分析发现，它们的条件机制将方向控制与几何传输纠缠在一起，当来自不同视角的图像被简单组合时，这两种功能相互冲突。基于这一分析，我们提出了ROAR-3D，这是一种轻量级方法，可将预训练的单视图模型升级为接受任意数量的未配准图像。一个逐token的视角路由器将每个3D潜变量token分配给其最相关的视图，从而在无需显式位姿输入的情况下隐式建立2D到3D对应关系。双流注意力设计保持了预训练的主视角行为，同时通过一个专门用于几何强化的独立路径路由辅助视图。方向扰动策略确保辅助路径学习与视角无关的几何传输。这些组件引入极少的可训练参数，并且相对于单视图基线增加了可忽略的推理开销。ROAR-3D实现了多视图3D生成质量的最优水平，支持从1到12+视图的测试时视图扩展，且性能持续提升。

</details>


### [92] [Linear-DPO: Linear Direct Preference Optimization for Diffusion and Flow-Matching Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21123)
*Kesong Li,Yixuan Xu,Kuo-kun Tseng,Weiyi Lu,Kan Liu,Tao Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Linear-DPO，通过将标准DPO的sigmoid效用函数替换为线性效用函数并引入EMA更新参考模型，解决了DPO在文生图任务中（扩散模型和流匹配模型）的目标不匹配问题，实验证明其优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有DPO在文生图任务中存在两个问题：(1) 仅局限于去噪扩散模型，忽略了流匹配模型；(2) 将基于NLP的离散DPO直接应用于回归型生成任务存在目标不匹配问题。

Method: (1) 通过统一的逆向SDE框架推导出覆盖扩散模型和流匹配模型的广义DPO目标；(2) 从梯度角度指出标准DPO对文生图非最优；(3) 提出Linear-DPO：用持续的线性效用函数替代激进的sigmoid效用函数，并引入EMA更新的参考模型。

Result: 在扩散模型（SD1.5, SDXL）和流匹配模型（SD3-Medium）上的定性和定量实验均表明Linear-DPO优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: Linear-DPO通过线性效用函数和EMA参考模型有效解决了DPO在文生图任务中的目标不匹配问题，且兼容扩散和流匹配两种生成范式。

Original Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is successful for alignment in LLMs but still faces challenges in text-to-image generation. Existing studies are confined to denoising diffusion models while overlooking flow-matching, and suffer from an objective mismatch when applying discrete NLP-based DPO to regression-based generative tasks.\ In this paper, we derive a generalized DPO objective that covers both diffusion and flow-matching via a unified reverse-time SDE framework, and point out from a gradient perspective that the standard DPO objective is suboptimal for text-to-image generation. Consequently, we propose Linear-DPO, which replaces the aggressive sigmoid-based utility function with a sustained linear utility and incorporates an EMA-updated reference model. Qualitative and quantitative experiments on diffusion models (SD1.5, SDXL) and flow-matching model (SD3-Medium) demonstrate the superiority of our approach over existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 直接偏好优化（DPO）在大语言模型的对齐中取得了成功，但在文生图任务中仍面临挑战。现有研究局限于去噪扩散模型，忽略了流匹配模型，并且将基于NLP的离散DPO直接应用于回归型生成任务时存在目标不匹配问题。本文通过统一的逆向SDE框架推导出覆盖扩散模型和流匹配模型的广义DPO目标，并从梯度角度指出标准DPO目标对于文生图任务并非最优。因此，我们提出Linear-DPO，将激进的sigmoid基效用函数替换为持续的线性效用函数，并引入EMA更新的参考模型。在扩散模型（SD1.5, SDXL）和流匹配模型（SD3-Medium）上的定性和定量实验均证明了我们的方法优于现有基线。

</details>


### [93] [VersusQ: Pairwise Margin Reasoning for Generalizable Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21130)
*Shibei Meng,Binxin Yang,Yuan Liu,Jiexuan Zhang,Zhengyao Lv,Hubery Yin,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VersusQ，一个基于成对比较和边际推理的多模态视频质量评估框架，通过直接比较而非绝对评分来消除数据集校准偏差，结合Margin-Coupled GRPO实现细粒度排序，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能并展现强跨域泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视频质量评估方法大多预测每个视频的绝对分数，这种逐点监督方式将感知质量与数据集特定的校准偏差（如标注协议、评分习惯、分数分布）混合在一起，导致模型在未见领域上的迁移性能差。相对比较可以消除绝对尺度校准偏差，专注于纯粹的感知差异。

Method: 提出VersusQ框架，核心包括：(1) 基于LMM的成对比较推理，对两个视频进行视觉和时间质量差异比较，预测有符号的连续边际，同时反映偏好选择和差异程度；(2) Margin-Coupled GRPO，联合优化基于rollout的关系推理和连续边际回归，对齐可解释的比较理由与细粒度数值差异。

Result: 在多个公开VQA基准上，VersusQ达到了最先进的性能，展现了强大的跨域泛化能力，并且在异构评估场景下实现了可靠的细粒度排序。

Conclusion: 相对比较范式相较于绝对分数预测能有效消除数据集校准偏差，成对边际推理框架在视频质量评估任务上具有显著优势，在跨域泛化和细粒度排序方面均表现优异。

Original Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have shown promise for video quality assessment, but most methods still predict an absolute score for each video. Such pointwise supervision often mixes perceptual quality with dataset-specific calibration, including annotation protocols, rating habits, and score distributions. As a result, the learned scoring rule may work well within a benchmark but transfer poorly across unseen domains. We argue that relative comparisons alleviate the absolute-scale calibration bias by focusing purely on perceptual differences rather than dataset-specific rating habits. Consequently, we propose \textbf{VersusQ}, a pairwise margin reasoning framework driven entirely by direct comparisons. Specifically, VersusQ performs LMM-based comparison between two videos, reasons about their visual and temporal quality differences, and predicts a signed continuous margin that captures both the preferred choice and the degree of difference. Furthermore, to align interpretable comparison rationales with fine-grained numerical differences, we introduce Margin-Coupled GRPO, which jointly optimizes rollout-based relational reasoning and continuous margin regression. Extensive experiments on multiple public VQA benchmarks demonstrate that VersusQ achieves state-of-the-art performance, strong cross-domain generalization, and reliable fine-grained ranking under heterogeneous evaluation scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 大型多模态模型（LMMs）在视频质量评估中展现出潜力，但大多数方法仍然为每个视频预测一个绝对分数。这种逐点监督方式往往将感知质量与数据集特定的校准偏差（包括标注协议、评分习惯和分数分布）混合在一起。因此，学习到的评分规则可能在某个基准内表现良好，但在未见过的领域上迁移效果较差。我们认为，相对比较通过纯粹关注感知差异而非数据集特定的评分习惯，能够减轻绝对尺度校准偏差。因此，我们提出了\textbf{VersusQ}，一个完全由直接比较驱动的成对边际推理框架。具体而言，VersusQ对两个视频进行基于LMM的比较，推理它们的视觉和时间质量差异，并预测一个有符号的连续边际，该边际同时捕获了偏好选择和差异程度。此外，为了将可解释的比较理由与细粒度数值差异对齐，我们引入了Margin-Coupled GRPO，它联合优化了基于rollout的关系推理和连续边际回归。在多个公开VQA基准上的大量实验表明，VersusQ在异构评估场景下实现了最先进的性能、强大的跨域泛化能力以及可靠的细粒度排序。

</details>


### [94] [UniT: Unified Geometry Learning with Group Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21131)
*Haotian Wang,Yusong Huang,Zhaonian Kuang,Hongliang Lu,Xinhu Zheng,Meng Yang,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniT是一个基于分组自回归Transformer的统一3D几何感知模型，通过将传感器观测分组作为自回归单元，结合无锚点关系建模、尺度自适应几何损失和模态注意力模块，在七个代表性任务的十个基准上达到了最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈模型在稠密3D结构感知方面能力碎片化，分散在多个不兼容的范式（在线感知、离线重建、多模态融合、长时序扩展、度量尺度估计）中，缺乏统一的框架来整合这些能力。

Method: 提出UniT模型，核心创新包括：(1) 分组自回归Transformer，将传感器观测组作为基本自回归单元，无锚点、尺度自适应地预测点图；(2) 通过调整组大小统一在线（单帧组、多步自回归）和离线（多帧组、单次前向）模式；(3) 队列式KV缓存机制，通过无锚点关系建模减少早期帧的长程依赖，支持长时序有界记忆；(4) 尺度自适应几何损失，耦合相对几何约束与部分绝对尺度项；(5) 模态注意力模块用于集成辅助模态信息。

Result: 在七个代表性任务的十个基准上取得最先进性能，验证了统一几何感知框架的有效性。

Conclusion: UniT通过分组自回归框架成功将多种异构的3D几何感知能力统一在一个模型中，实现了在线/离线、多模态、长时序、度量尺度估计等能力的协同，为该领域提供了统一的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Recent feed-forward models have significantly advanced geometry perception for inferring dense 3D structure from sensor observations. However, its essential capabilities remain fragmented across multiple incompatible paradigms, including online perception, offline reconstruction, multi-modal integration, long-horizon scalability, and metric-scale estimation. We present UniT, a unified model built upon a novel Group Autoregressive Transformer, which reformulates these seemingly disparate capabilities within a single framework. The key idea is to treat groups of sensor observations as the basic autoregressive units and predict the corresponding point maps in an anchor-free and scale-adaptive manner. More specifically, diverse view configurations in both online and offline settings are naturally unified within a single group autoregression process. By varying the group size, online mode operates over multiple autoregressive steps with single-frame groups, whereas offline mode aggregates a multi-frame group in a single forward pass. Meanwhile, a queue-style KV caching mechanism ensures bounded autoregressive memory over long horizons. This is enabled by reducing long-range dependencies on early frames through anchor-free relational modeling, thereby allowing outdated memory to be discarded on the fly. To improve metric-scale generalization across scenes, a scale-adaptive geometry loss is further introduced within this framework. It couples relative geometric constraints with a partial absolute scale term, implicitly regularizing global scale and inducing a progressive transition from scale-invariant geometry to metric-scale solutions. Together with a dedicated modal attention module for integrating auxiliary modalities, UniT achieves state-of-the-art performance in unified geometry perception, as validated on ten benchmarks spanning seven representative tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，前馈模型在从传感器观测推断稠密3D结构的几何感知方面取得了显著进展。然而，其核心能力仍分散在多个不兼容的范式中，包括在线感知、离线重建、多模态融合、长时序扩展和度量尺度估计。本文提出UniT，一种基于新型分组自回归Transformer的统一模型，将上述看似分散的能力重新整合到单一框架中。其关键思想是将传感器观测组视为基本自回归单元，以无锚点和尺度自适应方式预测对应的点图。具体而言，在线和离线设置中的多种视角配置被自然统一在单一分组自回归过程中。通过调整组大小，在线模式以单帧组在多个自回归步骤中运行，而离线模式则在单次前向传播中聚合多帧组。同时，队列式KV缓存机制确保了长时序下的有界自回归记忆。这是通过无锚点关系建模减少对早期帧的长程依赖实现的，从而允许实时丢弃过时记忆。为提升跨场景的度量尺度泛化能力，该框架进一步引入了尺度自适应几何损失。它将相对几何约束与部分绝对尺度项耦合，隐式正则化全局尺度，并引导从尺度不变几何到度量尺度解的渐进过渡。结合用于集成辅助模态的专用模态注意力模块，UniT在涵盖七个代表性任务的十个基准上取得了统一几何感知的最先进性能。

</details>


### [95] [SurgOnAir: Hierarchy-Aware Real-Time Surgical Video Commentary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21132)
*Jingyi He,Yue Zhou,Long Bai,Kun Yuan,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgOnAir是一个流式视觉语言模型，能够逐帧处理手术视频并实时生成多层级（动作、步骤、阶段）的文字叙述，同时生成状态转换标记以捕捉手术流程中的关键变化，实现了细粒度、层级感知的实时手术工作流理解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有手术工作流理解方法多为离线处理或粗粒度分析，只能在处理完视频片段后生成描述，无法实现即时响应，难以满足手术场景中对细微器械运动和组织状态变化的实时感知需求。

Method: 提出SurgOnAir流式视觉语言模型，逐帧顺序处理视频（无未来帧访问），渐进式生成叙述token；基于构建的层级数据集SurgOnAir-11k（包含动作、步骤、阶段三级标注）进行训练；引入特殊转换token显式标记状态变化，实现多层级统一流式理解。

Result: 实验表明SurgOnAir能够通过单一视觉语言模型实现手术工作流跨多层级（动作、步骤、阶段）的实时流式理解，生成优于现有方法且具有层级感知能力的叙述。

Conclusion: SurgOnAir首次实现了基于单一视觉语言模型的多层级实时手术工作流理解，通过流式生成和转换token机制有效支持手术过程中的即时感知与响应，为智能手术系统提供了关键技术基础。

Original Abstract: Understanding surgical workflow in real time is fundamental for intelligent surgical embodiment, where AI systems continuously perceive and respond as surgery proceeds. In the operating room, critical decisions depend on subtle, moment-to-moment changes, such as fine instrument movements and evolving tissue states, where even slight perceptual delays can limit assistance or compromise safety. Yet existing methods remain offline or operate at coarse temporal scales, generating descriptions only after processing clips, preventing immediate reaction. We address this by proposing SurgOnAir, a streaming vision-language model that processes frames sequentially without future access and progressively generates narration tokens as visual input arrives. SurgOnAir achieves fine-grained frame-to-token generation, enabling instant responsiveness to evolving surgical dynamics. Built upon our curated hierarchical dataset SurgOnAir-11k spanning action-, step-, and phase-level supervision, the model is trained to produce multi-level textual responses that reflect the inherent hierarchy of surgical procedures. Furthermore, special transition tokens are generated to explicitly mark state changes, allowing SurgOnAir to capture and signal key workflow transitions as they occur. Experiments show that SurgOnAir enables real-time understanding through a single vision-language model that unifies streaming across multiple hierarchies of the surgical workflow, generating superior and hierarchy-aware narrations. Code and dataset will be public.

中文摘要翻译: 实时理解手术工作流是实现智能手术具身化的基础，即AI系统在手术进行过程中持续感知并做出响应。在手术室中，关键决策依赖于细微的、逐帧变化的信息（如精细的器械运动和变化的组织状态），即使是轻微的感知延迟也可能限制辅助效果或危及安全。然而，现有方法仍停留在离线处理或粗粒度时间尺度上，仅在处理完视频片段后才生成描述，无法实现即时反应。为解决这一问题，我们提出SurgOnAir——一个流式视觉语言模型，该模型逐帧顺序处理图像（无未来帧访问），并随着视觉输入到达而渐进式生成叙述标记。SurgOnAir实现了细粒度的帧到标记生成，能够对不断变化的手术动态进行即时响应。基于我们精心构建的层级数据集SurgOnAir-11k（涵盖动作、步骤和阶段三级标注），该模型被训练生成多层级的文本响应，以反映手术过程固有的层次结构。此外，模型生成特殊的转换标记显式标记状态变化，使SurgOnAir能够实时捕获并指示关键的工作流转换。实验表明，SurgOnAir通过一个统一的视觉语言模型实现了手术工作流多层级（动作、步骤、阶段）的流式理解，生成优于现有方法的、具有层级感知能力的叙述。代码和数据集将开源。

</details>


### [96] [Distill to Think, Foresee to Act: Cognitive-Physical Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21139)
*Yang Wu,Qiang Meng,Zhaojiang Liu,Youquan Liu,Jian Yang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoPhy提出了一种认知-物理双驱动的强化学习框架，通过蒸馏VLM知识到BEV编码器实现零推理成本的认知能力，并构建自回归BEV世界模型作为可解释的物理沙盒，结合双重奖励机制（物理奖励+认知奖励）优化驾驶策略，在NAVSIM基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶模型受限于模仿学习的行为克隆天花板，而强化学习缺乏两个关键基础设施：(1)理解交通语义和驾驶意图的认知基础；(2)能预判候选动作后果的前瞻性物理环境。

Method: (1)认知通道：将VLM知识蒸馏到BEV编码器后完全丢弃VLM，实现零推理成本的认知能力，且认知通道可作为可插拔接口接收人类语言指令。(2)物理通道：构建自回归BEV世界模型，显式预测基于候选动作的未来语义地图，作为可解释的物理沙盒并直接导出安全指标。(3)优化策略：采用GRPO和双重奖励机制——来自BEV rollout的物理奖励强制执行硬安全约束，来自语言对齐评分器的认知奖励确保意图符合性。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上达到SOTA结果；通过认知信息驱动的场景合规性实现更安全的驾驶；支持通过用户定义的语言指令进行灵活的意图控制。

Conclusion: CoPhy通过认知-物理双基础设施的强化学习框架成功突破了模仿学习的性能上限，同时保持了零推理成本的认知能力和可解释的物理安全约束，为自动驾驶提供了更智能、更安全、更可控的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Current end-to-end autonomous driving models are fundamentally constrained by the behavioral cloning ceiling of imitation learning. While reinforcement learning offers a path to smarter autonomy, it demands two missing pieces of infrastructure: (1) a cognitive foundation that understands traffic semantics and driving intent, and (2) a foresighted physical environment that can anticipate the consequences of candidate actions. To this end, we propose CoPhy, a CognitivePhysical reinforcement learning framework for autonomous driving. To distill to think, we distill VLM knowledge into the BEV encoder and then discard the VLM entirely, retaining cognitive ability at zero inference cost while releasing the cognitive channel as a pluggable interface for optional human language commands. To foresee to act, we build an auto-regressive BEV world model that explicitly predicts future semantic maps conditioned on candidate actions, serving as an interpretable physical sandbox from which safety metrics are directly derived. Built upon this dual infrastructure, we optimize the driving policy via GRPO with a novel dual-reward mechanism: a physical reward derived from BEV rollouts enforces hard safety constraints, while a cognitive reward from a language-aligned scorer ensures intent compliance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoPhy not only achieves state-of-the-art results on NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, but also enables safer driving via cognitively informed scene compliance and flexible intent control through user-defined language instructions.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的端到端自动驾驶模型从根本上受到模仿学习的行为克隆天花板的限制。虽然强化学习提供了实现更智能自主性的途径，但它需要两个缺失的基础设施：(1)一个理解交通语义和驾驶意图的认知基础，以及(2)一个能够预判候选动作后果的前瞻性物理环境。为此，我们提出了CoPhy，一个用于自动驾驶的认知-物理强化学习框架。为提炼思考能力，我们将视觉语言模型（VLM）的知识蒸馏到BEV编码器中，然后完全丢弃VLM，以零推理成本保留认知能力，同时将认知通道作为可插拔接口用于可选的人类语言指令。为预判行动，我们构建了一个自回归BEV世界模型，该模型显式地预测基于候选动作的未来语义地图，作为一个可解释的物理沙盒，从中直接导出安全指标。基于这一双基础设施，我们通过GRPO结合一种新颖的双重奖励机制来优化驾驶策略：来自BEV rollout的物理奖励强制执行硬安全约束，而来自语言对齐评分器的认知奖励确保意图符合性。大量实验表明，CoPhy不仅在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上取得了最先进的结果，而且通过认知信息驱动的场景合规性实现了更安全的驾驶，并通过用户定义的语言指令实现了灵活的意图控制。

</details>


### [97] [Comparative Analysis of Military Detection Using Drone Imagery Across Multiple Visual Spectrums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21157)
*Sourov Roy Shuvo,Prajwal Panth,Rajesh Chowdhury,Sorup Chakraborty,Sudip Chakrabarty,Prasant Kumar Pattnaik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机军事目标检测中现有数据集缺乏真实场景多样性的问题，构建了四种模拟不同环境条件（灰度、热成像、夜视、遮蔽视觉）的数据集，并基于YOLOv11-small模型进行训练与检测，以提升无人机在多样战场环境下的目标检测性能与可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有KIIT-MiTA数据集虽为无人机军事目标检测提供了基础，但未覆盖多种真实战场场景（如低可见度、热成像、夜间等），因此需要构建多样化环境数据集来评估和提升模型在复杂条件下的检测能力。

Method: 构建了四种模拟真实环境的数据集：灰度视觉、热成像视觉、夜视视觉和遮蔽视觉；采用YOLOv11-small模型在这些数据集上进行训练和物体检测。
