Result: 通过在四种不同环境条件下的数据集上训练并评估YOLOv11-small模型，验证了该模型在多样化场景下的目标检测性能，提升了无人机在防御与进攻任务中的检测系统可靠性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过构建多样化的环境数据集并基于YOLOv11-small进行检测，显著提升了无人机在多种复杂战场环境下的目标检测性能与可靠性，对无人机攻防任务中的先进检测系统发展具有重要贡献。

Original Abstract: In modern warfare, drones are becoming an essential part of intelligence gathering and carrying out precise attacks in different kinds of hostile environments. Their ability to operate in real-time and hostile environments from a safe distance makes them invaluable for surveillance and military operations. The KIIT-MiTA dataset is comprised of images of different military scenarios taken from drones, and these provide a foundation for detecting military objects, but it does not take into account the various types of real-world scenarios. With that in mind, to evaluate how the models are performing under varying conditions, four different types of datasets are created: Gray Scale, Thermal Vision, Night Vision, and Obscura Vision. These simulate the real-world environments such as low visibility, heat-based imagery, and nighttime conditions. The YOLOv11-small model is trained and used to detect objects across diverse settings. This research boosts the performance and reliability of drone-based operations by contributing to the development of advanced detection systems in both defensive and offensive missions.

中文摘要翻译: 在现代战争中，无人机已成为在各类敌对环境中执行情报收集和精确打击任务的重要组成部分。它们能够在安全距离外实时运行于敌对环境的能力，使其在监视和军事行动中具有不可替代的价值。KIIT-MiTA数据集包含从无人机拍摄的不同军事场景图像，为军事目标检测提供了基础，但未充分考虑各种真实场景类型。为此，为评估模型在不同条件下的表现，我们创建了四种不同类型的数据集：灰度视觉、热成像视觉、夜视视觉和遮蔽视觉。这些数据集模拟了低可见度、热基成像和夜间条件等真实战场环境。采用YOLOv11-small模型进行训练，并在多种环境下检测目标。本研究通过促进防御与进攻任务中先进检测系统的发展，提升了无人机任务的性能与可靠性。

</details>


### [98] [FTerViT: Fully Ternary Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21171)
*Szymon Ruciński,Pietro Bonazzi,Engin Türetken,Simon Narduzzi,Michele Magno,Nadim Maamari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出全三值化视觉Transformer（FTerViT），将所有权重矩阵和归一化参数三值化，并引入TernaryBitConv2d和TernaryLayerNorm两个新算子，配合知识蒸馏和量化感知恢复训练，在ESP32-S3微控制器上首次实现了三值化ViT的部署，达到79.64%的ImageNet-1K Top-1准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有三值化ViT方法仅将编码器层三值化，而patch嵌入、LayerNorm参数和分类头仍保留全精度。在面向微控制器等资源受限处理器的紧凑模型中，这些剩余的全精度组件决定了总内存占用，严重限制了部署效率和设备端可行性。

Method: 提出FTerViT，将所有权重矩阵和归一化参数三值化；引入两个新算子：带逐通道缩放的三值化卷积（TernaryBitConv2d）用于patch嵌入，以及三值化LayerNorm（TernaryLayerNorm）；采用知识蒸馏训练，随后进行轻量级量化感知恢复（QAR）阶段。

Result: W2A8的DeiT-III-S在384×384分辨率下达到82.43% ImageNet-1K Top-1准确率，仅6.09MB（约15倍压缩，相比FP32下降2.42个百分点），优于先前三值化ViT方法最多8个百分点；在ESP32-S3双核XTensa LX7微控制器上部署FTerViT-Small（224×224，5.81MB），达到79.64% Top-1准确率。

Conclusion: 全三值化ViT能够在保持高精度的同时实现极致的模型压缩，并首次在真实微控制器上成功部署，证明了在资源极度受限的物联网设备上运行视觉Transformer的可行性。

Original Abstract: Ternary Vision Transformers offer substantial model compression, however state-of-the-art methods only ternarize the encoder layers, leaving patch embeddings, LayerNorm parameters, and classifier heads in full precision. In compact models targeting resource-constrained processors, such as microcontrollers, these remaining full-precision components determine the total memory footprint, severely limiting deployment efficiency and on-device feasibility. In this work, we introduce a fully ternarized Vision Transformer in which \emph{all} weight matrices and normalization parameters are ternarized (FTerViT). To this end, we introduce two novel operators : TernaryBitConv2d with per-channel scaling for patch embedding and TernaryLayerNorm. FTerViT is trained using knowledge distillation, followed by a lightweight quantization-aware recovery phase. Our ternary W2A8 DeiT-III-S at 384$\times$384 resolution achieves 82.43\% ImageNet-1K top-1 at 6.09\,MB (${\sim}$15$\times$ compression, $-$2.42\,pp vs.\ FP32), outperforming prior ternary ViTs methods up to 8 pp. Finally, we demonstrate the first implementation of ternary vision transformers on a dual cores XTensa LX7 microcontroller inside the ESP32-S3 system-on-chip. By deploying FTerViT-Small (based on DeiT-III-Small at 224$\times$224 resolution, 5.81\,MB), we achieve 79.64\% ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 三元视觉Transformer提供了显著的模型压缩，然而最先进的方法仅将编码器层进行三值化，而patch嵌入、LayerNorm参数和分类头仍保留全精度。在面向资源受限处理器（如微控制器）的紧凑模型中，这些剩余的全精度组件决定了总内存占用，严重限制了部署效率和设备端可行性。本文提出了一种全三值化视觉Transformer（FTerViT），其中\emph{所有}权重矩阵和归一化参数均被三值化。为此，我们引入了两个新算子：带逐通道缩放的TernaryBitConv2d用于patch嵌入，以及TernaryLayerNorm。FTerViT采用知识蒸馏训练，随后进行轻量级量化感知恢复阶段。我们的W2A8 DeiT-III-S在384$\times$384分辨率下在ImageNet-1K上达到82.43\%的Top-1准确率，仅占用6.09\,MB（约15倍压缩，相比FP32下降2.42个百分点），优于先前三值化ViT方法最多8个百分点。最后，我们首次在ESP32-S3系统级芯片内部的双核XTensa LX7微控制器上实现了三值化视觉Transformer的部署。通过部署FTerViT-Small（基于DeiT-III-Small，224$\times$224分辨率，5.81\,MB），我们在ImageNet-1K上取得了79.64\%的Top-1准确率。

</details>


### [99] [SAM-Sode: Towards Faithful Explanations for Tiny Bacteria Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21186)
*Wanying Tan,Shuo Yan,Dazhi Huang,Yazheng Liu,Zili Shao,Rufeng Chen,Hechang Chen,Mude Shi,Tianxing Ji,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对微小细菌检测中传统可解释性方法前景边界模糊和特征归因弥散的问题，提出SAM-Sode框架，利用基础模型SAM的几何感知先验知识对特征归因图进行空间细化和形态重构，并引入基于物理意义和几何对齐的双重约束机制进行实例级去噪，生成更符合专家直觉的一致性解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 微小细菌检测中，目标形态特征极端稀疏且复杂背景干扰严重，传统可解释性方法在前景边界模糊和特征归因弥散方面存在局限，无法提供逻辑一致的形态学证据，影响临床辅助诊断的可信度。

Method: 提出SAM-Sode框架：(1) 将初始特征归因图创新性地转化为几何感知提示，利用基础模型SAM3的先验知识对解释映射进行空间细化和形态重构；(2) 引入基于物理意义和几何对齐的双重约束机制，进行实例级去噪，生成与人类专家直觉更一致的一致性解释。

Result: 在自建的含复杂电路背景的细菌数据集（2,524张图像）及其他公开数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法有效抑制了背景冗余，显著提升了微小目标检测的决策透明性。

Conclusion: SAM-Sode框架能够有效解决微小细菌检测中传统可解释性方法的缺陷，通过几何感知提示和双重约束机制生成更清晰、更符合专家直觉的解释，提升了微小目标检测的决策透明度。

Original Abstract: Interpretability in object detection provides crucial confidence support for clinical auxiliary diagnosis. However, in tiny bacteria detection, traditional explanation methods often suffer from blurred foreground boundaries and diffuse feature attribution due to the extreme sparsity of target morphological features and severe interference from complex backgrounds. Such limitations hinder the provision of logically coherent morphological evidence. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel eXplainable AI (XAI) framework, SAM-Sode. The framework innovatively transforms initial feature attribution maps into geometry-aware prompts, leveraging the prior knowledge of the foundation model (SAM3) to achieve spatial refinement and morphological reconstruction of the explanatory mappings. Furthermore, we introduce a dual-constraint mechanism based on physical significance and geometric alignment to perform instance-level denoising, generating coherent explanations that better align with human expert intuition. Experimental results on our self-constructed bacteria dataset with complex circuit backgrounds (containing 2,524 images) and other public datasets demonstrate that the proposed method effectively suppresses background redundancy and significantly enhances the decision-making transparency of tiny object detection.

中文摘要翻译: 在目标检测中，可解释性为临床辅助诊断提供了关键的置信度支持。然而，在微小细菌检测中，由于目标形态特征的极端稀疏性以及复杂背景的严重干扰，传统解释方法常面临前景边界模糊和特征归因弥散的问题。这些局限性阻碍了提供逻辑连贯的形态学证据。为弥补这一差距，我们提出了一种新颖的可解释人工智能（XAI）框架——SAM-Sode。该框架创新性地将初始特征归因图转化为几何感知提示，利用基础模型（SAM3）的先验知识实现对解释映射的空间细化和形态重构。此外，我们引入了基于物理意义和几何对齐的双重约束机制进行实例级去噪，生成与人类专家直觉更加一致的一致性解释。我们在自建的含复杂电路背景的细菌数据集（包含2,524张图像）及其他公开数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法有效抑制了背景冗余，显著增强了微小目标检测的决策透明性。

</details>


### [100] [RankE: End-to-End Post-Training for Discrete Text-to-Image Generation with Decoder Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21195)
*Siyong Jian,Siyuan Li,Luyuan Zhang,Zedong Wang,Xin Jin,Ying Li,Cheng Tan,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对离散自回归文生图模型中策略优化导致解码器输入分布偏移（潜在协变量偏移）的问题，提出首个端到端后训练框架RankE，通过交替联合优化策略和解码器，打破忠实度-对齐度权衡，同时提升FID和CLIP指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有离散自回归文生图模型的后训练管线仅优化策略（policy）而冻结VQ解码器，但策略进化会导致其生成的token分布偏离解码器训练时所见的真实分布（潜在协变量偏移），使得奖励分数提升而解码图像质量下降。作者类比扩散模型中的VAE对齐瓶颈，提出需要对解码器也进行优化。

Method: 提出RankE端到端后训练框架：采用交替优化（alternating optimization）策略，让策略和解码器协同进化。每个模块最大化基于排序的对齐目标（ranking-based alignment objective），同时用适合其参数空间的稳定性保持锚点（stability-preserving anchor）进行正则化，防止优化过度偏移。

Result: 在LlamaGen-XL (775M)上，标准RL提升CLIP但恶化FID，而RankE同时改善两者（MS-COCO 30K: FID 15.21, CLIP 33.76）。在Janus-Pro (1B)上的一致提升验证了解码器协同进化能将奖励优化可靠转化为像素级质量改进。

Conclusion: 解码器协同进化是离散自回归文生图模型后训练的关键环节，RankE通过联合优化策略和解码器有效解决了忠实度-对齐度权衡问题，将奖励优化转化为实际的像素空间质量提升。

Original Abstract: Discrete autoregressive (AR) text-to-image (T2I) models pair a VQ tokenizer with an AR policy, and current post-training pipelines optimize only the policy while keeping the VQ decoder frozen. Recent diffusion T2I work, exemplified by REPA-E, has shown that the VAE itself constitutes a key alignment bottleneck, yet no analogous investigation exists for discrete AR models. We show that policy-only optimization induces Latent Covariate Shift: as the policy evolves, the resulting token distribution diverges from the ground-truth distribution on which the decoder was trained, such that reward scores improve while decoded image quality degrades. To address this mismatch, we propose RankE, the first end-to-end post-training framework for discrete T2I generation. Rather than optimizing the policy against a fixed decoder, RankE co-evolves both components through alternating optimization: each module maximizes a ranking-based alignment objective while being regularized by a stability-preserving anchor suited to its parameter space. This co-evolution breaks the fidelity--alignment trade-off that plagues frozen-decoder approaches: on LlamaGen-XL (775M), standard RL improves CLIP but degrades FID, whereas RankE improves both simultaneously (FID 15.21, CLIP 33.76 on MS-COCO 30K). Consistent gains on Janus-Pro (1B) confirm that decoder co-evolution reliably converts reward optimization into pixel-space quality improvements.

中文摘要翻译: 离散自回归（AR）文生图（T2I）模型将VQ分词器与AR策略配对，当前的后训练管线仅优化策略而冻结VQ解码器。最近的扩散T2I工作（以REPA-E为代表）表明，VAE本身构成了关键的对齐瓶颈，然而针对离散AR模型尚无类似研究。我们证明，仅优化策略会引发潜在协变量偏移（Latent Covariate Shift）：随着策略进化，所生成的token分布偏离了解码器训练时所依赖的真实分布，导致奖励分数提升而解码图像质量下降。为解决这一失配问题，我们提出RankE——首个面向离散T2I生成的端到端后训练框架。RankE并非针对固定解码器优化策略，而是通过交替优化让两个模块协同进化：每个模块最大化基于排序的对齐目标，同时用适合其参数空间的稳定性保持锚点进行正则化。这种协同进化打破了困扰冻结解码器方法的忠实度-对齐度权衡：在LlamaGen-XL（775M）上，标准RL提升了CLIP但恶化了FID，而RankE同时改善了两者（MS-COCO 30K: FID 15.21, CLIP 33.76）。在Janus-Pro（1B）上的一致性增益证实，解码器协同进化能够可靠地将奖励优化转化为像素空间的质量提升。

</details>


### [101] [SR-Ground: Image Quality Grounding for Super-Resolved Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21244)
*Artem Borisov,Evgeney Bogatyrev,Khaled Abud,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SR-Ground，一个用于超分辨率图像细粒度伪影分割的大规模数据集，包含63,000张图像和6种伪影类型，并通过1,062名参与者的众包验证确保质量。基于该数据集训练的IQA模型具备可解释的定位能力，并可用于减少SR输出中的可感知伪影。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超分辨率方法（尤其是扩散模型）虽然取得了前所未有的保真度，但引入了新型视觉伪影。而现有的图像质量评估方法只能提供整体质量分数，缺乏可解释性，无法区分不同类型的伪影。因此，需要一种细粒度的伪影分割数据集和方法来填补这一空白。

Method: 1）构建SR-Ground数据集：使用多种SOTA超分辨率模型处理图像，生成像素级伪影标注，涵盖6种伪影类型；2）开展大规模众包研究（1,062名参与者）验证和优化自动生成的分割结果；3）基于该数据集训练具有定位能力的IQA模型；4）引入微调流程，利用定位模型减少SR输出中的可感知伪影。

Result: 在SR-Ground上训练具有定位能力的IQA模型显著提升了下游任务的性能；所提出的微调流程能够有效减少超分辨率输出中的可感知伪影，证明了数据集的实用价值。

Conclusion: SR-Ground数据集能够赋予IQA模型细粒度的伪影定位能力，不仅提升了质量评估性能，还可用于优化超分辨率模型输出，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用意义。

Original Abstract: Super-Resolution (SR) has advanced rapidly in recent years, with diffusion-based models achieving unprecedented fidelity at the cost of introducing new types of visual artifacts. While existing Image Quality Assessment (IQA) methods provide holistic quality scores, they lack interpretability and fail to distinguish between different artifact types arising from modern SR approaches.
  To address this gap, we introduce SR-Ground, a large-scale dataset specifically designed for fine-grained artifact segmentation in super-resolved images. The dataset comprises images processed by a diverse set of state-of-the-art SR models, with pixel-level annotations for multiple artifact categories. We conduct a large-scale crowdsourcing study involving 1,062 participants to validate and refine automatically generated segmentations, resulting in a high-quality dataset of 63,000 images spanning 6 distinct artifact types.
  We demonstrate that training IQA models with grounding capabilities on SR-Ground significantly improves performance on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we introduce a fine-tuning pipeline that leverages our grounding model to reduce perceptible artifacts in SR outputs, showcasing the practical utility of our dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 超分辨率（SR）技术在近年取得了快速发展，基于扩散的模型在实现前所未有的保真度的同时，也引入了新型的视觉伪影。现有的图像质量评估（IQA）方法虽然能提供整体质量分数，但缺乏可解释性，无法区分现代SR方法产生的不同伪影类型。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了SR-Ground，一个专门用于超分辨率图像细粒度伪影分割的大规模数据集。该数据集涵盖了由多种最先进SR模型处理的图像，并包含多个伪影类别的像素级标注。我们开展了一项涉及1,062名参与者的大规模众包研究，以验证和优化自动生成的分割结果，最终形成了一个包含63,000张图像、涵盖6种不同伪影类型的高质量数据集。我们证明，在SR-Ground上训练具有定位能力的IQA模型能够显著提升下游任务的性能。此外，我们引入了一个微调流程，利用我们的定位模型来减少SR输出中的可感知伪影，展示了该数据集的实用价值。

</details>


### [102] [STiTch: Semantic Transition and Transportation in Collaboration for Training-Free Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21261)
*Miaoge Li,Dongsheng Wang,Zening Sun,Jinsen Zhang,Wenhan Luo,Jingcai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的零样本组合图像检索方法ST2，通过语义过渡向量精炼LLM生成的描述，并利用双向运输距离实现集合到集合的跨模态对齐，提升检索性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于LLM的零样本组合图像检索方法存在两个问题：1) LLM生成的描述因参考图像与文本修饰之间的语义差距而引入无关特征；2) 检索阶段的点对点对齐无法捕获多样化的组合信息。

Method: 1) 利用LLM生成组合描述后，通过过渡向量在嵌入空间中精炼描述，使其更接近目标图像，聚焦核心修饰意图。2) 将描述和图像建模为离散分布，将检索任务转化为集合到集合的对齐任务。3) 提出双向运输距离，实现跨模态的细粒度对齐并计算检索分数。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法具有通用性、有效性和有益性，适用于多种组合图像检索任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义过渡与运输协作框架有效解决了零样本CIR中描述噪声和点对点对齐不足的问题，是一种通用且有效的检索方案。

Original Abstract: Training-free zero-shot composed image retrieval models are recently gaining increasing research interest due to their generalizability and flexibility in unseen multimodal retrieval. Recent LLM-based advances focus on generating the expected target caption by exploring the compositional ability behind the LLMs. Although efficient, we find that 1) the generated captions tend to introduce unexpected features from the reference image due to the semantic gap between the input image and text modification, where the image contains much more details than the text; 2) the point-to-point alignment during the retrieval stage fails to capture diverse compositions. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel Semantic Transition and Transportation in collaboration framework for training-free zero-shot CIR tasks. Specifically, given the composed caption inferred by an LLM, we aim to refine it through a transition vector in the embedding space and make it closer to the target image. Combining LLMs with user instruction, the refined caption concentrates more on the core modification intent and thus filters out unnecessary noise. Moreover, to explore diverse alignment during the retrieval stage, we model the caption and image as discrete distributions and reformulate the retrieval task as a set-to-set alignment task. Finally, a bidirectional transportation distance is developed to consider fine-grained alignments across modalities and calculate the retrieval score. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method can be general, effective, and beneficial for many CIR tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，无需训练的零样本组合图像检索模型因其在未见多模态检索中的泛化性和灵活性而受到越来越多的研究关注。最近基于大语言模型（LLM）的研究通过探索LLM背后的组合能力来生成期望的目标描述。尽管高效，但我们发现：1）由于输入图像与文本修饰之间的语义差距，图像包含的细节远多于文本，生成的描述往往引入参考图像中的意外特征；2）检索阶段的点对点对齐无法捕获多样化的组合信息。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的语义过渡与运输协作框架，用于无需训练的零样本组合图像检索任务。具体而言，给定由LLM推断出的组合描述，我们旨在通过嵌入空间中的过渡向量对其进行精炼，使其更接近目标图像。结合LLM与用户指令，精炼后的描述更聚焦于核心修饰意图，从而过滤掉不必要的噪声。此外，为了探索检索阶段的多样化对齐，我们将描述和图像建模为离散分布，并将检索任务重新表述为集合到集合的对齐任务。最后，我们开发了一种双向运输距离，以考虑跨模态的细粒度对齐并计算检索分数。大量实验表明，我们的方法具有通用性、有效性和有益性，适用于多种组合图像检索任务。

</details>


### [103] [MONET: A Massive, Open, Non-redundant and Enriched Text-to-image dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21272)
*Benjamin Aubin,Gonzalo Iñaki Quintana,Onur Tasar,Sanjeev Sreetharan,Urszula Czerwinska,Damien Henry,Clément Chadebec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MONET是一个包含约1.049亿图像-文本对的开源数据集，通过多阶段过滤和重标注从29亿原始对中构建，并验证了在该数据集上训练的4B参数扩散模型能达到有竞争力的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大规模文本到图像训练数据的收集、过滤、去重和重标注流程成本高昂且复杂，阻碍了该领域的开放和可复现研究。

Method: 从异构开源来源收集29亿原始图像-文本对，依次经过安全过滤、领域过滤、精确与近似去重，使用多种视觉语言模型进行短到长描述的重新标注，并补充合成样本，最终形成104.9M对的数据集，同时提供预计算的嵌入和标注以加速下游使用。

Result: 基于MONET训练的4B参数潜在扩散模型在GenEval和DPG评分上达到了有竞争力的水平，证明了该数据集能够降低大规模可复现文本到图像研究的门槛。

Conclusion: MONET数据集通过开源的方式有效降低了大规模文本到图像研究的门槛，支持可复现的研究。

Original Abstract: Training large text-to-image models requires high-quality, curated datasets with diverse content and detailed captions. Yet the cost and complexity of collecting, filtering, deduplicating, and re-captioning such corpora at scale hinders open and reproducible research in the field. We introduce MONET, an open Apache 2.0 dataset of approx. 104.9M image--text pairs collected from 2.9B raw pairs across heterogeneous open sources through successive stages of safety filtering, domain-based filtering, exact and near-duplicate removal, and re-captioning with multiple vision-language models covering short to long-form descriptions, and further augmented with synthetically generated samples. Each image is shipped with pre-computed embeddings and annotations to accelerate downstream use. To validate the effectiveness of MONET, we train a 4B-parameter latent diffusion model exclusively on it and reach competitive GenEval and DPG scores, demonstrating that our dataset lowers the barrier to large-scale, reproducible text-to-image research.

中文摘要翻译: 训练大规模文本到图像模型需要高质量、精心策划的数据集，这些数据集应具有多样的内容和详细的描述。然而，在大规模下收集、过滤、去重和重标注此类语料库的成本和复杂性阻碍了该领域开放和可复现的研究。我们推出了MONET，一个开放的Apache 2.0数据集，包含约1.049亿图像-文本对，这些对是从异构开源来源的29亿原始对中，经过安全过滤、基于领域的过滤、精确与近似去重、以及使用多种覆盖短到长描述的视觉语言模型进行重标注，并进一步通过合成生成的样本进行增强而得到的。每张图像都附带预计算的嵌入和标注，以加速下游使用。为了验证MONET的有效性，我们仅在其上训练了一个4B参数的潜在扩散模型，并达到了有竞争力的GenEval和DPG评分，证明我们的数据集降低了大规模、可复现文本到图像研究的门槛。

</details>


### [104] [DriveMA: Rethinking Language Interfaces in Driving VLAs with One-Step Meta-Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21273)
*Weicheng Zheng,Yixin Huang,Qiao Sun,Derun Li,Hang zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveMA，用一步元动作替代冗长的自然语言推理作为驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型的语言接口，结合动作中心监督训练和回合级强化学习，在Waymo端到端驾驶挑战中取得新SOTA（2B模型RFS 8.060，4B模型RFS 8.079），同时比自然语言推理具有更好的表达力-可预测性-推理效率权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有驾驶VLA使用自然语言推理作为中间接口存在三个实际瓶颈：高质量推理标注难以获取、紧凑模型难以生成和理解长推理链、推理延迟显著增加。因此需要重新设计语言接口，寻找更简洁有效的替代方案。

Method: 提出DriveMA框架：(1) 使用一步元动作（concise one-step meta-actions）替代冗长的自然语言推理，元动作可自动从专家轨迹导出，提供语义决策基础且低熵；(2) 结合动作中心的监督训练；(3) 提出回合级信用分配强化学习框架，联合优化元动作正确性、轨迹质量和轨迹-元动作一致性。

Result: 2B模型在Waymo端到端驾驶挑战中达到RFS 8.060，4B模型进一步提升至8.079，均为新SOTA；在NAVSIM上也取得有竞争力性能。消融实验表明一步元动作在表达力、可预测性和推理效率之间比自然语言推理或更细粒度动作序列提供更好的实际权衡。

Conclusion: 一步元动作是驾驶VLA中冗长推理的一种简单而有效的替代方案，能够实现可扩展的监督和可靠的轨迹条件控制，结合强化学习框架可显著提升端到端驾驶性能。

Original Abstract: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models (Driving VLAs) commonly introduce natural-language reasoning as an intermediate interface for end-to-end planning, but reasoning-centric interfaces face three practical bottlenecks: obtaining high-quality reasoning annotations is difficult, generating and understanding long reasoning chains is challenging for compact models, and inference latency is substantially increased. In this paper, we rethink the design of language interfaces in Driving VLAs and show that concise one-step meta-actions are a simple yet effective alternative to verbose reasoning. Meta-actions provide semantic decision grounding while remaining low-entropy, and being automatically derivable from expert trajectories, enabling scalable supervision and reliable trajectory conditioning. Building on this interface, we propose DriveMA, which combines action-centric supervised training with a turn-level credit-assignment reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes meta-action correctness, trajectory quality, and trajectory--meta-action consistency. Experiments show that DriveMA already achieves a new state of the art on the Waymo End-to-End Driving Challenge with a 2B model, reaching a Rater Feedback Score (RFS) of 8.060, while its 4B version further improves the state of the art to 8.079; DriveMA also obtains competitive performance on NAVSIM. Ablations demonstrate that one-step meta-actions offer a better practical trade-off between expressiveness, predictability, and inference efficiency than natural-language reasoning or finer-grained action sequences. Code, data, and models will be released to facilitate future research.

中文摘要翻译: 驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型（Driving VLAs）通常引入自然语言推理作为端到端规划的中间接口，但以推理为中心的接口面临三个实际瓶颈：获取高质量推理标注较为困难、紧凑模型难以生成和理解长推理链、以及推理延迟显著增加。本文重新思考了Driving VLA中语言接口的设计，并展示了一步元动作（concise one-step meta-actions）是冗长推理的一种简单而有效的替代方案。元动作在保持低熵的同时提供了语义决策基础，且可从专家轨迹自动导出，从而实现可扩展的监督和可靠的轨迹条件控制。基于这一接口，我们提出了DriveMA，它将以动作为中心的监督训练与回合级信用分配强化学习框架相结合，联合优化元动作正确性、轨迹质量以及轨迹与元动作之间的一致性。实验表明，DriveMA的2B模型已在Waymo端到端驾驶挑战中取得新的最优结果，达到8.060的Rater Feedback Score（RFS），其4B版本进一步将最优结果提升至8.079；DriveMA在NAVSIM上也取得了有竞争力的性能。消融实验表明，与自然语言推理或更细粒度的动作序列相比，一步元动作在表达力、可预测性和推理效率之间提供了更好的实际权衡。代码、数据和模型将公开发布以促进未来研究。

</details>


### [105] [Let EEG Models Learn EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21280)
*Yifan Wang,Yijia Ma,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于条件流匹配的JET框架，直接对原始EEG序列进行连续轨迹建模，无需离散去噪或领域特定表示，大幅提升了生成质量，TS-FID降低超40%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG生成方法采用离散去噪目标，无法充分反映神经活动内在的连续时间动态和频谱结构，在保持长程时间依赖和频谱-时间结构匹配方面存在不足。

Method: 基于条件流匹配的生成框架JET，学习一个平滑向量场将噪声传输到EEG数据分布，直接建模原始EEG序列的连续演化轨迹，并引入保持频谱结构、时间平稳性和信号级统计量的原则性约束。

Result: 在三个大规模基准上实现SOTA，TS-FID相比强基线降低超40%；深入分析表明JET能捕获神经动力学的关键结构特性。

Conclusion: 直接对神经信号的连续演化过程进行建模是更有效的EEG生成方式，JET提供了一种可扩展且原则性的解决方案。

Original Abstract: High-fidelity EEG generation is critical for alleviating data scarcity and addressing privacy constraints in large-scale neural modeling. Despite recent progress, most existing approaches formulate EEG generation via discrete denoising objectives, which inadequately reflect the inherently continuous temporal dynamics and spectral structure of neural activity. As a result, these methods often struggle to preserve long-range temporal dependencies and exhibit mismatches in the spectral and temporal structure of the generated signals. In this work, we argue that effective EEG generation requires models that operate directly on the continuous evolution of neural signals. We introduce Just EEG Transformer (JET), a generative framework based on conditional flow matching that models EEG as raw sequences evolving along continuous trajectories. By learning a smooth vector field that transports noise to the EEG data distribution, JET captures temporal continuity and transient dynamics without relying on discretized denoising schemes or domain-specific representations. To ensure that the learned dynamics remain consistent with key properties of EEG signals, we introduce principled constraints that preserve spectral structure, temporal stationarity, and signal-level statistics. Across three large-scale benchmarks, JET consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing TS-FID by over 40% compared to strong baselines. Extensive analyses show that JET captures key structural properties of neural dynamics, providing a scalable and principled approach to EEG generation. Project page: https://y-research-sbu.github.io/JET/ .

中文摘要翻译: 高保真脑电图（EEG）生成对于缓解大规模神经建模中的数据稀缺性和隐私约束至关重要。尽管近期取得了进展，但大多数现有方法通过离散去噪目标来构建EEG生成任务，这种做法无法充分反映神经活动内在的连续时间动态和频谱结构。因此，这些方法常常难以保持长程时间依赖关系，并在生成信号的频谱和时间结构上出现失配。在本文中，我们认为有效的EEG生成需要模型直接对神经信号的连续演化过程进行操作。我们提出了Just EEG Transformer（JET），一个基于条件流匹配的生成框架，将EEG建模为沿连续轨迹演化的原始序列。通过学习一个将噪声传输到EEG数据分布的平滑向量场，JET能够捕获时间连续性和瞬态动态，而无需依赖离散化的去噪方案或领域特定的表征。为了确保学习到的动态与EEG信号的关键属性保持一致，我们引入了保持频谱结构、时间平稳性和信号级统计量的原则性约束。在三个大规模基准上，JET持续达到最先进的性能，与强基线相比，TS-FID降低了超过40%。大量分析表明，JET能够捕获神经动力学的关键结构属性，为EEG生成提供了一种可扩展且原则性的方法。项目页面：https://y-research-sbu.github.io/JET/。

</details>


### [106] [Reducing Object Hallucination in LVLMs via Emphasizing Image-negative Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21300)
*Meng Shen,Minghao Wu,Deepu Rajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现LVLM生成的大多数token受图像影响极小，模型更偏向文本生成而非图像理解，从而导致目标幻觉。为此，提出根据token视觉依赖度调整训练权重并过滤高幻觉数据，有效减少幻觉且不增加推理开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 目标幻觉阻碍了大型视觉-语言模型的实际应用，现有研究认为其根源之一是模型倾向于优先进行文本生成而忽略与图像的有效交互。本文旨在验证这一假设并据此提出缓解方法。

Method: 1. 将生成token按对输入图像的视觉依赖度分为三类：图像正相关、不变、负相关；2. 根据视觉依赖度调整不同token的训练权重，控制幻觉；3. 移除训练数据中可能包含较多幻觉的部分，作为数据过滤策略。

Result: 大多数生成token受图像信息影响极小，表明训练阶段模型更注重学习如何遵循文本指令而非从图像中提取信息。所提方法在不牺牲回复长度且不增加推理计算成本的前提下，有效降低了幻觉率，并在三个LVLM变体上验证了有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 通过调整训练权重和数据过滤两种策略均可有效减少LVLM的目标幻觉，且不引入额外推理开销，方法在不同模型上具有良好的泛化性。

Original Abstract: Object hallucination is a significant challenge that hinders the application of large vision-language models (LVLMs) in practice. We hypothesize that one possible origin of hallucination is the model's tendency to prioritize text generation over meaningful interaction with images. To explore this, we examine the generation process and categorize text tokens into three groups: image-positive, invariant, and negative, based on their visual dependence on input image tokens. Our analysis reveals that most generated tokens are minimally influenced by the image information. This suggests that during the model's training stage, more emphasis is placed on learning how to follow textual instructions, rather than extracting information from images. Based on this finding, we propose adjusting the training weights of different tokens depending on their visual dependence to control hallucination. Additionally, we remove a portion of the training data that potentially contains more hallucinations as a data filtering strategy. Both methods achieve a reduction in hallucination without compromising response length or introducing additional computational costs during inference. We validate our methods across three LVLM variants, demonstrating the effectiveness and general applicability.

中文摘要翻译: 目标幻觉是阻碍大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）实际应用的一个重大挑战。我们假设幻觉的一个可能来源是模型倾向于优先进行文本生成，而非与图像进行有意义的交互。为探究这一点，我们考察了生成过程，并根据文本token对输入图像token的视觉依赖度将其分为三类：图像正相关、不变和负相关。我们的分析表明，大多数生成的token受图像信息的影响极小。这暗示在模型的训练阶段，更多重点被放在了学习如何遵循文本指令上，而非从图像中提取信息。基于这一发现，我们提出根据token的视觉依赖度调整其训练权重，以控制幻觉。此外，我们移除了训练数据中可能包含更多幻觉的一部分，作为一种数据过滤策略。两种方法均在不牺牲回复长度且不引入额外推理计算成本的前提下实现了幻觉的减少。我们在三个LVLM变体上验证了所提方法，证明了其有效性和通用适用性。

</details>


### [107] [OcclusionFormer: Arranging Z-Order for Layout-Grounded Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21343)
*Ziye Li,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对布局到图像生成中的物体间遮挡问题，构建了带有显式遮挡顺序和像素级标注的大规模数据集SA-Z，并提出OcclusionFormer框架，通过解耦实例并使用体渲染合成来显式建模Z-order优先级，同时引入查询对齐损失以增强空间精度和语义一致性，有效减少了重叠区域的生成模糊性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有布局到图像模型在空间可控性上取得了显著进展，但在处理物体间遮挡时表现不佳。当边界框重叠时，大多数方法缺乏显式遮挡信息，导致重叠区域的生成存在内在模糊性，难以确定复杂的遮挡关系，常产生纠缠纹理或物理不一致的图层叠加。

Method: 1）构建了SA-Z数据集，包含显式遮挡顺序和像素级标注；2）提出OcclusionFormer框架，一种遮挡感知的扩散Transformer，通过解耦实例并使用体渲染合成来显式建模Z-order优先级；3）引入查询对齐损失，显式监督单个实例并增强语义一致性。

Result: 所提方法有效减少了重叠区域的模糊性，强制了正确的遮挡依赖关系，保持了结构完整性，在多种场景下均取得了显著的精度提升。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式建模遮挡顺序有效解决了布局到图像生成中的物体间遮挡问题，为提升空间可控生成的质量提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Recent layout-to-image models have achieved remarkable progress in spatial controllability. However, they still struggle with inter-object occlusion. When bounding boxes overlap, most existing methods lack explicit occlusion information, which makes the generation in intersection regions inherently ambiguous and hinders the determination of complex occlusion relationships. As a result, they often produce entangled textures or physically inconsistent layering in the overlapped areas. To address this issue, we first construct SA-Z, a large-scale dataset enriched with explicit occlusion ordering and pixel-level annotations. Building upon our proposed dataset, we introduce OcclusionFormer, a novel occlusion-aware Diffusion Transformer framework that explicitly models Z-order priority by decoupling instances and compositing them via volume rendering. Furthermore, to ensure fine-grained spatial precision, we introduce a queried alignment loss that explicitly supervises individual instances and enhances semantic consistency. The proposed method effectively reduces ambiguity in overlapping regions, enforces correct occlusion dependencies, and preserves structural integrity, leading to substantial accuracy gains across diverse scenes.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，布局到图像模型在空间可控性方面取得了显著进展。然而，它们仍然难以处理物体间的遮挡问题。当边界框重叠时，大多数现有方法缺乏显式的遮挡信息，这使得重叠区域的生成存在内在模糊性，并阻碍了复杂遮挡关系的确定。因此，它们往往在重叠区域产生纠缠的纹理或物理上不一致的图层叠加。为解决这一问题，我们首先构建了SA-Z——一个包含显式遮挡顺序和像素级标注的大规模数据集。基于所提出的数据集，我们引入了OcclusionFormer，一种新颖的遮挡感知扩散Transformer框架，该框架通过解耦实例并通过体渲染合成来显式建模Z-order优先级。此外，为确保细粒度的空间精度，我们引入了一种查询对齐损失，该损失显式监督单个实例并增强语义一致性。所提出的方法有效减少了重叠区域的模糊性，强制了正确的遮挡依赖关系，并保持了结构完整性，从而在多种场景下取得了显著的精度提升。

</details>


### [108] [A Non-Reference Diffusion-Based Restoration Framework for Landsat 7 ETM+ SLC-off Imagery in Antarctica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21371)
*Leyue Tang,Jonathan Louis Bamber,Gang Qiao,Yuanhang Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffGF，一种无需参考影像的扩散模型框架，用于修复Landsat 7 ETM+ SLC故障导致的南极影像缺失像素，并构建了专用数据集SLCANT，实验表明该方法在高保真恢复和下游应用（如冰裂隙分割）中表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 南极地区存在极夜和频繁云覆盖，获取可用光学影像十分困难。Landsat是最重要的光学遥感数据源之一，但2003年扫描线校正器（SLC）故障导致Landsat 7 ETM+影像约22%像素缺失。南极地表变化快速且剧烈，常规的基于参考影像的缺填充方法难以适用，亟需一种无需参考影像的修复方法。

Method: DiffGF采用两阶段设计：1）潜空间扩散过程（latent-space diffusion process）；2）像素空间精炼（pixel-space refinement）。该方法无需任何外部参考数据，并构建了专用的南极数据集SLCANT用于训练和评估。

Result: 定量和定性结果表明DiffGF能够高保真地恢复南极SLC-off影像；下游冰裂隙分割应用进一步验证了其实用价值。

Conclusion: DiffGF为利用南极地区Landsat 7 SLC-off存档数据提供了一种有效方法，能够从历史记录中提取有价值的信息，支持相关南极研究。

Original Abstract: Acquiring usable optical imagery in Antarctica is inherently challenging due to prolonged polar nights and frequent cloud cover. Landsat provides the longest and most continuous optical observations and constitutes one of the most important remote sensing data sources for Antarctic studies. However, the scan-line corrector (SLC) failure in 2003 resulted in approximately 22% missing pixels in Landsat 7 ETM+ SLC-off imagery, severely limiting its usability. Unlike many non-polar environments, Antarctic surfaces undergo rapid and substantial changes, which makes it difficult to obtain reliable reference imagery and reduces the applicability of conventional reference-based gap-filling methods. To address this challenge, we propose DiffGF, a non-reference diffusion-based framework for restoring Landsat 7 SLC-off imagery without requiring any external reference data. DiffGF adopts a two-stage design consisting of a latent-space diffusion process and a pixel-space refinement. A dedicated Antarctic dataset, SLCANT, is constructed for training and evaluation. Quantitative and qualitative results demonstrate that DiffGF restores Antarctic SLC-off imagery with high fidelity. Its practical value is further examined through a downstream crevasse segmentation application. The results suggest that DiffGF provides a useful approach for exploiting Landsat 7 SLC-off archives in Antarctica, enabling the extraction of valuable information from historical records and supporting related Antarctic studies.

中文摘要翻译: 在南极获取可用的光学影像本身就充满挑战，这是因为长期极夜和频繁云覆盖所致。Landsat提供了最长且最连续的光学观测记录，是南极研究中最重要的遥感数据源之一。然而，2003年扫描线校正器（SLC）故障导致Landsat 7 ETM+ SLC-off影像中约22%的像素缺失，严重限制了其可用性。与许多非极地环境不同，南极地表经历着快速且剧烈的变化，这使得获取可靠的参考影像变得困难，并降低了传统基于参考影像的缺填充方法的适用性。为应对这一挑战，我们提出DiffGF，一种无参考的基于扩散的框架，用于修复Landsat 7 SLC-off影像，且无需任何外部参考数据。DiffGF采用两阶段设计，包括潜空间扩散过程和像素空间精炼。我们构建了一个专用的南极数据集SLCANT用于训练和评估。定量和定性结果表明，DiffGF能够高保真地恢复南极SLC-off影像。通过下游的冰裂隙分割应用进一步检验了其实用价值。结果表明，DiffGF为在南极地区利用Landsat 7 SLC-off存档数据提供了一种有效方法，能够从历史记录中提取有价值的信息，并支持相关南极研究。

</details>


### [109] [Closed Loop Dynamic Driving Data Mixture for Real-Synthetic Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21372)
*Hongzhi Ruan,Pei Liu,Weiliang Ma,Zhengning Li,Xueyang Zhang,Jun Ma,Dan Xu,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AutoScale，一种自动化的闭环数据引擎，通过场景表示、数据混合优化和检索，在有限训练预算下自适应地选择高价值合成数据用于自动驾驶端到端学习，优于朴素联合训练和跨域基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实驾驶数据标注成本高且存在场景偏差，而合成数据虽近乎无限但直接混合会导致分布偏移；如何在有限训练预算下优化真实与合成数据的混合比例（场景类型和数量）是一个关键但未充分探索的问题。

Method: 将数据混合近似为动态优化过程，迭代调整训练数据混合以最大化模型性能，受闭环评估反馈引导。具体提出：(1) Graph Regularized AutoEncoder (Graph-RAE) 用于驾驶场景表示；(2) Cluster-aware Gradient Ascent (Cluster-GA) 进行聚类级重要性估计与重加权；(3) 聚类引导的向量检索选择高价值样本。

Result: 在NavSim上，AutoScale优于朴素联合训练和跨域基线方法，在有限预算下使用更少的合成样本取得了更好的性能。

Conclusion: 训练数据的混合需要明确的场景类型和数量指导；AutoScale通过闭环数据引擎自动优化数据混合，有效提升了有限预算下的模型性能。

Original Abstract: Data scaling is fundamental to modern deep learning, and grows increasingly critical as autonomous driving shifts to end-to-end learning. Real-world driving data is expensive to annotate and scene-biased, making real-synthetic co-training with near-infinite synthetic data a promising direction. However, naively incorporating all available synthetic data is inefficient and leads to distribution shifts, and optimizing data mixture under practical training budgets remains a critical yet under-explored problem. In this sense, we claim that the mixture of training data requires clear guidance in terms of scene types and quantities. Particularly in this work, we conceptualize the data mixture approximately as a dynamic optimization process that iteratively adjusts the training data mixture to maximize model performance, guided by closed-loop evaluation feedback, and propose AutoScale, a fully automated closed-loop data engine unifying scene representation, data mixture optimization and retrieval, as well as model training and evaluation. Specifically, we propose Graph Regularized AutoEncoder (Graph-RAE) for driving scene representations, introduce Cluster-aware Gradient Ascent (Cluster-GA) for cluster-wise importance estimation and reweighting, and perform cluster-guided vector retrieval to select high-value samples. Experiments on NavSim demonstrate that AutoScale outperforms vanilla co-training and cross-domain baselines, achieving better performance with fewer synthetic samples under constrained budgets.

中文摘要翻译: 数据缩放是现代深度学习的基础，并且随着自动驾驶转向端到端学习而变得愈加关键。真实驾驶数据标注成本高且存在场景偏差，这使得将近乎无限的合成数据进行真实-合成联合训练成为一个有前景的方向。然而，不加区分地纳入所有可用合成数据效率低下且会导致分布偏移，在有限训练预算下优化数据混合仍然是一个关键但尚未充分探索的问题。基于此，我们主张训练数据的混合需要在场景类型和数量方面获得明确的指导。具体而言，在本文中，我们将数据混合近似为一个动态优化过程，该过程迭代调整训练数据混合以最大化模型性能，并由闭环评估反馈引导。我们提出了AutoScale，一个完全自动化的闭环数据引擎，统一了场景表示、数据混合优化与检索以及模型训练与评估。具体地，我们提出了用于驾驶场景表示的图正则化自动编码器（Graph-RAE），引入聚类感知梯度上升（Cluster-GA）进行聚类级重要性估计与重加权，并执行聚类引导的向量检索以选择高价值样本。在NavSim上的实验表明，AutoScale优于朴素联合训练和跨域基线方法，在有限预算下以更少的合成样本实现了更好的性能。

</details>


### [110] [RoadTones: Tone Controllable Text Generation from Road Event Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21411)
*Chirag Parikh,Siddhi Pravin Lipare,Ravi Kiran Sarvadevabhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了 RoadTones-51K 数据集、RoadTones-VL-CoT 可控视频到文本模型以及 RoadTones-Eval 评估套件，用于实现道路视频字幕的语调可控生成，兼顾事实一致性与表达风格控制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型能够生成道路事件的客观描述，但无法控制表达的语调、紧迫感或风格，限制了在沟通关键场景中的部署，因为信息有效性不仅取决于内容准确度，还取决于表达方式。

Method: 1) 构建人工验证的数据生成流程，扩展道路视频语料库并添加多样化语调标注和多语调字幕，形成 RoadTones-51K 数据集；2) 提出 RoadTones-VL-CoT 可控视频到文本模型，结合语调条件化的 Chain-of-Thought 中间草稿以实现可解释性；3) 设计 RoadTones-Eval 评估套件，联合衡量事实一致性和语调遵循度。

Result: 通过用户研究验证了字幕质量、语调控制和事实一致性。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为上下文敏感的语调可控视频字幕生成奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Existing video-language models can generate factual descriptions of road events but lack control over how these events are expressed: their tone, urgency, or style. This limits deployment in communication-critical settings where the effectiveness of a message depends on both content and presentation, not just factual accuracy. To mitigate this, we introduce a comprehensive dataset-model-evaluation suite for tone-controllable road video captioning. Our human-validated data generation pipeline expands road-video corpora with diverse tonal annotations and multi-tone captions, yielding the RoadTones-51K dataset. We propose RoadTones-VL-CoT, a controllable video-to-text model that also generates tone-conditioned Chain-of-Thought intermediate drafts for interpretability. We also introduce RoadTones-Eval, a new evaluation suite that jointly measures factual consistency and tone adherence. In addition, we conducted a user study whose results validate caption quality, tone control, and factual consistency. Together, these contributions lay the foundation for context-sensitive tone-controllable video captioning.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的视频-语言模型能够生成道路事件的事实性描述，但缺乏对这些事件表达方式——即其语调、紧迫感或风格——的控制能力。这限制了它们在沟通关键场景中的部署，因为在这些场景中，信息有效性不仅取决于事实准确性，还取决于内容和表达方式。为解决这一问题，我们引入了一套面向语调可控的道路视频字幕生成的数据集-模型-评估综合套件。我们的人工验证数据生成流程扩展了道路视频语料库，添加了多样化的语调标注和多语调字幕，形成了 RoadTones-51K 数据集。我们提出了 RoadTones-VL-CoT，一个可控的视频到文本模型，该模型还能生成受语调条件约束的思维链（Chain-of-Thought）中间草稿以实现可解释性。我们还引入了 RoadTones-Eval，一个联合衡量事实一致性与语调遵循度的新评估套件。此外，我们进行了一项用户研究，其结果验证了字幕质量、语调控制和事实一致性。这些贡献共同为上下文敏感的语调可控视频字幕生成奠定了基础。

</details>


### [111] [Ordering Matters: Rank-Aware Selective Fusion for Blended Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21417)
*Junghyun Lee,Hyunseo Kim,Hanna Jang,Junhyug Noh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种秩感知的多编码器融合框架，通过注意力门控选择top-n最优编码器，并解耦为存在头和显著头进行概率融合，在混合情绪识别任务上取得第二名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 混合情绪识别具有挑战性，因为情绪往往以微妙且重叠的多模态线索混合形式表达，而非单一主导信号。现有方法难以有效融合多种异构编码器的互补信息。

Method: 提出秩感知多编码器框架：(1) 将异构编码器特征投影到共享潜在空间；(2) 通过基于注意力的门控模块估计样本级编码器重要性；(3) 仅融合top-n个最具信息量的编码器；(4) 将预测解耦为存在头(presence head)和显著头(salience head)，通过概率级融合对齐；(5) 引入无伪标签的特征级无监督域自适应以提高鲁棒性。

Result: 在BlEmoRE挑战赛上，所提框架优于强个体编码器和朴素的多编码器融合基线，最终系统在竞赛中排名第二。

Conclusion: 秩感知的选择性融合策略对于细粒度混合情绪识别是有效的。

Original Abstract: Blended emotion recognition is challenging because emotions are often expressed as mixtures of subtle and overlapping multimodal cues rather than a single dominant signal. We propose a rank-aware multi-encoder framework that selectively combines complementary representations from diverse pre-extracted video and audio encoders. Our method projects heterogeneous encoder features into a shared latent space, estimates sample-wise encoder importance through an attention-based gating module, and fuses only the top-n most informative encoders. To better model blended emotions, we decouple prediction into presence and salience heads and align them through probability-level fusion. We further incorporate feature-level unsupervised domain adaptation without pseudo-labeling to improve robustness under distribution shift. Experiments on the BlEmoRE challenge show that the proposed framework outperforms strong individual encoders and naïve multi-encoder fusion baselines. Our final system ranked 2nd in the competition, supporting the effectiveness of rank-aware selective fusion for fine-grained blended emotion recognition.

中文摘要翻译: 混合情绪识别具有挑战性，因为情绪往往以微妙且重叠的多模态线索混合形式表达，而非单一主导信号。我们提出了一个秩感知的多编码器框架，该框架从多种预提取的视频和音频编码器中选择性地融合互补表示。我们的方法将异构编码器特征投影到共享潜在空间，通过基于注意力的门控模块估计样本级别的编码器重要性，并仅融合top-n个最具信息量的编码器。为了更好地建模混合情绪，我们将预测解耦为存在头(presence head)和显著头(salience head)，并通过概率级融合对其对齐。我们进一步引入了无需伪标签的特征级无监督域自适应，以提高在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。在BlEmoRE挑战赛上的实验表明，所提框架优于强个体编码器和朴素的多编码器融合基线。我们的最终系统在竞赛中排名第二，支持了秩感知选择性融合在细粒度混合情绪识别中的有效性。

</details>


### [112] [iTryOn: Mastering Interactive Video Virtual Try-On with Spatial-Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21431)
*Jun Zheng,Zhengze Xu,Mengting Chen,Jing Wang,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Kaifu Zhang,Bo Zheng,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出交互式视频虚拟试穿（Interactive VVT）新任务，并构建iTryOn框架，利用多级交互注入机制和Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding（A-RoPE）解决手-衣交互中的语义模糊和复杂形变问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VVT方法局限于非交互场景（模特静态展示服装），忽略了真实世界中人体与服装的主动交互（如拉扯、整理衣物）。为填补这一空白，作者正式提出Interactive VVT任务。

Method: 基于大规模视频扩散Transformer的iTryOn框架。包含：(1) 空间级：引入服装无关的3D手部先验，提供精细的手-衣接触引导；(2) 语义级：利用全局标题和带时间戳的动作标题，通过Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding (A-RoPE)对齐交互时序。

Result: 在传统VVT基准上达到SOTA，并在新提出的交互式场景下显著领先。

Conclusion: iTryOn有效解决了交互式视频虚拟试穿中语义歧义和复杂形变两大挑战，向更动态、可控的虚拟试穿体验迈出了重要一步。

Original Abstract: Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) aims to seamlessly replace a garment on a person in a video with a new one. While existing methods have made significant strides in maintaining temporal consistency, they are predominantly confined to non-interactive scenarios where models merely showcase garments. This limitation overlooks a crucial aspect of real-world apparel presentation: active human-garment interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce and formalize a new challenging task: Interactive Video Virtual Try-On (Interactive VVT), where subjects in the video actively engage with their clothing. This task introduces unique challenges beyond simple texture preservation, including: (1) resolving the semantic ambiguity of interactions from standard pose information, and (2) learning complex garment deformations from video where interactive moments are sparse and brief. To address these challenges, we propose iTryOn, a novel framework built upon a large-scale video diffusion Transformer. iTryOn pioneers a multi-level interaction injection mechanism to guide the generation of complex dynamics. At the spatial level, we introduce a garment-agnostic 3D hand prior to provide fine-grained guidance for precise hand-garment contact, effectively resolving spatial ambiguity. At the semantic level, iTryOn leverages global captions for overall context and time-stamped action captions for localized interactions, synchronized via our novel Action-aware Rotational Position Embedding (A-RoPE). Extensive experiments demonstrate that iTryOn not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on traditional VVT benchmarks but also establishes a commanding lead in the new interactive setting, marking a significant step towards more dynamic and controllable virtual try-on experiences.

中文摘要翻译: 视频虚拟试穿（VVT）旨在无缝地将视频中人物身上的服装替换为新款。尽管现有方法在保持时序一致性方面取得了显著进展，但它们主要局限于非交互场景，即模特仅静态展示服装。这一限制忽视了真实世界服装展示中的一个关键方面：主动的人-衣交互。为了弥补这一差距，我们引入并形式化了一个新的挑战性任务：交互式视频虚拟试穿（Interactive VVT），其中视频中的主体主动与其服装进行互动。该任务带来了超越简单纹理保持的独特挑战，包括：(1) 从标准姿态信息中解析交互的语义歧义，以及(2) 从交互时刻稀疏且短暂的视频中学习复杂的服装形变。为应对这些挑战，我们提出iTryOn，一个基于大规模视频扩散Transformer的新型框架。iTryOn首创了一种多级交互注入机制，用于指导复杂动态的生成。在空间层面，我们引入服装无关的3D手部先验，为精确的手-衣接触提供细粒度引导，有效解决空间歧义。在语义层面，iTryOn利用全局标题获取整体上下文，并利用带时间戳的动作标题进行局部交互，通过我们新颖的动作感知旋转位置编码（A-RoPE）实现同步。大量实验表明，iTryOn不仅在传统VVT基准上达到当前最优性能，还在新的交互场景中建立了显著领先优势，标志着向更动态、可控的虚拟试穿体验迈出了重要一步。

</details>


### [113] [ReMATF: Recurrent Motion-Adaptive Multi-scale Turbulence Mitigation for Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21440)
*Zhiming Liu,Zhicheng Zou,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级循环框架ReMATF，仅用两帧输入即可有效恢复受大气湍流退化视频的空间细节与时间稳定性，在计算效率和恢复质量上均优于多帧Transformer基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于Transformer和3D架构的多帧湍流视频恢复方法计算开销大、内存占用高，难以在资源受限场景下实现实时部署。

Method: ReMATF采用轻量级循环架构，结合多尺度编码器-解码器、时间扭曲和运动自适应时间融合模块，仅利用两帧输入进行逐像素融合，在不扩大时间窗口的前提下增强时序一致性。

Result: 在合成和真实湍流数据集上，ReMATF在PSNR、SSIM和感知质量（LPIPS）上均取得一致提升，且推理速度显著快于多帧Transformer基线方法。

Conclusion: ReMATF是一种高效且有效的湍流视频恢复框架，特别适合资源受限场景下的实时部署。

Original Abstract: Atmospheric turbulence severely degrades video quality by introducing distortions such as geometric warping, blur, and temporal flickering, posing significant challenges to both visual clarity and temporal consistency. Current state-of-the-art methods are based on transformer, 3D architectures and require multi-frame input, but their large computational cost and memory usage limit real-time deployment, especially in resource-constrained scenarios. In this work, we propose ReMATF, a lightweight recurrent framework that restores videos using only two frames at a time while preserving spatial detail and temporal stability. ReMATF combines a multi-scale encoder-decoder with temporal warping and a motion-adaptive temporal fusion module that performs per-pixel fusion between the warped previous output and the current prediction to enhance coherence without enlarging the temporal window. This design reduces flicker, sharpens details, and remains efficient. Experiments on synthetic and real turbulence datasets show consistent improvements in PSNR/SSIM and perceptual quality (LPIPS), along with substantially faster inference than multi-frame transformer baselines, making ReMATF suitable turbulence mitigation in resource-constrained scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 大气湍流会引入几何扭曲、模糊和时间闪烁等失真，严重降低视频质量，对视觉清晰度和时间一致性构成重大挑战。当前最先进的方法基于Transformer和3D架构，需要多帧输入，但其巨大的计算成本和内存消耗限制了实时部署，尤其是在资源受限场景中。本文提出ReMATF，一种轻量级循环框架，每次仅利用两帧即可恢复视频，同时保持空间细节和时间稳定性。ReMATF结合了多尺度编码器-解码器、时间扭曲以及运动自适应时间融合模块，在扭曲后的前一输出与当前预测之间进行逐像素融合，从而在不扩大时间窗口的前提下增强时序一致性。这种设计减少了闪烁、锐化了细节，并保持了高效性。在合成和真实湍流数据集上的实验表明，ReMATF在PSNR/SSIM和感知质量（LPIPS）上均取得了一致提升，且推理速度显著快于多帧Transformer基线方法，使其适用于资源受限场景下的湍流缓解。

</details>


### [114] [TempGlitch: Evaluating Vision-Language Models for Temporal Glitch Detection in Gameplay Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21443)
*Yakun Yu,Ashley Wiens,Adrián Barahona-Ríos,Benedict Wilkins,Saman Zadtootaghaj,Nabajeet Barman,Cor-Paul Bezemer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TempGlitch基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在视频游戏中检测时间性故障的能力，发现现有VLM在此任务上几乎随机，且增加帧采样或模型规模无法可靠改善性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究将游戏故障视为静态视觉异常，忽略了时间性故障（需跨帧变化才能识别）与空间性故障（单帧可见）的关键区别，而初步实验表明VLM对时间性故障的检测远弱于空间性故障，因此需要系统评估这一未被充分探索的场景。

Method: 构建TempGlitch基准，包含五种时间性故障类型、平衡样本量及配对的无故障视频；评估12种开源和闭源VLM，采用多种帧采样设置进行二元故障检测实验。

Result: 现有VLM在TempGlitch上几乎随机水平，要么过度保守（漏检大多数故障），要么过度敏感（将正常视频标记为故障）；更密集的帧采样和更大的模型规模无法可靠解决这些问题。

Conclusion: TempGlitch为时间推理、鲁棒游戏理解和自动化故障检测提供了聚焦的测试平台，当前VLM在时间性故障检测上仍有显著不足。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being explored for video game quality assurance, especially gameplay glitch detection. Most existing evaluations, however, treat glitches as static visual anomalies, asking models to detect failures from a single frame. We argue that this framing misses a key distinction: some glitches are spatial and visible in an isolated frame, whereas others are temporal and become evident only through changes across ordered frames. A preliminary study confirms this gap, showing that temporal glitches are substantially harder for VLMs to detect than spatial ones. To enable systematic evaluation of this underexplored setting, we introduce TempGlitch, a controlled gameplay video benchmark for temporal glitch detection. TempGlitch covers five temporal glitch types with balanced per-category samples, together with paired glitch-free videos that enable reliable binary evaluation. We evaluate 12 proprietary and open-weight VLMs across multiple frame-sampling settings. Our results show that current VLMs remain near chance on TempGlitch, often collapsing into either overly conservative behavior that misses most glitches or overly sensitive behavior that flags clean videos as glitchy. Moreover, denser frame sampling and larger model size do not reliably resolve these failures. TempGlitch provides a focused testbed for temporal reasoning, robust gameplay understanding, and automated glitch detection with VLMs. Code and data are available at the project website.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）正越来越多地被探索用于视频游戏质量保证，尤其是游戏玩法故障检测。然而，现有大多数评估将故障视为静态视觉异常，要求模型从单帧中检测故障。我们认为这种设定忽略了一个关键区别：某些故障是空间性的，可以在孤立帧中可见；而其他故障是时间性的，只有通过有序帧之间的变化才能显现。初步研究证实了这一差距，表明VLM检测时间性故障远比空间性故障困难。为了系统评估这一未被充分探索的场景，我们提出了TempGlitch——一个用于时间性故障检测的受控游戏视频基准。TempGlitch涵盖五种时间性故障类型，每类样本平衡，并配有成对的无故障视频，从而支持可靠的二元评估。我们在多种帧采样设置下评估了12个专有和开源VLM。结果表明，当前VLM在TempGlitch上几乎处于随机水平，通常要么陷入过度保守的行为而漏检大多数故障，要么过度敏感地将干净视频标记为有故障。此外，更密集的帧采样和更大的模型规模并不能可靠地解决这些失败。TempGlitch为时间推理、鲁棒游戏理解和基于VLM的自动化故障检测提供了一个聚焦的测试平台。代码和数据可在项目网站上获取。

</details>


### [115] [StreamGVE: Training-Free Video Editing via Few-Step Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21466)
*Guanlong Jiao,Chenyangguang Zhang,Jia Jun Cheng Xian,Zewei Zhang,Renjie Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamGVE，一种基于流式生成模型的噪声到数据视频编辑方法，通过双分支快速采样、自注意力桥接和跨注意力定位/增强等机制，在少量采样步骤下实现高质量视频编辑，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法采用数据到数据范式，与生成模型的噪声到数据生成方式不兼容，导致需要大量迭代且编辑质量不高。作者旨在弥合这一差距，从噪声到数据视角重新审视视频编辑。

Method: 基于预训练的流式生成模型，StreamGVE提出：(1) 双分支快速采样框架，配以自注意力桥接和跨注意力定位/增强模块，同时满足采样和条件注入需求；(2) 源导向引导策略提升目标生成质量；(3) 视觉提示策略增强编辑灵活性和实用性。

Result: 在多种视频编辑任务上的大量实验表明，StreamGVE在少步采样设置下以极小的时间成本一致地优于现有方法，且方法有效、鲁棒、可泛化至不同模型。

Conclusion: 噪声到数据范式相比数据到数据范式更适用于现代生成模型的视频编辑，StreamGVE通过精心设计的机制在保持高效采样同时实现了高质量视频编辑，具有强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Although existing video editing methods are generally feasible, they often require many costly iterations and still struggle to deliver high-quality yet satisfying editing results. We attribute this limitation to the prevalent data-to-data paradigm, which is less compatible with modern generative models than noise-to-data generation. To address this gap, we revisit video editing from a noise-to-data perspective and propose Streaming-Generation-based Video Editing (StreamGVE), which preserves few-step sampling while seamlessly injecting source-video conditions. Built on pre-trained streaming generation models, StreamGVE introduces dual-branch fast sampling with a self-attention bridge and cross-attention grounding/boosting to satisfy both sampling and conditioning requirements. We further propose source-oriented guidance to improve target-generation quality, and a visual prompting strategy to enhance editing flexibility and practicality. The method is effective, robust, and generalizable across different models. Extensive experiments on diverse video editing tasks show that StreamGVE consistently outperforms existing approaches, even in few-step settings with minimal time cost.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管现有的视频编辑方法通常可行，但它们往往需要大量昂贵的迭代，并且仍然难以提供高质量且令人满意的编辑结果。我们将这一局限性归因于普遍采用的数据到数据范式，该范式与噪声到数据生成相比，与现代生成模型的兼容性较差。为弥合这一差距，我们从噪声到数据的视角重新审视视频编辑，并提出基于流式生成的视频编辑（StreamGVE），该方法在保持少步采样的同时无缝注入源视频条件。构建于预训练的流式生成模型之上，StreamGVE引入了带有自注意力桥接和跨注意力定位/增强的双分支快速采样，以满足采样和条件注入两方面的需求。我们进一步提出了源导向引导以提升目标生成质量，以及视觉提示策略以增强编辑的灵活性和实用性。该方法有效、鲁棒且可泛化至不同模型。在多种视频编辑任务上的大量实验表明，即使是在时间成本极小的少步设置下，StreamGVE也始终优于现有方法。

</details>


### [116] [Stream3D: Sequential Multi-View 3D Generation via Evidential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21472)
*Kaichen Zhou,Zeyang Bai,Xinhai Chang,Mengyu Wang,Paul Liang,Fangneng Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream3D 提出了一种无需重新训练、即插即用的流式机制，通过紧凑的基于证据分数的记忆缓存，将冻结的视图条件3D生成器（如SAM 3D、TRELLIS、Hunyuan3D）转变为流式生成器，解决了单目视频流中逐帧独立应用3D生成器导致的时序不一致问题，在光度与几何指标上优于KV-cache重用和流式特征编辑等基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视图条件3D生成器（SAM 3D、TRELLIS、Hunyuan3D）能够从单视图生成高质量3D重建，但真实场景中的视觉观测往往以长单目视频流形式出现。将这些生成器逐帧独立应用于流式输入会导致严重的时序不一致问题，而现有方法缺乏能够直接赋予冻结生成器流式能力且不增加内存开销的解决方案。

Method: Stream3D 提出训练自由的流式机制，核心是维护一个紧凑的“证据记忆”（evidential memory）。该记忆基于提出的证据分数（evidence score）机制，选择性缓存最具信息量的历史帧；随着视频流推进，记忆动态更新以保持固定数量信息帧，避免内存随序列长度线性增长。整个框架无需重新训练、修改架构或引入辅助损失，底层3D生成器完全冻结。

Result: 在真实与合成流式基准上，Stream3D 在光度指标（如图像质量、颜色一致性）和几何指标（如形状一致性、深度准确度）上均优于潜在传输基线方法，包括KV-cache重用和基于流的特征编辑。

Conclusion: Stream3D 成功地将冻结的视图条件3D生成器转变为流式生成器，在不修改原有模型的前提下实现了时序一致的3D重建，为长视频流中的3D生成提供了一种高效、轻量且通用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: View-conditioned 3D generators such as SAM 3D, TRELLIS and Hunyuan3D produce high-quality object reconstructions from a single view, but real-world visual observation often arrives as long monocular streams. Naively applying these generators to each streaming frame independently leads to severe temporal inconsistency in the generated results. To address this problem, we propose Stream3D, the first training-free streaming mechanism that turns a frozen view-conditioned 3D generator into a streaming generator with constant cross-chunk memory. Stream3D achieves this by maintaining a compact evidential memory, which selectively caches the most informative historical frames based on a proposed evidence score mechanism. As the stream progresses, the memory dynamically updates to retain a fixed number of informative frames, preventing the memory footprint from growing linearly with sequence length. This also prevents degradation over long sequences and keeps the underlying generator completely unchanged without retraining, architectural modifications, or auxiliary losses. Evaluated on both realistic and synthetic streaming benchmarks, Stream3D outperforms latent-transport baselines, including KV-cache reuse and flow-based feature editing, across both photometric and geometric metrics. More details can be found at: https://anonymous-submission-20.github.io/streaming3D.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 视图条件3D生成器（如SAM 3D、TRELLIS和Hunyuan3D）能够从单视图生成高质量的对象重建，但真实世界的视觉观测通常以长单目视频流的形式出现。将这些生成器逐帧独立应用于流式输入会导致生成结果出现严重的时序不一致。为解决这一问题，我们提出了Stream3D——首个无需训练的流式机制，能将冻结的视图条件3D生成器转变为具有恒定跨块记忆的流式生成器。Stream3D通过维护一个紧凑的证据记忆来实现这一点，该记忆基于提出的证据分数机制选择性缓存最具信息量的历史帧。随着视频流的推进，记忆动态更新以保留固定数量的信息帧，防止内存占用随序列长度线性增长。这也避免了长序列上的性能退化，并保持底层生成器完全不变，无需重新训练、架构修改或辅助损失。在真实和合成流式基准上的评估表明，Stream3D在光度指标和几何指标上均优于潜在传输基线方法，包括KV-cache重用和基于流的特征编辑。更多细节请访问：https://anonymous-submission-20.github.io/streaming3D.github.io/

</details>


### [117] [Latent Dynamics for Full Body Avatar Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21478)
*Shichong Peng,Chengxiang Yin,Fei Jiang,Zhongshi Jiang,Lingchen Yang,Qingyang Tan,Amin Jourabloo,Jason Saragih,Ke Li,Christian Häne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于姿态驱动全身3D高斯虚拟人模型，通过引入动力学残差潜变量和Transformer解码器，学习服装等动态元素的历史依赖运动，无需显式物理模拟或分层模板，实现高保真、时间连贯的动画生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有姿态驱动虚拟人模型无法处理宽松衣物等动态元素的非刚体运动（其变形依赖于历史、惯性和接触），而显式模拟或分层方法需专用模板或高运行时成本；数据驱动方法虽避免分层，但仅用静态潜变量无法建模潜变量的时间演化，且现有架构难以捕捉细粒度细节，导致模糊渲染和时间伪影。

Method: 在姿态条件化的3D高斯虚拟人基础上，添加Transformer解码器和动力学残差潜变量。推理时，学习的动力学模型根据短时姿态历史和前一潜状态更新残差潜变量，将每次更新分解为驱动力、恢复力和耗散力三个分量。不同初始条件产生多样且合理的运动轨迹，力分解还暴露了刚度等控制参数。

Result: 在9段包含多种宽松衣物的日常运动序列上，定量指标和感知用户研究均表明，该方法在动画质量上优于近期数据驱动基线方法。模型产生时间连贯、历史依赖的 rollout，且额外计算成本可忽略。

Conclusion: 通过引入动力学残差潜变量和显式的力分解机制，有效建模了宽松衣物的历史依赖动态运动，实现了高质量、时间连贯的虚拟人动画，同时保持了低计算开销和可控性。

Original Abstract: Pose-driven full-body avatars built on neural rendering produce high-quality novel views of a captured subject. Yet loose clothing and other dynamic elements deform in ways pose alone cannot explain: the same pose can correspond to many different states, because their motion depends on history, inertia, and contact. Explicit simulation and layered-garment methods can model such dynamics, but they require either a dedicated garment template, which raw multi-view capture does not naturally provide, or a test-time physics simulator with non-trivial runtime cost. A parallel line of work learns data-driven clothing avatars that avoid explicit garment layers. These methods add an auxiliary latent for variation beyond pose; at inference, they fix it, regress it from pose, or retrieve it from training data, without explicitly modeling how the latent evolves with its own dynamics. Additionally, even in everyday motion with loose clothing, existing architectures often struggle to capture fine-grained detail, producing blurry renderings and temporal artifacts. We augment a pose-conditioned 3D Gaussian avatar with a transformer-based decoder and a dynamics residual latent that captures temporal appearance and geometry variation beyond the driving signals. At inference, a learned latent dynamics model evolves the residual latent from a short pose history and the previous latent state. The model decomposes each update into driving, restoring, and dissipative forces, producing temporally coherent, history-dependent rollouts with negligible added cost. Different initial conditions yield diverse yet plausible motion trajectories, and the force decomposition exposes controls such as stiffness. Across nine captured sequences of everyday motion with diverse loose garments, quantitative metrics and a perceptual user study show improved animation quality over recent data-driven baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 基于神经渲染的姿态驱动全身虚拟人模型能够生成捕获对象的高质量新视角图像。然而，宽松衣物及其他动态元素的变形方式无法仅由姿态解释：相同的姿态可能对应多种不同的状态，因为这些元素的运动依赖于历史、惯性和接触。显式模拟和分层服装方法可以建模此类动力学，但它们要么需要专用的服装模板（原始多视角捕获无法自然提供），要么需要测试时运行物理模拟器，带来不容忽视的运行时开销。另一条并行的工作路线是学习数据驱动的服装虚拟人，避免显式分层。这些方法在姿态之外添加辅助潜变量以捕捉变异；推理时，它们固定该变量、从姿态回归，或从训练数据中检索，而未显式建模潜变量如何随自身动力学演化。此外，即使在包含宽松衣物的日常运动中，现有架构也常难以捕捉细粒度细节，产生模糊渲染和时间伪影。我们在姿态条件化的3D高斯虚拟人基础上，增加了基于Transformer的解码器和动力学残差潜变量，用于捕捉驱动信号之外的时间外观和几何变化。推理时，一个学习到的潜动力学模型根据短时姿态历史和前一潜状态来演化残差潜变量。该模型将每次更新分解为驱动力、恢复力和耗散力三个分量，以可忽略的额外成本产生时间连贯、历史依赖的 rollout。不同的初始条件能产生多样且合理的运动轨迹，而力的分解暴露了刚度等控制参数。在九段包含多种宽松衣物的日常运动捕捉序列上，定量指标和感知用户研究表明，我们的方法在动画质量上优于近期数据驱动基线。

</details>


### [118] [WikiVQABench: A Knowledge-Grounded Visual Question Answering Benchmark from Wikipedia and Wikidata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21479)
*Basel Shbita,Pengyuan Li,Anna Lisa Gentile*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了WikiVQABench，一个由人工精心整理的知识驱动型VQA基准数据集，通过结合维基百科图像、标题和结构化知识构建，用于评估视觉语言模型在需要外部知识的知识密集型推理任务上的能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的VQA基准大多侧重于仅从视觉内容即可解决的感知型任务，而现实场景中许多问题需要外部知识才能正确回答。因此，迫切需要构建一个能够评估视觉语言模型在知识密集型推理任务上表现的基准。

Method: 通过系统性地结合维基百科图像、关联的文章标题以及维基数据的结构化知识，利用大语言模型生成候选的多选题-图像-答案集，再由人工标注者对所有生成实例进行审核和整理，确保事实正确性、视觉-文本一致性以及每个问题都需要外部知识才能解决。

Result: 对15个视觉语言模型（参数量从256M到90B）进行评估，准确率范围从24.7%到75.6%，表明该基准能够有效区分不同模型在知识密集型推理上的能力。

Conclusion: WikiVQABench作为一个高质量的知识驱动型VQA基准，能够有效评估和区分视觉语言模型在知识密集型推理任务上的表现，填补了现有基准偏重感知任务的空白。

Original Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) benchmarks have largely emphasized perception-based tasks that can be solved from visual content alone. In contrast, many real-world scenarios require external knowledge that is not directly observable in the image to answer correctly. We introduce WikiVQABench, a human-curated knowledge-grounded VQA benchmark constructed by systematically combining Wikipedia images, their associated article captions, and structured knowledge from Wikidata. Our pipeline uses large language models (LLMs) to generate candidate multiple-choice image-question-answer sets. All generated instances are subsequently reviewed and curated by human annotators to ensure factual correctness, visual-text consistency, and that each question requires external knowledge in addition to visual evidence for correct resolution. WikiVQABench comprises a substantial collection of Wikipedia images with curated multiple-choice questions designed to benchmark knowledge-aware vision-language models (VLMs). Evaluation of fifteen VLMs (256M-90B parameters) reveals a wide performance range (24.7%-75.6% accuracy), demonstrating that the benchmark effectively discriminates model capabilities on knowledge-intensive reasoning. The dataset and benchmarking code are publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉问答（VQA）基准大多侧重于仅从视觉内容即可解决的感知型任务。然而，许多现实场景需要外部知识——即图像中无法直接观察到的信息——才能正确回答。我们提出了WikiVQABench，这是一个人工整理的知识驱动型VQA基准，通过系统性地结合维基百科图像、其关联的文章标题以及来自维基数据的结构化知识构建而成。我们的流程使用大语言模型（LLM）生成候选的单选题-图像-答案集。所有生成的实例随后由人工标注者进行审核和整理，以确保事实正确性、视觉-文本一致性，并且每个问题除了视觉证据外还需要外部知识才能正确解答。WikiVQABench包含大量维基百科图像及精心整理的多选题，旨在评估知识感知的视觉语言模型（VLM）。对15个VLM（参数量从256M到90B）的评估结果显示，准确率范围从24.7%到75.6%不等，表明该基准能够有效区分模型在知识密集型推理上的能力。数据集和基准测试代码均已公开。

</details>


### [119] [Uni-Edit: Intelligent Editing Is A General Task For Unified Model Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21487)
*Dian Zheng,Manyuan Zhang,Hongyu Li,Hongbo Liu,Kai Zou,Kaituo Feng,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Uni-Edit，将智能图像编辑作为统一多模态模型（UMM）的通用训练任务，仅用单一任务、单阶段训练和单一数据集即可同时提升图像理解、生成和编辑三种能力，无需复杂的多任务混合策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有UMMs依赖混合多任务训练来增强图像理解、生成和编辑能力，但任务冲突导致需要复杂多阶段流程和大量数据平衡技巧，最终只能实现性能折衷而非真正的相互增强。作者希望打破这一范式，寻找一种能同时提升三种能力的通用任务。

Method: (1) 识别图像编辑为理想的通用任务，因其天然同时需要视觉理解和生成能力；(2) 提出首个自动化、可扩展的数据合成流水线，将多样化的VQA数据转换为包含嵌入问题和嵌套逻辑的复杂编辑指令；(3) 构建Uni-Edit-148k数据集，包含推理密集型指令与高质量编辑图像对；(4) 仅用该数据集对UMMs（BAGEL和Janus-Pro）进行单任务、单阶段微调。

Result: 在BAGEL和Janus-Pro上的大量实验表明，仅用Uni-Edit进行微调即可实现图像理解、生成和编辑三种能力的全面增强，无需任何辅助操作。

Conclusion: 智能图像编辑可以作为统一多模态模型的有效通用训练任务，通过单一任务替代复杂的多任务混合流程，实现多种能力的协同提升。

Original Abstract: Currently, enhancing Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) with image understanding, generation, and editing capabilities mainly relies on mixed multi-task training. Due to inherent task conflicts, such strategy requires complex multi-stage pipelines, massive data mixing, and balancing tricks, merely resulting in a performance trade-off rather than true mutual reinforcement. To break this paradigm, we propose Uni-Edit, an intelligent image editing task that serves as the first general task for UMM tuning. Unlike complex mixed pipelines, Uni-Edit improves performance across all three abilities at once using only one task, one training stage, and one dataset. Specifically, we first identify image editing as an inherently ideal general task, as it naturally demands both visual understanding and generation. However, existing editing data relies on simplistic instructions that severely underutilize a model's understanding capacity. To address this, we introduce the first automated and scalable data synthesis pipeline for intelligent editing, transforming diverse VQA data into complex and effective editing instructions with embedded questions and nested logic. This yields Uni-Edit-148k, pairing diverse reasoning-intensive instructions with high-quality edited images. Extensive experiments on BAGEL and Janus-Pro demonstrate that tuning solely on Uni-Edit achieves comprehensive enhancements across all three capabilities without any auxiliary operations.

中文摘要翻译: 目前，增强具备图像理解、生成和编辑能力的统一多模态模型（UMMs）主要依赖混合多任务训练。由于固有的任务冲突，该策略需要复杂的多阶段流程、大规模数据混合和平衡技巧，最终只能实现性能折衷而非真正的相互增强。为打破这一范式，我们提出Uni-Edit，将智能图像编辑任务作为UMM调优的首个通用任务。与复杂的混合流程不同，Uni-Edit仅使用一个任务、一个训练阶段和一个数据集即可同时提升所有三种能力。具体而言，我们首先认定图像编辑天然是理想的通用任务，因为它同时需要视觉理解和生成能力。然而，现有的编辑数据依赖过于简单的指令，严重低估了模型的理解能力。为解决这一问题，我们引入了首个自动化、可扩展的智能编辑数据合成流水线，将多样化的VQA数据转换为包含嵌入问题和嵌套逻辑的复杂有效编辑指令。由此构建了Uni-Edit-148k数据集，其中包含推理密集型指令与高质量编辑图像对。在BAGEL和Janus-Pro上的大量实验表明，仅使用Uni-Edit进行微调即可实现三种能力的全面增强，无需任何辅助操作。

</details>

### [120] [GraphDiffMed: Knowledge-Constrained Differential Attention with Pharmacological Graph Priors for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20188)
*Krati Saxena,Tomohiro Shibata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDiffMed提出了一种基于双尺度差分注意力v2的用药推荐框架，通过在就诊内和就诊间两个层面过滤噪声信号，并结合药物药理约束（如药物相互作用），在MIMIC-III数据集上实现了比现有方法更优的推荐质量和安全性平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有用药推荐方法要么擅长跨就诊时序建模，要么擅长药理知识整合（如药物相互作用），但很少能同时兼顾两者并稳健地抑制电子健康记录中的噪声信号。

Method: 提出GraphDiffMed框架，核心是双尺度差分注意力v2（Differential Attention v2），在就诊内（intra-visit）和就诊间（inter-visit）两个层面应用差分注意力机制，过滤单次就诊内部和纵向历史中的虚假信号，同时在学习过程中融入药理学约束。

Result: 在MIMIC-III数据集上的实验和消融研究表明，该设计在推荐质量和排序指标上一致优于强基线方法，且实现了更有利的安全性-性能平衡；最强的配置仅使用人口统计学辅助特征。

Conclusion: 将噪声感知的注意力机制与药理学约束相结合，能够生成更可靠、更具临床意义的用药推荐方案。

Original Abstract: Recommending safe and effective medication combinations from electronic health records (EHRs) is a core clinical AI problem, yet it remains difficult because patient trajectories are long, noisy, and clinically heterogeneous. Existing methods typically excel at either temporal modeling across visits or pharmacological knowledge integration (e.g., drug-drug interactions, DDIs), but rarely achieve both while robustly suppressing noise. We present GraphDiffMed, a knowledge-constrained medication recommendation framework built on dual-scale Differential Attention v2. Differential attention is applied at both intra-visit and inter-visit levels to filter spurious signals within encounters and across longitudinal history, while pharmacological constraints are incorporated during learning. Experiments on MIMIC-III and ablation studies show that this design consistently improves recommendation quality and ranking over strong baselines while achieving a more favorable safety performance balance. We further find that the strongest-performing configuration uses only demographic auxiliary features under our experimental setting. Overall, GraphDiffMed demonstrates that combining noise-aware attention with pharmacological constraints yields more reliable and clinically meaningful medication recommendation. We open-source our code at https://github.com/saxenakrati09/GraphDiffMed.

中文摘要翻译: 从电子健康记录（EHR）中推荐安全有效的药物组合是一个核心的临床AI问题，但由于患者轨迹长、噪声大且临床异质性高，这一问题仍然具有挑战性。现有方法通常要么擅长跨就诊的时序建模，要么擅长药理知识整合（例如药物-药物相互作用，DDIs），但很少有方法能同时实现两者并稳健地抑制噪声。我们提出GraphDiffMed，一个基于双尺度差分注意力v2的药理约束药物推荐框架。差分注意力在就诊内和就诊间两个层面应用，以过滤单次就诊内部和纵向历史中的虚假信号，同时在学习过程中融入药理学约束。在MIMIC-III数据集上的实验和消融研究表明，该设计在推荐质量和排序指标上一致优于强基线方法，同时实现了更有利的安全性-性能平衡。我们进一步发现，在我们的实验设置下，性能最强的配置仅使用人口统计学辅助特征。总体而言，GraphDiffMed证明将噪声感知的注意力机制与药理学约束相结合，能够生成更可靠、更具临床意义的用药推荐。我们在https://github.com/saxenakrati09/GraphDiffMed开源了代码。

</details>


### [121] [Online Conformal Prediction with Corrupted Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20515)
*Bowen Wang,Matteo Zecchin,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Modern artificial intelligence systems require calibrated uncertainty estimates that remain reliable in sequential and non-stationary environments. Online conformal prediction (OCP) addresses this challenge through adaptively updated prediction sets that provide deterministic long-run miscoverage guarantees. These guarantees, however, hinge on the assumption of perfect feedback about the coverage of past prediction sets. In practice, the observed miscoverage indicator may be corrupted by noise, communication failures, or adversarial manipulation, which can severely degrade OCP's calibration guarantees. In this paper, we study OCP under corrupted feedback. We first model feedback corruption as an arbitrary binary flip sequence, and analyze how feedback corruption affects and degrades the miscoverage performance of standard OCP. We then propose two robust schemes: robust OCP via filtering, which leverages the structural properties of the predicted threshold to filter corrupted feedback, and robust OCP via active compensation, which incorporates an active compensation mechanism to mitigate the effect of corrupted feedback. For both methods, we establish explicit miscoverage guarantees, which are further specialized for an independent stochastic flip model and for an arbitrary error model with memory bounds. Experiments on real-world datasets validate the proposed approach, showing markedly improved calibration and significantly smaller prediction sets compared with baseline OCP methods under corrupted feedback.

中文摘要翻译: Abstract translation unavailable

### [122] [Provably Learning Diffusion Models under the Manifold Hypothesis: Collapse and Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20235)
*Wei Huang,Andi Han,Mingyuan Bai,Huanjian Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Taiji Suzuki,Kenji Fukumizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型在低维流形数据上训练时通过“塌缩-精炼”机制避免维数灾难，并基于此设计了SiLD框架，该框架将流形学习与密度估计统一于去噪分数匹配目标中，理论与实验均验证了其有效性与维度无关的样本复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型虽在高维数据生成上表现优异，但其训练过程如何高效学习分数函数并在数据位于低维流形时绕过维数灾难，尚缺乏理论解释。

Method: 提出分数诱导潜在扩散（SiLD）两阶段框架：小噪声尺度下，分数函数的发散奇异性驱动去噪映射发生维度塌缩，将数据投影到低维流形上；中等噪声尺度下，训练精炼流形上的内在密度分布。整个过程通过单一的去噪分数匹配目标同时完成流形学习和密度估计，替代了VAE潜扩散模型中启发式的KL正则化。

Result: 理论上证明了样本复杂度取决于内在维度而非环境维度；实验上在Stacked MNIST、CelebA变体及分子生成基准上，SiLD在生成质量上匹配或优于基于VAE的潜扩散模型，并在重建任务上持续提升。

Conclusion: 扩散模型通过分数函数几何驱动的塌缩-精炼机制有效应对低维流形上的维数灾难；SiLD提供了一种理论更清晰且无需启发式KL正则化的潜扩散替代方案。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data with remarkable quality, yet how their training efficiently learns the score function, bypassing the curse of dimensionality when data is supported on low-dimensional manifolds, remains theoretically unexplained. We identify a collapse-and-refine mechanism driven by the geometry of the score function itself: at small noise scales, the diverging singularity of the score drives a rapid dimensional collapse of the induced denoising map onto the data manifold projection; at moderate noise scales, training refines the intrinsic density on the learned manifold. We instantiate this principle as Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD), a two-stage framework in which both manifold learning and density estimation emerge from a single denoising score matching objective, replacing the heuristic KL regularization of VAE-based latent diffusion models. We prove that the resulting sample complexity depends on the intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension. Experiments on Stacked MNIST, CelebA variants, and molecular generation benchmarks show that SiLD matches or outperforms VAE-based LDMs in generation quality and consistently improves reconstruction, validating our theoretical predictions.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型能以卓越质量生成高维数据，但其训练过程如何高效学习分数函数，并在数据支撑于低维流形时绕过维数灾难，这一机制在理论上仍未得到解释。我们发现了一种由分数函数自身几何性质驱动的“塌缩-精炼”机制：在小噪声尺度下，分数函数的发散奇异性驱动去噪映射发生快速维度塌缩，将数据投影到数据流形上；在中等噪声尺度下，训练过程精炼了所学流形上的内在密度分布。我们将这一原理实例化为分数诱导潜在扩散（SiLD），这是一个两阶段框架，其中流形学习和密度估计均来自单一的去噪分数匹配目标，取代了基于VAE的潜在扩散模型中启发式的KL正则化。我们证明了所得到的样本复杂度取决于内在维度而非环境维度。在Stacked MNIST、CelebA变体及分子生成基准上的实验表明，SiLD在生成质量上匹配或优于基于VAE的潜在扩散模型，并在重建任务上持续提升，验证了我们的理论预测。

</details>


### [123] [MagBridge-Battery: A Synthetic Bridge Dataset for Li-ion Magnetometry and State-of-Health Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20240)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布了MagBridge-Battery v1.0，一个包含6,760个磁场信号签名与电池健康状态标签的合成数据集，将真实磁形态数据与退化标签桥接，为磁传感电池诊断提供公开基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前电池健康诊断主要依赖电化学信号，磁传感虽能捕获终端测量遗漏的信息，但缺乏带有退化标签的公共电池磁测量数据集，阻碍了该方法的发展。

Method: 通过将Mohammadi-Jerschow OSF档案中的真实磁形态数据与PulseBat数据集中的SOH标签桥接，生成合成磁场签名数据集。包含5,600个PulseBat条件样本、600个合成传感器异常样本和560个低压外推样本，并设计了无泄漏的主基准划分。

Result: 定义了SOH回归、二次生命分类和异常检测三项基准任务。标签洗牌消融实验将SOH回归的R²从约0.77降至约0，证实桥接编码了输入SOH的非平凡信息而非产生标签对齐的伪影。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁传感电池诊断提供了首个公共基准数据集和评估套件，在配对磁-电化学实测数据仍然稀缺的情况下，推动了该领域的方法开发。

Original Abstract: Battery health diagnostics today rely overwhelmingly on electrochemical signals measured at the cell terminals. A parallel literature has shown that magnetic sensing can resolve information that terminal-only measurements miss, but method development is limited by the absence, to the best of our knowledge, of public battery magnetic-measurement datasets paired with degradation labels. We release MagBridge-Battery v1.0, a synthetic dataset of 6,760 magnetic-field signatures that bridges real magnetic morphology from the Mohammadi-Jerschow Open Science Framework (OSF) archive with state-of-health (SOH) labels from the PulseBat dataset. The release contains 5,600 PulseBat-conditioned grounded samples, 600 synthetic sensor-anomaly samples derived from clean parents, and 560 low-voltage Regime-B extrapolation samples. A cell-disjoint, parent-child-leakage-free primary benchmark split is verified to contain zero overlapping cells, zero cross-split parent-child pairs, and zero sample-ID overlap. We define three primary benchmark tasks: SOH regression, second-life classification, and anomaly detection, plus an auxiliary anomaly-subtype classification task. A controlled label-shuffle ablation collapses SOH regression from R^2 approximately 0.77 to approximately 0, confirming that the bridge encodes input SOH non-trivially rather than producing label-aligned artifacts. The dataset is released on Zenodo under CC-BY-4.0, and the bridge code and benchmark suite are released under Apache-2.0. This work provides a public benchmark for magnetic-sensing battery diagnostics while paired magnetic-electrochemical measurements remain scarce.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的电池健康诊断主要依赖在电池端子处测得的电化学信号。已有平行研究表明，磁传感能够解析仅靠端子测量无法获取的信息，但由于据我们所知缺乏带有退化标签的公共电池磁测量数据集，相关方法的发展受到限制。我们发布了MagBridge-Battery v1.0，这是一个包含6,760个磁场特征签名的合成数据集，它将来自Mohammadi-Jerschow开放科学框架（OSF）档案的真实磁形态与来自PulseBat数据集的健康状态（SOH）标签相桥接。该数据集包含5,600个PulseBat条件约束的接地样本、600个由干净父样本衍生的合成传感器异常样本，以及560个低压Regime-B外推样本。经验证，一个无电池重叠、无跨划分亲子对、无样本ID重叠的电池互斥、无亲子泄漏的主基准划分是成立的。我们定义了三项主要基准任务：SOH回归、二次寿命分类和异常检测，以及一项辅助的异常子类型分类任务。受控标签洗牌消融实验将SOH回归的R²从约0.77降至约0，证实桥接编码了输入SOH的非平凡信息，而非产生与标签对齐的伪影。该数据集在Zenodo上以CC-BY-4.0许可发布，桥接代码和基准套件以Apache-2.0许可发布。本工作在配对磁-电化学实测数据仍然稀缺的情况下，为磁传感电池诊断提供了一个公共基准。

</details>


### [124] [Geometry-Lite: Interpretable Safety Probing via Layer-Wise Margin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20241)
*Woo Seob Sim,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Geometry-Lite方法，通过分析多层安全信号在表示空间中的几何结构（边界位置、层间变化和粗粒度形状），揭示了大语言模型在prompt级安全检测中，安全证据主要依赖于持久的边界位置几何（最终/极值边界和unsafe侧层占用），而非层间运动信号。该方法在9个指令微调模型和7个安全基准上接近全层堆叠性能，并揭示了低误报率阈值下的基准漂移偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有prompt级安全探针具备较强的平均检测性能，但其几何分离机制尚不明确：安全证据如何跨层形成、何种层间几何支持低误报率决策、以及基准漂移下存在何种几何偏差。本文旨在对此进行经验性分解分析。

Method: 提出Geometry-Lite轻量级prompt级探针，将每层最终prompt token的表示映射到质心、局部邻域和监督线性边界三类读出头下的带符号边界（signed margins），然后通过边界位置、层间变化和粗粒度形状三个维度汇总边界轮廓。在9个指令微调模型（1.2B-70B）和7个安全基准上评估。

Result: 1) 安全证据主要表达为持续的边界位置几何：最终或极值边界、unsafe侧层占用主导聚合检测性能；2) 有限差分漂移和结构摘要对整体AUROC贡献很小，但在低误报率阈值下可提供少量面向召回的修正；3) 基准漂移下，优化线性边界在训练混合集上尖锐，而类别条件均值几何在预定义的困难保留子集上更可靠地保持分离。

Conclusion: Prompt级安全证据并非主要是层间运动信号，而是一种持久的层间边界几何，其有用组件和读出头级偏差在决策关键场景（如低误报率）中变得可见。

Original Abstract: Prompt-level safety probes for large language models use hidden-state representations to separate safe from unsafe prompts, but strong average detection performance does not explain the geometry of this separation. In particular, it remains unclear how safety evidence is formed across layers, which aspects of that layer-wise geometry support low-false-positive decisions, and which geometric biases remain stable under benchmark shift. We study this as an empirical decomposition problem and introduce Geometry-Lite, a compact prompt-level probe that maps each layer's final prompt-token representation to signed margins under centroid, local-neighborhood, and supervised linear-boundary readouts, then summarizes the resulting margin profiles by boundary position, layer-to-layer change, and coarse shape. Across nine instruction-tuned backbones ($1.2$B--$70$B) and seven safety benchmarks, Geometry-Lite improves over single-layer probes while remaining close to raw multi-layer score stacking, making it a useful instrument for analyzing the multi-layer safety signal. The decomposition shows that safety evidence is expressed primarily through persistent boundary-position geometry: final or extremal margins and unsafe-side layer occupancy dominate aggregate detection performance. In contrast, finite-difference drift and structural summaries add little to pooled AUROC, although drift can provide small recall-oriented corrections under shifted low-FPR thresholds. Under benchmark shift, optimized linear boundaries are sharp on the training mixture, whereas class-conditional mean geometry retains separation more reliably on a predefined hard held-out subset. Overall, prompt-level safety evidence is not primarily a layer-to-layer motion signal, but a persistent layer-wise margin geometry whose useful components and readout-level biases become visible in decision-critical regimes.

中文摘要翻译: 针对大语言模型的提示级安全探针利用隐藏状态表示来区分安全与不安全提示，但较强的平均检测性能并不能解释这种分离的几何结构。特别是，安全证据如何在各层之间形成、哪些层间几何特征支持低假阳性率决策、以及在基准漂移下哪些几何偏差保持稳定，这些问题仍不清楚。我们将其作为一个经验性分解问题进行研究，并提出了Geometry-Lite——一种紧凑的提示级探针。它将每层最终提示令牌的表示映射到质心、局部邻域和监督线性边界读出头下的带符号边界，然后通过边界位置、层间变化和粗粒度形状来汇总生成的边界轮廓。在9个指令微调骨干模型（1.2B-70B）和7个安全基准上，Geometry-Lite相比单层探针有所提升，同时接近原始多层得分堆叠的性能，使其成为分析多层安全信号的有效工具。分解结果表明，安全证据主要通过持久的边界位置几何来表达：最终或极值边界以及不安全侧层占用主导了聚合检测性能。相比之下，有限差分漂移和结构摘要对整体AUROC贡献很小，尽管漂移在低假阳性率阈值偏移下可以提供少量面向召回的修正。在基准漂移下，优化线性边界在训练混合集上表现尖锐，而类别条件均值几何在预定义的困难保留子集上更可靠地保持分离。总体而言，提示级安全证据主要不是层间运动信号，而是一种持久的层间边界几何，其有用组件和读出头级偏差在决策关键场景中变得可见。

</details>


### [125] [LEAP: A closed-loop framework for perovskite precursor additive discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20242)
*Xin-De Wang,Zhi-Rui Chen,Ze-Feng Gao,Peng-Jie Guo,Cheng Mu,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Efficient discovery of precursor additives is essential for improving the performance of perovskite solar cells, yet the large chemical space makes conventional trial-and-error screening inefficient. We develop LEAP(LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites), an expert-in-the-loop closed framework that couples a domain-specialized large language model(LLM) with active learning for iterative additive prioritization. The LLM is trained to extract mechanism-relevant knowledge from the perovskite additive literature and to represent candidate molecules through interpretable descriptors, which are further integrated into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions. Benchmark results on unseen literature show that the domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose models in mechanism-consistent reasoning. Experimental validation in an expert-in-the-loop proof-of-concept study suggests improved additive prioritization across three screening rounds, leading to average device PCEs of 20.13% and 20.87% for the later-round 6-CDQ- and 2-CNA-treated devices, respectively, compared with 19.25% for the control, with a champion PCE of 21.32%. These results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors, when coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert feasibility review, can support mechanism-aware additive prioritization in perovskite photovoltaics.

中文摘要翻译: Abstract translation unavailable
