### [126] [GROW: Aligning GRPO with State-Action Modeling for Open-World VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20246)
*Xiongbin Wu,Zhihao Luo,Shanzhe Lei,Lechao Zhang,Xuhong Wang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Xin Tan,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GROW框架，通过将完整轨迹分解为状态-动作样本并在样本间计算优势函数，解决了标准GRPO在多轮视觉-语言智能体强化学习中因完整轨迹过长导致的噪声问题，在800+个Minecraft任务上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开世界VLM智能体主要依赖专家示范的监督微调（SFT），而先进的强化学习算法GRPO因需要完整轨迹作为训练样本，导致上下文过长和噪声过大，无法有效用于多轮RL任务。

Method: 提出GROW框架，将收集到的完整轨迹分解为状态-动作样本对，在这些样本之间而非完整轨迹之间计算优势函数；并提供替代分析证明，即使分组样本依赖于不同局部状态而非相同提示上下文，该目标在简化假设下仍能保留GRPO的核心相对策略优化信号。

Result: 在800+个Minecraft任务上的实验表明，该方法达到了最新最优（SOTA）性能，验证了所提出的RL框架在开世界VLM智能体中的有效性。

Conclusion: GROW通过轨迹分解和样本级优势计算，成功将GRPO引入开世界VLM智能体的多轮RL训练中，显著提升了任务性能。

Original Abstract: Recently, vision-language model (VLM) agents have shown promising progress in open-world tasks, where successful task completion often requires multiple turns of visual perception and action execution. However, existing methods still rely primarily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with expert demonstrations, while the advanced reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has not been effectively employed for multi-turn RL in these tasks because standard GRPO requires full trajectories as training samples which leads to excessively long context and noise. To address this issue, we propose GROW, a RL framework for open-world VLM agents that decomposes collected trajectories into state-action samples, and computes advantages between these samples rather than treating a full trajectory as a single entity. We further provide a surrogate analysis indicating that, even though the grouped samples are conditioned on different local states rather than an identical prompt context, the objective can preserve the core relative policy optimization signal of GRPO under simplifying assumptions. Experiments on more than 800 Minecraft tasks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed RL framework for open-world VLM agents.

中文摘要翻译: 最近，视觉-语言模型（VLM）智能体在开放世界任务中展现出令人瞩目的进展，这类任务的成功完成通常需要多轮的视觉感知和动作执行。然而，现有方法仍主要依赖基于专家演示的监督微调（SFT），而先进的强化学习（RL）算法——特别是组相对策略优化（GRPO），尚未被有效应用于这些任务的多轮强化学习中，这是因为标准的GRPO需要完整轨迹作为训练样本，导致上下文过长且噪声过大。为解决这一问题，我们提出GROW，一个面向开放世界VLM智能体的强化学习框架，该框架将收集到的轨迹分解为状态-动作样本，并在这些样本之间而非将完整轨迹视为单一实体来计算优势函数。我们进一步提供了一项替代分析，表明即使分组样本依赖于不同的局部状态而非相同的提示上下文，该目标在简化假设下仍能保留GRPO的核心相对策略优化信号。在超过800个Minecraft任务上的实验表明，我们的方法达到了最先进的（SOTA）性能，验证了所提出的强化学习框架在开放世界VLM智能体中的有效性。

</details>


### [127] [CP-MoE: Consistency-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20247)
*Yang Liu,Toan Nguyen,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CP-MoE 提出了一种基于瞬态专家的持续学习框架，通过一致性保持路由偏置和瞬态专家引导正则化，在 MoE 架构中平衡任务间知识迁移与灾难性遗忘，在单模态和多模态基准上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于 LoRA 的 MoE 持续学习方法存在根本性权衡：要么过于激进地隔离专家，限制跨任务知识迁移；要么允许任务特定更新覆盖重要参数，导致严重遗忘。CP-MoE 旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: CP-MoE 引入一个瞬态专家来捕获早期任务特定更新，并指导其整合到稳定专家中。包含两个核心组件：(1) 一致性保持路由偏置，利用瞬态专家估计与稳定专家的表示相似性，引导路由选择更兼容的专家；(2) 瞬态专家引导正则化机制，在合并过程中选择性保护重要的历史参数。

Result: 在 SuperNI 基准上取得 SOTA 性能，并对未见任务展现出更强的零样本迁移能力；在 VQA v2 数据集上有效扩展到多模态视觉推理，持续减少遗忘，优于强 MoE 基线。

Conclusion: CP-MoE 通过瞬态专家机制有效降低了参数干扰和遗忘，同时保留了跨任务知识迁移能力，在单模态和多模态持续学习场景中均验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a major obstacle to continual learning in large language models (LLMs) and vision--language models (VLMs). Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer an efficient path to scaling, existing LoRA-based MoE continual learning methods still face a fundamental trade-off: they either isolate experts too aggressively, limiting knowledge transfer across tasks, or allow task-specific updates to overwrite important existing parameters, leading to severe forgetting. To address this, we propose CP-MoE, a continual learning framework built around a transient expert that captures early task-specific updates and guides their integration into stable experts. CP-MoE introduces a consistency-preserving routing bias, which uses the transient expert to estimate representation similarity with stable experts and steer routing towards more compatible expert selection, and a transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism, which selectively protects important historical parameters during merging. Together, these components reduce parameter interference and forgetting while preserving cross-task knowledge transfer. We validate CP-MoE on both unimodal and multimodal continual learning benchmarks with LLM-based and VLM-based MoE models. On SuperNI benchmark, spanning diverse sequential language tasks, CP-MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger zero-shot transfer to unseen tasks. On VQA v2 dataset, it scales effectively to multimodal visual reasoning, consistently reduces forgetting, and outperforms strong MoE baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 灾难性遗忘仍是大型语言模型（LLM）和视觉-语言模型（VLM）中持续学习的主要障碍。尽管混合专家（MoE）架构为扩展提供了高效路径，但现有基于 LoRA 的 MoE 持续学习方法仍面临根本性权衡：它们要么过于激进地隔离专家，限制跨任务知识迁移；要么允许任务特定更新覆盖重要的现有参数，导致严重遗忘。为解决这一问题，我们提出 CP-MoE，一种围绕瞬态专家构建的持续学习框架，该瞬态专家捕获早期任务特定更新并指导其整合到稳定专家中。CP-MoE 引入了一种一致性保持路由偏置，利用瞬态专家估计与稳定专家的表示相似性，引导路由选择更兼容的专家；以及一种瞬态专家引导的正则化机制，在合并过程中选择性地保护重要的历史参数。这些组件共同降低了参数干扰和遗忘，同时保留了跨任务知识迁移。我们在基于 LLM 和 VLM 的 MoE 模型上，对单模态和多模态持续学习基准进行了验证。在涵盖多种序列语言任务的 SuperNI 基准上，CP-MoE 取得了最先进的性能，并对未见任务展现出更强的零样本迁移能力。在 VQA v2 数据集上，它有效扩展到多模态视觉推理，持续减少遗忘，并优于强 MoE 基线。

</details>


### [128] [Graph Transductive Sharpening: Leveraging Unlabeled Predictions in Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20248)
*Brown Zaz,Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

Original Abstract: In the transductive setting, where the full graph is observed but node labels are only partially available, progress in semi-supervised node classification has largely focused on architectural innovation. In this paper, we revisit an orthogonal axis: the training objective. We start from a simple observation: transductive models produce predictions for every node during training, including nodes without labels. These unlabeled-node predictions may contain useful training signal, but standard supervised objectives discard them because no ground-truth labels are available. Inspired by the decomposition of cross-entropy into a label-dependent alignment term and a label-independent entropy term, we propose prediction confidence as a natural way to extract this signal in the absence of labels. This motivates Transductive Sharpening (TS): a loss-level modification that minimizes prediction entropy on unlabeled nodes while counterbalancing this effect on labeled nodes. We evaluate Transductive Sharpening across a wide range of node-classification benchmarks and observe consistent performance improvements without requiring any changes to the backbone architecture. Code is available at https://github.com/transductive-sharpening/tunedGNN.

### [129] [Automated Kernel Discovery Towards Understanding High-dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20249)
*Taeyoung Yun,Woocheol Shin,Inhyuck Song,Jaewoo Lee,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Gaussian Process (GP) kernels are central to Bayesian optimization (BO), yet designing effective kernels for high-dimensional problems still relies on extensive manual engineering. Existing automated approaches struggle in high dimensions for two bottlenecks: their kernel search space is limited to additions and multiplications of base kernels, and LLM-based approaches require conditioning on raw observations, which becomes infeasible due to context-length limits and the difficulty of extracting meaningful patterns. We introduce \textbf{Kernel Discovery}, a LLM-driven evolutionary framework for high-dimensional BO that searches a broader kernel space beyond predefined composition rules and does not require conditioning on observations. Motivated by the observation that directly prompting an LLM to generate kernel code yields syntactically varied but functionally identical kernels, we adopt a two-stage approach: an LLM first proposes novel mathematical forms, then a second LLM call converts each form into validated, executable code. We also propose a leave-one-out continuous ranked probability score (LOO-CRPS) as a selection criterion that penalizes overfitted kernels. On five high-dimensional BO benchmarks, our method achieves an average rank of \textbf{1.2 out of 17}, outperforming competitive baselines.
  We further analyze the discovered kernels to identify which kernels lead to improvements in high-dimensional BO.

### [130] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Safe Autonomous Driving Under Pedestrian Behavioral Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20255)
*Prakash Aryan,Kaushik Raghupathruni,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Simulation-based testing of self-driving cars (SDCs) typically relies on scripted or simplified pedestrian models that do not capture the heterogeneity and uncertainty of real human crossing behavior. This limits the realism of safety assessments, especially in scenarios involving jaywalking, which is governed by latent personality traits that the vehicle cannot observe. We hypothesize that jointly training pedestrians and the SDC with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) produces more realistic interaction scenarios than training the SDC against fixed pedestrian policies, and that the resulting behavior gap between predictable and unpredictable crossings can be measured directly from trajectories. This paper describes a MARL environment in which an SDC and 12 pedestrians are co-trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO). Pedestrian locomotion follows scripted Dijkstra pathfinding, while an RL policy controls high-level go/wait decisions. Jaywalking probability depends on a per-pedestrian personality trait sampled at episode start and hidden from the SDC. In 500-episode evaluations, the co-trained SDC reached 78% of goals with a 14% collision rate, compared to 35% goals and 33% collisions for the best rule-based baseline. A speed differential metric shows that the SDC traveled 2.65 m/s faster near jaywalkers than near crosswalk users at close range (0-3 m), indicating that jaywalking encounters were not anticipated. Jaywalking accounted for 13% of crossing events but was associated with 62% of collisions. Co-training with MARL pedestrians reduced collisions by 30% relative to single-agent RL, as pedestrians learned to wait when the SDC approached at speed.

### [131] [FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20256)
*Xikai Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Likang Xiao,Yingze Zhang,Yanhua Cheng,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Wenjun Wu,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FBOS-RL提出了一种反馈驱动的双目标协同强化学习框架，通过反馈引导探索增强和两个相互增强的训练目标（利用导向策略对齐EPA与探索导向能力培养ECC），解决了GRPO在策略模型能力不足时因采样质量低导致训练停滞的问题，显著提升了训练效率和最终性能上限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GRPO及其变体在强化学习训练中采用简单的采样方案，所有rollout都基于同一原始提示条件生成。当任务超出策略模型当前能力时，该采样方案难以产生高质量的rollout，导致策略模型在参数更新时缺乏有意义的梯度方向，进而使训练陷入停滞。

Method: 提出FBOS-RL框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 反馈引导探索增强（Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement），基于环境提供的反馈引导模型进行更有效的探索；(2) 双目标协同训练：利用导向策略对齐（EPA）和探索导向能力培养（ECC），二者相互增强形成正向飞轮效应。

Result: 在相同rollout数量下，FBOS-RL相比GRPO和基于反馈的基线方法学习速度显著更快，最终达到更高的性能上限，同时在整个训练过程中表现出更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数。

Conclusion: EPA和ECC可以相互增强，形成正向飞轮效应，显著提升强化学习的训练效率和最终性能天花板。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for aligning and unlocking the reasoning capabilities of large-scale models. At its core, the training loop of GRPO and its variants alternates between rollout sampling and policy update. Unlike supervised learning, where each gradient step is anchored to an explicit ground-truth target, the optimal gradient direction for updating model parameters in this setting is not known a priori; the high-quality rollouts drawn during the sampling stage therefore act as the implicit "teacher" that guides every parameter update. However, GRPO adopt a simple sampling scheme that conditions all rollouts on the same original prompt. When a task lies beyond the policy model's current capability, this sampling scheme rarely yields a high-quality rollout, leaving the policy model without a meaningful gradient direction when updating its parameters, which causes training to stall. To address this issue, we propose FBOS-RL, a Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, we let the model perform Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement based on the feedback provided by the environment, and on top of this we design two mutually reinforcing training objectives: Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment(EPA) and Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation(ECC). Extensive experiments demonstrate that EPA and ECC can mutually reinforce each other, forming a positive flywheel effect that significantly improves both the training efficiency and the final performance ceiling of reinforcement learning. Specifically, under an identical number of rollouts, FBOS-RL learns substantially faster than GRPO and feedback-based baselines and ultimately attains a higher performance ceiling, while exhibiting higher policy entropy and lower gradient norms throughout training.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习已成为对齐和激发大规模模型推理能力的基石。GRPO及其变体的训练循环核心在于交替进行rollout采样和策略更新。与监督学习不同——其中每个梯度步骤都锚定于明确的真实目标——在此设置下更新模型参数的最优梯度方向无法先验获知；因此，采样阶段抽取的高质量rollout充当了引导每次参数更新的隐式"教师"。然而，GRPO采用了一种简单的采样方案，所有rollout都基于同一原始提示条件生成。当任务超出策略模型当前能力时，这种采样方案很难产生高质量的rollout，导致策略模型在参数更新时缺乏有意义的梯度方向，从而使训练陷入停滞。为解决这一问题，我们提出了FBOS-RL，一种反馈驱动的双目标协同强化学习框架。具体而言，我们让模型基于环境提供的反馈进行反馈引导探索增强，并在此基础上设计了两个相互促进的训练目标：利用导向策略对齐（EPA）和探索导向能力培养（ECC）。大量实验表明，EPA和ECC可以相互增强，形成正向飞轮效应，显著提升强化学习的训练效率和最终性能上限。具体而言，在相同的rollout数量下，FBOS-RL比GRPO和基于反馈的基线方法学习速度显著更快，最终达到更高的性能上限，同时在整个训练过程中表现出更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数。

</details>


### [132] [Instance Discrimination for Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20257)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Mathieu Lefort,Rémy Cazabet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自监督学习中的链接预测任务，提出基于链接表示（而非节点表示）的新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL，并引入基于社区结构的图增强方法，在无属性图上表现优异，达到与有监督方法相当的水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督实例判别模型在图像和节点分类任务上表现良好，但在链接预测任务上的研究较少，缺乏系统性评估和有效方法。

Method: 1) 对现有自监督模型在链接预测任务上进行严格评估；2) 提出基于社区结构的结构性增强方法；3) 提出两个基于链接表示（而非节点表示）的新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL。

Result: 1) 链接预测性能主要取决于数据增强过程；2) 提出的L-GRACE和L-BGRL在无属性图上显著提升性能；3) 在监督和自监督场景下均达到与现有最佳方法相当的水平。

Conclusion: 基于链接表示的自监督模型能够有效改进链接预测任务，尤其在无属性图上表现突出，且性能可与有监督方法相媲美。

Original Abstract: Recently, instance discrimination models have emerged as a major solution for self-supervised learning. Having already demonstrated its effectiveness in the image domain, instance discrimination learning is now proving equally convincing in the graph domain, in particular for node classification. However, fewer contributions have tackled the link prediction task. In this contribution, we propose to adapt existing methods to this context. We first provide a rigorous evaluation of existing self-supervised models in the field of link prediction, showing that the main performance depends on the augmentation process (like in computer vision). We then propose a new structural augmentation based on the community structure that is relevant for link prediction. Our main contribution introduces two new models, L-GRACE and L-BGRL, based on link representations instead of node representations, which improve the performance of the existing methods, especially on unattributed graphs, and we show that they perform on par with the state of the art, both in supervised and self-supervised contexts.

中文摘要翻译: 近期，实例判别模型已成为自监督学习的主要解决方案。实例判别学习已在图像领域证明了其有效性，目前在图领域同样展现出令人信服的效果，尤其是在节点分类任务中。然而，针对链接预测任务的贡献仍然较少。本文提出将现有方法适配到这一场景。首先，我们对链接预测领域的现有自监督模型进行了严格评估，表明其主要性能取决于增强过程（与计算机视觉类似）。接着，我们提出了一种基于社区结构的新型结构性增强方法，该方法与链接预测任务高度相关。我们的主要贡献是引入了两个基于链接表示而非节点表示的新模型——L-GRACE和L-BGRL，这些模型提升了现有方法的性能，特别是在无属性图上，并且我们证明了它们在监督和自监督场景下均能达到与现有最佳方法相当的性能。

</details>


### [133] [It Takes Two: Complementary Self-Distillation for Contextual Integrity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20258)
*Sangwoo Park,Woongyeong Yeo,Seanie Lee,Yumin Choi,Hyomin Lee,Kangsan Kim,Jinheon Baek,Seong Joon Oh,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Original Abstract: Contextual Integrity (CI) defines privacy not merely as keeping information hidden, but as governing information flows according to the norms of a given context. As large language models are increasingly deployed as personal agents handling sensitive workflows, adhering to CI becomes critical. However, even frontier models remain unreliable in making disclosure decisions, and existing mitigation strategies often degrade underlying task performance. To overcome this privacy-utility trade-off, we propose SELFCI, a complementary self-distillation framework that decouples information suppression from task resolution. SELFCI jointly optimizes two independent reverse KL divergences over distinct teacher distributions derived from feedback: one encourages preserving task-relevant information for utility, while the other enforces minimal and appropriate disclosure. This complementary formulation induces a Product-of-Experts (PoE) target, aligning the policy with the intersection of capability and privacy requirements. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that SELFCI, without relying on costly external supervision, consistently outperforms competitive baselines such as online reinforcement learning algorithms (e.g., GRPO). These trends further extend to out-of-domain settings involving agentic workflows and accumulated private context, suggesting that SELFCI provides a practical path toward CI alignment.

中文摘要翻译: Abstract translation unavailable

### [134] [Catching a Moving Subspace: Low-Rank Bandits Beyond Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20269)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Original Abstract: Many bandit deployments (recommendation, clinical dosing, ad targeting) share two facts prior work handles only in isolation: rewards live on a low-dimensional latent subspace, and that subspace drifts. Stationary low-rank bandits exploit rank but break under subspace change; non-stationary linear bandits adapt to drift but pay ambient rate $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T})$. We study piecewise-stationary low-rank linear contextual bandits with scalar feedback: $θ_t = B_k^\star w_t$ with rank-$r$ factor $B_k^\star\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}$ constant within each of $K$ unknown segments and able to shift at boundaries. Our results are tight along three axes. (i) Identification boundary. With single-play scalar rewards, the moving subspace is recoverable through quadratic functionals of rewards iff three probe-side conditions hold: known noise variance, bounded state-noise coupling, and full-dimensional probe support. Each is necessary in the unrestricted-second-moment problem, and jointly they are sufficient, characterizing the boundary of the solvable region. (ii) Algorithm and dynamic regret. SPSC interleaves isotropic probes with windowed projected ridge-UCB exploitation inside the learned $r$-dimensional subspace; a CUSUM-style variant discovers segment boundaries online. The costed dynamic regret is $\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$, replacing the ambient $d\sqrt{T}$ rate with the intrinsic rank. (iii) Empirics. On eleven benchmarks spanning synthetic, UCI/MovieLens, semi-synthetic clinical, and ZOZOTOWN production-log data, SPSC outperforms non-stationary and low-rank baselines whenever $d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$, matching the analytical crossover. To our knowledge, this is the first work to characterize the identification boundary and attain the intrinsic-rank dynamic-regret rate in this setting.

中文摘要翻译: Abstract translation unavailable

</details>


### [135] [Conformal Selective Acting: Anytime-Valid Risk Control for RLVR-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20270)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Selective Acting (CSA)，一种用于在线更新的大语言模型部署的逐轮安全包装器，满足任意路径选择性风险控制，填补了现有方法在适应性流上的安全认证空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有包装器无法为在线自适应更新的LLM部署流提供每轮安全认证：离线共形风险方法要求可交换性，在线共形方法仅控制长期平均，非可交换扩展仅边缘有效，最接近的A-RCPS控制边缘风险而非选择性风险。监管组织需要无跨部署池化、不等待长期平均的逐轮安全证书。

Method: CSA作为逐轮包装器，在每个阈值上维护Ville型e-process（基于Bonferroni网格），针对RLVR过滤进行评估。利用可预测更新和保序校准的单调风险，实现任意路径下的选择性风险控制。

Result: 在8个专家基准（480条流）、16个对抗性分布偏移单元（160条流）和5个实时Expert-Iteration RLVR单元（在线LoRA，4个基础模型，3个架构族，10300轮）上，CSA是10种对比方法中唯一满足路径有效性和无拒绝部署的方法。理论证明风险界R_T^act ≤ α + O(N_T^{-1/2})，最优认证匹配Θ(η̄^{-2} log(1/δ))，以及与时间范围无关的发布率间隙。

Conclusion: CSA是部署侧的补充组件，与模型正交，适用于无法使用前沿API的运营商。不提出新LLM、训练算法或策略类，而是为已训练好的模型提供安全认证包装。

Original Abstract: A local specialist LLM, fine-tuned with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on operator-local data, is installed in a regulated organization with per-deployment error budget $α$. The operator needs a safety certificate for this deployment's stream at every round: no pooling across deployments, no waiting for a long-run average. Existing wrappers cannot deliver this on adaptive, online-updated streams: offline conformal-risk methods require exchangeability; online-conformal methods bound only long-run averages; non-exchangeable extensions are marginally valid; and the closest anytime wrapper, A-RCPS, controls marginal rather than selective risk. Using a (test statistic, validity guarantee, deployment rule) framework, we identify one empty cell forced by deployment requirements: e-process per threshold, selective risk, anytime-pathwise validity, max-certified-threshold rule. Conformal Selective Acting (CSA) fills it as a per-round wrapper maintaining a Ville-type e-process per threshold on a Bonferroni grid, evaluated against the RLVR filtration. Under predictable updates and isotonic-calibrated monotone risk we prove (i) an anytime-pathwise selective-risk bound $R_T^{\mathrm{act}}\leα+O(N_T^{-1/2})$, (ii) rate-optimal certification matching $Θ(\barη^{-2}\log(1/δ))$, and (iii) a horizon-independent release-rate gap. Across eight specialist benchmarks ($480$ streams), sixteen adversarial distribution-shift cells ($160$ streams), and five live Expert-Iteration RLVR cells with online LoRA over four base models in three architecture families ($10{,}300$ rounds), CSA is the only method among ten compared that satisfies pathwise validity and non-refusing deployment on every cell. We do not propose a new LLM, training algorithm, or policy class; CSA is the deployment-side complement, orthogonal to the model, for operators who cannot use a frontier API.

中文摘要翻译: 一个本地专家LLM，通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在运营商本地数据上进行微调，被部署在一个受监管的组织中，每次部署的误差预算为α。运营商需要在每一轮为部署的数据流获取安全证书：不允许跨部署池化，也不等待长期平均。现有包装器无法为自适应、在线更新的数据流提供这种保障：离线共形风险方法要求可交换性；在线共形方法仅能约束长期平均；非可交换扩展仅具有边缘有效性；最接近的任意时间包装器A-RCPS控制的是边缘风险而非选择性风险。通过使用（检验统计量，有效性保证，部署规则）框架，我们识别出被部署需求强制要求的一个空白单元：每个阈值的e-process、选择性风险、任意时间路径有效性、最大认证阈值规则。共形选择性行动（CSA）填补了这一空白，它作为一个逐轮包装器，在Bonferroni网格上为每个阈值维护一个Ville型e-process，并针对RLVR过滤进行评估。在可预测更新和保序校准的单调风险下，我们证明了：(i) 任意时间路径选择性风险界 R_T^act ≤ α + O(N_T^{-1/2})，(ii) 与 Θ(η̄^{-2} log(1/δ)) 匹配的率最优认证，以及 (iii) 与时间范围无关的发布率间隙。在八个专家基准（480条流）、十六个对抗性分布偏移单元（160条流）以及五个基于Expert-Iteration RLVR的实时单元上（使用在线LoRA，涵盖三个架构族的四个基础模型，共10,300轮），CSA是十种对比方法中唯一在所有单元上同时满足路径有效性和无拒绝部署的方法。我们并未提出新的LLM、训练算法或策略类；CSA是部署侧的补充组件，与模型正交，适用于无法使用前沿API的运营商。

</details>


### [136] [Smaller Abstract State Spaces Enable Cross-Scale Generalization in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20272)

Original Abstract: While humans readily generalize abstract concepts to more complex or larger tasks, building Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems with this ability remains elusive. Here, we present the first theoretical model of how such Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization can be achieved in RL agents. Our approach considers Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) and assumes that an intelligent agent uses an abstraction function to determine which experiences can be treated as equivalent and which must be distinguished. First, we extend the existing state abstraction framework and proof techniques to POMDPs. Then, we define a successor-weighted model reduction, a model reduction variant that enables compression into smaller abstract spaces than prior definitions allow. We derive a bound on the agent's OOD test performance, thereby defining the conditions under which OOD generalization is achievable. This bound decomposes an agent's performance loss into approximation and estimation errors, revealing how reducing an agent's abstract state space size improves test performance and OOD generalization. Our analysis suggests that constraining an agent to operate over a small, finite set of abstract states is necessary for achieving generalization to more complex tasks. Our results motivate further research into learning RL architectures that scale across tasks of varying complexity levels.

### [137] [Modality-Decoupled Online Recursive Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20273)

Original Abstract: Online model editing for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) requires assimilating a stream of corrections under tight compute and memory budgets. Yet editors developed for text-only LLMs often degrade on MLLMs: visually dominant activations skew the statistics that shape updates, causing cross-modal conflict, while sequential writes become entangled in a shared edit space and amplify long-horizon interference, causing inter-edit interference. To address these, we propose M-ORE, a modality-decoupled online recursive editor for lifelong MLLM adaptation. M-ORE is derived from a unified proximal-projection formulation and admits a closed-form update with a Sherman-Morrison recursion, yielding constant per-edit overhead. It maintains module-wise locality statistics for the text stack and the visual projector to avoid visually dominated update shaping and performs continual updates in a fixed orthogonal low-rank edit subspace via a Sherman-Morrison recursion to mitigate long-horizon interference. Experiments on multiple MLLM backbones and online editing benchmarks show that our M-ORE method consistently improves reliability, generality, and locality over strong baselines, while achieving favorable quality-efficiency scaling. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/lab-klc/M-ORE.

### [138] [OmniISR: A Unified Framework for Centralized and Federated Learning via Intermediate Supervision and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20276)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Guangxu Zhu,Ming Tang,Chen Zhang,Lisheng Wu,Lei Zhou,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OmniISR统一框架，通过中间监督与正则化信号融合集中学习、联邦学习及混合模式，理论证明收敛性并降低CL-FL性能差距22.60%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 边缘智能全球部署面临异质法律框架：部分地区允许集中学习（CL），而其他地区强制数据本地化需用联邦学习（FL）。这两种模式存在不兼容的优化机制（CL中无偏全局梯度但存在内部协变量偏移，FL中有偏、漂移的局部更新），简单融合缺乏严格理论保证。

Method: 提出OmniISR框架，在多个隐藏层引入中间监督与正则化信号：（1）使用互信息（MI）作为中间监督，对齐CL中的内部协变量偏移和FL中的客户端漂移表示；（2）采用负熵（NE）作为中间正则化器，惩罚过度自信预测、保持表示不确定性、避免设备特定坍缩。

Result: 理论方面：推导了统一的O(1/√T)收敛界、FL漂移界、梯度对齐保证和逃逸时间界。实验方面：在集中式和联邦式范式中均提升模型性能，将CL-FL差距降低22.60%，在多个FL算法上获得37/48的配对指标胜利。

Conclusion: OmniISR通过中间监督与正则化有效统一了CL和FL训练模式，兼具理论收敛保证和实际性能提升，为异质法律框架下的边缘智能部署提供了可行的统一优化方案。

Original Abstract: The global deployment of edge intelligence operates across heterogeneous legal frameworks. While some regions permit centralized learning (CL) via cloud data aggregation, others enforce strict data localization, necessitating federated learning (FL). This operational dichotomy introduces two incompatible optimization regimes (i.e., unbiased global gradients yet coupled with internal covariate shift in CL versus biased, drift-prone local updates in FL), resulting in that any naive integration of the two lacks rigorous theoretical guarantees. To fill this gap, we propose OmniISR, a unified framework that fuses pure CL, pure FL, and hybrid CL-FL training modes via equipping intermediate supervision and regularization (ISR) signals at multiple hidden layers. Specifically, we propose (i) to use mutual-information (MI) as intermediate supervision to align shifting internal covariate in CL and client-drifting representations in FL, and (ii) to adopt negative-entropy (NE) as intermediate regularizer to penalize overconfident prediction, preserve representational uncertainty, and avoid device-specific collapse. On the theory side, we derive (i) a unified, ISR-agnostic, and non-asymptotic O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence bound that shows the introduced ISR does not violate standard SGD convergence, (ii) a federated drift-bound that quantifies the ISR-reduced client drift, (iii) a gradient-alignment guarantee that ensures non-conflicting CL and FL updates under mild bias, and (iv) an explicit escape-time bound that indicates that CL-FL hybrid mixing enlarges effective stochasticity and accelerates escape from strict saddles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniISR consistently improves model performance in both centralized and federated paradigms, reduces the CL-FL gap by 22.60%, and yields 37/48 paired metric wins across multiple FL algorithms.

中文摘要翻译: 边缘智能的全球部署运行在异质法律框架下。一些地区允许通过云端数据聚合进行集中学习（CL），而其他地区则强制执行严格的数据本地化，必须使用联邦学习（FL）。这种操作上的二分法产生了两种不兼容的优化机制（即CL中无偏的全局梯度但伴随内部协变量偏移，与FL中有偏的、易漂移的局部更新），导致任何简单的两者融合都缺乏严格的理论保证。为填补这一空白，我们提出了OmniISR，一个通过在多隐藏层引入中间监督与正则化（ISR）信号来融合纯CL、纯FL以及混合CL-FL训练模式的统一框架。具体来说，我们提出：（i）使用互信息（MI）作为中间监督，以对齐CL中偏移的内部协变量和FL中客户端漂移的表示；（ii）采用负熵（NE）作为中间正则化器，以惩罚过度自信的预测、保持表示不确定性并避免设备特定的坍缩。在理论方面，我们推导出：（i）一个统一的、与ISR无关的、非渐进的O(1/√T)收敛界，表明引入的ISR不会违反标准SGD收敛；（ii）一个联邦漂移界，量化了ISR减少的客户端漂移；（iii）一个梯度对齐保证，确保在轻微偏差下CL和FL更新不冲突；（iv）一个显式的逃逸时间界，表明CL-FL混合训练增大了有效随机性并加速了从严格鞍点的逃逸。大量实验表明，OmniISR在集中式和联邦式范式中均能持续提升模型性能，将CL-FL差距降低22.60%，并在多个FL算法上取得了37/48的配对指标胜利。

</details>


### [139] [ClaimDiff-RL: Fine-Grained Caption Reinforcement Learning through Visual Claim Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20278)
*Tianle Li,Xuyang Shen,Yan Ma,Rongxin Guo,Shaoxiang Chen,Jiacheng Chen,Haochen Wang,Hongyang Tang,Yucong Zhou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ClaimDiff-RL框架，利用原子化的图文声明差异作为奖励单元进行图像描述强化学习，分别衡量幻觉与遗漏事实，实现更细粒度、可诊断的描述优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长文本图像描述强化学习存在奖励粒度问题：整体序列级奖励将局部错误（幻觉、遗漏关键事实）压缩为单一信号，无法区分事实性与覆盖度之间的权衡。

Method: ClaimDiff-RL框架：给定图像、演员描述和参考描述，多模态裁判枚举视觉上可验证的原子声明差异，逐条核验，赋予开放式错误类型和严重等级，生成每条差异的统计量用于奖励组合。

Result: 整体标量奖励在减少幻觉的同时会增加遗漏事实；ClaimDiff-RL揭示了这一权衡，在160张图像人工标注诊断基准、公开描述基准和VQA基准上取得了更好的幻觉-遗漏事实平衡，并在物体计数、空间关系、场景识别等细粒度能力维度上超越Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。

Conclusion: 类型化、可验证的声明差异是一种有效的细粒度奖励单元，可用于可诊断的图像描述强化学习。

Original Abstract: Long-form image captioning exposes a reward granularity problem in RL: captions are judged as whole sequences, while the important errors occur at the level of individual visual claims. A good dense caption should be both faithful and informative, avoiding hallucination without omitting salient details. Yet pairwise preferences, reference-based metrics, and holistic scalar rewards compress these local errors into a single sequence-level signal, obscuring the tradeoff between factuality and coverage. We introduce ClaimDiff-RL, a framework that uses reference-conditioned atomic claim differences as the reward unit for caption RL. Given an image, an actor caption, and a reference caption, a multimodal judge enumerates visually grounded differences, verifies each difference against the image, assigns open-vocabulary error types and severity levels, and produces per-difference statistics for reward composition. This makes hallucinated claims and omitted salient facts separately measurable and tunable. Experiments show that holistic scalar rewards can reduce hallucination by increasing missing facts, while ClaimDiff-RL exposes this faithfulness and coverage tradeoff and enables more balanced operating points. On a 160-image human-labeled diagnostic benchmark, public captioning benchmarks, and VQA benchmarks, ClaimDiff-RL improves the hallucination--missing-fact balance, preserves general capability, and even surpasses Gemini-3-Pro-Preview on several fine-grained Capability dimensions such as object counting, spatial relations, and scene recognition. These results suggest that typed, verifiable claim differences are an effective reward unit for fine-grained and diagnosable caption RL.

中文摘要翻译: 长文本图像描述揭示了强化学习中的奖励粒度问题：描述被作为整体序列进行评判，而重要的错误却发生在单个视觉声明层面。一个好的密集描述应兼具忠实性和信息量，既避免幻觉又不遗漏显著细节。然而，成对偏好、基于参考的度量以及整体标量奖励将这些局部错误压缩为单一的序列级信号，模糊了事实性与覆盖度之间的权衡。我们提出ClaimDiff-RL框架，该框架使用基于参考条件的原子化声明差异作为描述强化学习的奖励单元。给定一张图像、一个演员描述和一个参考描述，多模态裁判枚举视觉上可验证的差异，逐条核验其与图像的一致性，赋予开放式错误类型和严重等级，并生成每一条差异的统计量用于奖励组合。这使得幻觉声明和遗漏的关键事实可以分别被测量和调整。实验表明，整体标量奖励在减少幻觉的同时会增加遗漏事实，而ClaimDiff-RL揭示了这一忠实性与覆盖度的权衡，并实现了更平衡的工作点。在160张图像的人工标注诊断基准、公开描述基准和VQA基准上，ClaimDiff-RL改善了幻觉-遗漏事实的平衡，保持了通用能力，甚至在物体计数、空间关系、场景识别等多个细粒度能力维度上超越了Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。这些结果表明，类型化、可验证的声明差异是用于细粒度且可诊断的描述强化学习的一种有效奖励单元。

</details>


### [140] [Introspective X Training: Feedback Conditioning Improves Scaling Across all LLM Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20285)
*Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,Jaehun Jung,Syeda Nahida Akter,Hyunwoo Kim,Yuxiao Qu,David Acuna,Shrimai Prabhumoye,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Original Abstract: We tackle the question of how to scale more efficiently across the many, ever-growing stages of current LLM training pipelines. Our guiding intuition stems from the fact that the dynamics of later stages of the pipeline, e.g. post-training, can be used to inform earlier stages such as pre-training. To this end, we propose Introspective Training (or IXT), inspired by offline reward-conditioned reinforcement learning and applicable to any stage of training. IXT uses a thinking reward model to annotate data with natural language critique based feedback, enabling quality aware training from the earliest stages of the pipeline. Models are then trained by prefix-conditioning the data with the generated feedback -- ensuring that not all tokens are treated equally starting much earlier in training than usual. Comprehensive experiments on 7.5-12B transformer-based dense LLMs trained from scratch all the way up to 18 Trillion tokens seen show that our method: bends scaling curves resulting in up to 2.8x more compute efficiency generally; and reaches performance levels unachievable for models trained otherwise in domains such as math and code.

### [141] [FusionCell: Cross-Attentive Fusion of Layout Geometry and Netlist Topology for Standard-Cell Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20287)
*Haoyi Zhang,Kairong Guo,Bojie Zhang,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang*

Original Abstract: Standard cells form the building blocks of digital circuits, so their delay and power critically influence chip-level performance; yet characterization still relies on slow simulation sweeps, and many fast predictors ignore layout geometry, missing coupling and layout-dependent effects. The challenge is to jointly represent layout geometry and netlist topology so models capture fine-grained spatial details together with structural connectivity for accurate performance prediction. We introduce FusionCell, a dual-modality predictor that treats routed layout geometry and netlist topology as inputs and fuses them explicitly in a unified model. A DeiT encoder processes three-layer routed layouts, while a graph transformer models heterogeneous device/net graphs. The modalities are integrated through a topology-guided mechanism, where the netlist acts as a structural "map" to actively query relevant physical regions in the layout for joint geometric and topological reasoning. We build a 7nm dataset based on the ASAP7 PDK with over 19.5k cells spanning 149 types using automatic tools, targeting six metrics: signal rise/fall delay, transition, and power. Experimental results demonstrate that FusionCell reduces regression error, with an average MAPE of 0.92 percent, and improves Spearman/Kendall ranking over baselines, while accelerating the characterization process by orders of magnitude compared to circuit simulation.

### [142] [Plug-and-Play Spiking Operators: Breaking the Nonlinearity Bottleneck in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20289)
*Xinzhe Yuan,Xiang Peng,Bin Gu,Huan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的框架，将Transformer非线性算子（如Softmax、SiLU、归一化）分解为除法、指数和ℓ2范数三种原语，通过LIF神经元群和轻量位移缩放实现脉冲友好近似，避免浮点运算，在无需微调的情况下实现ANN到SNN的高效转换，精度损失小于1%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ANN-to-SNN转换方法主要支持Transformer的线性代数运算，但对非线性算子的支持有限，这些非线性算子（如除法、指数、范数计算）与标准LIF神经元的脉冲动力学不兼容，限制了整体框架在神经形态硬件上的执行。

Method: 将Transformer非线性计算分解为除法、指数和ℓ2范数三种基本原语，利用LIF神经元群实现群体计算（population computation），并结合轻量级位移缩放（bit-shift scaling）避免浮点运算；将这些原语组合成模块化算子块，支持常见的Transformer非线性操作（Softmax、SiLU、归一化），无需微调即可集成到现有ANN-to-SNN流程中。

Result: 在多种LLM Transformer上的实验表明，选择性替换目标非线性算子后，所有评估任务的精度下降均小于1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的即插即用框架有效填补了现有ANN-to-SNN转换中对非线性算子支持的空白，实现了全脉冲驱动的Transformer非线性运算，且无需训练成本。

Original Abstract: ANN-to-SNN conversion offers a practical, training-free route to spiking large language models. However, current pipelines primarily focus on spike-driven realizations for Transformer linear-algebra operations, while providing limited support for key nonlinear operators. This gap limits compatibility with neuromorphic-style execution constraints, where such nonlinearities typically require division, exponentiation, or norm computations that are not naturally supported by standard leaky integrate-and-fire dynamics. To solve this problem, we propose a plug-and-play framework that implements spike-friendly approximations for Transformer nonlinearities and integrates into existing ANN-to-SNN pipelines. Our method decomposes these nonlinear computations into three recurring primitives -- division, exponentiation, and $\ell_2$ norms -- and realizes them via population computation using LIF neuron groups, combined with lightweight bit-shift scaling to avoid floating-point arithmetic. By composing these primitives as modular operator blocks, our framework supports common Transformer nonlinearities (e.g., Softmax, SiLU, and normalization) without any fine-tuning. Experiments on a range of LLMs Transformers show that selectively replacing the targeted nonlinear operators incurs less than a $1\%$ accuracy drop across all evaluated tasks.

中文摘要翻译: ANN到SNN的转换为脉冲大语言模型提供了一条实用且无需训练的途径。然而，当前的转换流程主要关注Transformer线性代数运算的脉冲驱动实现，而对关键非线性算子的支持有限。这一差距限制了与神经形态执行约束的兼容性，因为这些非线性操作通常需要除法、指数或范数计算，而标准泄漏积分发放（LIF）动力学无法自然支持这些运算。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种即插即用框架，该框架实现了Transformer非线性算子的脉冲友好近似，并可集成到现有的ANN-to-SNN转换流程中。我们的方法将这些非线性计算分解为三种基本原语——除法、指数和ℓ2范数——并通过使用LIF神经元群的群体计算（population computation）结合轻量级位移缩放（避免浮点运算）来实现它们。通过将这些原语组合成模块化算子块，我们的框架无需任何微调即可支持常见的Transformer非线性操作（如Softmax、SiLU和归一化）。在多种LLM Transformer上的实验表明，选择性替换目标非线性算子后，所有评估任务的精度下降均小于1%。

</details>


### [143] [TreeText-CTS: Compact, Source-Traceable Tree-Path Evidence for Irregular Clinical Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20292)
*Kwanhyung Lee,Juhwan Choi,Jongheon Kim,Joohyung Lee,Hyeongwon Jang,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Numerical time-series models can effectively process irregular electronic health record (EHR) trajectories, but they do not naturally expose the measurements and temporal patterns supporting each risk estimate as readable evidence. Existing text-based interfaces improve readability, but typically rely on either raw serialization, which is lengthy and redundant, or patient-level free-form summaries, which are difficult to trace to source measurements and time windows. To bridge this gap, we introduce TreeText-CTS (Clinical Time-Series), which converts irregular EHR trajectories into human-readable, compact, source-traceable tree-path evidence units without patient-level summarization or inference-time autoregressive decoding. TreeText-CTS routes multi-scale window summaries through frozen XGBoost models and verbalizes activated tree paths as deterministic, source-traceable evidence units composed of threshold conditions. An evidence selector assembles an informative subset of these units, which a language-model encoder then integrates for prediction. Across PhysioNet 2012 mortality, MIMIC-III mortality, and PhysioNet 2019 sepsis-onset forecasting, TreeText-CTS achieves the best AUROC and AUPRC among evaluated text-based EHR time-series interfaces, improving AUPRC by 6.0 to 9.7 absolute percentage points over the strongest prior text-based interface while remaining competitive with numerical time-series models. Ablations show that tree-path evidence construction, evidence selection, and language-model composition each contribute to performance. Because every span passed to the language-model encoder is constructed from activated tree-path threshold conditions, TreeText-CTS makes the evidence supplied to the final predictor inspectable and source-traceable.

中文摘要翻译: Abstract translation unavailable

</details>


### [144] [Quant.npu: Enabling Efficient Mobile NPU Inference for on-device LLMs via Fully Static Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20295)
*Jinghe Zhang,Daliang Xu,Chenghua Wang,Weikai Xie,Tao Qi,Yun Ma,Mengwei Xu,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Quant.npu，一个面向移动设备NPU的全静态量化框架，通过可学习量化参数、旋转矩阵以及感知旋转与比特宽度的初始化和两阶段优化策略，实现低比特激活-权重量化，在真实NPU上推理延迟降低15.1%且精度与SOTA相当。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法（PTQ）主要依赖动态激活量化，与移动设备NPU必须使用全静态量化的硬件约束不兼容，导致高精度PTQ与NPU推理之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出Quant.npu框架，包括：(1) 可学习量化参数和旋转矩阵，实现纯整数全静态量化；(2) 旋转与比特宽度感知的初始化策略，适应不同激活分布；(3) 分布感知的两阶段选择性优化管道，解决梯度不稳定性；(4) 敏感度引导的自适应混合精度方案，平衡精度与效率。

Result: 在真实移动设备NPU上，Quant.npu在精度上与SOTA方法相当，同时推理延迟最高降低15.1%。

Conclusion: 通过合理的初始化与选择性优化策略，全静态量化可以在NPU硬件约束下达到与动态量化相媲美的精度，同时显著提升推理效率。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile devices, where Neural Processing Units (NPUs) necessitate fully static quantization for optimal inference efficiency. However, existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods predominantly rely on dynamic activation quantization, rendering them incompatible with NPU hardware constraints. To bridge the gap between high-fidelity PTQ and NPU-constrained inference, we propose Quant.npu, a integer-only fully static quantization framework. It incorporates learnable quantization parameters and rotation matrices, enabling low-bit activation-weight quantization without runtime quantization parameters re-computation. Crucially, we identify that initialization and selective optimization of quantization parameters is pivotal for optimization stability, as improper initialization and naive joint optimization induce gradient instability that disrupts the optimization of rotation matrices. To address this, we propose a rotation-and-bit-width-aware initialization tailored to diverse activation profiles and a distribution-aware selective optimization (two-stage quantization pipeline) tailored to rotated and unrotated tensors. Furthermore, we introduce a sensitivity-guided adaptive mixed-precision scheme to balance accuracy with inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on real-world mobile NPUs demonstrate that Quant.npu achieves comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art methods, while reducing inference latency by up to 15.1%.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地部署在移动设备上，而神经处理单元（NPU）要求全静态量化以实现最优推理效率。然而，现有的后训练量化（PTQ）方法主要依赖动态激活量化，与NPU硬件约束不兼容。为弥合高保真PTQ与NPU约束推理之间的鸿沟，我们提出Quant.npu，一个纯整数全静态量化框架。该框架引入可学习量化参数和旋转矩阵，能够在无需运行时重新计算量化参数的情况下实现低比特激活-权重量化。关键地，我们发现量化参数的初始化和选择性优化对优化稳定性至关重要，因为不当的初始化和朴素的联合优化会导致梯度不稳定，破坏旋转矩阵的优化。为此，我们提出一种面向不同激活分布的旋转与比特宽度感知的初始化策略，以及一种面向旋转张量和未旋转张量的分布感知选择性优化（两阶段量化管道）。此外，我们引入敏感度引导的自适应混合精度方案，以平衡精度与推理效率。在真实移动设备NPU上的广泛实验表明，Quant.npu在精度上与最先进方法相当，同时推理延迟最高降低15.1%。

</details>


### [145] [Spectral Unforgetting: Post-Hoc Recovery of Damaged Capabilities Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20296)
*Aarash Abro,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DG-Hard的后验修复方法，通过对微调权重更新矩阵进行谱硬阈值处理，在保留目标任务性能的同时恢复被灾难性遗忘的通用能力，无需依赖任何数据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 语言模型在目标任务微调后往往会灾难性遗忘预训练阶段获得的通用能力，现有修复方法要么需要数据、要么简单回退损害目标任务性能，亟需一种无需数据、能同时保留目标任务增益并恢复受损能力的后验修复方案。

Method: 将微调权重更新Δ=W_ft-W_base视为低秩任务对齐信号与IID类噪声残差的叠加，对每个权重增量矩阵应用Donoho-Gavish硬奇异值阈值（SVD滤波），保留高能量结构化部分，去除谱主体噪声，实现闭式后验修复。

Result: 在14个（模型，任务）设置和9个跨域保留基准上，DG-Hard在事后修复基线中取得了最强平衡修复效果；同时在不使用任何对齐数据的情况下，恢复了良性微调导致的安全对齐退化。

Conclusion: 微调导致的部分能力损失并非专业化的不可避免后果，而是权重更新中可移除的谱残留；DG-Hard通过简单的闭式SVD过滤即可有效恢复受损能力。

Original Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model for a target task routinely degrades capabilities the training data never explicitly threatened. We study this phenomenon, known as catastrophic forgetting, and propose a post-hoc repair solution that uses only the pretrained checkpoint $W_{\mathrm{base}}$ and its fine-tuned descendant $W_{\mathrm{ft}}$. The goal is not merely to revert the model toward the base checkpoint, but to recover capabilities damaged by fine-tuning while preserving both the target-task gains and any beneficial held-out improvements. We introduce DG-Hard, a checkpoint-only spectral repair method for the fine-tuning update $Δ= W_{\mathrm{ft}} - W_{\mathrm{base}}$. DG-Hard treats $Δ$ as a low-rank task-aligned signal embedded in an IID-like noise residual that gradient descent has no incentive to remove, and applies the Donoho-Gavish hard singular-value threshold to each weight-delta matrix, keeping the structured high-energy part of the update and removing the spectral bulk. This reduces repair to a closed-form SVD filtering step requiring no data-dependent tuning. A central difficulty is evaluation: average accuracy hides per-benchmark failures, while naive recovery scores reward models that simply revert toward the base. We therefore introduce a partition-conditional metric that separately tracks healing, preservation, non-damage, and target-task retention. Across $14$ (model, task) settings and nine cross-domain held-out benchmarks, DG-Hard achieves the strongest balanced repair among post-hoc baselines. DG-Hard also restores safety alignment degraded by benign fine-tuning on three independent safety axes, despite using no alignment data. These results suggest that part of fine-tuning-induced capability loss is not an unavoidable consequence of specialization, but a removable spectral residue in the weight update itself.

中文摘要翻译: 对语言模型进行目标任务微调通常会损害训练数据中从未明确威胁过的能力。我们研究这一被称为灾难性遗忘的现象，并提出一种仅使用预训练检查点W_base及其微调后代W_ft的后验修复方案。目标不仅仅是让模型回退到基础检查点，而是在保留目标任务增益和任何有益的保留改进的同时，恢复被微调损害的能力。我们引入DG-Hard，一种针对微调更新Δ=W_ft-W_base的仅基于检查点的谱修复方法。DG-Hard将Δ视为嵌入在梯度下降没有动机去除的IID类噪声残差中的低秩任务对齐信号，并对每个权重增量矩阵应用Donoho-Gavish硬奇异值阈值，保留更新的结构化高能部分并移除谱主体。这将修复简化为一个无需数据依赖调参的闭式SVD过滤步骤。一个核心难点在于评估：平均准确率隐藏了每个基准上的失败，而朴素的恢复分数会奖励那些简单回退到基础模型的方案。因此，我们引入了一个分区条件指标，分别追踪修复、保留、无损害和目标任务保持。在14个（模型，任务）设置和九个跨域保留基准上，DG-Hard在事后修复基线中取得了最强的平衡修复。尽管未使用任何对齐数据，DG-Hard还恢复了良性微调导致的安全对齐退化（在三个独立安全轴线上）。这些结果表明，微调导致的部分能力损失并非专业化的不可避免后果，而是权重更新中可移除的谱残留。

</details>


### [146] [Robust Subspace-Constrained Quadratic Models for Low-Dimensional Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20300)
*Zheng Zhai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），用于从高维数据中学习低维结构。该模型扩展了SQMF框架，能够处理包括广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯在内的多种噪声分布，并设计了基于梯度的回溯线搜索优化算法。实验表明，该方法在鲁棒性和重建精度上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有子空间约束二次矩阵分解（SQMF）方法对噪声分布的假设较为受限，难以同时适应重尾噪声和轻尾噪声等不同噪声特性，导致在多样化数据场景下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出鲁棒子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），在SQMF框架基础上引入广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯等更广泛的噪声分布模型；采用基于梯度的回溯线搜索算法求解非凸优化问题；并对ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数进行敏感性分析。

Result: 理论分析揭示了ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数在不同噪声特性下的行为差异；大量数值实验验证了该方法在鲁棒性和重建精度上 consistently 优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SCQM模型通过扩展噪声分布假设并配合高效的优化算法，有效提升了从高维数据中学习低维结构的鲁棒性与精度，为实际应用中应对多样化噪声环境提供了更优的解决方案。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose a robust subspace-constrained quadratic model (SCQM) for learning low-dimensional structure from high-dimensional data. Building upon the subspace-constrained quadratic matrix factorization (SQMF) framework, the proposed model accommodates a broad class of noise distributions, including generalized Gaussian and radial Laplace models. This generalization enables reliable performance under both heavy-tailed and light-tailed noise, thereby substantially enhancing robustness across diverse data regimes. To efficiently address the resulting nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a gradient-based algorithm equipped with a backtracking line-search strategy that ensures stable and efficient convergence. In addition, we present a sensitivity analysis of the $\ell_p^p$ and $\ell_2$ loss functions, elucidating their distinct behaviors under varying noise characteristics. Extensive numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing methods in terms of robustness and reconstruction accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），用于从高维数据中学习低维结构。该模型基于子空间约束二次矩阵分解（SQMF）框架，能够适应包括广义高斯分布和径向拉普拉斯分布在内的广泛噪声分布类别。这一泛化能力使得模型在重尾噪声和轻尾噪声下均能实现可靠性能，从而显著增强了在多样化数据场景中的鲁棒性。为高效求解由此产生的非凸优化问题，我们开发了一种配备回溯线搜索策略的梯度下降算法，保证了稳定且高效的收敛。此外，我们对ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数进行了敏感性分析，阐明了它们在不同噪声特性下的不同行为。大量数值实验验证了理论分析，并表明所提出的方法在鲁棒性和重建精度方面 consistently 优于现有方法。

</details>


### [147] [Neural Collapse by Design: Learning Class Prototypes on the Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20302)
*Panagiotis Koromilas,Theodoros Giannakopoulos,Mihalis A. Nicolaou,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出交叉熵（CE）和监督对比学习（SCL）均未达到理论最优的神经坍缩（NC），并证明两者本质上是同一方法——单位超球面上的原型对比。从CE侧提出NTCE和NONL两种归一化损失，引入对比学习的关键要素；从SCL侧证明其目标已隐式优化了基于类均值嵌入的分类器，线性探测冗余且有害。实验表明该方法在多个基准上逼近NC、提升迁移学习与类别不平衡性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 监督分类的两个主流范式——交叉熵（CE）和监督对比学习（SCL）在实践中均未达到理论最优的神经坍缩（NC）。CE在径向自由度上无约束，收敛到退化几何结构；SCL虽在预训练阶段驱动特征趋向NC，但在后续线性探测阶段丢弃了该结构。因此需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 从CE侧提出NTCE和NONL两种归一化损失，将对比优化中缺失的要素（大型有效负样本集、解耦的对齐与均匀性项）引入分类器学习。从SCL侧证明其目标已在整个训练过程中隐式优化了一个原则性分类器（权重为类均值嵌入），因此线性探测既冗余又有害，提出固定原型直接作为分类器。

Result: 在包括ImageNet-1K的四个基准上：NTCE和NONL超越CE准确率，接近NC（≥95%），并在5个指标中的4个上以不到7.5%的迭代次数匹配CE的收敛NC；SCL固定原型匹配线性探测精度且无需小时级分类器训练；迁移学习平均提升+5.5%，严重类别不平衡下提升+8.7%，ImageNet-C上mCE更低。

Conclusion: 监督学习可被重新定义为超球面上的原型学习，两条路径均可按设计达到神经坍缩（NC），弥合了理论与实践的差距。

Original Abstract: Supervised classification has a theoretical optimum, Neural Collapse (NC), yet neither of its two dominant paradigms reaches it in practice. Cross entropy (CE) leaves radial degrees of freedom unconstrained and converges to a degenerate geometry, while supervised contrastive learning (SCL) drives features toward NC during pretraining but discards this structure in a post hoc linear probing phase. We show that both paradigms are different appearances of the same method, prototype contrast on the unit hypersphere, and that closing the gap requires fixing each at its specific point of failure. From the CE side, we propose NTCE and NONL, two normalized losses that import contrastive optimization's missing ingredients into classifier learning: a large effective negative set and decoupled alignment and uniformity terms. From the SCL side, we prove that SCL's objective already optimizes throughout training for a principled classifier whose weights are the class mean embeddings, making linear probing both redundant and harmful. Empirically, on four benchmarks including ImageNet-1K, NTCE and NONL surpass CE accuracy, closely approximate NC ($\geq 95\%$), and match CE's converged NC on 4/5 metrics in under $7.5\%$ of its iterations, while SCL with fixed prototypes matches linear probing without the hours-long classifier training phase. The learned geometry yields $+5.5\%$ mean relative improvement in transfer learning, up to $+8.7\%$ under severe class imbalance, and lower mCE on ImageNet-C, recasting supervised learning as prototype learning on the hypersphere, with NC reached by design on both paths.

中文摘要翻译: 监督分类存在一个理论最优状态——神经坍缩（Neural Collapse, NC），然而其两大主流范式在实践中均未达到该状态。交叉熵（CE）在径向自由度上未加约束，收敛到退化的几何结构；而监督对比学习（SCL）虽然在预训练阶段驱动特征趋向NC，但在后续的线性探测阶段丢弃了该结构。我们证明这两个范式本质上是同一方法的不同表现形式——单位超球面上的原型对比，并指出弥合差距需要分别修复各自的失效点。从CE侧，我们提出NTCE和NONL两种归一化损失，将对比优化中缺失的关键要素引入分类器学习：大型有效负样本集以及解耦的对齐与均匀性项。从SCL侧，我们证明SCL的目标在整个训练过程中已经隐式优化了一个原则性分类器，其权重为类均值嵌入，这使得线性探测既冗余又有害。实验上，在包括ImageNet-1K的四个基准测试中，NTCE和NONL超越了CE的准确率，高度逼近NC（≥95%），并在5个指标中的4个上以不到CE 7.5%的迭代次数匹配了CE收敛后的NC；而使用固定原型的SCL在无需数小时分类器训练阶段的情况下匹配了线性探测的精度。学习到的几何结构在迁移学习中带来平均+5.5%的相对提升，在严重类别不平衡下提升高达+8.7%，并在ImageNet-C上取得更低的mCE，从而将监督学习重新定义为超球面上的原型学习，两条路径均可按设计达到神经坍缩。

</details>


### [148] [AirfoilGen: A valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20303)
*Zhijie Yang,Min Tang,Qiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AirfoilGen提出了一种结合圆扫表示、Transformer编码和条件扩散模型的潜空间扩散方法，实现了高几何有效性和气动性能可控的翼型生成，平均性能条件精度达98.41%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度生成方法在翼型设计中存在几何无效性和气动性能可控性不足的问题：生成形状缺乏有效控制、无效几何频出，且无法有效以气动性能为条件进行生成。

Method: 提出AirfoilGen方法：1) 圆扫表示（circle sweeping representation）约束生成过程以保证几何有效性；2) 用Transformer将翼型编码为向量嵌入；3) 条件扩散模型在潜空间中将高斯噪声去噪为嵌入，并以目标气动性能（升力系数、阻力系数）为条件进行生成。

Result: 发布了包含20多万个翼型的新数据集（远超UIUC数据集的1,650个）；实验表明AirfoilGen在几何有效性和气动性能可控性上显著优于现有方法，平均性能条件精度达98.41%。

Conclusion: AirfoilGen通过有效的表示设计和条件扩散模型，实现了兼具几何有效性与气动性能可控性的翼型生成，为深度学习驱动的翼型设计提供了更实用的方案。

Original Abstract: Airfoil shape design is a fundamental task in aerospace engineering, with a direct impact on flight stability and fuel consumption. Deep learning has recently emerged as a promising tool for this task, but existing deep generative approaches remain limited in both geometric validity and physical controllability. They offer little control over the generated shapes, yielding invalid geometries, and they typically do not condition effectively on aerodynamic performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes AirfoilGen, a valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil. It first introduces a novel airfoil representation scheme, the circle sweeping representation, to constrain the generative process so that output shapes respect essential airfoil characteristics. It then enables explicit control over aerodynamic performance (e.g., lift and drag coefficients) by operating in a learned latent space: a transformer model encodes airfoil shapes into vector embeddings, and a conditional diffusion model denoises Gaussian noise into these latent embeddings while incorporating target aerodynamic performance. In addition, this paper presents a new dataset of over 200,000 airfoils, which is substantially larger than the widely used UIUC airfoil dataset (1,650 airfoils) and more suitable for training modern deep generative models. Experiments demonstrate that AirfoilGen enables airfoil generation with far greater geometric validity and aerodynamic performance controllability than previously achievable, with an average performance-conditioning accuracy of 98.41%.

中文摘要翻译: 翼型形状设计是航空航天工程中的一项基础任务，直接影响飞行稳定性与燃油消耗。深度学习近年来已成为该任务中一种有前景的工具，但现有的深度生成方法在几何有效性和物理可控性方面仍然存在局限：它们对生成形状的控制能力有限，会产生无效几何形状，并且通常无法有效以气动性能为条件进行生成。为了解决这些问题，本文提出了AirfoilGen——一种天然有效且性能感知的翼型潜扩散模型。它首先引入了一种新颖的翼型表示方案——圆扫表示（circle sweeping representation），以约束生成过程，使输出形状符合翼型的基本特征。然后，它通过在学习的潜空间中操作，实现了对气动性能（如升力系数和阻力系数）的显式控制：一个Transformer模型将翼型形状编码为向量嵌入，一个条件扩散模型将高斯噪声去噪为这些潜嵌入，同时纳入目标气动性能作为条件。此外，本文还发布了一个包含超过20万个翼型的新数据集，该数据集远大于广泛使用的UIUC翼型数据集（1,650个翼型），更适合训练现代深度生成模型。实验表明，AirfoilGen在翼型生成中实现了远高于以往的几何有效性和气动性能可控性，平均性能条件精度达到98.41%。

</details>


### [149] [Less Data, Faster Training: repeating smaller datasets speeds up learning via sampling biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20314)
*Jingwen Liu,Ezra Edelman,Surbhi Goel,Bingbin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了“小数据重复训练优于大数据单次训练”的现象（small-vs-large gap），发现使用更少样本但重复更多轮次能节省计算资源，并指出这一加速源于采样偏差带来的按层生长（layer-wise growth）效应，尤其在小数据集时更明显。理论分析和实验均表明，小数据集高重复并非数据不足时的退而求其次，而可主动用作优化中的有利归纳偏置，尤其适用于推理任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有理论无法解释“小数据集重复训练比大数据集更省计算”这一现象，作者希望揭示其内在机制并验证其可主动利用性。

Method: 通过理论分析和多种干预实验（包括不同算法任务、架构和优化器）来探究小数据集重复训练带来计算节省的原因，重点关注采样偏差如何促进适当的按层生长（layer-wise growth）。

Result: 小数据集重复训练带来的加速源于采样偏差诱导的按层生长，该效应在数据集更小时更为显著；实验在多种算法任务、架构和优化器上均观测到此现象。

Conclusion: 小数据集高重复不仅是一种数据不足时的后备策略，更可主动作为优化中的有利归纳偏置，尤其适用于推理任务。

Original Abstract: This work investigates the ``small-vs-large gap'', where repeating on fewer samples can lead to compute saving during training compared to using a larger dataset. This is observed across algorithmic tasks, architectures and optimizers and cannot be explained using prior theory. We argue that the speedup comes from appropriate layer-wise growth enabled by sampling biases, which is more pronounced when the dataset size is smaller. We provide both theoretical analysis and empirical evidence from various interventions. Our results suggest that using a smaller dataset with more repetitions is not just a fallback strategy under data scarcity, but can be proactively leveraged as a favorable inductive biases for optimization, particularly in reasoning tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究探讨了“小数据集与大数据的差距”现象，即在训练中使用较少样本但重复更多轮次，相比于使用更大的数据集可以节省计算资源。该现象在多种算法任务、模型架构和优化器中均有观察到，且无法用现有理论解释。我们认为这一加速源于采样偏差诱导的适当按层生长（layer-wise growth），当数据集规模较小时该效应更为显著。我们提供了理论分析以及来自多种干预实验的经验证据。研究结果表明，使用更小数据集并增加重复次数，不仅仅是一种数据稀缺时的后备策略，更可以主动作为优化中有利的归纳偏置（inductive bias），尤其在推理任务中效果显著。

</details>


### [150] [Symmetrization of Loss Functions for Robust Training of Neural Networks in the Presence of Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20347)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了损失函数的对称化方法，从多类损失函数的唯一分解出发，推导出多类unhinged损失作为唯一凸多类对称损失，并提出了SGCE和α-MAE两种插值损失函数，在噪声标签基准上展现了竞争力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标注训练集昂贵且易出错，需要设计对标签噪声鲁棒的损失函数。对称性条件为此提供了理论保证，但现有对称损失函数在多类场景下的研究不足。

Method: 基于多类损失函数的唯一分解（对称分量+类不敏感项），对交叉熵损失进行对称化得到多类unhinged损失。证明了该损失是唯一凸多类对称损失，并指出其在线性近似中的局部基础作用。进一步提出SGCE和α-MAE两种损失函数，在多类unhinged损失与MAE之间插值，并允许控制损失的β-光滑性。

Result: 理论上证明了多类unhinged损失是唯一凸多类对称损失，且任何对称损失在等分量得分向量附近的线性近似等价于该损失。实验上在标准噪声标签基准上取得了与现有鲁棒损失函数相比有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 多类unhinged损失为标签噪声鲁棒学习提供了一种理论基础扎实的凸对称损失函数，SGCE和α-MAE通过插值策略在实际应用中表现优异。

Original Abstract: Labeling a training set is often expensive and susceptible to errors, making the design of robust loss functions for label noise an important problem. The symmetry condition provides theoretical guarantees for robustness to such noise. In this work, we study a symmetrization method arising from the unique decomposition of any multi-class loss function into a symmetric component and a class-insensitive term. In particular, symmetrizing the cross-entropy loss leads to a linear multi-class extension of the unhinged loss. Unlike in the binary case, the multi-class version must have specific coefficients in order to satisfy the symmetry condition. Under suitable assumptions, we show that this multi-class unhinged loss is the unique convex multi-class symmetric loss. We also show that it has a fundamental local role: the linear approximation of any symmetric loss around score vectors with equal components is equivalent to the multi-class unhinged loss. We then introduce SGCE and alpha-MAE, two loss functions that interpolate between the multi-class unhinged loss and the Mean Absolute Error while allowing control of the beta-smoothness of the loss. Experiments on standard noisy-label benchmarks show competitive performance compared with existing robust loss functions.

中文摘要翻译: 标注训练集通常成本高昂且容易出错，因此设计对标签噪声具有鲁棒性的损失函数是一个重要问题。对称性条件为此类噪声提供了理论上的鲁棒性保证。本文研究了一种源于任何多类损失函数唯一分解（分解为对称分量和类不敏感项）的对称化方法。特别地，对交叉熵损失进行对称化会得到一个线性多类扩展的unhinged损失。与二分类情况不同，多类版本必须具有特定的系数才能满足对称性条件。在适当假设下，我们证明该多类unhinged损失是唯一凸多类对称损失。我们还表明它具有基础性的局部作用：任何对称损失在等分量得分向量附近的线性近似都等价于多类unhinged损失。随后，我们引入了SGCE和α-MAE两种损失函数，它们在多类unhinged损失与平均绝对误差（MAE）之间插值，同时允许控制损失的β-光滑性。在标准噪声标签基准上的实验表明，与现有鲁棒损失函数相比，本文方法具有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [151] [Nonlocal operator learning for fMRI encoding and decoding tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20389)
*Andreas Kramer,Saugat Acharya,Alice Giola,Emanuele Zappala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经积分算子的框架，用于fMRI数据的编码和解码任务，重点研究非局部时空上下文的作用。实验表明，更大的时间窗口能提升性能并产生更结构化的潜在空间表示，神经积分算子是一种有前景的fMRI动力学建模方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: fMRI数据具有高维时空结构，使得预测和解码任务具有挑战性。现有方法对非局部时空上下文的利用不足，本文旨在探索神经积分算子模型在fMRI编码与解码任务中的有效性，并系统研究时空上下文的影响。

Method: 实现了一个潜在神经积分算子框架，在辅助空间中执行不动点迭代，然后通过解码器进行分类和刺激预测。实验系统比较了短/长时间窗口以及视觉皮层与全脑记录的影响，并分析了性能与潜在空间几何结构。

Result: 在解码实验中，更大的时间窗口普遍提升结果，学习的潜在空间比原始数据提供更清晰的类别分离；在编码实验中，更长的时间窗口仍带来一致的性能提升，但绝对性能因任务难度而中等；更大的时间窗口产生更结构化的表示。

Conclusion: 神经积分算子为fMRI动力学建模提供了有前景的框架，更广泛的时空上下文有利于预测和表示学习。利用脑动力学的分布式非局部结构需要专门设计能够捕获此类依赖关系的模型架构。

Original Abstract: Functional MRI data exhibit high-dimensional spatiotemporal structure, making both prediction and decoding challenging. In this work, we investigate neural integral-operator-based models for encoding and decoding tasks in fMRI, with particular emphasis on the role of nonlocal spatiotemporal context. We implement a latent neural integral operator framework that performs fixed point iterations in an auxiliary space from which classification and stimuli prediction is performed via a decoder. We evaluate our model on two open-source fMRI datasets.
  Our experiments examine both decoding of stimuli from fMRI recordings and encoding of fMRI dynamics from stimulus representations. A main focus is the effect of spatiotemporal context: we systematically compare short and long temporal windows, as well as the use of visual cortex vs whole brain recordings, and analyze their influence on performance and latent-space geometry. Across tasks and datasets, larger temporal windows generally improve results and produce more structured learned representations. In decoding experiments, the learned latent space often provides clearer class separation than the raw data. In encoding experiments, although absolute performance remains moderate due to the difficulty of the task, longer temporal windows still yield consistent gains.
  These findings suggest that neural integral operators provide a promising framework for modeling fMRI dynamics and that broader spatiotemporal context can be beneficial for both prediction and representation learning. More broadly, the results indicate that exploiting distributed nonlocal structure in brain dynamics requires model architectures specifically designed to capture such dependencies.

中文摘要翻译: 功能磁共振成像（fMRI）数据呈现高维时空结构，这使得预测和解码任务均具有挑战性。本文研究了基于神经积分算子的模型在fMRI编码与解码任务中的应用，重点探讨非局部时空上下文的作用。我们实现了一个潜在神经积分算子框架，该框架在辅助空间中执行不动点迭代，并通过解码器进行类别分类与刺激预测。我们在两个开源fMRI数据集上评估了模型。实验涵盖从fMRI记录中解码刺激信号以及从刺激表示中编码fMRI动态两个方面。主要关注时空上下文的影响：我们系统比较了短时间窗口与长时间窗口，以及使用视觉皮层记录与全脑记录，并分析了它们对性能和潜在空间几何结构的影响。跨任务与数据集而言，更大的时间窗口总体上能提升结果并产生更结构化的学习表示。在解码实验中，学习的潜在空间通常比原始数据提供更清晰的类别分离。在编码实验中，尽管由于任务难度导致绝对性能中等，但更长的时间窗口仍带来一致的性能提升。这些发现表明，神经积分算子为fMRI动力学建模提供了有前景的框架，更广泛的时空上下文有利于预测和表示学习。更广泛地说，研究结果表明，利用脑动力学的分布式非局部结构需要专门设计能够捕获此类依赖关系的模型架构。

</details>


### [152] [Score-Based Causal Discovery of Latent Variable Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20396)
*Ignavier Ng,Xinshuai Dong,Haoyue Dai,Biwei Huang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于评分的方法来识别包含因果相关潜变量的因果结构，并提供了可识别性保证，实验验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于约束的潜变量因果结构发现方法存在测试顺序依赖、错误传播和显著性水平选择困难等经验性问题，而基于评分的方法（如GES）在无潜变量场景下能缓解这些问题，但将其推广到含潜变量的场景极具挑战。

Method: 证明了适当设计的评分函数在潜变量因果模型上能实现评分等价性和一致性；给出了观测变量边际化后自由度的刻画，并据此开发了精确和连续两种基于评分的结构学习方法，统一了多种现有基于约束的方法。

Result: 实验结果表明所提方法在识别含潜变量的因果结构方面是有效的。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了具有可识别性保证的基于评分的潜变量因果结构学习方法，为潜变量因果发现提供了统一视角和有效工具。

Original Abstract: Identifying latent variables and the causal structure involving them is essential across various scientific fields. While many existing works fall under the category of constraint-based methods (with e.g. conditional independence or rank deficiency tests), they may face empirical challenges such as testing-order dependency, error propagation, and choosing an appropriate significance level. These issues can potentially be mitigated by properly designed score-based methods, such as Greedy Equivalence Search (GES) (Chickering, 2002) in the specific setting without latent variables. Yet, formulating score-based methods with latent variables is highly challenging. In this work, we develop score-based methods that are capable of identifying causal structures containing causally-related latent variables with identifiability guarantees. Specifically, we show that a properly formulated scoring function can achieve score equivalence and consistency for structure learning of latent variable causal models. We further provide a characterization of the degrees of freedom for the marginal over the observed variables under multiple structural assumptions considered in the literature, and accordingly develop both exact and continuous score-based methods. This offers a unified view of several existing constraint-based methods with different structural assumptions. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

中文摘要翻译: 识别潜变量及其因果结构在多个科学领域中至关重要。尽管现有许多工作属于基于约束的方法（例如使用条件独立性或秩不足检验），但它们可能面临经验性问题，如测试顺序依赖、错误传播以及显著性水平选择困难。这些问题可以通过适当设计的基于评分的方法（如无潜变量特定设置下的贪婪等价搜索（GES）（Chickering, 2002））得到潜在缓解。然而，在含潜变量场景下制定基于评分的方法极具挑战性。本文开发了基于评分的方法，能够识别包含因果相关潜变量的因果结构，并具有可识别性保证。具体而言，我们证明了适当设计的评分函数可以在潜变量因果模型上实现评分等价性和一致性。我们进一步针对文献中考虑的多种结构假设，给出了观测变量边际化后自由度的刻画，并据此开发了精确和连续两种基于评分的方法。这为多种具有不同结构假设的现有基于约束的方法提供了统一视角。实验结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [153] [Decomposing MXFP4 quantization error for LLM reinforcement learning: reducible bias, recoverable deadzone, and an irreducible floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20402)
*Xiaocan Li,Shiliang Wu,Zheng Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: MXFP4 arithmetic can dramatically accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs), yet the quantization error introduces severe accuracy degradation. Existing work treats the quantization error as a monolithic noise term, missing the distinct mechanisms upon interpreting how quantization error damages training. We prove an exact three-way decomposition of quantization error and show how each component dominates a distinct RL training pathway. Our theoretical and empirical analysis decomposes the MXFP4 quantization error into three additive components: "scale bias" from power-of-two rounding, "deadzone truncation" from zeroing small values, and "grid noise" from rounding to the nearest 4-bit grid. Each component dominates a distinct RL failure mode: scale bias accumulates multiplicatively through the backward pass, affecting gradient accuracy; deadzone truncation degrades rollout quality; and grid noise raises the policy's entropy. We combine corrections that are RL failure mode-targeted but not component-exclusive: Macro-block scaling to reduce scale bias, Outlier Fallback recovers deadzone entries, but also partially reduces scale bias induced error, and Adaptive Quantization Noise (AQN) for controlling the policy entropy. On Qwen2.5-3B dense and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base mixture-of-experts model, the targeted corrections recover BF16 accuracy to within 0.7% and 3.0% respectively.

中文摘要翻译: Abstract translation unavailable

</details>


### [154] [Spectral Souping: A Unified Framework for Online Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20408)
*Yinlam Chow,Guy Tennenholtz,Ted Yun,James Harrison,Arthur Gretton,Andre Barreto,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Souping框架，利用LLM中发现的通用谱表示进行模型合并，先离线学习多个针对细粒度偏好维度的专用策略，再在线融合这些策略，实现高效、可扩展的个性化偏好对齐，无需昂贵在线重训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLHF方法只能对齐聚合偏好，无法处理个体用户多样且冲突的偏好需求，需要一种高效在线自适应方法来实现个性化对齐。

Method: 首先在LLM中发现通用谱表示（universal spectral representation），并证明其高度适合模型合并；基于此提出两阶段方法：阶段一离线学习一组针对不同细粒度偏好维度的专用策略基；阶段二在线自适应算法在推理时通过合并输出或参数来"混煮"这些策略，实现快速模型自适应。

Result: 在在线偏好对齐基准上，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，证明了其可扩展性和计算效率。

Conclusion: Spectral Souping是一种统一框架，通过谱表示驱动的模型合并实现了高效、可扩展的在线个性化偏好对齐，为动态适配LLM至个体用户偏好提供了一种实用解决方案。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) effectively aligns Large Language Models (LLMs) with aggregate human preferences but often fails to address the diverse and conflicting needs of individual users. To overcome this issue, we introduce Spectral Souping, a unified framework for efficient, online preference alignment. Our contribution is the discovery of a universal spectral representation within LLMs, which is proven to be highly amenable to model merging. This theoretical insight enables a two-phase methodology: we first learn a basis of specialized policies offline, each focused on a distinct, fine-grained preference dimension. An online adaptation algorithm then efficiently ``soups'' these policies at inference time, either by merging their outputs or parameters, enabling rapid model adaptation without the need for costly online retraining w.r.t. tailored preference rewards. Experiments on online preference alignment benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves significant performance improvements over existing state-of-the-art approaches, presenting a scalable and computationally efficient solution for dynamically adapting LLMs to individual user preferences.

中文摘要翻译: 基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）能有效地将大型语言模型（LLM）与聚合性人类偏好对齐，但往往无法处理个体用户多样且相互冲突的需求。为解决这一问题，我们提出了Spectral Souping，一个用于高效在线偏好对齐的统一框架。我们的贡献在于发现了LLM内部的通用谱表示，并证明其高度适合模型合并。这一理论洞见催生了一种两阶段方法：我们首先离线学习一组专用策略基，每个基专注于一个不同的细粒度偏好维度；随后，一种在线自适应算法在推理时高效地"混煮"这些策略——通过合并它们的输出或参数——从而在无需针对定制化偏好奖励进行昂贵的在线重训练的情况下，实现快速的模型自适应。在在线偏好对齐基准上的实验表明，我们的方法相比现有最先进方法取得了显著的性能提升，为动态适配LLM至个体用户偏好提供了一种可扩展且计算高效的解决方案。

</details>


### [155] [Supervised Latent Restructuring for Small-Data Quantum Learning in Plant Phenomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20413)
*Alakananda Mitra,David H. Fleisher,Vangimalla Reddy,Chittaranjan Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维小样本生物数据分类难题，提出PCA降维结合LDA监督重构的混合流程，并在NVIDIA L40S上实现GPU加速的量子核对齐（QKA）。实验表明监督重构能提升几何可分性（Silhouette系数从-0.006提至0.197），但经典分类器表现不一，且QKA在该压缩监督空间中仍存在训练挑战，揭示了表征几何在量子小样本学习中的关键作用与局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高维生物数据常面临特征维度与样本量严重不匹配的问题，在极小样本场景下分类困难。核方法在潜在压缩未能保留类别分离结构时容易丧失判别能力。本文旨在探索如何通过对深度图像嵌入进行监督式潜在空间重构来提升小样本下的分类性能，并评估量子核方法在该环境下的实际可行性。

Method: 提出混合工作流：首先将1280维深度图像嵌入通过PCA压缩至64维，再使用线性判别分析（LDA）重构为11维监督潜在空间，最后在NVIDIA L40S硬件上执行GPU加速的量子核对齐（QKA）。

Result: 监督潜在重构显著提升了压缩表示的几何可分离性：原始嵌入空间Silhouette系数为0.003，PCA-64空间为-0.006，而监督LDA-11空间升至0.197。然而，经典分类器表现出现明显压缩权衡：线性SVM和XGBoost在重构空间中提升，RBF-SVM和随机森林在11维瓶颈下性能下降。QKA在该约束优化预算下仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 表征几何是极小样本量子学习中的核心设计变量；仅靠良好的潜在几何不足以实现强可训练量子性能。从激进压缩的生物表征中恢复非线性判别结构在实践中仍面临困难。

Original Abstract: High-dimensional biological data often exhibit a severe mismatch between feature dimensionality and sample size, making reliable classification difficult in extremely small-data regimes. In these settings, kernel methods can lose discriminative power when latent compression fails to preserve class-separating structure. We study this problem in fine-grained plant phenomics and propose a hybrid workflow that compresses 1280-dimensional deep image embeddings into a 64-dimensional PCA space and then restructures them into an 11-dimensional supervised latent space using Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA), followed by GPU-accelerated Quantum Kernel Alignment (QKA) on NVIDIA L40S hardware. Empirically, supervised latent restructuring substantially improves the geometric separability of the compressed representation, increasing the Silhouette coefficient from 0.003 in the raw embedding space and -0.006 in PCA-64 to 0.197 in the supervised LDA-11 space. However, downstream classical evaluation reveals a clear compression trade-off: Linear SVM and XGBoost improve in the restructured latent space, whereas RBF-SVM and Random Forest degrade under the same 11-dimensional bottleneck. Under a constrained optimization budget, QKA in this regime remains challenging, indicating that latent geometry alone is not sufficient for strong trainable quantum performance. These findings position representation geometry as a central design variable in small-data quantum learning and expose the practical difficulty of recovering nonlinear discriminative structure from aggressively compressed biological representations.

中文摘要翻译: 高维生物数据通常面临特征维度与样本量之间的严重不匹配，使得在极小样本场景下进行可靠分类变得困难。在这些设定中，当潜在压缩未能保留类别分离结构时，核方法可能丧失判别能力。本文以细粒度植物表型组学为研究对象，提出了一种混合工作流：将1280维深度图像嵌入压缩至64维PCA空间，再通过线性判别分析（LDA）将其重构为11维监督潜在空间，随后在NVIDIA L40S硬件上进行GPU加速的量子核对齐（QKA）。实验表明，监督潜在重构显著提升了压缩表示的几何可分离性，Silhouette系数从原始嵌入空间的0.003和PCA-64空间的-0.006提升至监督LDA-11空间的0.197。然而，下游经典评估揭示了明显的压缩权衡：线性SVM和XGBoost在重构潜在空间中性能提升，而RBF-SVM和随机森林在相同的11维瓶颈下性能下降。在约束优化预算下，QKA在此设定中仍然具有挑战性，表明仅依靠潜在几何不足以实现强可训练的量子性能。这些发现将表征几何定位为极小样本量子学习中的核心设计变量，并揭示了从激进压缩的生物表征中恢复非线性判别结构在实践中面临的困难。

</details>


### [156] [Miller-Index-Based Latent Crystallographic Fracture Plane Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20416)
*Qinwu Xu,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: We study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can leverage crystallographic plane indices (Miller indices) as a structured latent representation for reasoning about fracture geometry. We formulate Miller indices $z = (h,k,l)$ as a latent variable governing idealized planar fracture and evaluate two complementary capabilities: (i) latent inference, where the model maps visual observations to plane hypotheses under physically valid conditions, and (ii) latent applicability assessment, where the model determines whether such a representation is meaningful for a given fracture image.
  Through extensive experiments spanning synthetic data, controlled 2D--3D geometric pairs, and real-world fracture images across multiple material classes -- including ceramics, glass, metals, and concrete -- we show that MLLMs can reliably perform latent inference in idealized settings and, critically, can reject the latent representation when the underlying physics does not support it. These results suggest that MLLMs can act as physics-aware reasoning systems conditioned on structured latent priors, provided that the domain of validity is explicitly modeled.

### [157] [Can Conversational XAI Improve User Performance? An Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20439)
*Sven Kruschel,Julian Rosenberger,Lasse Bohlen,Mathias Kraus,Patrick Zschech*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实验设计来评估对话式XAI助手与问答式XAI助手在帮助用户超越模型性能方面的有效性，初步实验表明两种方式均能显著提升预测准确性，但彼此间无显著差异，且用户参与度整体较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可解释AI（XAI）技术虽旨在提供模型洞察并提升用户表现，但实际效果常不尽如人意。对话式XAI助手有望克服这些局限，然而其对客观性能指标影响的实证证据仍然不足。

Method: 采用可解释设计的预测模型，构建用户可通过识别并补偿系统错误来超越模型性能的实验条件；比较对话式辅助与基于问答的辅助两种方式，通过预测准确率、模型理解和错误识别三个指标评估解释辅助效果。

Result: 初步实验（N=42）显示，两种辅助方式下的参与者均显著优于模型，但两种辅助方式之间无性能差异，且整体用户参与度不高。

Conclusion: 研究结果为计划的完整研究提供了改进方向，包括增强用户参与度的干预措施，以及探索推动预测性能提升的内在机制。

Original Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) techniques aim to provide insights into predictive models and enhance user performance, yet they often fall short of these expectations. Conversational XAI assistants promise to overcome such limitations, but empirical evidence on their impact on objective performance measures remains limited. We propose an experimental design for evaluating explanation assistance through prediction accuracy, model understanding, and error identification. Using an explainable-by-design prediction model, we create conditions where users can outperform the model by identifying and compensating for systematic errors. We compare conversational assistance against Q&A-based assistance to assess which better supports users in working with model explanations. Preliminary results from testing our experimental design show that participants (N=42) in both treatments significantly outperformed the model but reveal no performance differences between assistance types and modest engagement overall. These findings inform refinements for our planned full study, including enhanced engagement interventions and investigation of the mechanisms driving improved predictions.

中文摘要翻译: 可解释人工智能（XAI）技术旨在为预测模型提供洞察并提升用户表现，然而它们常常无法达到这些预期。对话式XAI助手有望克服此类局限性，但关于其对客观性能指标影响的实证证据仍然有限。我们提出了一种实验设计，通过预测准确率、模型理解和错误识别来评估解释辅助效果。利用一个可解释设计的预测模型，我们构建了用户可通过识别并补偿系统错误来超越模型性能的实验条件。我们比较了对话式辅助与基于问答的辅助，以评估哪种方式能更好地支持用户处理模型解释。初步实验结果显示，两种处理下的参与者（N=42）均显著优于模型，但两种辅助方式之间未发现性能差异，且整体参与度不高。这些发现为计划中的完整研究提供了改进方向，包括增强参与度的干预措施以及探索驱动预测性能提升的内在机制。

</details>


### [158] [Weight Decay Regimes in Grokking Transformers: Cheap Online Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20441)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer在模算术任务中训练时，权重衰减作为标量经验控制参数如何调控记忆、泛化（grokking）和崩溃三个相变阶段，并提出了两种低成本在线诊断指标（注意力头余弦相似度均值与熵标准差），通过大量实验验证了该控制模式，同时排除了该结论向语言模型或普适性类别的直接推广。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 揭示Transformer在模算术任务中从记忆到泛化再到崩溃的相变机制，并找到低成本、可在线监测的注意力级诊断指标，以替代计算开销较大的损失景观诊断方法。

Method: (1) 将权重衰减λ作为标量经验控制参数；(2) 提出两种在线诊断指标：成对注意力头余弦相似度均值和熵标准差；(3) 在11种实验条件、三种模型规模（0.82M-85M参数）下进行系统实验；(4) 使用逻辑斯蒂拟合和幂律拟合确定相变边界与经验指数；(5) 进行多任务复制实验、注意力头重初始化实验、权重范数裁剪实验以及三种跨架构探针实验（MLP、LSTM、Mamba）。

Result: (1) 权重衰减轴清晰分离了记忆、发展性grokking和崩溃三个区域；(2) 记忆-发展边界位于λ_c=0.0158（95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200]）；(3) 经验指数ν=0.757（CI [0.725, 0.799]），排除参考指数ν=1/2和3D Ising ν≈0.63；(4) 多任务复制保留控制模式；(5) 注意力头重初始化在λ=0.05时改变Phase-2振幅（Cohen's d=-1.190, p=4.5×10^{-3}），而权重范数裁剪无效；(6) 三种跨架构探针均复制了权重衰减控制的相变，但架构相关λ_c不同。

Conclusion: 权重衰减是Transformer在模算术任务中调控记忆-泛化-崩溃相变的有效经验控制参数，提出的注意力级诊断指标可低成本监测训练动态。但该方法结论受限于小规模Transformer注意力模型下的模算术任务，不适用于语言模型或普适性类别判定。

Original Abstract: Transformers trained on modular arithmetic exhibit sharp transitions between memorization, generalization, and collapse. We show that weight decay acts as a scalar empirical control parameter for these regimes, and introduce two cheap online diagnostics, mean pairwise attention-head cosine similarity and entropy standard deviation, that track training dynamics from attention activations alone and complement loss-landscape diagnostics at lower compute cost. Across eleven experimental conditions and three model scales (0.82M to 85M parameters), the weight-decay axis separates memorization, developmental grokking, and collapse. A near-transition logistic fit localizes the memorization-to-developmental boundary at $λ_c=0.0158$ (95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200], N=210); a power-law fit gives an empirical exponent $ν=0.757$ (CI [0.725, 0.799]). Reference exponents $ν=1/2$ and 3D Ising $ν\approx 0.63$ lie outside this empirical CI under our four-bin grid, so we report $ν$ as empirical and defer universality-class identification to denser finite-size-scaling work. A horizon-matched multi-task replication (n=280, four modular operations) preserves the weight-decay control pattern; a paired attention-head re-initialization experiment at $λ=0.05$ changes Phase-2 amplitude (Cohen's $d=-1.190$, n=10, $p_t=4.5 \times 10^{-3}$), while matched weight-norm clipping does not. Three cross-architecture probes (4L MLP, 4L LSTM, and 4L Mamba; each n=70) replicate the weight-decay-controlled transition with architecture-specific $λ_c$ values. Main diagnostic claims are scoped to modular arithmetic in small transformer attention models; the non-attention experiments are scope probes, and architecture-wide, language-model, and universality-class claims are out of scope.
