中文摘要翻译: 在模算术任务上训练的Transformer在记忆、泛化和崩溃之间表现出尖锐的相变。我们发现权重衰减作为这些区域的标量经验控制参数，并提出了两种低成本在线诊断指标——成对注意力头余弦相似度均值和熵标准差——它们仅从注意力激活就能追踪训练动态，并以更低的计算成本补充损失景观诊断。在十一种实验条件和三种模型规模（0.82M到85M参数）下，权重衰减轴分离了记忆、发展性grokking和崩溃。近相变的逻辑斯蒂拟合将记忆到发展的边界定位在λ_c=0.0158（95%置信区间[0.0109, 0.0200]，N=210）；幂律拟合给出经验指数ν=0.757（置信区间[0.725, 0.799]）。参考指数ν=1/2和3D伊辛模型ν≈0.63在我们的四格网格下均落于此经验置信区间之外，因此我们将ν报告为经验值，并将普适性类别判定留给更密集的有限尺寸标度工作。一项视界匹配的多任务复制实验（n=280，四种模运算）保留了权重衰减的控制模式；在λ=0.05下的配对注意力头重初始化实验改变了Phase-2振幅（Cohen's d=-1.190，n=10，p_t=4.5×10^{-3}），而匹配的权重范数裁剪则没有。三种跨架构探针实验（4层MLP、4层LSTM和4层Mamba；各n=70）复制了权重衰减控制的相变，但具有架构特定的λ_c值。主要诊断结论的范围限于小规模Transformer注意力模型中的模算术任务；非注意力实验属于范围探针，而架构整体、语言模型和普适性类别判定均不在范围之内。

</details>


### [159] [LLM Pretraining Shapes a Generalizable Manifold: Insights into Cross-Modal Transfer to Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20449)
*Alexis Roger,Prateek Humane,Zhenghan Tai,Gwen Legate,Andrei Mircea,Vasilii Feofanov,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言预训练Transformer能够有效进行时间序列预测的原因：语言预训练构建了一个可复用的流形结构，微调仅需进行低维对齐，而非从头学习时间动力学。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究语言预训练Transformer为何能有效应用于时间序列预测，并从几何视角解释跨模态迁移的机理。

Method: 通过线性探针、检索实验、梯度分析、损失景观分析、低秩更新和子空间对齐等方法，系统分析了预训练初始化对时间序列训练的影响。

Result: (1) 冻结的LLM状态经线性探针即可解码真实时间序列轨迹；(2) 投影空间中的检索能产生有竞争力的预测；(3) 预训练初始化产生连贯梯度和高度各向异性的损失景观；(4) 微调表现为低维对齐，复用已有方向而非从头学习时间基元。

Conclusion: 语言预训练构建了可复用的流形结构，微调将数值动力学投影到任务相关的方向上，从而实现了从语言模型到时间序列的有效迁移。

Original Abstract: Can language-pretrained transformers become effective time-series forecasters, and why? In this paper, we show that cross-modal transfer arises because language pretraining preconditions time series training with a reusable manifold. A linear probe on frozen LLM states decodes realistic time-series trajectories without paired supervision, and retrieval in this projected space yields competitive forecasts, showing that structure and dynamics exist before finetuning. Pretrained initialization also improves optimization, producing coherent gradients and a highly anisotropic loss landscape unlike random initialization. Finetuning then acts as low-dimensional alignment, reusing existing directions rather than learning temporal primitives from scratch, as evidenced by low-rank updates, subspace alignment, and shared features for periodicity, trend, and repetition. Together, these results support a geometric account of LLM-to-time-series transfer: language pretraining builds the manifold, and finetuning projects numerical dynamics onto task-relevant directions.

中文摘要翻译: 语言预训练的Transformer能否成为有效的时间序列预测器？原因是什么？本文表明，跨模态迁移之所以发生，是因为语言预训练通过一个可复用的流形为时间序列训练提供了先验条件。对冻结的大语言模型（LLM）状态进行线性探针，无需配对监督即可解码出真实的时间序列轨迹；在该投影空间中进行检索能够产生有竞争力的预测结果，这表明在微调之前，结构和动力学已经存在。预训练初始化还改善了优化过程，产生连贯的梯度并形成高度各向异性的损失景观，这与随机初始化形成鲜明对比。微调随后表现为低维对齐，即复用已有方向而非从头学习时间基元——低秩更新、子空间对齐以及周期性、趋势和重复性的共享特征均证实了这一点。综合来看，这些结果支持了从LLM到时间序列迁移的几何解释：语言预训练构建了流形，而微调将数值动力学投影到任务相关的方向上。

</details>


### [160] [CASCADE Conformal Prediction: Uncertainty-Adaptive Prediction Intervals for Two-Stage Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20468)
*Ricardo Diaz-Rincon,Muxuan Liang,Adolfo Ramirez-Zamora,Benjamin Shickel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASCADE框架，利用分类任务的不确定性来动态缩放回归任务的预测区间，在帕金森病药物管理中比标准共形预测方法更高效（置信患者区间窄38.9%），同时保证不确定情况下的鲁棒覆盖。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 帕金森病药物管理因疾病进展异质性、患者反应差异和药物副作用而具有挑战性；现有AI模型虽能预测左旋多巴等效日剂量（LEDD），但标准不确定性量化方法无法区分高置信度和低置信度预测，导致临床决策中高风险与低风险预测被同等对待。

Method: 提出CASCADE（基于共形和分布估计的校准自适应缩放）框架：先通过筛选分类器（判断是否需要调整药物）生成Venn-Abers多概率不确定性，将该认知不确定性直接映射为非一致性分数，动态缩放下游回归任务（预测调整幅度）的预测区间，形成从分类到回归的“级联效应”。

Result: 对于高置信度患者，预测区间比标准共形基线窄38.9%；对于不确定病例，区间自动扩展以保证鲁棒覆盖率。实现了离散临床决策与连续剂量预测之间的有效桥接。

Conclusion: CASCADE通过级联不确定性传播机制，在保持统计覆盖保证的同时大幅提高了预测效率，弥合了帕金森病中离散临床决策与连续剂量预测之间的差距。

Original Abstract: Effective medication management in Parkinson's Disease (PD) is challenging due to heterogeneous disease progression, variable patient response, and medication side effects. While AI models can forecast levodopa equivalent daily dose (LEDD) as a measure of medication needs, standard uncertainty quantification often fails to communicate the reliability of these predictions, treating high and low confidence clinical decisions identically. We introduce CASCADE (Calibrated Adaptive Scaling via Conformal And Distributional Estimation), a novel conformal prediction framework that propagates epistemic uncertainty from a screening classifier to adapt downstream predictions. Unlike standard conformal methods that rely on auxiliary residual regression, we leverage epistemic uncertainty from a primary classification task (identifying whether a medication change is needed) to dynamically scale the prediction intervals of a secondary regression task (predicting how much change). By mapping Venn-Abers multi-probabilistic uncertainty directly to non-conformity scores, our framework achieves continuous risk adaptation. We demonstrate that this ``cascade effect'' produces highly efficient intervals for confident patients (38.9% narrower than standard conformal baselines) while automatically expanding intervals to ensure robust coverage for uncertain cases, bridging the gap between discrete clinical decision-making and continuous dose forecasting in PD.

中文摘要翻译: 由于疾病进展的异质性、患者反应的差异性以及药物副作用的存在，帕金森病（PD）的有效药物管理极具挑战性。虽然AI模型能够预测左旋多巴等效日剂量（LEDD）作为用药需求的度量指标，但标准的不确定性量化方法往往无法传达这些预测的可靠性，将高置信度与低置信度的临床决策等同对待。我们提出了CASCADE（基于共形和分布估计的校准自适应缩放）框架，这是一种新颖的共形预测框架，通过从筛选分类器中传播认知不确定性来自适应调整下游预测。与依赖辅助残差回归的标准共形方法不同，我们利用来自初级分类任务（判断是否需要调整药物）的认知不确定性来动态缩放次级回归任务（预测调整幅度）的预测区间。通过将Venn-Abers多概率不确定性直接映射为非一致性分数，我们的框架实现了连续的风险自适应。我们证明，这种"级联效应"可为高置信度患者生成高效区间（比标准共形基线窄38.9%），同时自动扩展区间以确保对不确定病例的鲁棒覆盖，从而弥合帕金森病中离散临床决策与连续剂量预测之间的差距。

</details>


### [161] [Training Language Agents to Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20477)
*Yuval Shalev,Zifeng Ding,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出情境训练（ICT）任务和基于强化学习的训练流程，使语言智能体能够从交互经验中提炼可重复使用的系统提示，从而提升在未见任务上的表现，并验证了跨任务和跨环境的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于反思的方法只能让语言智能体在同一任务实例内进行自我纠正，无法将经验提炼为可复用的教训以提升未来未见任务的性能。因此，需要探索如何让智能体从交互经验中学习并泛化到新任务。

Method: 提出ICT任务框架，包含一个反射器模型（reflector）和一个执行器模型（actor）。反射器观察执行器收集的轨迹，生成系统提示来改进执行器在未来未见任务上的表现。采用基于强化学习的训练流程直接让反射器从经验中学习，无需人工提供的示例。

Result: 在ALFWorld和MiniHack基准上，训练后的反射器在大多数保留任务族上优于未训练的基线；在某些情况下，能够泛化到训练时未见且差异显著的新环境。

Conclusion: 从经验中学习的能力本身是可以被学习的。此外，提出了MetaGym库以支持未来关于自改进语言智能体的研究。

Original Abstract: Language agents can adapt from experience in interactive environments, but current reflection-based methods can only self-correct within a single task instance. Whether such experience can be distilled into reusable lessons that improve performance on future unseen tasks remains unclear. We address this problem by introducing the In-context Training (ICT) task, a framework for evaluating cross-task self-improvement in language agents. In ICT, a reflector model observes trajectories collected by an actor model and generates system prompts intended to improve the actor's performance on future unseen tasks. We then propose an RL-based training pipeline for learning such reflections directly from experience, without human-provided examples. Across ALFWorld and MiniHack, our trained reflectors outperform an untrained baseline on most held-out task families, showing that the ability to learn from experience can itself be learned. In some cases, we observe generalisation beyond the benchmark on which the reflector was trained, to substantially different environments. Finally, we introduce MetaGym, a generic Python library for constructing meta-environments, enabling future research on self-improving language agents.

中文摘要翻译: 语言智能体可以在交互式环境中从经验中适应，但当前基于反思的方法只能在同一任务实例内进行自我纠正。这种经验能否提炼为可重复使用的教训，以提升在未来未见任务上的表现，仍不清楚。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了情境训练（In-context Training, ICT）任务，这是一个评估语言智能体跨任务自我改进能力的框架。在ICT中，反射器模型观察由执行器模型收集的轨迹，并生成旨在改进执行器在未来未见任务上表现的系统提示。接着，我们提出了一种基于强化学习的训练流程，使得这类反思能够直接从经验中学习，而无需人工提供的示例。在ALFWorld和MiniHack上，训练后的反射器在大多数保留任务族上优于未训练的基线，这表明从经验中学习的能力本身是可以被学习的。在某些情况下，我们观察到模型能够泛化到训练所用基准之外、环境差异显著的新场景。最后，我们介绍了MetaGym，一个用于构建元环境的通用Python库，旨在促进未来关于自改进语言智能体的研究。

</details>


### [162] [ZEBRA: Zero-shot Budgeted Resource Allocation for LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20485)
*May Hamri,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZEBRA是一种零样本框架，将多阶段预算分配转化为连续非线性背包问题，通过LLM估计效用曲线和水填充搜索实现推理时的预算拆分，在编码和问答任务上显著优于LLM直接分配。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有预算感知方法要么在单智能体内逐步控制推理，要么通过RL学习资源分配策略，没有研究如何在多智能体管线的不同阶段间拆分预算。需要一种轻量级的推理时算法来指导多智能体系统的经济行为。

Method: 将多阶段预算分配建模为连续非线性背包问题：LLM控制器估计每个阶段的效用曲线，然后在拉格朗日乘子上进行水填充搜索（water-filling search）得到每个阶段的预算分配。加法和乘法聚合方式统一在同一求解器下。

Result: 在APPS编码基准（150个任务）上，ZEBRA的两种变体在所有聚合指标上均优于LLM直接分配。在预算α=0.5时，ZEBRA恢复了无约束质量的94.4%，而LLM直接分配仅为88.1%。在3阶段HotpotQA管线上，ZEBRA比LLM直接分配高出14.3个百分点，分配结果适应管线结构（接近平衡 vs. 偏向精炼阶段）。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时算法指导可以改善自主多智能体系统的经济行为。ZEBRA证明了零样本的算法引导能够有效提升预算约束下的多阶段管线性能。

Original Abstract: As autonomous agents increasingly execute end-to-end tasks under fixed monetary budgets, the pressing open question shifts from whether the budget is respected, to how to spend it effectively. Existing budget-aware methods typically control reasoning step-by-step within a single agent, or learn resource allocation policies via RL. None address how to split a budget across the composing phases of a multi-agent pipeline at inference time. We propose ZEBRA, a zero-shot framework that reduces multi-phase budget allocation to a continuous nonlinear knapsack problem: an LLM controller estimates per-phase utility curves, and a water-filling search on the Lagrange multiplier returns the per-phase split. Additive and multiplicative aggregations are unified under the same solver. On a $150$-task APPS coding benchmark, both ZEBRA variants outperform LLM-direct (budget allocation directly by an LLM) on every aggregate metric. At a budget of $α= 0.5$ of the unconstrained spend, ZEBRA recovers $94.4\%$ of unconstrained quality, versus $88.1\%$ for LLM-direct. The advantage is statistically significant and transfers beyond coding: on a $3$-phase HotpotQA pipeline, ZEBRA beats LLM-direct by $14.3$pp, with allocations empirically robust to curve-estimation noise. On HotpotQA, ZEBRA arrives at a different budget split (near-balanced) compared to the APPS one (skewed towards a refinement phase), showing adaptation to the pipeline structure. More broadly, we show that lightweight algorithmic guidance at inference time can improve the economic behavior of autonomous multi-agent systems.

中文摘要翻译: 随着自主智能体在固定货币预算下越来越多地执行端到端任务，紧迫的开放性问题从预算是否被遵守转变为如何有效使用预算。现有的预算感知方法通常控制单一智能体内部的逐步推理，或通过强化学习学习资源分配策略。没有方法研究如何在推理时将预算拆分到多智能体管线的组成阶段。我们提出ZEBRA，一个零样本框架，将多阶段预算分配约简为连续非线性背包问题：一个LLM控制器估计每个阶段的效用曲线，对拉格朗日乘子进行水填充搜索返回每个阶段的拆分。加法和乘法聚合方式统一在同一求解器下。在包含150个任务的APPS编码基准上，ZEBRA的两种变体在所有聚合指标上均优于LLM直接分配（直接由LLM进行预算分配）。在预算为无约束支出的α=0.5时，ZEBRA恢复了无约束质量的94.4%，而LLM直接分配仅为88.1%。这一优势具有统计显著性，并可迁移到编码之外：在3阶段的HotpotQA管线上，ZEBRA比LLM直接分配高出14.3个百分点，且分配结果对曲线估计噪声具有经验鲁棒性。在HotpotQA上，ZEBRA得出了与APPS不同的预算拆分（接近平衡），而APPS倾向于精炼阶段，展示了其对管线结构的适应性。更广泛地说，我们表明推理时的轻量级算法指导可以改善自主多智能体系统的经济行为。

</details>


### [163] [Tippett-minimum Fusion of Representation-space Diffusion Models for Multi-Encoder Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20502)
*Neelkamal Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EncoderMin2L，一种基于多编码器融合和表示空间扩散模型（RDM）的OOD检测方法，通过统计诊断指标（η²和Δμ）识别各编码器对特定分布偏移的敏感性，并利用两级min(·)门控和Tippett最小p值组合无标签地融合多编码器似然信号，在四种分布偏移类型上同时达到≥0.94 AUROC，参数成本降低2.3倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法多针对单一类型分布偏移设计，难以泛化到全局域变化、语义分歧、纹理差异和协变量扰动等全谱系分布偏移场景；且多编码器融合方法通常需要OOD标签或高参数成本。

Method: 提出EncMin2L框架：1）对每个编码器的表示空间训练扩散模型（RDM）计算似然分数；2）通过两个仅需ID数据的诊断指标η²（基于类条件F检验）和Δμ（基于合成扰动的对数似然偏移）量化各编码器对不同偏移类型的敏感性；3）采用编码器无关的两级min(·)门控机制（EncoderMin2L）结合并校准各编码器的扩散似然检测器；4）利用Tippett最小p值组合方法聚合各编码器得分，生成单一且校准稳定的OOD信号。

Result: 在全局域变化、语义分歧、纹理差异和协变量扰动四种分布偏移类型上同时达到≥0.94 AUROC；相比单编码器多编码器基线，参数成本降低2.3倍；在重叠基准上优于现有最先进的表示空间扩散OOD检测器。

Conclusion: 通过仅利用ID数据的诊断指标指导多编码器融合，无需OOD标签即可实现高效、稳健的全谱系分布偏移检测，证明了编码器专业化与两级min(·)门控融合策略的有效性。

Original Abstract: We address out-of-distribution (OOD) detection across the full spectrum of distribution shifts -- global domain changes, semantic divergence, texture differences, and covariate corruptions -- through a multi-encoder fusion of per-encoder representation-space diffusion models (RDMs). We statistically identify each encoder's sensitivity to specific shift types from ID data alone and introduce EncMin2L -- an encoder-agnostic two-level $\min(\cdot)$-gate that combines and calibrates per-encoder diffusion-based likelihood detectors without OOD labels, outperforming monolithic multi-encoder baselines at $2.3\times$ lower parameter cost. Two ID-data diagnostics: $η^2$ (class-conditional F-test) and $Δμ$ (log-likelihood shift under synthetic corruptions) -- quantify encoder specialization, while a Tippett minimum $p$-value combination aggregates per-encoder scores into a single, calibration-stable OOD signal. EncMin2L achieves $\geq 0.94$ AUROC across all four shift types simultaneously, outperforming the state-of-the-art representation-space diffusion OOD detectors across overlapping benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们通过多编码器融合每个编码器表示空间扩散模型（RDM）的方式，处理从全局域变化、语义分歧、纹理差异到协变量扰动的全谱系分布偏移下的分布外（OOD）检测问题。我们仅从ID数据中统计识别每个编码器对特定偏移类型的敏感性，并引入EncMin2L——一种编码器无关的两级min(·)门控机制，用于在无需OOD标签的情况下组合和校准各编码器基于扩散的似然检测器，相比单块多编码器基线以2.3倍更低的参数成本取得更优性能。两个仅需ID数据的诊断指标——η²（类条件F检验）和Δμ（合成扰动下的对数似然偏移）——量化了编码器的专业化程度，而Tippett最小p值组合方法将各编码器得分聚合为一个校准稳定的OOD信号。EncMin2L在四种偏移类型上同时达到≥0.94 AUROC，在重叠基准上优于现有最先进的表示空间扩散OOD检测器。

</details>


### [164] [Reinforcing Human Behavior Simulation via Verbal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20506)
*Weiwei Sun,Xuhui Zhou,Jiarui Liu,Weihua Du,Haojia Sun,Yiqing Xie,Qianou Ma,Sihao Chen,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sherry Wu,Sean Welleck,Graham Neubig,Yiming Yang,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DITTO，将口头反馈作为强化学习中的一等信号来训练LLM模拟人类行为；同时提出SOUL基准，包含10个任务。DITTO在SOUL上平均提升36%，在6/10任务上超越GPT-5.4。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM从反馈中学习主要集中在代码和数学等可验证领域（标量奖励），但模拟人类行为需要处理口头、主观、多方面的反馈信号，目前缺乏此类方法。

Method: DITTO：在强化学习中将口头反馈作为一等信号。每次rollout后接收口头反馈，生成基于反馈的改进版本；通过GRPO联合优化两个输出，将口头指导蒸馏到基础策略中，测试时无需反馈。

Result: 在SOUL基准10个任务上，DITTO相比基础模型平均提升36%，在6/10任务上超越GPT-5.4。

Conclusion: 带有口头反馈的强化学习是训练LLM模拟人类行为的一个有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Humans learn social norms and behaviors from verbal feedback (e.g., a parent saying "that was rude" or a friend explaining "here's why that hurt"). Yet, learning from feedback for LLMs has largely focused on domains like code and math, where RL rewards are directly verifiable and condensed into scalar values. As LLMs are increasingly used to simulate human behavior, e.g., standing in for users, patients, students, and other personas, there is a pressing need to make them more human-like, which requires embracing a fundamentally different kind of signal: feedback that is verbal, subjective, and multi-faceted. We present DITTO, a model trained by treating verbal feedback as a first-class signal in reinforcement learning. After each rollout, DITTO receives verbal feedback and generates a feedback-conditioned improved rollout; both outputs are jointly optimized with GRPO, distilling verbal guidance into the base policy without requiring feedback at test time. We also introduce SOUL (Simulation gym Of hUman-Like behavior), a unified benchmark and training data suite spanning 10 tasks across six categories: Theory of Mind, character role play, social skill, learner simulation, user simulation, and persona simulation. DITTO achieves an average 36% improvement over the base model and exceeds GPT-5.4 on 6 of 10 SOUL benchmarks, demonstrating that RL with verbal feedback is a promising direction for training LLMs to simulate human behavior.

中文摘要翻译: 人类通过语言反馈（例如家长说“这很无礼”或朋友解释“这就是为什么那会伤人”）来学习社会规范和行为的。然而，目前LLM从反馈中学习主要集中在代码和数学等领域，这些领域的强化学习奖励可以直接验证并被压缩为标量值。随着LLM越来越多地被用于模拟人类行为，例如替代用户、患者、学生以及其他角色，我们迫切需要让它们更接近人类，这需要接纳一种根本不同类型的信号：口头、主观且多方面的反馈。我们提出DITTO，一种将口头反馈作为强化学习中一等信号来训练的模型。在每次rollout后，DITTO接收口头反馈并生成基于反馈的改进版本；两个输出通过GRPO联合优化，将口头指导蒸馏到基础策略中，测试时无需反馈。我们还引入了SOUL（模拟人类行为健身房），一个统一的基准测试和训练数据集，涵盖六个类别（心理理论、角色扮演、社交技能、学习者模拟、用户模拟和角色模拟）中的10个任务。DITTO相比基础模型平均提升36%，在10个SOUL基准中的6个任务上超过GPT-5.4，表明带有口头反馈的强化学习是训练LLM模拟人类行为的一个有前景的方向。

</details>


### [165] [An exponential mechanism based on quadratic approximations for fine-tuning machine learning models with privacy guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20521)
*Hoang Tran,Jorge Ramirez,Jiayi Wang,Alberto Bocchinfuso,Christopher Stanley,M. Paul Laiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指数机制的随机算法，用于在差分隐私保护下对预训练模型进行微调，通过构建结合局部二次近似的效用函数实现封闭形式的多元正态分布精确采样，并引入随机投影策略以适应高维模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 微调预训练模型到小规模敏感数据集时，模型容易记忆个体数据点，导致攻击者能够提取敏感信息。现有方法在隐私保护与模型性能之间难以取得良好平衡。

Method: 基于指数机制构建随机算法，设计一个结合预训练模型局部二次近似与新数据集信息的简单效用函数，使得指数机制能够从多元正态分布中实现封闭形式的精确采样；同时引入随机投影策略使方法可扩展至高维模型。

Result: 在MNIST基准和MIMIC临床数据集上的数值实验表明，该方法在与现有差分隐私微调技术的比较中展现出具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在理论上提供了隐私保证、灵敏度界限和精度估计，在实践中通过随机投影策略实现了对高维模型的可扩展性，并在标准数据集上取得了有竞争力的表现。

Original Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts a pretrained machine learning model to a small, sensitive dataset, but this process risks memorizing individual new data points, making the model vulnerable to adversaries who seek to extract sensitive information. In this work, we develop a randomized algorithm based on the exponential mechanism for fine-tuning while ensuring differential privacy. Our key idea is to construct a simple utility function that combines a local quadratic approximation of the pretrained model with information from the new dataset. The resulting exponential mechanism admits exact sampling from a multivariate normal distribution in closed form. We establish theoretical privacy guarantees, sensitivity bounds, and accuracy estimations for our method. We further introduce a random-projection strategy that makes the approach scalable to high-dimensional models. Numerical experiments on the MNIST benchmark and the MIMIC clinical dataset demonstrate competitive performance against existing differentially private fine-tuning techniques.

中文摘要翻译: 微调将预训练的机器学习模型适配到小型敏感数据集上，但这一过程存在记忆个体新数据点的风险，使模型容易受到试图提取敏感信息的攻击者的攻击。在本文中，我们基于指数机制开发了一种随机算法，用于在确保差分隐私的同时进行微调。我们的关键思想是构建一个简单的效用函数，将预训练模型的局部二次近似与新数据集的信息相结合。由此产生的指数机制允许从多元正态分布中以封闭形式进行精确采样。我们为所提方法建立了理论上的隐私保证、灵敏度界限和精度估计。我们进一步引入了一种随机投影策略，使该方法可扩展至高维模型。在MNIST基准测试和MIMIC临床数据集上的数值实验表明，该方法与现有的差分隐私微调技术相比具有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [166] [Ada2MS: A Hybrid Optimization Algorithm Based on Exponential Mixing of Elementwise and Global Second-Moment Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20533)
*Meng Zhu,Quan Xiao,Weidong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ada2MS优化算法，通过元素级与全局二阶矩估计的连续指数插值，实现AdamW与动量SGD的平滑过渡，在视觉任务上取得有竞争力的结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AdamW更新稳定、鲁棒性强但泛化性能有时弱于动量方法；动量SGD调优后可获得更好泛化但对梯度尺度变化和超参数更敏感。为平衡两类优化范式的优缺点，需要一种能实现两者平滑过渡的优化算法。

Method: 提出Ada2MS算法，通过元素级二阶矩估计与全局二阶矩估计之间的连续指数插值，实现AdamW类行为与动量SGD类行为的平滑过渡。

Result: 在视觉任务上，Ada2MS在统一的优化器对比协议下取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: Ada2MS有效融合了AdamW的稳定性和动量SGD的泛化优势，通过连续插值机制在两者之间取得平衡，为优化算法设计提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Optimization algorithms are core methods by which machine learning models iteratively minimize loss functions, update parameters, learn from data, and improve performance. Momentum SGD and AdamW represent two important optimization paradigms. AdamW produces stable updates and usually has strong robustness across training scenarios, but its generalization performance is sometimes weaker than that of momentum methods. Momentum SGD can often obtain better generalization after careful tuning, but it is more sensitive to gradient-scale variation and hyperparameter settings. To balance the strengths and weaknesses of the two paradigms, this paper proposes Ada2MS, an optimization algorithm that achieves a smooth transition between AdamW-like behavior and momentum-SGD-like behavior through continuous exponential interpolation between elementwise second-moment estimates and global second-moment estimates. On the visual tasks evaluated in this study, Ada2MS obtains competitive results under a unified optimizer-comparison protocol. The code will be released at https://github.com/mengzhu0308/Ada2MS

中文摘要翻译: 优化算法是机器学习模型迭代地最小化损失函数、更新参数、从数据中学习并提升性能的核心方法。动量SGD和AdamW代表了两种重要的优化范式。AdamW产生稳定的更新，在多种训练场景中通常具有很强的鲁棒性，但其泛化性能有时弱于动量方法。动量SGD经过仔细调优后通常能获得更好的泛化效果，但对梯度尺度变化和超参数设置更为敏感。为了平衡两种范式的优缺点，本文提出了Ada2MS，一种通过元素级二阶矩估计与全局二阶矩估计之间的连续指数插值，实现AdamW类行为与动量SGD类行为平滑过渡的优化算法。在本研究评估的视觉任务上，Ada2MS在统一的优化器对比协议下取得了有竞争力的结果。代码将在https://github.com/mengzhu0308/Ada2MS开源。

</details>


### [167] [Latent Process Generator Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20547)
*Lukas Billera,Hedwig Nora Nordlinder,Ben Murrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜过程生成匹配框架，将观测生成状态视为可处理马尔可夫过程的确定性投影，从而推广了生成匹配从静态潜变量到时间依赖潜条件过程的情形，并统一了现有离散潜过程结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成匹配理论仅形式化了对静态潜随机变量的条件化，而许多流匹配和扩散生成模型在训练中依赖辅助随机动力学，但辅助状态在生成时难以采样或不属于期望输出。需要一种通用框架来处理时间依赖的潜条件过程。

Method: 将观测生成状态 $X_t$ 表示为可处理马尔可夫过程 $Y_t$ 的确定性投影 $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$，在此设定下学习像空间上随机过程的生成器，使其单时间边际分布与投影过程相同。

Result: 推广并统一了文献中离散潜过程的结果，将生成匹配从静态潜变量扩展到丰富的时间依赖潜条件过程族。

Conclusion: 潜过程生成匹配是一个通用框架，概括了已有的增强状态构造投影结果的特殊情况，为流匹配和扩散模型中的辅助随机动力学提供统一的理论基础。

Original Abstract: Many recent flow-matching and diffusion-style generative models rely on auxiliary stochastic dynamics during training: a richer process is simulated to define conditional targets, but the auxiliary state is either intractable to sample at generation time or simply not part of the desired output. Existing Generator Matching theory formalises conditioning on static latent random variables, and several recent papers prove special cases of projection results for particular augmented-state constructions. We introduce latent process generator matching, a general framework that treats the observed generative state as a deterministic image $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$ of a tractable Markov process $Y_t$. We show that in this setting one may learn the generator of a stochastic process on the image space which has the same one-time marginal distributions as the projected process. This generalizes and subsumes the discrete latent process results from the literature, and extends Generator Matching from static latent variables to a rich family of time-dependent latent conditional processes.

中文摘要翻译: 许多最近的流匹配和扩散式生成模型在训练中依赖辅助随机动力学：模拟一个更丰富的过程来定义条件目标，但辅助状态在生成时要么难以采样，要么根本不是期望输出的一部分。现有的生成匹配理论形式化了对静态潜随机变量的条件化，且最近的几篇论文证明了特定增强状态构造的投影结果的特例。我们提出潜过程生成匹配，一个通用框架，将观测生成状态视为可处理马尔可夫过程 $Y_t$ 的确定性像 $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$。我们表明，在此设定下可以学习像空间上随机过程的生成器，该生成器具有与投影过程相同的单时间边际分布。这推广并统一了文献中离散潜过程的结果，并将生成匹配从静态潜变量扩展到丰富的时间依赖潜条件过程族。

</details>


### [168] [Complementing reinforcement learning with SFT through logit averaging in the post training of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20555)
*Xingwei Gan,Ying Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在GRPO中对冻结的参考策略（如SFT）和可训练策略进行logits平均的新方法，无需KL正则化或critic，在多个数学和推理基准上取得与KL正则化GRPO相当或更高的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的RLVR方法通常依赖KL正则化或critic来约束策略更新，但KL正则化可能限制可训练策略的推理能力，而SFT则具有良好的格式化优势。作者希望在不引入KL正则化或critic的情况下，既能发挥可训练策略的推理专长，又能保持SFT的格式化优势。

Method: 将冻结的参考策略（如SFT）和可训练策略的logits进行平均，并将该结构融入GRPO框架中。通过这种logits平均的耦合结构，替代传统的KL正则化或critic机制，使可训练策略与参考锚点相互约束。

Result: 在MATH、cn-k12和MMLU三个基准上评估，所提方法相比经典的KL正则化GRPO取得了更高的准确率或至少相当的准确率。

Conclusion: 提出的logits平均方法是一种有效的GRPO变体，能够在无需KL正则化或critic的情况下，平衡可训练策略的推理能力和SFT的格式化优势，并取得有竞争力的性能。

Original Abstract: We introduce a novel method that averages the logits of a frozen reference policy (e.g., SFT) and a trainable policy, and incorporate the method into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). In contrast to Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) methods, our proposal does not involve a Kullback Leibler (KL) regularization or critic; the trainable policy and the reference anchor are coupled through the logit averaging structure to leverage the reasoning expertise of the trainable policy while maintaining the formatting advantage of SFT. Our method is evaluated on MATH, cn-k12, and MMLU, and the results show a higher accuracy or at least comparable accuracy relative to the canonical KL-regularized GRPO.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种新颖的方法，对冻结的参考策略（如SFT）和可训练策略的logits进行平均，并将该方法融入组相对策略优化（GRPO）中。与基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法不同，我们的方案不涉及Kullback-Leibler（KL）正则化或critic；可训练策略与参考锚点通过logits平均结构耦合，以在利用可训练策略推理专长的同时，保持SFT的格式化优势。我们在MATH、cn-k12和MMLU上评估了该方法，结果表明，与经典的KL正则化GRPO相比，该方法取得了更高的准确率或至少相当的准确率。

</details>


### [169] [TriForces: Augmenting Atomistic GNNs for Transferable Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20581)
*Ali Ramlaoui,Alexandre Duval,Hannah Bull,Victor Schmidt,Hugues Talbot,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Joseph Musielewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriForces提出了一种模型无关的三流框架，将成分与结构信息分离，并结合自监督学习来保持MLIP的可迁移表示，在有限数据下显著提升能量和力的预测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）在大规模DFT数据上表现优异，但在迁移到目标化学体系时，表示会丢失成分和结构信息，且适应新领域需要昂贵的小样本数据集。

Method: 提出TriForces，一个模型无关的三流框架，将成分信息和结构信息分离处理，并结合自监督学习以保持可迁移的表示。

Result: 在MatBench和QM9上无需DFT标签即超越基线；在OMat24上，仅用20K样本能量MAE降低57%，各样本量下力MAE均有改善。

Conclusion: TriForces通过分离成分与结构信息并引入自监督学习，有效提升了MLIP在有限数据下的迁移性能和表示可迁移性。

Original Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) achieve excellent accuracy when trained on large Density Functional Theory (DFT) data. To be useful in practice, they must often be adapted to target chemistries using small and expensive task-specific datasets. However, MLIPs transfer inconsistently across domains, with representations that often loose accessible composition and structure information. To address this, we present TriForces, a model-agnostic three-stream framework that separates composition and structure information, combined with self-supervised learning to preserve transferable representations. TriForces improves performance on MatBench and QM9 over baselines without needing DFT labels and enables efficient similar structure retrieval through its learned latent space. On OMat24, in limited-data training regime, TriForces reduces energy MAE by 57% at 20K samples only and improves force MAE across sample sizes. We release pretrained TriForces variants across multiple MLIP architectures with code at https://github.com/Ramlaoui/triforces.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习原子间势（MLIP）在大规模密度泛函理论（DFT）数据上训练时能够达到优异的精度。要在实际应用中发挥作用，它们通常需要使用小而昂贵的任务特定数据集来适应目标化学体系。然而，MLIP在不同领域间的迁移表现不一致，其表示往往会丢失可访问的成分和结构信息。为解决这一问题，我们提出了TriForces，一种模型无关的三流框架，将成分信息和结构信息分离，并结合自监督学习以保持可迁移的表示。TriForces在MatBench和QM9基准上无需DFT标签即超越基线方法，并通过其学习到的潜在空间实现高效的相似结构检索。在OMat24上，在有限数据训练条件下，TriForces仅用20K样本就将能量平均绝对误差（MAE）降低了57%，并在各样本量下均改善了力MAE。我们发布了跨多种MLIP架构的预训练TriForces变体，代码开源在https://github.com/Ramlaoui/triforces。

</details>


### [170] [ReversedQ: Opportunities for Faster Q-Learning in Episodic Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20592)
*Sofia R. Miskala-Dinc,Aviva Prins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有无模型后验采样Q学习方法依赖延迟学习导致效率低下，提出三种加速策略（值函数更新顺序、更新频率、初始化方式），并将改进后的ReversedQ方法在BDCL和链式MDP任务上分别将累计奖励从9.53%提升至78.78%、从21.76%提升至61.81%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于后验采样的无模型Q学习算法依赖延迟学习来保证理论收敛性，导致学习效率低下；作者希望识别并消除这些不必要的延迟，从而加速学习过程。

Method: 以RandomizedQ为基础，提出三项改进：(i) 改变值函数更新顺序（如反向更新），(ii) 调整更新频率，(iii) 改进值函数初始化方式；三者组合形成ReversedQ方法。

Result: 在双向恶魔组合锁（BDCL）环境中，ReversedQ将标度化平均累积奖励从RandomizedQ的9.53%提升至78.78%；在链式MDP环境中，从21.76%提升至61.81%。

Conclusion: 通过消除后验采样Q学习方法中的延迟学习问题，结合反向更新、合理更新频率和优化初始化三项改进，能够显著提升无模型强化学习在有限时域情景MDP中的学习效率和累积奖励。

Original Abstract: We study model-free Q-learning in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with stationary dynamics across episodes. We identify a central issue in nascent model-free posterior-sampling works: the reliance on delayed learning in order to prove theoretical guarantees. In particular, we identify three opportunities for faster learning - (i) value-function update order, (ii) update frequencies, and (iii) value-function initialization. Using Wang et al.'s RandomizedQ as a basis, we illustrate these changes and their individual (as well as cumulative) impact in multiple empirical studies. We find that our combined modifications, termed ReversedQ, improve scaled mean cumulative reward compared to RandomizedQ, from 9.53% to 78.78% in the Bidirectional Diabolical Combination Lock (BDCL), and from 21.76% to 61.81% in a chain MDP.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了具有跨情景平稳动态的有限时域情景马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中的无模型Q学习。我们指出现有的无模型后验采样方法中存在一个核心问题：依赖延迟学习来证明理论保证。具体而言，我们识别出三种加速学习的机会——（i）值函数更新顺序，（ii）更新频率，以及（iii）值函数初始化。以Wang等人的RandomizedQ为基础，我们通过多项实证研究展示了这些改进及其各自（以及累积）的效果。我们发现，我们所提出的组合改进方法（称为ReversedQ）将标度化平均累积奖励相比RandomizedQ在双向恶魔组合锁（BDCL）中从9.53%提升至78.78%，在链式MDP中从21.76%提升至61.81%。

</details>


### [171] [Compositional Transduction with Latent Analogies for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20609)
*Junseok Kim,Dohyeong Kim,Mineui Hong,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离线目标条件强化学习中的组合泛化问题，提出了一种基于类比转导的新方法，通过任务内源类比表示实现跨上下文的行为组合，在OGBench环境中显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有离线GCRL方法主要依赖时间连续片段间的轨迹拼接，限制了行为在不同上下文间的组合能力。为克服这一局限，本文引入类比转导机制来实现跨上下文的组合泛化。

Method: 首先形式化类比转导为通过将任务内源类比与给定上下文组合来合成新计划的过程，并提出一种新颖的类比表示——该表示捕捉最优任务执行下的变化量，对上下文变化保持不变，且足以实现最优目标到达。然后针对未见类比-上下文对的泛化难题，提出一种新的离线GCRL方法，使类比转导能够超越已见组合推广到未见过的新组合。

Result: 在OGBench操作环境上的实验表明，该方法显著优于不进行类比转导的先前方法，验证了类比转导在离线GCRL中实现组合泛化的有效性。

Conclusion: 类比转导是实现离线GCRL中组合泛化的有效范式，通过精心设计的类比表示和方法，能够超越已见组合泛化到未见组合，为处理新颖上下文下的未见过目标提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Compositional generalization is essential for reaching unseen goals under novel contextual variations in offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), where a generalist goal-reaching agent must be learned from limited data. Most prior approaches pursue this via trajectory stitching over temporally contiguous segments, which limits composing behaviors across varying contexts. To overcome this limitation, we formalize analogy transduction as synthesizing new plans by composing task-endogenous analogies with given contexts and propose a novel analogy representation tailored for it. Grounded in our theory, this analogy representation captures what changes under optimal task execution, remains invariant to contextual variations, and is sufficient for optimal goal reaching. We further contend that generalization to unseen analogy-context pairs is a practical obstacle in analogy transduction, and introduce a new approach for offline GCRL that enables analogy transduction beyond seen pairs to unseen combinations. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on OGBench manipulation environments, substantially outperforming prior methods that do not perform analogy transduction. Project page: https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/CTA/

中文摘要翻译: 组合泛化对于在离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中，于新颖上下文变化下达到未见目标至关重要，在此场景中必须从有限数据中学习一个通用目标到达智能体。大多数现有方法通过时间连续片段间的轨迹拼接来实现这一目标，这限制了行为在不同上下文间的组合能力。为克服这一局限，我们形式化了类比转导——通过将任务内源类比与给定上下文组合来合成新计划，并为此提出了一种新颖的类比表示。基于我们的理论，该类比表示捕捉了最优任务执行下的变化量，对上下文变化保持不变，且足以实现最优目标到达。我们进一步指出，泛化到未见过的类比-上下文对是类比转导面临的实际障碍，并提出了一种新的离线GCRL方法，使类比转导能够超越已见组合推广到未见过的新组合。我们在OGBench操作环境上的实验证明了该方法的有效性，显著优于不进行类比转导的先前方法。项目页面：https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/CTA/

</details>


### [172] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Xinyue Xu,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCBM是一种统一的架构，通过将概念组织为嵌套层次结构，实现单模型内自适应的概念粒度推理，将期望干预成本从线性降至对数级$O(\log K)$，同时保证性能单调提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在实际部署中面临测试时干预成本高的问题：要么使用单一静态概念集导致穷举标注成本高昂，要么训练多个不同概念预算的模型导致计算和维护开销巨大。亟需一种能自适应调整概念粒度且无需重训练的统一架构。

Method: 提出Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM)，受Matryoshka表示学习启发，将概念基于最大相关性和最小冗余度组织成嵌套层次结构，使单个模型支持多个粒度的概念推理，无需重新训练。

Result: 理论上证明MCBM将期望干预成本从线性降至对数阶$O(\log K)$，并保证性能单调提升；实验上，MCBM在性能上与独立训练的多个模型相当，同时实现了动态高效的专家交互。

Conclusion: MCBM通过嵌套层次化的概念组织方式，在保持模型性能的同时大幅降低了干预成本，为概念瓶颈模型的实际部署提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

中文摘要翻译: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）已成为可解释深度学习中的一种重要范式，它通过将预测过程植根于人类可理解的概念来实现可解释性。然而，其实际部署受到测试时干预成本高昂的阻碍，因为纠正模型错误通常需要人类专家手动检查和验证大量预测的概念。现有方法存在一个根本性的结构局限：它们要么采用单一的静态概念集，迫使专家穷举标注概念，导致干预成本过高；要么训练多个针对不同概念预算的模型，从而产生大量的计算和维护开销。为了应对这一挑战，我们提出了套娃概念瓶颈模型（MCBM），这是一种统一的架构，能够在单一模型内实现自适应概念利用。受套娃表示学习启发，MCBM基于最大相关性和最小冗余度原则将概念组织成嵌套层次结构，从而无需重新训练即可在多个概念粒度层次上进行推理。理论上，我们证明了MCBM将期望干预成本从线性阶降至对数阶$O(\log K)$，同时保证性能单调提升。在实证方面，大量实验表明，MCBM在性能上与独立训练的多个模型相当，同时实现了动态且高效的专家交互。

</details>


### [173] [SURF: Steering the Scalarization Weight to Uniformly Traverse the Pareto Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20619)
*Liuyuan Jiang,Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多目标优化中均匀采样权重导致帕累托前沿覆盖不均匀的问题，提出SURF方法，通过几何分析标量化的路径速度并构建弧长CDF映射，实现权重自适应的均匀覆盖，在理论收敛性和实验效果上均优于基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在多目标优化中，标量化方法虽简单可扩展，但均匀采样权重通常导致帕累托前沿（PF）覆盖不均匀。现有方法未系统解释该不匹配现象，缺乏基于几何分析的权重采样原理，因此需要一种能实现PF均匀覆盖的权重采样方法。

Method: 通过几何分析标量化路径，发现权重变化时解沿PF移动速度不均匀，该速度诱导出弧长累积分布函数（CDF）。通过反转该CDF映射，得到产生均匀PF覆盖的权重采样规则。SURF方法：对结构化问题（如双目标bandits）推导CDF闭式解和PF感知权重采样规则；对一般问题交替进行CDF重构和权重采样。

Result: 理论上证明了在可证明条件下SURF线性收敛到不可避免的有限采样下限。实验上在bandits、多目标gymnasium和多目标LLM对齐任务中，SURF比基线方法更高效地实现了均匀PF覆盖。

Conclusion: SURF通过几何驱动的弧长CDF反转机制，系统性地解决了标量化权重采样与PF覆盖之间的不匹配问题，在理论和实验上均验证了其实现均匀PF覆盖的有效性和收敛保证。

Original Abstract: Scalarization is widely used in multi-objective optimization owing to its simplicity and scalability. In many applications, the goal is to generate solutions that represent diverse user preferences, ideally with uniform coverage of the Pareto front (PF). However, uniformly sampling scalarization weights usually induces non-uniform coverage of the PF. We explain this mismatch through a geometric analysis of the scalarization path. As the scalarization weight varies, the corresponding solutions trace the PF with a generally non-uniform traversal speed. This speed induces an arc-length cumulative distribution function (CDF); inverting this CDF map yields a principled rule for selecting weights that produce uniform PF coverage. Building on this insight, we propose SURF (Sampling Uniformly along the PaReto Front). For structured problems, including bi-objective bandits, we derive closed-form expressions for this CDF map and the resulting PF-aware weight sampling rule. For general problems, SURF alternates between CDF reconstruction and weight sampling. Theoretically, we show that under provable conditions, SURF converges linearly to an unavoidable finite-sampling floor. Empirically, experiments on bandits, multi-objective-gymnasium, and multi-objective LLM alignment demonstrate that SURF efficiently achieves more uniform PF coverage than baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 标量化因其简单性和可扩展性被广泛应用于多目标优化。在许多应用中，目标是生成能够代表多样化用户偏好的解，理想情况下实现对帕累托前沿（PF）的均匀覆盖。然而，均匀采样标量化权重通常会导致对PF的非均匀覆盖。我们通过标量化路径的几何分析解释了这一不匹配现象。当标量化权重变化时，对应的解以通常非均匀的遍历速度沿PF移动。该速度诱导出一个弧长累积分布函数（CDF）；反转该CDF映射可得到一条产生均匀PF覆盖的权重选择原则性规则。基于这一见解，我们提出了SURF（沿帕累托前沿均匀采样）。对于结构化问题，包括双目标bandits，我们推导了该CDF映射及由此产生的PF感知权重采样规则的闭式表达式。对于一般问题，SURF交替进行CDF重构和权重采样。理论上，我们证明了在可证明条件下，SURF线性收敛到一个不可避免的有限采样下限。实验上，在bandits、多目标gymnasium和多目标LLM对齐上的实验表明，SURF比基线方法更高效地实现了均匀PF覆盖。

</details>


### [174] [Dynamic Shapley Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20620)
*Xuan Yang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen,Jian Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D-Shap动态估值框架，将Shapley值建模为玩家×任务矩阵，利用局部性实现高效更新，无需完全重算。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Shapley数据估值方法计算成本高，在任务和训练玩家动态变化的场景下需要完全重算，缺乏可重用性。

Method: 将Shapley值表示为玩家×任务矩阵，利用效用局部性和联盟局部性，通过结构感知插值推断新任务估值，将新玩家引起的更新限制在局部矩阵块；引入自估值机制从训练数据直接构建初始矩阵。

Result: 任务更新毫秒级完成，玩家更新成本降低三个数量级，估值质量与完全重算相当。

Conclusion: D-Shap通过结构化矩阵维护实现了动态环境下高效且高质的Shapley数据估值。

Original Abstract: Shapley-based data valuation provides a principled way to quantify the contribution of training data, but its high computational cost makes it impractical in dynamic settings where tasks and training players evolve. Existing methods treat Shapley computation as a one-shot process and collapse contributions into aggregated scores, preventing reuse and requiring recomputation under any change. We introduce a new perspective that represents Shapley values as a player-by-task matrix and formulates dynamic valuation as a structured matrix maintenance problem. We exploit the fact that each task depends on a small subset of training players and that similar tasks yield similar valuations, leading to utility locality and coalition locality. Based on these insights, we propose D-Shap, a dynamic valuation framework that enables efficient updates by modifying only a small portion of the matrix: new task valuations are inferred via structure-aware interpolation, while updates induced by new players are confined to affected local matrix blocks. To eliminate the need for pre-specified evaluation tasks, we introduce self-valuation, which constructs the initial matrix directly from training data, supported by scalable subset reuse and coverage-aware anchor selection. Experiments across diverse models show that D-Shap performs task updates in milliseconds and reduces the cost of player updates by up to three orders of magnitude, while achieving valuation quality competitive with full recomputation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Shapley的数据估值提供了一种量化训练数据贡献的原则性方法，但其高计算成本使其在任务和训练玩家动态变化的场景中难以实用。现有方法将Shapley计算视为一次性过程，并将贡献压缩为聚合分数，导致无法重用，且在任何变化下都需要重新计算。我们引入一种新视角，将Shapley值表示为玩家×任务矩阵，并将动态估值形式化为结构化矩阵维护问题。我们利用每个任务仅依赖少量训练玩家且相似任务产生相似估值这一事实，从而引出效用局部性和联盟局部性。基于这些洞察，我们提出D-Shap，一种动态估值框架，通过仅修改矩阵的一小部分实现高效更新：新任务估值通过结构感知插值推断，而新玩家引起的更新被限制在受影响的局部矩阵块内。为消除对预指定评估任务的需求，我们引入自估值机制，直接从训练数据构建初始矩阵，并辅以可扩展的子集复用和覆盖感知锚点选择。跨多种模型的实验表明，D-Shap在毫秒级完成任务更新，将玩家更新成本降低多达三个数量级，同时实现与完全重算相竞争的估值质量。

</details>


### [175] [Same Target, Different Basins: Hard vs. Soft Labels for Annotator Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20642)
*Mirerfan Gheibi,Gashin Ghazizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在标注者意见不一致时，使用硬标签交付（hard-label delivery）替代软标签训练和多数投票的方法。实验表明，在少量标注下硬标签方法优于软标签训练，而当完整标注分布可用时两者性能相当。多遍法（multipass）作为实用默认方法表现强劲，随机标签采样（SLS）作为轻量级替代也具竞争力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标注者之间的分歧可能反映认知不确定性而非简单标签噪声。传统做法要么将投票合并为单一标签，要么直接训练经验软标签分布，但硬标签交付作为一种替代方案尚未被充分研究。

Method: 研究了两种硬标签交付方法：(1) 多遍法（multipass），循环遍历观测到的投票，保持数据集大小固定；(2) 随机标签采样（SLS），每个周期开始时为每个样本采样一个标签。还设计了确定性控制（deterministic control）作为多遍法的消融实验，以及打乱SLS（shuffled SLS）作为破坏样例与分布匹配的控制实验。

Result: 在CIFAR-10H上，当每个样本仅有少量标注时，硬标签交付优于软标签训练，且在经验目标与完整标注分布差距较大时提升更显著。当完整标注分布可用时，两种硬标签方法与软标签训练性能相当。SLS与软标签交叉熵优化相同期望目标。硬标签交付收敛到更平坦的盆地，在SVHN和CIFAR-100上的OOD检测提供了支持性证据。

Conclusion: 多遍法是在原始投票计数可用时的强力实用默认方法，SLS作为轻量级替代方案，在每样本仅有少量标注时保持竞争力，在完整标注分布可用时与软标签训练性能匹配。

Original Abstract: When annotators disagree, that disagreement can reflect epistemic uncertainty rather than simple label noise. We study hard-label delivery as an alternative to the usual choices of collapsing votes to a single label or training directly on the empirical soft-label distribution. We focus on two primary hard-label methods: multipass, which cycles through observed votes while keeping the dataset size fixed, and stochastic label sampling (SLS), which samples one label per example at the start of each epoch. On CIFAR-10H, we find that when only a small number of annotations per example is available, hard-label delivery improves over soft-label training, with larger improvements where the sparse empirical target is farther from the full annotator distribution. When full annotator distributions are available, both hard-label methods match soft-label training. We use deterministic control as an ablation of multipass and shuffled SLS as a control that breaks the example-to-distribution match. We also show that SLS and soft-label cross-entropy optimize the same expected objective. Hard-label delivery also converges to flatter basins, with supporting descriptive evidence from OOD detection on SVHN and CIFAR-100. Overall, these results suggest that multipass is a strong practical default when raw vote counts are available, while SLS offers a lightweight alternative that remains competitive when only a few votes per example are available and matches soft-label training when full annotator distributions are available.

中文摘要翻译: 当标注者之间存在分歧时，这种分歧可能反映的是认知不确定性，而非简单的标签噪声。我们研究了硬标签交付作为通常选项——即将投票合并为单一标签或直接在经验软标签分布上训练——的替代方案。我们聚焦于两种主要的硬标签方法：多遍法（multipass），它在保持数据集大小固定的同时循环遍历观测到的投票；以及随机标签采样（SLS），它在每个周期开始时为每个样本采样一个标签。在CIFAR-10H上，我们发现当每个样本仅有少量标注可用时，硬标签交付优于软标签训练，并且在稀疏经验目标与完整标注分布差距较大时改进更为显著。当完整的标注分布可用时，两种硬标签方法均能与软标签训练性能相当。我们使用确定性控制作为多遍法的消融实验，并采用打乱SLS作为破坏样例与分布匹配的控制实验。我们还证明了SLS与软标签交叉熵优化相同的期望目标。硬标签交付还能收敛到更平坦的盆地，来自SVHN和CIFAR-100上OOD检测的描述性证据支持了这一观点。总体而言，这些结果表明，当原始投票计数可用时，多遍法是一个强力的实用默认方法，而SLS提供了一种轻量级替代方案，在每样本仅有少量标注时保持竞争力，并且在完整标注分布可用时能与软标签训练性能相匹配。

</details>


### [176] [AVSD: Adaptive-View Self-Distillation by Balancing Consensus and Teacher-Specific Privileged Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20643)
*Duy Nguyen,Hanqi Xiao,Archiki Prasad,Zaid Khan,Anirban Das,Austin Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Hyunji Lee,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AVSD（自适应视角自蒸馏）方法，通过从多个特权信息视图中分离稳定跨视图共识与视图特定残差信号，解决自蒸馏中教师-学生信息不对称及视图选择困难问题，在数学竞赛和代码生成基准上取得一致提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自蒸馏中教师依赖学生无法访问的特权信息，造成根本性的信息不对称；且最佳特权信息类型往往与任务相关，难以选择单一教师视图，因此需要一种能同时应对这两个挑战的方法。

Method: AVSD方法利用多个特权信息视图，通过分离稳定的跨视图共识信号（提供可靠更新方向）与视图特定残差信号，在残差信号与共识方向对齐且比例适当时，选择性添加残差信号以调整更新幅度，从而重构token级监督。

Result: 在数学竞赛基准（AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25）上，AVSD在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B上平均Avg@8增益分别比最强基线高3.1%和2.2%；在代码生成基准（Codeforces、LiveCodeBench v6）上，AVSD在Qwen3-8B上比单视图自蒸馏基线平均高出2.4%。

Conclusion: AVSD通过多视图自适应蒸馏有效解决了自蒸馏中信息不对称和视图选择困难问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上均优于单视图自蒸馏和GRPO方法。

Original Abstract: Self-distillation enables language models to learn on-policy from their own trajectories by using the same model as both student and teacher, with the teacher being conditioned on privileged information unavailable to the student. Such information can come in different types or views, such as solutions, demonstrations, feedback, or final answers. This setup provides dense token-level feedback without relying on a separate external model, but creates a fundamental asymmetry: the teacher may rely on view-specific information that the student cannot access at inference time. Moreover, the best type of privileged information is often task-dependent, making it difficult to choose a single teacher view. In this work, we address both these challenges jointly by introducing AVSD (Adaptive-View Self-Distillation), a novel method of self-distillation with multiple privileged-information views, which reconstructs token-level supervision by separating stable cross-view consensus from view-specific residual signals. AVSD identifies the consensus signal shared across views, which provides a reliable update direction, and then selectively adds the view-specific residual signal to adjust the update magnitude when it both aligns with the consensus direction and remains proportionate to the consensus signal. Experiments on math competition benchmarks (AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25) show that AVSD consistently outperforms both single-view self-distillation baselines and GRPO, achieving average Avg@8 gains of 3.1% and 2.2% over the strongest baselines on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B, respectively. Moreover, on code-generation benchmarks (Codeforces, LiveCodeBench v6) using Qwen3-8B, AVSD outperforms the single-view self-distillation baseline by 2.4% on average.

中文摘要翻译: 自蒸馏使得语言模型能够通过将同一模型同时作为学生和教师，从自身的轨迹中进行策略内学习，其中教师以学生无法访问的特权信息为条件。这些信息可以不同类型或视角的形式出现，例如解决方案、演示、反馈或最终答案。这种设置无需依赖外部独立模型即可提供密集的token级反馈，但造成了一个根本性的不对称：教师可能依赖于推理时学生无法访问的视角特定信息。此外，最佳特权信息类型往往依赖于任务，使得选择单一教师视图变得困难。在本工作中，我们通过引入AVSD（自适应视角自蒸馏）来联合解决这两个挑战，这是一种利用多个特权信息视图的新型自蒸馏方法，通过将稳定的跨视图共识与视图特定残差信号分离来重构token级监督。AVSD识别出跨视图共享的共识信号（提供可靠的更新方向），然后当视图特定残差信号与共识方向对齐且与共识信号比例适当时，选择性地添加该残差信号以调整更新幅度。在数学竞赛基准（AIME24、AIME25和HMMT25）上的实验表明，AVSD在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B上分别平均比最强基线高出3.1%和2.2%的Avg@8增益。此外，在Qwen3-8B的代码生成基准（Codeforces、LiveCodeBench v6）上，AVSD平均比单视图自蒸馏基线高出2.4%。

</details>


### [177] [Design for Manufacturing: A Manufacturability Knowledge-Integrated Reinforcement Learning Framework for Free-Form Pipe Routing in Aeroengines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20644)
*Caicheng Wang,Zili Wang,Shuyou Zhang,Yongzhe Xiang,Zheyi Li,Liangyou Li,Jianrong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Frenet框架的管路布线优化框架（FPRO），将制造知识嵌入强化学习，实现航空发动机自由形态管道的可制造性设计，并在六轴自由弯曲机上验证了可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前航空发动机管路布线设计与下游制造环节严重脱节，依赖人工试错迭代才能达到可制造性，缺乏将制造知识融入设计阶段的系统性方法。

Method: 将管路布线问题建模为Frenet标架下的边值问题，路径由曲率和挠率轮廓通过三次Hermite插值生成；嵌入领域制造知识作为曲率与挠率的可行域约束；采用PPO算法结合随机探索和分阶段引导奖励机制进行路径优化；通过统一映射将优化路径转换为六轴自由弯曲机的运动轨迹。

Result: FPRO能持续生成无碰撞、可制造的管路路径，几何轮廓比笛卡尔方法更平滑；在终端对齐、路径长度、避障和可制造性方面优于现有强化学习基线，收敛速度更快；实物验证表明制造出的管路与数字设计几何吻合良好。

Conclusion: FPRO有效整合了设计与制造环节，为航空发动机复杂管路提供了端到端的可制造性设计解决方案，具有实际工程可行性。

Original Abstract: Design for manufacturing plays a critical role in advanced aeroengine development, where complex components necessitate careful consideration of manufacturability. However, current practices in pipe routing remain largely decoupled from down-stream manufacturing, leading to labor-intensive, trial-and-error iterations to achieve manufacturable designs. To address this problem, this study proposes the Frenet-based pipe routing optimization (FPRO) framework, a manufacturability knowledge-integrated reinforcement learning approach for free-form pipe design in aeroengines. FPRO formulates the routing problem as a boundary value problem in the Frenet frame. In this framework, the pipe path is represented by curvature and torsion profiles, which are generated using cubic Hermite interpolation. To integrate design and manufacturing, domain-specific manufacturing knowledge is embedded as constraints on the permissible ranges of curvature and torsion. The path optimization is performed using the proximal policy optimization algorithm with stochastic exploration and a stage-guided reward mechanism. A unified mapping formulation then translates the optimized path into motion trajectories for the bending die, enabling direct fabrication on a six-axis free-bending machine. Experimental results demonstrate that FPRO consistently generates collision-free, manufacturable paths with smoother geometric profiles compared to Cartesian-based methods. It also achieves faster convergence and superior performance in terminal alignment, path length, obstacle avoidance, and manufacturability compared to state-of-the-art reinforcement learning baselines. Real-world validation confirms the close geometric correspondence between the manufactured pipe and its digital design, validating the practical feasibility of FPRO.

中文摘要翻译: 面向制造的设计在先进航空发动机开发中扮演着关键角色，复杂组件需要仔细考虑可制造性。然而，当前管路布线实践仍与下游制造环节严重脱节，导致需要大量人工试错迭代才能获得可制造的设计。针对这一问题，本研究提出了基于Frenet框架的管路布线优化（FPRO）框架，一种将制造知识集成的强化学习方法，用于航空发动机自由形态管道设计。FPRO将布线问题建模为Frenet标架下的边值问题。在该框架中，管路路径由曲率和挠率轮廓表示，并通过三次Hermite插值生成。为整合设计与制造，领域特定的制造知识被嵌入为曲率和挠率可行域的约束。路径优化采用近端策略优化算法，结合随机探索和分阶段引导的奖励机制。随后，统一的映射公式将优化后的路径转换为弯曲模具的运动轨迹，从而在六轴自由弯曲机上实现直接制造。实验结果表明，与基于笛卡尔坐标的方法相比，FPRO能够持续生成无碰撞、可制造的路径，且几何轮廓更平滑。与最先进的强化学习基线相比，FPRO在终端对齐、路径长度、避障和可制造性方面实现了更快的收敛速度和更优性能。实际验证确认了制造出的管路与其数字设计之间的几何高度吻合，验证了FPRO的实际工程可行性。

</details>


### [178] [LT2: Linear-Time Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20670)
*Chunyuan Deng,Yizhe Zhang,Rui-Jie Zhu,Yuanyuan Xu,Jiarui Liu,T. S. Eugene Ng,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LT2框架，用线性时间和稀疏注意力替代循环Transformer中的二次注意力，实现质量不减的同时大幅提升效率，并展示了预训练模型向LT2的高效转换。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 循环Transformer虽强大但全注意力带来二次复杂度，计算昂贵且速度慢，亟需更高效的注意力机制以提升可扩展性。

Method: 提出LT2系列架构：(1) LT2-linear（线性注意力），(2) LT2-sparse（稀疏注意力），(3) LT2-hybrid混合变体（如GDN+DSA交叠线性与稀疏注意力，Full+GDN混合少量全注意力）。支持从预训练LT模型转换。

Result: 循环与线性/稀疏注意力产生独特协同效应：线性注意力实现迭代记忆精炼，稀疏注意力渐进扩大感受野。LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA)以完全线性时间代价匹配标准循环Transformer质量；LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN)超越标准模型。转换模型Ouro-hybrid-1.4B仅用约1B token训练，超越工业级1B模型，与4B模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 实验结果清晰表明，LT2为循环Transformer规模化提供了可行路径，推动了高效且能力强的小型语言模型发展。

Original Abstract: Looped Transformers (LT) have emerged as a powerful architecture by iterating their layers multiple times before decoding the final token. However, pairing them with full attention retains quadratic complexity, making them computationally expensive and slow. We introduce LT2 (Linear-Time Looped Transformers), a family of looped architectures that replace quadratic softmax attention with subquadratic, linear-time attention. We study two variants: LT2-linear with linear attention and LT2-sparse with sparse attention. We find that looping uniquely synergizes with these variants: it enables iterative memory refinement in linear attention and progressively expands the effective receptive field in sparse attention. We formalize these benefits theoretically and demonstrate consistent empirical gains across controlled recall, state-tracking, and language modeling tasks. We then explore LT2-hybrid, which combines different attention variants in a looped setting. Two variants are especially promising: LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA), which interleaves linear and sparse attention to maximize efficiency and matches the standard looped transformer's quality at fully linear-time cost; and LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN), which interleaves GDN with a small fraction of full attention layers to maximize quality, surpassing the standard looped transformer in both performance and efficiency. We also show how to convert a pre-trained LT into an LT2-hybrid model. With about 1B tokens of training, our converted model, Ouro-hybrid-1.4B, outperforms industry-level 1B models and is competitive with industry-level 4B models while retaining the speed benefits of linear-time attention. Together, these results show a clear path toward making looped transformers more scalable and advancing efficient, capable small language models.

中文摘要翻译: 循环Transformer（LT）通过在解码最终token之前多次迭代其层，已成为一种强大的架构。然而，将其与全注意力配对会保留二次复杂度，使其计算昂贵且速度缓慢。我们提出LT2（线性时间循环Transformer），这是一系列循环架构，用次二次/线性时间注意力替代二次softmax注意力。我们研究了两种变体：具有线性注意力的LT2-linear和具有稀疏注意力的LT2-sparse。我们发现循环与这些变体独特地协同作用：它在线性注意力中实现迭代记忆精炼，并在稀疏注意力中逐步扩展有效感受野。我们从理论上形式化了这些优势，并在受控召回、状态追踪和语言建模任务上展示了一致的经验提升。随后我们探索了LT2-hybrid，它在循环设置中结合不同的注意力变体。两种变体尤其有前景：LT2-hybrid（GDN+DSA），它交错线性注意力和稀疏注意力以最大化效率，并以完全线性时间代价匹配标准循环Transformer的质量；以及LT2-hybrid（Full+GDN），它用一小部分全注意力层交错GDN以最大化质量，在性能和效率上均超越标准循环Transformer。我们还展示了如何将预训练的LT转换为LT2-hybrid模型。使用约1B token训练，我们的转换模型Ouro-hybrid-1.4B在保持线性时间注意力速度优势的同时，超越了工业级1B模型，并与工业级4B模型竞争。这些结果共同指明了使循环Transformer更具可扩展性并推进高效、能力强的小型语言模型的清晰路径。

</details>


### [179] [Dynamic TMoE: A Drift-Aware Dynamic Mixture of Experts Framework for Non-Stationary Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20678)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Di Weng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic TMoE框架，通过MMD检测分布漂移动态实例化/剪枝专家池，并使用时序记忆路由器实现上下文感知的专家选择，在非平稳时间序列预测任务上取得SOTA性能（MSE降低10.4%，MAE降低7.8%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有混合专家（MoE）模型在处理非平稳时间序列时，受限于固定专家池和无记忆路由机制，难以适应突发的分布漂移和模式转变。

Method: 1）利用最大均值差异（MMD）检测分布漂移，动态实例化异构专家并剪枝冗余专家以优化容量；2）设计时序记忆路由器，结合循环状态和异常存储库，实现稳定、上下文感知的专家选择，无需测试时更新。

Result: 在九个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，MSE降低10.4%，MAE降低7.8%。

Conclusion: 动态架构演化与时间连续性的统一范式能有效应对非平稳时间序列中的分布漂移挑战，且无需测试时更新即可实现鲁棒的预测。

Original Abstract: Non-stationary time series forecasting is challenged by evolving distribution shifts that static models struggle to capture. While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer a promising paradigm for decoupling complex drift patterns, existing approaches are limited by fixed expert pools and memoryless routing, hampering their ability to adapt to abrupt regime shifts. To address this, we propose Dynamic TMoE, a framework that unifies architectural evolution with temporal continuity during learning phase. By detecting distribution shifts via Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), we dynamically instantiate heterogeneous experts and prune redundant ones to optimize capacity. Additionally, a temporal memory router leverages recurrent states and an anomaly repository to ensure stable, context-aware expert selection without requiring test-time updates. Experiments on nine benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing MSE by 10.4% and MAE by 7.8%. Code is available at https://github.com/andone-07/Dynamic-TMoE.

中文摘要翻译: 非平稳时间序列预测面临着静态模型难以捕捉的演化分布漂移挑战。虽然混合专家（MoE）架构为解耦复杂漂移模式提供了一种有前景的范式，但现有方法受限于固定的专家池和无记忆路由，阻碍了其适应突变的机制转变的能力。为解决这一问题，我们提出了Dynamic TMoE，一个在学习阶段统一架构演化与时间连续性的框架。通过利用最大均值差异（MMD）检测分布漂移，我们动态实例化异构专家并剪枝冗余专家以优化容量。此外，时序记忆路由器利用循环状态和异常存储库确保稳定且上下文感知的专家选择，无需测试时更新。在九个基准上的实验表明，该方法达到了最先进的性能，MSE降低10.4%，MAE降低7.8%。代码开源在https://github.com/andone-07/Dynamic-TMoE。

</details>


### [180] [Distributed Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20696)
*Zhanhong Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对分布式环境下直接偏好优化（DPO）的收敛性和时间复杂度进行了理论分析，涵盖联邦学习和去中心化学习两种场景，并提出了具有鲁棒性和可扩展性的方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于偏好的强化学习在分布式场景中缺乏理论理解，尤其是当偏好数据分散在异构用户之间时，通信约束和非独立同分布的偏好数据从根本上改变了优化动力学，而DPO在联邦和去中心化训练下缺乏收敛性保证。

Method: 将个性化离线强化学习建模为具有用户特定偏好分布的问题，刻画全局优化景观；针对联邦DPO推导收敛率，量化客户端漂移、通信频率和偏好异质性的影响；针对去中心化DPO，建立一般通信图上的收敛性，揭示谱连通性如何控制优化速度和共识。

Result: 在标准对齐基准上验证了理论见解，所提出的方法不仅具有强理论保证，而且在实际中展现了鲁棒和可扩展的性能。

Conclusion: 首次为分布式环境下的DPO提供了收敛性和时间复杂度分析，理论结果与实验验证共同表明所提方法兼具理论保证和实际性能。

Original Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (RL) is a key paradigm for aligning policies with human judgments, yet its theoretical behavior in distributed settings where preference data are fragmented across heterogeneous users remains poorly understood. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) avoids explicit reward modeling but lacks convergence guarantees under federated and decentralized training, where communication constraints and non-IID preferences fundamentally alter optimization dynamics. We provide the first convergence and time-complexity analysis of DPO in distributed environments. Modeling personalized offline RL with user-specific preference distributions, we characterize the induced global optimization landscape. For federated DPO, we derive convergence rates that quantify the impact of client drift, communication frequency, and preference heterogeneity; for decentralized DPO, we establish convergence over general communication graphs and show how spectral connectivity governs optimization speed and consensus. Empirically, we corroborate our theoretical insights on standard alignment benchmarks, demonstrating that our proposed methods not only enjoy strong theoretical guarantees but also deliver robust and scalable performance in practice. The code base is available here.

中文摘要翻译: 基于偏好的强化学习是将策略与人类判断对齐的关键范式，然而当偏好数据分散在异构用户之间时，其在分布式场景下的理论行为仍鲜为人知。直接偏好优化（DPO）避免了显式的奖励建模，但在联邦学习和去中心化训练下缺乏收敛性保证——在这些场景中，通信约束和非独立同分布的偏好数据从根本上改变了优化动力学。本文首次对分布式环境下的DPO进行了收敛性和时间复杂度分析。通过将个性化离线强化学习建模为具有用户特定偏好分布的问题，我们刻画了由此产生的全局优化景观。对于联邦DPO，我们推导了收敛率，量化了客户端漂移、通信频率和偏好异质性的影响；对于去中心化DPO，我们建立了一般通信图上的收敛性，并揭示了谱连通性如何控制优化速度和共识。在实验上，我们在标准对齐基准上验证了我们的理论见解，表明所提出的方法不仅具有强理论保证，而且在实际中展现了鲁棒和可扩展的性能。代码库已在此处公开。

</details>


### [181] [Decision-Path Patterns as Tree Reliability Signals: Path-based Adaptive Weighting for Random Forest Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20716)
*Youngjoon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策路径拓扑模式（标签翻转模式）的类条件比率加权方法，通过重加权随机森林中的树投票来提升分类性能，在30个二分类数据集上显著优于传统随机森林及其他对比方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统随机森林对所有树等权投票，忽略了不同树的可靠性差异；而树从根到叶的决策路径上主导类标签的翻转模式蕴含着树可靠性的信号，但若直接利用该信号会与预测类别产生结构混淆，因此需要一种无偏的利用方式。

Method: 提出类条件比率加权（class-conditional ratio weighting）方法：基于每棵树决策路径上标签翻转的拓扑模式，构建一个类条件比率权重，该权重在构造上保证零期望类偏差，从而在保持无偏性的前提下利用树可靠性信号对样本进行加权投票。

Result: 在30个二分类基准数据集上（共享森林、共享分裂协议，30次重复），所提方法是四种对比方案（RF、加权RF、KNORA-Eliminate、KNORA-Union）中唯一在准确率上显著优于RF的方法（Wilcoxon p=0.018），另三种均未显著提升（p>0.5）。该方法也是唯一没有多数类召回率退化的方案，少数类召回率退化仅发生在3/30个数据集上，而经典动态集成选择方法易受此影响。该增益在100到1000棵树的森林规模下均保持稳健。

Conclusion: 基于决策路径拓扑模式的类条件比率加权能有效提升随机森林的分类性能，且不引入类偏差，在多数类性能和少数类稳定性上均优于传统加权RF和动态集成选择方法。

Original Abstract: Random forests aggregate tree votes by simple majority, treating all trees as equally informative. We observe that the topological pattern along each tree's root-to-leaf decision path -- where and how often the dominant class label flips along it -- carries a signal of tree reliability that is exploitable for per-sample reweighting. The naive use of this signal is structurally confounded with the predicted class, so we propose a class-conditional ratio weighting that guarantees zero expected class bias by construction. On 30 binary classification benchmarks under a shared-forest, shared-split protocol with 30 repeats, the proposed method is the only one among four compared schemes -- RF, weighted RF, KNORA-Eliminate, KNORA-Union -- to yield a statistically significant accuracy improvement over RF (Wilcoxon p = 0.018), while the three alternatives all fail to do so (p > 0.5). It is also the only scheme without majority-recall regressions, with minority-recall regressions limited to 3/30 datasets -- a one-sided loss to which classical dynamic ensemble selection methods are susceptible. The gain is robust across forest sizes from 100 to 1000 trees.

中文摘要翻译: 随机森林通过简单多数投票聚合所有树的预测结果，将每棵树视为同等信息量。我们观察到，每棵树从根到叶的决策路径上的拓扑模式——即主导类标签沿路径翻转的位置与频次——承载着树可靠性的信号，可被用于每个样本的加权重投票。然而，对该信号的直接使用在结构上与预测类别存在混淆，因此我们提出一种类条件比率加权方法，该方法在构造上保证零期望类偏差。在30个二分类基准数据集上，采用共享森林、共享分裂协议并重复30次，所提方法是四种对比方案——RF、加权RF、KNORA-Eliminate、KNORA-Union——中唯一在准确率上取得相较RF统计显著提升的方法（Wilcoxon p=0.018），而其他三种方案均未成功（p>0.5）。该方法也是唯一没有出现多数类召回率退化的方案，其少数类召回率退化仅出现在3/30个数据集上——而经典动态集成选择方法易受此类单侧损失的影响。该增益在100至1000棵树的森林规模下均保持稳健。

</details>


### [182] [Hack-Verifiable Environments: Towards Evaluating Reward Hacking at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20744)
*Amit Roth,Ankur Samanta,Matan Halevy,Yoav Levine,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估范式，通过在环境中嵌入可检测的奖励黑客机会，实现对智能体奖励黑客行为的确定性、自动化测量，并基于此构建了TextArena测试平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法主要事后分析智能体轨迹来检测奖励黑客行为，缺乏可靠的大规模测量方法。

Method: 将可检测的奖励黑客机会直接嵌入到环境中，使得对漏洞的利用在设计中就是可验证的，从而实现确定性和自动化的测量。

Result: 实例化了TextArena并发布了Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台，在该基准上分析了多种语言模型在不同环境中的奖励黑客行为。

Conclusion: 该评估范式能够可靠地测量奖励黑客行为，为后续研究提供了标准化测试平台。

Original Abstract: Aligning autonomous agents with human intent remains a central challenge in modern AI. A key manifestation of this challenge is reward hacking, whereby agents appear successful under the evaluation signal while violating the intended objective. Reward hacking has been observed across a wide range of settings, yet methods for reliably measuring it at scale remain lacking. In this work, we introduce a new evaluation paradigm for measuring reward hacking. Whereas prior studies have primarily analyzed it post hoc by inspecting agent trajectories, we instead embed detectable reward hacking opportunities directly into environments. This makes their exploitation verifiable by design, enabling deterministic and automated measurement of whether and how agents exploit such vulnerabilities. We instantiate this approach in $\textit{TextArena}$ and release $\textit{Hack-Verifiable TextArena}$, a testbed in which reward hacking can be measured reliably. Using this benchmark, we analyze reward hacking behavior across language models in diverse environments and settings. We open source the code at https://github.com/MajoRoth/hack-verifiable-environments/.

中文摘要翻译: 使自主智能体与人类意图对齐仍然是现代AI的核心挑战。这一挑战的一个关键表现是奖励黑客行为，即智能体在评估信号下看似成功，实则违反了预期目标。奖励黑客行为已在多种场景中被观察到，但目前仍缺乏可靠的大规模测量方法。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种新的评估范式来衡量奖励黑客行为。以往研究主要通过事后分析智能体轨迹来检测该行为，而我们则直接将可检测的奖励黑客机会嵌入到环境中。这使得智能体对漏洞的利用在设计中就是可验证的，从而能够确定性地、自动化地测量智能体是否以及如何利用这些漏洞。我们将该方法实例化为TextArena，并发布了Hack-Verifiable TextArena——一个能够可靠测量奖励黑客行为的测试平台。利用该基准，我们分析了多种语言模型在不同环境和设置下的奖励黑客行为。我们已在https://github.com/MajoRoth/hack-verifiable-environments/开源了代码。

</details>


### [183] [The Hidden Signal of Verifier Strictness: Controlling and Improving Step-Wise Verification via Selective Latent Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20745)
*Yefan Zhou,Yilun Zhou,Austin Xu,Soroush Vosoughi,Shafiq Joty,Jiang Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生成式验证器在逐步验证中存在严格度偏差（过松或过严），并提出VerifySteer方法，利用隐藏状态中的验证信号，通过在段落边界选择性干预来调节验证器严格度，无需微调即可提升性能，计算效率优于自一致性方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成式验证器在逐步验证中容易出现校准不良的问题：要么过于宽松（漏过错误步骤），要么过于严格（拒绝正确推理）。现有方法缺乏对验证器这种“严格度”倾向的有效控制。

Method: 首先发现验证器在逐步验证时，其接受或拒绝某一步骤的倾向编码在对应验证段落的边界附近的隐藏状态中。基于此，提出VerifySteer方法：(1) 利用潜在的正确性信号进行样本级路由；(2) 在段落边界进行选择性干预，从而调节验证器严格度，无需微调。

Result: 在ProcessBench和Hard2Verify基准上，VerifySteer优于提示优化和激活引导基线，与自一致性方法性能相当但推理计算量减少4-7倍，并且与验证微调互补，能在微调验证器基础上进一步提升。

Conclusion: 验证器的严格度可以通过隐藏状态干预进行有效控制，VerifySteer提供了一种轻量、高效且与微调互补的验证器行为调控方案。

Original Abstract: Generative verifiers have emerged as a promising paradigm for step-wise verification, but their verification behavior is often poorly calibrated: they may be under-critical and miss erroneous steps, or over-critical and reject correct reasoning. We refer to this tendency to be overly lenient or overly critical as verifier strictness. In this work, we study whether verifier strictness can be controlled through hidden-state intervention. We uncover a verification-specific hidden-state signal: in step-wise verification, a verifier's tendency to accept or reject a solution step is encoded near the boundary of the corresponding verification paragraph. Exploiting this signal, we show that hidden-state steering can directly modulate verifier strictness without fine-tuning. However, uniform steering induces a trade-off between error detection and correctness certification. To address this, we propose VerifySteer, which exploits latent correctness signals for sample-level routing and selectively intervenes on paragraph boundaries. Experiments on ProcessBench and Hard2Verify show that VerifySteer outperforms prompt optimization and activation steering baselines, and is competitive with self-consistency while requiring 4-7x less inference compute. VerifySteer is also complementary to verification fine-tuning, providing further gains on top of fine-tuned verifiers. The code is available at https://github.com/YefanZhou/VerifySteer.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式验证器已成为逐步验证的一种有前景的范式，但其验证行为往往校准不良：它们可能过于宽松，漏掉错误的步骤，也可能过于严格，拒绝正确的推理。我们将这种过于宽容或过于严苛的倾向称为验证器严格度。在这项工作中，我们研究了是否可以通过隐藏状态干预来控制验证器严格度。我们发现了一种验证特定的隐藏状态信号：在逐步验证中，验证器接受或拒绝某个步骤的倾向编码在对应验证段落的边界附近。利用这一信号，我们证明了隐藏状态引导可以直接调节验证器严格度，而无需微调。然而，均匀引导会在错误检测与正确性认证之间引入权衡。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了VerifySteer方法，该方法利用潜在的正确性信号进行样本级路由，并在段落边界进行选择性干预。在ProcessBench和Hard2Verify基准上的实验表明，VerifySteer优于提示优化和激活引导基线，且与自一致性方法性能相当，同时推理计算量减少4-7倍。VerifySteer还与验证微调互补，能在微调验证器的基础上进一步带来性能提升。代码已在https://github.com/YefanZhou/VerifySteer开源。

</details>


### [184] [Correcting Stochastic Update Bias in Preconditioned Language Model Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20756)
*Nikhil Nayak,Julia White,Urchade Zaratiana,Kelton Zhang,Henrijs Princis,Dhruv Atreja,Henry Fawcett,Matthew Thomas,George Hurn-Maloney,Ash Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了预条件优化器（如AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo）在有限样本下的两种偏差——梯度-预条件子耦合偏差和逆非线性偏差，并提出了一种单批次偏差校正框架（交叉拟合预条件和方差校正求逆），在Qwen2.5-0.5B预训练中使损失分别降低0.15、0.07和0.11 nats。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的预条件优化器通常将随机更新规则视为总体预条件下降的直接近似，忽略了有限样本下的两种偏差：1）梯度和预条件子来自同一个小批次导致的耦合偏差；2）即使预条件子估计无偏，其逆或逆根也会因非线性变换产生偏差。作者旨在提出一种统一的偏差校正框架。

Method: 提出单批次偏差校正框架，包含两个核心组件：1）交叉拟合预条件（cross-fitted preconditioning）：将梯度和预条件子从独立的微批次组中估计，以消除耦合偏差；2）方差校正求逆（variance-corrected inversion）：利用微批次变异性减去Delta方法的领先阶偏差项，以校正逆非线性偏差。该框架适用于对角动量（AdamW）、对角曲率（Sophia）和矩阵预条件（Shampoo）方法。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B模型上，偏差校正使预训练损失分别降低0.15（AdamW）、0.07（Sophia）和0.11（Shampoo）nats；在混合质量预训练和下游指令微调中，效果一致为中性到正向。

Conclusion: 偏差校正是减少有限样本更新偏差、提升预条件优化器性能的实用机制。

Original Abstract: Preconditioned optimizers are central to language model training, but their stochastic update rules are usually treated as direct approximations to population preconditioned descent. We show that this view misses two finite-sample biases. First, the gradient and preconditioner are typically estimated from the same minibatch, introducing gradient--preconditioner coupling bias. Second, even when the preconditioner estimate is unbiased, its inverse or inverse-root is generally biased because inversion is nonlinear. We propose a single-batch bias-correction framework that addresses both effects: cross-fitted preconditioning estimates the numerator and preconditioner from independent microbatch groups, while variance-corrected inversion uses microbatch variability to subtract the leading delta-method bias term. The framework applies to diagonal moment, diagonal curvature, and matrix preconditioning methods, instantiated in AdamW, Sophia, and Shampoo. Bias correction reduces held-out pretraining loss on Qwen2.5-0.5B by $0.15$, $0.07$, and $0.11$ nats, respectively; the effects on mixed-quality pretraining and downstream instruction tuning are consistently neutral-to-positive. Together, these results establish bias correction as a practical mechanism for reducing finite-sample update bias and improving the performance of preconditioned optimizers.

中文摘要翻译: 预条件优化器对语言模型训练至关重要，但其随机更新规则通常被视为总体预条件下降的直接近似。本文指出这一观点忽略了两种有限样本偏差。第一，梯度和预条件子通常来自同一个小批次，引入了梯度-预条件子耦合偏差。第二，即使预条件子估计无偏，其逆或逆根通常也是有偏的，因为求逆是非线性操作。我们提出一个单批次偏差校正框架，同时解决这两个问题：交叉拟合预条件从独立的微批次组中估计分子和预条件子，而方差校正求逆利用微批次变异性减去Delta方法的领先阶偏差项。该框架适用于对角动量法、对角曲率法和矩阵预条件法，具体实例化为AdamW、Sophia和Shampoo。偏差校正使Qwen2.5-0.5B模型上的预训练损失分别降低0.15、0.07和0.11 nats；对混合质量预训练和下游指令微调的效果一致为中性到正向。综上，这些结果确立了偏差校正作为减少有限样本更新偏差和提升预条件优化器性能的实用机制。

</details>


### [185] [ShapeBench: A Scalable Benchmark and Diagnostic Suite for Standardized Evaluation in Aerodynamic Shape Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20763)
*Shaghayegh Fazliani,Krissh Chawla,Jack Guo,Yiren Shen,Matthias Ihme,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ShapeBench，一个包含103个任务、覆盖8种形状类别和多种优化场景的气动形状优化(ASO)基准测试平台，并引入领域专用进化LLM基线ShapeEvolve。实验表明优化器排名在不同类别间差异显著（平均Spearman ρ=0.013），单任务结论无法泛化，基准远未饱和。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 气动形状优化(ASO)的快速发展缺乏标准化的评估框架，现有比较难以公平进行。需要一个统一的基准测试，涵盖多样的形状类别、目标函数，并配备匹配预算的最先进基线，以实现公平、可复现的比较。

Method: 构建ShapeBench基准平台，包含统一API、103个任务（8种形状类别）、验证过的替代模型用于快速搜索，以及高保真CFD管线用于最终验证。提供可复现协议和配置良好的基线，并引入领域专用的进化LLM基线ShapeEvolve。

Result: 优化器排名在不同形状类别和问题形式间差异极大（平均成对Spearman ρ=0.013），单任务结论无法可靠泛化。经典方法很少能适用于所有形状类别和任务，基准远未饱和，亟需更通用的优化方法。

Conclusion: ShapeBench揭示了ASO领域缺乏通用优化方法的现状，强调了开发更通用、跨类别适用的优化方法的必要性，并为公平比较提供了标准化框架。

Original Abstract: Rapid progress in aerodynamic shape optimization (ASO) has outpaced currently-available standardized evaluation frameworks. Fair comparison requires a unified benchmark spanning diverse shape classes, objective formulations, and matched-budget state-of-the-art baselines. We introduce ShapeBench, an open-source ASO benchmark with a unified API spanning 103 tasks across eight shape categories and multiple optimization regimes. Each ShapeBench task includes a validated surrogate for fast search; when feasible, a high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) pipeline for final verification is available, enabling systematic fidelity-gap analysis. ShapeBench provides a reproducible protocol with well-configured baselines to compare fairly using a consistent budget metric, allowing for comparison among both classical and LLM-driven methods, including general-purpose optimizers and a new domain-specialized evolutionary LLM baseline, ShapeEvolve. Results on ShapeBench demonstrate substantial variance in optimizer rankings across shape categories and problem formulations, with mean pairwise Spearman $ρ= 0.013$, so single-task conclusions do not reliably generalize across problem classes. The benchmark is also far from saturation; classical methods are rarely applicable across all shape categories and tasks, further highlighting the need for more general-purpose approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 气动形状优化（ASO）的快速发展已超出了目前可用的标准化评估框架。公平比较需要一个涵盖多种形状类别、目标函数公式以及匹配预算的最先进基线的统一基准。我们提出了ShapeBench，一个开源的ASO基准测试平台，其统一API涵盖了8种形状类别和多种优化场景下的103个任务。每个ShapeBench任务都包含一个经过验证的替代模型用于快速搜索；在可行的情况下，还提供了高保真计算流体动力学（CFD）管线用于最终验证，从而能够进行系统性的保真度差距分析。ShapeBench提供了一个可复现的协议，配有配置良好的基线，以使用一致的预算指标进行公平比较，从而允许在经典方法和LLM驱动方法之间进行比较，包括通用优化器和一个新的领域专用进化LLM基线——ShapeEvolve。在ShapeBench上的结果表明，优化器排名在不同形状类别和问题形式之间差异显著，平均成对Spearman ρ=0.013，因此单任务结论无法可靠地泛化到不同问题类别。该基准也远未饱和；经典方法很少能适用于所有形状类别和任务，这进一步凸显了对更通用方法的需求。

</details>


### [186] [Cumulative Meta-Learning from Active Learning Queries for Robustness to Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20771)
*Kin Whye Chew,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAML框架，通过主动学习选择的信息样本进行元学习来更新先验（归纳偏置），而非简单扩充标注集，有效解决了虚假相关导致模型依赖无关模式的问题。理论证明该累积公式能捕捉标准元学习缺失的交互项，实验在多个基准上提升少数群体准确率最高达29.9%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实数据中的虚假相关导致模型依赖无关模式，损害可靠性、泛化性和公平性。主动学习虽有潜力，但标准方法仅将查询样本追加到标注集中，在过参数化深度模型中其影响被稀释或被记忆，无法有效区分核心特征与虚假特征。

Method: 提出累积主动元学习（CAML），将每次主动学习轮次构造为元学习任务：当前标注集作为元训练数据用于适应，新查询批次作为元测试数据用于评估泛化。与常规元学习假设任务独立同分布不同，CAML利用轮次间的序列依赖，维护一个逐步精化的累积归纳偏置，并通过累积公式引入交互项来耦合早期元学习偏置与后续查询目标。

Result: 在多个虚假相关基准上显著提升少数群体准确率：Dominoes提升27.8%，Waterbirds提升29.9%，SpuCo提升14.3%，CivilComments提升24.0%。理论证明累积公式能捕捉标准元学习缺失的依赖交互项。

Conclusion: CAML通过元学习主动查询样本来更新归纳偏置，有效缓解了虚假相关对深度模型的影响，在理论上和实验上均验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: Spurious correlations in real-world datasets cause machine learning models to rely on irrelevant patterns, undermining reliability, generalization, and fairness. Active learning offers a promising way to address this failure mode by querying informative samples that distinguish core features from spurious ones. However, standard active-learning methods simply append queried examples to the labeled set, effectively updating only the likelihood term. In deep learning regimes, the influence of these informative samples can be diluted by the larger labeled set and memorized by overparameterized models. We propose Cumulative Active Meta-Learning (CAML), an active-learning framework that uses queried examples to meta-learn the prior, or inductive bias, governing how the model adapts. CAML casts each active-learning round as a meta-learning task: the current labeled set serves as meta-train data for adaptation, while the newly queried batch serves as meta-test data for evaluating generalization. Unlike conventional meta-learning, which treats tasks as independent and identically distributed, CAML exploits the sequential dependence between active-learning rounds by maintaining a cumulative inductive bias that is progressively refined. Theoretically, we show that this cumulative formulation introduces interaction terms that couple earlier meta-learned inductive biases with later query-induced objectives, capturing dependencies absent from standard meta-learning. Empirically, CAML improves minority-group accuracy across spurious-correlation benchmarks and acquisition strategies, with gains of up to 27.8% on Dominoes, 29.9% on Waterbirds, 14.3% on SpuCo, and 24.0% on CivilComments.

中文摘要翻译: 真实世界数据集中的虚假相关性会导致机器学习模型依赖无关模式，损害其可靠性、泛化性和公平性。主动学习通过查询能够区分核心特征与虚假特征的信息样本，为解决这一失效模式提供了有前景的途径。然而，标准的主动学习方法只是将查询到的样本追加到标注集中，实际上仅更新了似然项。在深度学习场景下，这些信息样本的影响可能会被更大的标注集稀释，并被过参数化模型记忆。我们提出累积主动元学习（CAML），一种利用查询样本对先验（即控制模型适应方式的归纳偏置）进行元学习的主动学习框架。CAML将每一轮主动学习构建为一个元学习任务：当前标注集作为元训练数据进行适应，新查询批次作为元测试数据评估泛化能力。与将任务视为独立同分布的常规元学习不同，CAML通过维护一个逐步精化的累积归纳偏置，利用了主动学习轮次之间的序列依赖性。理论上，我们证明该累积公式引入了交互项，将早期元学习获得的归纳偏置与后期查询驱动的目标耦合起来，捕捉了标准元学习中缺失的依赖关系。实验上，CAML在多个虚假相关基准和采集策略上提升了少数群体的准确率，在Dominoes上提升高达27.8%，在Waterbirds上提升29.9%，在SpuCo上提升14.3%，在CivilComments上提升24.0%。

</details>


### [187] [Causal Machine Learning Is Not a Panacea: A Roadmap for Observational Causal Inference in Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20782)
*Donna Tjandra,Trenton Chang,Sonali Parbhoo,Rajesh Ranganath,Andre Kurepa Waschka,William Mitchell,Maggie Makar,Shalmali Joshi,Finale Doshi-Velez,Leo Anthony Celi,Jenna Wiens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将因果机器学习应用于观察性临床数据的路线图，强调了评估有效性假设和负责任应用的重要性，并提供模板以增强因果分析的严谨性和可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模观察性临床数据集的日益可用性以及随机对照试验实施的挑战，推动了使用因果机器学习进行因果推断的热情，但其局限性尚未得到充分认识，可能影响研究结果的有效性。

Method: 提出了一个应用于观察性数据的因果机器学习路线图，重点包括：评估数据中的有效性假设，为临床专家和机器学习从业者提供负责任的因果ML应用指导，以及提供增强因果分析严谨性和可解释性的模板。

Result: 因果机器学习在各个学科中的局限性仍未被充分认识，这种知识共享的差距可能影响研究结果的有效性；因果假设必须得到满足且建模选择需要得到合理解释，否则可能导致有偏或误导的结果。

Conclusion: 因果机器学习可以成为生成因果假设的强大工具，本文提供的模板有助于增强因果分析的严谨性和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Objective: The growing availability of large-scale observational clinical datasets and challenges in conducting randomized controlled trials have spurred enthusiasm in using causal machine learning (ML) for causal inference in observational data. We present a roadmap for applying causal ML to observational data. Materials and methods: We outline the importance of assessing validity assumptions within available data and applying causal ML responsibly for clinical experts using causal ML and ML practitioners with limited clinical expertise. Observations: Despite advances in causal ML, its limitations remain largely under-appreciated across disciplines. This gap in shared knowledge may impact the validity of findings. Discussion: Causal assumptions must be satisfied and modeling choices justified. Otherwise, these approaches risk producing biased or misleading results, with consequences for clinical research and patient care. Conclusion: Causal ML can be a powerful tool for generating causal hypotheses. We provide a template to strengthen the rigor and interpretability of causal analyses.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：大规模观察性临床数据集的日益可用性以及开展随机对照试验所面临的挑战，激发了人们对使用因果机器学习（ML）对观察性数据进行因果推断的热情。我们提出了一个将因果ML应用于观察性数据的路线图。材料与方法：我们概述了在现有数据中评估有效性假设的重要性，以及为使用因果ML的临床专家和临床专业知识有限的ML从业者负责任地应用因果ML的关键点。观察结果：尽管因果ML取得了进展，但其局限性在各个学科中仍未得到充分认识。这一知识共享的差距可能影响研究结果的有效性。讨论：因果假设必须得到满足，建模选择必须得到合理证明。否则，这些方法可能产生有偏或误导的结果，对临床研究和患者护理造成后果。结论：因果ML可以成为生成因果假设的强大工具。我们提供了一个模板，以增强因果分析的严谨性和可解释性。

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Numerical Features: CNN-Driven Algorithm Selection via Contour Plots for Continuous Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20797)
*Yiliang Yuan,Xiang Shi,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于等高线图视觉表征的算法选择方法，用于黑箱优化中从固定算法库自动选择最优求解器，无需手工设计的ELA特征。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有连续优化中的算法选择方法主要依赖数值描述符（如ELA特征或Deep-ELA嵌入），而本文探索一种互补的视觉表征——将探测到的景观等高线图作为输入，以利用空间结构信息进行算法选择。

Method: 使用CNN回归器，将多个实例特定的等高线视图（堆叠或逐视图编码后聚合）作为输入，预测每个求解器的性能，从而根据预测最优值进行算法选择。

Result: 在BBOB 2009单目标基准上，所提选择器显著优于单一最优求解器（SBS），且与基于特征的方法竞争力相当；在DeepELA设置下的双目标评估中，基于图像的方法在使用窗口等高线视图时同样具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单的视觉模型能够利用探测景观中的空间结构进行算法选择，而无需手工设计的ELA特征，为算法选择提供了新的有效途径。

Original Abstract: The present paper introduces a new representation-driven approach to per-instance algorithm selection, applied to black-box optimization, for automatically choosing the most promising solver from a fixed portfolio. Prior work in continuous optimization largely relies on numerical descriptors, including Exploratory Landscape Analysis features and learned embeddings such as Deep-ELA. This work studies a complementary representation: contour-map visualizations of probed landscapes. A CNN regressor takes multiple instance-specific contour views (stacked or encoded per view and aggregated) and predicts per-solver performance, enabling selection by the predicted best value. On the standard BBOB 2009 single-objective protocol, the resulting selectors significantly outperform the single best solver (SBS) and are competitive with feature-based baselines. A subsequent bi-objective evaluation under the DeepELA setting further indicates that the same image-based principle can be competitive when using windowed contour views. Overall, the results suggest that simple vision models can exploit spatial structure in probed landscapes for algorithm selection without handcrafted ELA features.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种新的基于表征的逐实例算法选择方法，应用于黑箱优化，用于从固定算法池中自动选择最有希望的求解器。先前在连续优化中的工作主要依赖数值描述符，包括探索性景观分析（ELA）特征以及诸如Deep-ELA等学习嵌入。本文研究了一种互补的表征方式：探测景观的等高线图可视化。一个CNN回归器接收多个实例特定的等高线视图（堆叠或逐视图编码后聚合），预测每个求解器的性能，从而通过预测最优值进行选择。在标准BBOB 2009单目标协议上，所得选择器显著优于单一最优求解器（SBS），且与基于特征的基线方法竞争力相当。随后在DeepELA设置下的双目标评估进一步表明，相同的基于图像的原则在使用窗口等高线视图时同样具有竞争力。总体而言，结果表明，简单的视觉模型能够利用探测景观中的空间结构进行算法选择，而无需手工设计的ELA特征。

</details>


### [189] [Most Transformer Modifications Still Do Not Transfer at 1-3B: A 2020-2026 Update to Narang et al. (2021) with Downstream Evaluation and a Noise Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20798)
*Yang Zhao,Jiahao Lu,Bin Huang,Guhua Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在1.2B和3B参数规模下，严格复现了Narang等人对Transformer改进方法的评估，发现20种2021年后的改进中仅两种通过Bonferroni校正，且注意力-输出模块的损失与下游性能差距显著扩大，表明噪声基线、下游评估和跨规模稳定性测试是当前架构比较的必要条件。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Narang等人(2021)在T5-base规模下评估了40多种Transformer改进，发现大多数无法迁移。但五年后，工作范式已转向1-3B参数、下游评估取代预训练困惑度、改进方法也发生了巨大变化。本文旨在重新审视这一问题，检验在更大规模和新设置下这些改进是否仍然有效。

Method: 在严格等数据、等计算量、等训练配置的控制条件下，测试20种2021年后的Transformer改进方法，规模为1.2B和3B参数。采用多种子基线噪声层、CLIMB-12下游评估作为主要指标，并进行Bonferroni校正和跨规模稳定性测试。

Result: 20种改进中仅两种在1.2B规模下通过Bonferroni校正；其中一种在3B规模下无法稳定训练。注意力-输出模块的损失-下游性能差距显著增大：两个明显的失败案例验证损失接近基线（仅差2-3%），但下游指标却下降6-16个CLIMB分数。

Conclusion: 在1-3B参数规模下进行架构比较时，噪声基线报告、下游评估和跨规模稳定性测试已成为不可忽视的前提条件。

Original Abstract: Narang et al. (2021) evaluated 40+ Transformer modifications at T5-base scale and concluded that most did not transfer. Five years later, the typical working regime has moved to 1-3B parameters, downstream evaluation has replaced pretraining perplexity, and a substantially different catalogue of modifications has emerged. We revisit their question by testing 20 post-2021 Transformer modifications at 1.2B and 3B under strict iso-data, iso-compute, iso-recipe control, with a multi-seed baseline noise floor and CLIMB-12 downstream evaluation as the primary metric. The central finding reproduces theirs at this curated set: most modifications do not transfer. Of the 20 modifications, only two clear Bonferroni correction at 1.2B; one of those two further fails to train stably at 3B under the shared recipe. We also find that the loss-downstream gap reported by Tay et al. (2023) enlarges several-fold for attention-output modifications: two significant failures converge to within 2-3% of baseline validation loss yet drop 6-16 CLIMB-points. We conclude that noise-floor reporting, downstream evaluation, and cross-scale stability testing are now prerequisites for architecture comparisons at 1-3B.

中文摘要翻译: Narang等人(2021)在T5-base规模下评估了40多种Transformer改进，并得出结论：大多数改进无法迁移。五年后，典型的工作范式已转向1-3B参数，下游评估取代了预训练困惑度，且出现了大量不同的改进方法。我们在严格等数据、等计算量、等训练配置的控制条件下，在1.2B和3B规模下重新审视了他们的问题，测试了20种2021年后的Transformer改进。采用多种子基线噪声层和以CLIMB-12下游评估为主要指标。核心发现重现了他们在该精选集合上的结论：大多数改进无法迁移。在这20种改进中，仅两种在1.2B规模下通过Bonferroni校正；其中一种在3B规模下使用共享训练配置时无法稳定训练。我们还发现，Tay等人(2023)报告的损失-下游差距在注意力-输出模块上扩大了几倍：两个显著的失败案例验证损失收敛到基线2-3%以内，但下游指标却下降了6-16个CLIMB分数。我们得出结论：在1-3B规模下进行架构比较时，噪声基线报告、下游评估和跨规模稳定性测试现在已是必要条件。

</details>


### [190] [Tunable MAGMAX: Preference-Aware Model Merging for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20803)
*Kei Hiroshima,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tunable MAGMAX框架，通过引入偏好向量控制模型合并时各任务向量的元素选择数量，实现了对持续学习中任务级性能的偏好感知控制，并能自动构建偏好向量以适应不同部署环境。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于大预训练模型和模型合并技术的持续学习方法主要关注所有任务的平均性能，未能充分解决如何根据不同的部署环境或用户偏好来构建模型的问题。

Method: 提出Tunable MAGMAX框架：引入偏好向量控制模型合并时从每个任务向量中选择的元素数量，从而调节合并模型的性能；进一步提出利用少量目标环境数据和训练任务数据自动构建偏好向量的方法，无需手动指定。

Result: 在持续学习基准任务上的实验表明，Tunable MAGMAX能够有效控制任务级性能，并成功将合并模型适应到各种目标环境，其性能达到或超越基线方法。

Conclusion: Tunable MAGMAX是一种实用的持续学习模型部署方案，能够根据不同环境对任务性能的偏好差异灵活调整模型，实现了偏好感知的任务级性能控制。

Original Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially on multiple tasks while mitigating catastrophic forgetting of previously learned knowledge.
