Together these results give a mechanistic account of contemporary tabular foundation models and identify which inductive biases govern both their accuracy and characteristic failures.

中文摘要翻译: 不同架构的表格基础模型在一系列分类和回归任务上的准确率趋于收敛。这引发了排行榜无法回答的问题：(i) 这些模型是否执行相同的上下文算法，(ii) 行、列和类别置换不变性的来源是什么，(iii) 它们在针对所推断机制设计的扰动下鲁棒性如何。本文对这三个方面进行了系统表征。不同模型家族实现了性质上不同的基于相似性的读取机制：从基于注意力权重的上下文标签投票，到类别条件均值读取，每种机制都通过因果干预得到了确认。我们发现，先前工作强调的表示坍缩对这些模型而言并非实际关注点。每个模型的置换不变性可追溯到特定的位置参数，去除这些参数可以在保持准确率的同时将近似不变性变为精确不变性。针对每种读取机制设计的扰动复现了预测的失败模式；中心攻击和排名攻击将这些失败模式与重新拟合的基线区分开来。这些结果共同为当代表格基础模型提供了机械论解释，并识别出哪些归纳偏差同时支配了它们的准确性和特征性失败模式。

</details>


### [222] [TimeSRL: Generalizable Time-Series Behavioral Modeling via Semantic RL-Tuned LLMs -- A Case Study in Mental Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21295)
*Yuang Fan,Lilin Xu,Millie Wu,Jingping Nie,Qingyu Chen,Yuzhe Yang,Zhuo Zhang,Xin Liu,Subigya Nepal,Xiaofan Jiang,Xuhai "Orson" Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeSRL，一个两阶段LLM框架，通过显式语义瓶颈将原始感知信号抽象为高层自然语言描述，再基于这些抽象进行行为预测。采用GRPO+RLVR端到端优化，无需人工标注中间抽象。在心理健康预测任务中，跨数据集泛化基准上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纵向被动感知数据存在跨数据集分布偏移问题，传统机器学习过拟合群体特定伪影，大语言模型难以对长序列异质时间序列进行可靠推理，需要一种能学习可泛化语义表征的方法。

Method: 提出TimeSRL两阶段框架：第一阶段将原始传感信号抽象为高层自然语言描述（语义瓶颈）；第二阶段仅基于这些抽象预测行为结果。采用群组相对策略优化（GRPO）结合基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）进行端到端优化，在不依赖人工标注中间抽象的情况下学习与结果对齐的语义抽象。

Result: 在心理健康预测跨数据集泛化基准上，采用严谨的留一数据集（LOSO）评估协议，焦虑预测MAE相比非LLM ML基线和LLM基线分别降低3.1-10.1%和9.5-44.1%；抑郁预测分别降低3.2-9.6%和27.4-57.6%（所有p<0.05）。跨不同感知管道的基准迁移中显著优于先前方法，且无需目标域微调即可达到与域内性能相当的水平。

Conclusion: 语义抽象具有可重用性，通过强化学习微调的LLM实现可泛化的行为建模是一个有前景的新方向。

Original Abstract: Longitudinal passive sensing enables continuous health prediction, yet models often fail under cross-dataset distribution shifts. Traditional ML overfits cohort-specific artifacts, while Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to reason reliably over long, heterogeneous time-series. We introduce TimeSRL, a two-stage LLM framework that routes predictions through an explicit semantic bottleneck. The model first abstracts raw signals into high-level natural language, then predicts behavioral outcomes from these abstractions alone. This forces the model to reason over semantic concepts that we argue generalize better than raw numbers. We optimize this process end-to-end using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), learning outcome-aligned abstractions without gold intermediate annotations. Instantiated on mental-health prediction, TimeSRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on a benchmark designed to stress-test cross-cohort generalization under a rigorous leave-one-dataset-out (LOSO) protocol, reducing mean absolute error (MAE) over strong non-LLM ML and LLM baselines by 3.1--10.1% and 9.5--44.1% for anxiety, and 3.2--9.6% and 27.4--57.6% for depression (all $p$s<0.05). TimeSRL significantly outperforms prior methods in cross-benchmark transfer across different sensing pipelines, rivaling its own within-domain performance without target-domain fine-tuning. These results demonstrate that semantic abstractions are reusable and point to a new direction for generalizable behavior modeling via RL-tuned LLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 纵向被动感知能够实现连续的健康预测，但模型在跨数据集分布偏移时往往失效。传统机器学习会过拟合群体特定的伪影，而大语言模型难以对长序列异质时间序列进行可靠推理。本文提出TimeSRL，一个两阶段LLM框架，通过显式语义瓶颈来路由预测。该模型首先将原始信号抽象为高层自然语言描述，然后仅基于这些抽象预测行为结果。这迫使模型推理语义概念，我们认为这些概念比原始数值具有更好的泛化性。我们使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习中的群组相对策略优化对此过程进行端到端优化，学习与结果对齐的抽象，无需人工标注中间抽象。将模型实例化到心理健康预测任务中，TimeSRL在专门设计用于压力测试跨队列泛化能力的基准上，采用严格的留一数据集协议，取得了最先进的性能：焦虑预测的均绝对误差相比强非LLM机器学习和LLM基线分别降低了3.1-10.1%和9.5-44.1%；抑郁预测分别降低了3.2-9.6%和27.4-57.6%（所有p值<0.05）。TimeSRL在跨不同感知管道的跨基准迁移中显著优于先前方法，无需目标域微调即可达到与自身域内性能相当的水平。这些结果表明语义抽象具有可重用性，并为通过强化学习微调的大语言模型实现可泛化的行为建模指明了新方向。

</details>


### [223] [Automatic Discovery of Disease Subgroups by Contrasting with Healthy Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21301)
*Robin Louiset,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier,Antoine Grigis,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Deep UCSL的对比性子组发现方法，通过将患者与健康对照进行对比，识别仅由病理因素驱动的患者子组，避免与健康人群共享的无关变异干扰。该方法结合深度特征提取器、基于条件联合似然的新损失函数和正则化项，在MNIST和四个真实医学影像数据集上取得了优于以往方法的子组质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在生物医学子组发现中，患者与健康对照共享许多无关的变异因素（如年龄、性别等），传统方法容易将这些混杂因素误判为疾病相关的子组特征。作者希望开发一种方法，能够通过对比患者与健康对照，仅关注疾病特异性变异，从而发现真正由病理因素驱动的同质性患者子组。

Method: 提出Deep UCSL框架，包含：(1) 深度特征提取器学习判别性表示空间；(2) 基于潜聚类和患者/对照标签的条件联合似然的新损失函数；(3) 采用期望最大化（EM）策略交替进行子组推断和特征编码器更新；(4) 引入正则化项鼓励表示捕获疾病特异性变异同时忽略与对照共享的变异。

Result: 在MNIST示例和四个不同真实医学影像数据集上，Deep UCSL在估计子组质量上定量优于以往相关工作。代码和数据集已开源在GitHub。

Conclusion: 通过对比性学习框架，Deep UCSL能够有效过滤与健康对照共享的无关变异，发现更具病理特异性和可解释性的患者子组，显著提升了生物医学子组发现的质量。

Original Abstract: In biomedical Subgroup Discovery, practitioners are interested in discovering interpretable and homogeneous subgroups within a group of patients. In this paper, assuming that healthy subjects (i.e., controls) share common but irrelevant factors of variation with the patients, we motivate and develop a Contrastive Subgroup Discovery method, entitled Deep UCSL. By contrasting patients with controls, Deep UCSL identifies subgroups driven solely by pathological factors, ignoring common variability shared with healthy subjects. Our framework employs a deep feature extractor to learn a discriminative representation space. Mathematically, we derive a novel loss based on the conditional joint likelihood of latent clusters and patient/control labels, optimized via an Expectation-Maximization strategy alternating between subgroup inference and feature encoder updates. A regularization term further encourages representations to capture disease-specific variability while ignoring variability shared with controls. Compared to previous related works, our approach quantitatively improves the quality of the estimated subgroups, as demonstrated on a MNIST example and four distinct real medical imaging datasets. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/rlouiset/deep_ucsl.

中文摘要翻译: 在生物医学子组发现中，实践者感兴趣的是发现一组患者中可解释且同质的子组。本文假设健康受试者（即对照组）与患者共享共同但不相关的变异因素，据此提出并开发了一种名为Deep UCSL的对比性子组发现方法。通过将患者与对照组进行对比，Deep UCSL识别出仅由病理因素驱动的子组，忽略了与健康受试者共享的常见变异。我们的框架采用深度特征提取器来学习判别性表示空间。在数学上，我们基于潜聚类和患者/对照标签的条件联合似然推导出一种新颖的损失函数，通过交替进行子组推断和特征编码器更新的期望最大化（EM）策略进行优化。正则化项进一步鼓励表示捕获疾病特异性变异，同时忽略与对照组共享的变异。与以往相关工作相比，我们的方法在MNIST示例和四个不同真实医学影像数据集上显著提升了估计子组的质量。代码和数据集可在https://github.com/rlouiset/deep_ucsl获取。

</details>


### [224] [CRAFT: Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21317)
*Ziqi Wang,Qiang Liu,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT 提出了一种基于几何校正的联邦学习聚合框架，通过闭式解优化冲突对齐约束，并采用逐层自适应机制，在异质数据分布下提升全局模型精度并缩小客户端性能差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有联邦学习在异构数据分布下，简单平均聚合会导致全局更新与部分客户端冲突，使得这些客户端性能下降。亟需一种能够解决冲突的聚合方法。

Method: 将聚合问题建模为带冲突-free对齐约束的几何校正优化问题，寻找最接近参考方向且不冲突的更新；推导出闭式解避免迭代开销；采用逐层自适应机制处理不同特征粒度的冲突。

Result: 理论分析表明CRAFT通过投影几何促进公共下降结构并缓解冲突；在异构基准数据集上的实验表明，与最先进基线相比，CRAFT提升了全局模型精度并缩小了客户端间的性能差异。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过几何约束和逐层自适应有效解决了联邦学习中的客户端更新冲突问题，实现了更公平且更准确的全局模型。

Original Abstract: The aggregation of conflicting client updates remains a fundamental bottleneck in federated learning (FL) under heterogeneous data distributions. Naive averaging can produce a global update that improves the global objective while conflicting with specific clients, causing degradation for those clients. In this work, we propose CRAFT (Conflict-Resolved Aggregation for Federated Training), a new aggregation framework that treats the global update as a geometric correction problem. We formulate aggregation as finding the update closest to a reference direction while satisfying conflict-free alignment constraints. We derive a closed-form expression for the constrained optimization problem, avoiding the computational overhead of iterative solvers. Furthermore, we use a layer-wise adaptation to address conflicts at varying feature granularities. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that CRAFT promotes a common-descent structure and mitigates conflicts through its projection geometry. Extensive experiments on heterogeneous benchmarks demonstrate that CRAFT improves the accuracy of the global model while reducing performance disparity across clients compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The source code for CRAFT is available at https://github.com/tum-pbs/CRAFT.

中文摘要翻译: 在异构数据分布下，客户端更新的冲突聚合仍然是联邦学习（FL）中的一个根本性瓶颈。朴素平均可能产生一个虽能改善全局目标但与特定客户端冲突的全局更新，导致这些客户端性能下降。在这项工作中，我们提出了CRAFT（冲突解决的联邦训练聚合框架），一种将全局更新视为几何校正问题的新聚合框架。我们将聚合问题建模为寻找最接近参考方向同时满足无冲突对齐约束的更新。我们推导了该约束优化问题的闭式解，避免了迭代求解器的计算开销。此外，我们采用逐层自适应机制来处理不同特征粒度上的冲突。理论分析表明，CRAFT通过其投影几何结构促进了公共下降结构并缓解了冲突。在异构基准数据集上的大量实验表明，与最先进的基线方法相比，CRAFT在提升全局模型精度的同时缩小了客户端间的性能差异。CRAFT的源代码可在https://github.com/tum-pbs/CRAFT获取。

</details>


### [225] [Optimized Federated Knowledge Distillation with Distributed Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21322)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon,Guilain Leduc,Sadi Alawadi,Feras M. Awaysheh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedKDNAS提出了一种结合客户端神经架构搜索与服务器协调知识蒸馏的联邦学习框架，每个客户端在精度-资源约束下自主选择轻量模型，仅共享公共参考集上的预测，实现统计异质性、系统异质性与通信效率的联合优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法几乎都假设固定的客户端架构，限制了模型对数据复杂度差异和硬件约束的适应性，导致精度与效率之间次优的权衡。

Method: 每个客户端在精度-资源约束下自主选择轻量模型，使用监督学习与知识蒸馏的混合目标进行本地训练，仅共享公共参考集上的预测；服务器聚合和平滑这些预测，可选地结合教师模型，生成稳定的蒸馏目标供下一轮使用。

Result: 在六个数据集上与六种代表性基线对比，FedKDNAS在非IID条件下精度提升达15%，客户端CPU使用降低约28%，通信开销减少高达44倍。

Conclusion: FedKDNAS通过架构搜索与知识蒸馏的协同设计，在统计异质性、系统异质性和通信效率三个维度上均实现了显著的帕累托改进。

Original Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing data. However, real-world deployments must simultaneously address statistical heterogeneity across client data (non-IID), system heterogeneity in device capabilities, and communication efficiency. Existing FL approaches mitigate these challenges through improved aggregation, personalization, or knowledge distillation, but they almost universally assume a fixed client architecture, limiting adaptability to heterogeneous data complexity and hardware constraints. This architectural constraint often leads to suboptimal trade-offs between accuracy and efficiency in real-world FL systems. This work introduces FedKDNAS, a distillation-driven FL framework that combines client-side neural architecture selection with distillation of server-coordinated knowledge. Each client autonomously selects a lightweight model under accuracy-resource constraints. It then trains it locally using a hybrid objective combining supervised learning and knowledge distillation and shares only predictions on a public reference set. The server then aggregates and smooths these predictions, optionally combining them with a teacher model, to produce stable distillation targets for the next round. Extensive evaluation on six datasets against six representative FL baselines (FedAvg, Ditto, FedMD, FedDF, FedDistill, Local-KD) demonstrates that FedKDNAS consistently achieves superior Pareto efficiency, improving accuracy by up to 15\% under non-IID conditions, reducing client CPU usage by approximately 28\%, and decreasing communication overhead by up to 44 times while maintaining lightweight logit-based communication.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习（FL）能够在无需集中数据的情况下实现协作模型训练。然而，实际部署必须同时应对客户端数据的统计异质性（非独立同分布）、设备能力的系统异质性以及通信效率问题。现有联邦学习方法通过改进聚合、个性化或知识蒸馏来缓解这些挑战，但它们几乎都假设固定的客户端架构，限制了模型对数据复杂度差异和硬件约束的适应性。这种架构约束通常导致实际联邦学习系统中的精度与效率之间出现次优权衡。本文提出FedKDNAS，一种蒸馏驱动的联邦学习框架，它将客户端神经架构选择与服务器协调的知识蒸馏相结合。每个客户端在精度-资源约束下自主选择轻量模型，然后使用监督学习与知识蒸馏的混合目标进行本地训练，并仅共享公共参考集上的预测。服务器聚合和平滑这些预测，可选地结合教师模型，为下一轮生成稳定的蒸馏目标。在六个数据集上对六种代表性联邦学习基线（FedAvg、Ditto、FedMD、FedDF、FedDistill、Local-KD）进行的广泛评估表明，FedKDNAS持续实现了优越的帕累托效率：在非独立同分布条件下精度提升高达15%，客户端CPU使用降低约28%，通信开销减少多达44倍，同时保持轻量级的logit通信。

</details>


### [226] [Fast and Stable Triangular Inversion for Delta-Rule Linear Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21325)
*Aleksandros Sobczyk,Gioele Gottardo,Christos K. Matzoros,Mirko De Vita,Filip Skogh,Anastasios Zouzias,Jiawei Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了三角矩阵求逆的直推法和迭代法，重点研究其在Delta-Rule线性注意力层中的数值稳定性、计算复杂度和硬件效率，在NPU上实现了最高4.3倍加速，且不损失模型精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 包含Delta-Rule的线性注意力模型需要频繁进行三角矩阵求逆，该操作是性能瓶颈且对数值误差高度敏感，若实现不当会显著损害端到端模型精度，因此需要系统分析并优化该子程序。

Method: 对直推法和迭代法两类三角矩阵求逆算法进行系统分析，重点关注富含矩阵乘法运算的方法以充分利用现代硬件；从数值稳定性、计算复杂度、硬件效率等多个维度进行评估，并在低精度浮点表示下进行实验验证。

Result: 在NPU上相比SGLang的最新三角矩阵求逆实现，最高可达4.3倍加速；在完整层级别上带来显著性能提升，同时保持端到端模型精度完全不变。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析不同三角矩阵求逆算法并选择适合硬件特性的方法，可以在不牺牲模型精度的前提下大幅提升包含Delta-Rule的线性注意力层的计算效率。

Original Abstract: Linear attention has emerged as a cornerstone for efficient long-context architectures, as evidenced by its integration into state-of-the-art open-source models including Qwen3.5/3.6, Kimi Linear, and RWKV-7. Models that incorporate linear attention layers with the so-called Delta-Rule involve the inversion of triangular matrices as a core sub-routine. This operation often forms a performance bottleneck, and, due to its high-sensitivity to numerical errors, it can significantly deteriorate end-to-end model accuracy if it is not carefully implemented. This work provides a systematic analysis of both direct and iterative triangular inversion algorithms, targeting methods that are rich in matrix products, and, therefore, have the potential to efficiently utilize modern hardware. To that end, our analysis covers a broad spectrum of mathematical and practical aspects, with a heavy focus on numerical stability, computational complexity, and, ultimately, hardware efficiency and practical considerations. We provide a rigorous experimental evaluation to verify these properties in practical scenarios, and in low-precision floating-point representations, highlighting the strengths and limitations of each method. Performance benchmarks on NPUs reveal up to $4.3\times$ speed-up against the state-of-the-art implementations of SGLang for triangular matrix inversion, leading to significant performance improvements on the entire layer level, while maintaining full end-to-end model accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 线性注意力已成为高效长上下文架构的基石，其在包括Qwen3.5/3.6、Kimi Linear和RWKV-7在内的最先进开源模型中的集成即为明证。采用所谓Delta-Rule的线性注意力层将三角矩阵求逆作为核心子程序。该操作通常构成性能瓶颈，且由于对数值误差高度敏感，若未仔细实现，会显著恶化端到端模型精度。本文对直推法和迭代法两类三角矩阵求逆算法进行了系统分析，旨在寻找富含矩阵乘法运算的方法，从而能高效利用现代硬件。为此，我们的分析涵盖了广泛数学和实践方面的问题，重点关注数值稳定性、计算复杂度，以及最终的硬件效率和实际考量。我们提供了严格的实验评估，以验证这些性质在实践场景和低精度浮点表示下的表现，凸显了每种方法的优势与局限。在NPU上的性能基准测试显示，相较于SGLang的最先进三角矩阵求逆实现，我们取得了最高4.3倍的加速，从而在完整层级别上带来了显著性能提升，同时保持了完全的端到端模型精度。

</details>


### [227] [Data-Efficient Neural Operator Training via Physics-Based Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21348)
*Alicja Polanska,Lorenzo Zanisi,Vignesh Gopakumar,Stanislas Pamela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的主动学习算法（Physics-based Acquisition），通过利用偏微分方程残差指导数据选择，在保持与最先进方法相当的数据效率的同时，将物理归纳偏置注入训练过程，从而降低神经算子训练的数据需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经算子求解偏微分方程虽能显著降低计算成本，但训练数据需求量大成为瓶颈，亟需通过主动学习以更少的数据高效训练模型。

Method: 提出一种基于物理信息的主动学习算法（physics-based acquisition），利用偏微分方程残差来指导训练数据的迭代选择，将物理归纳偏置注入训练过程。

Result: 在一维Burgers方程和二维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程上的数值实验表明，该方法在数据效率上始终优于随机采集，并达到与最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅具有数据高效的优点，还能将物理归纳偏置注入训练，确保计算资源投入在模型物理理解最薄弱的地方。

Original Abstract: Solving partial differential equations with neural operators significantly reduces computational costs but remains bottlenecked by high training data requirements. Active learning offers a natural framework to mitigate this by selectively acquiring the most informative samples in an iterative manner. We introduce physics-based acquisition - a novel physics-informed active learning algorithm that leverages the partial differential equation residual to guide data selection. We validate the method by presenting numerical experiments for the 1D Burgers equation and the 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations. We show that, in our experiments, physics-based acquisition consistently outperforms random acquisition and matches the state of the art in data efficiency. At the same time, it has the unique advantage of injecting a physics inductive bias into the training process, ensuring that simulation cost is spent where the model's physical understanding is weakest.

中文摘要翻译: 使用神经算子求解偏微分方程可显著降低计算成本，但高昂的训练数据需求仍然是主要瓶颈。主动学习通过迭代方式选择性获取最具信息量的样本，为缓解这一问题提供了自然框架。本文提出了一种基于物理信息的主动学习算法（physics-based acquisition），该算法利用偏微分方程残差指导数据选择。我们通过一维Burgers方程和二维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的数值实验验证了该方法。实验结果表明，基于物理信息的采集策略在数据效率上始终优于随机采集，并达到与最先进方法相当的水平。同时，该方法具有独特优势，能够将物理归纳偏置注入训练过程，确保计算开销用于模型物理理解最薄弱的区域。

</details>


### [228] [On the Regularity and Generalization of One-Step Wasserstein-guided Generative Models for PDE-Induced Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21388)
*Likun Lin,Zhongjian Wang,Jack Xin,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为生成模型在PDE诱导概率测度下的统计精度建立了理论框架，证明了最优传输映射的Hölder连续性，并以DeepParticle为例推导了过量风险界和鲁棒性估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型在科学计算中统计精度的理论分析大多较为悲观，缺乏对PDE诱导概率测度下传输映射正则性和泛化性能的严格理论理解。

Method: 1) 证明线性椭圆/抛物方程、扩散方程及Fokker-Planck方程诱导的目标测度满足倍测度条件；2) 结合倍测度最优传输正则性理论，证明从均匀源测度到目标测度的最优传输映射是Hölder连续的；3) 以DeepParticle为代表性实例，推导过量风险界和鲁棒性估计。

Result: 1) 证明了多种PDE诱导测度满足倍测度条件；2) 证明了最优传输映射的Hölder连续性；3) 为一步生成模型提供了近似理论依据；4) 针对DeepParticle推导了学习映射与总体最优映射之间差异的过量风险界；5) 建立了目标偏移下的鲁棒性估计；6) 实验验证了所推导的速率。

Conclusion: 本文为Wasserstein引导的一步生成模型在PDE诱导分布上的泛化性能提供了坚实的理论基础，证明了传输映射的正则性，并给出了可验证的理论速率。

Original Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of generative models, the available theory on their statistical accuracy in scientific computing remains largely pessimistic. This paper develops a theoretical framework for understanding the regularity of transport maps and the generalization properties of one-step Wasserstein-guided generative models for PDE-induced probability measures. We consider normalized target densities associated with linear elliptic and parabolic equations on bounded domains, as well as diffusion and Fokker--Planck equations on the torus. Under standard structural assumptions, we prove that these target measures satisfy doubling conditions. By combining this fact with regularity theory for optimal transport between doubling measures, we show that the optimal transport map from a uniform source measure to the target measure is Hölder continuous. This regularity yields an approximation-theoretic justification for one-step generative models that learn PDE-induced distributions via a single pushforward map. As a representative instance, we study DeepParticle and derive excess-risk bounds characterizing the discrepancy between the learned map and the population-optimal map. We also establish a robustness estimate under target shift and illustrate the theory with experiments which support the derived rates.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管生成模型在经验上取得了显著成功，但关于它们在科学计算中统计精度的现有理论在很大程度上仍然是悲观的。本文发展了一个理论框架，用于理解传输映射的正则性以及一步Wasserstein引导生成模型在偏微分方程诱导概率测度上的泛化性质。我们考虑了有界域上线性椭圆方程和抛物方程相关的归一化目标密度，以及环面上扩散方程和Fokker-Planck方程对应的目标密度。在标准结构假设下，我们证明了这些目标测度满足倍测度条件。结合这一事实与倍测度之间最优传输的正则性理论，我们证明了从均匀源测度到目标测度的最优传输映射是Hölder连续的。这一正则性为通过单个前推映射学习PDE诱导分布的一步生成模型提供了近似理论上的合理性证明。作为代表性实例，我们研究了DeepParticle，并推导了描述学习映射与总体最优映射之间差异的过量风险界。我们还建立了目标偏移下的鲁棒性估计，并通过实验验证了推导出的速率。

</details>


### [229] [HiRes: Inspectable Precedent Memory for Reaction Condition Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21420)
*Shreyas Vinaya Sathyanarayana,Raja Sekhar Pappala,Deepak Warrier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiRes是一种检索增强的反应条件推荐系统，通过层次化反应表示（图编码器、跨注意力、多流融合、k-NN检索）在USPTC-Condition基准上取得最优性能（催化剂Acc@1 0.929、溶剂0.534、试剂0.530），并证明检索机制能显著提升溶剂和试剂的预测准确性，弥合了预测精度与化学可解释性之间的差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在逆合成断开选择之后，化学家不仅需要准确的反应条件预测，还需要支撑这些预测的具体先例。现有纯参数化方法在预测精度上存在局限，且缺乏可解释性，无法提供可检验的化学先例来支持实际合成规划。

Method: HiRes采用层次化反应表示，结合图编码器、变形感知交叉注意力机制、多流反应融合以及k-NN检索层。其学到的反应空间既用作分类器特征，又用作可检索的先例记忆库，从而实现检索增强的条件推荐。

Result: 在主槽USPTC-Condition模型上，催化剂、溶剂和试剂的Acc@1分别达到0.929、0.534和0.530。催化剂指标持平最佳基线，溶剂和试剂指标超越REACON等基线。配对bootstrap分析表明，集成检索机制在溶剂和试剂选择上相比纯参数化方法有统计显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: HiRes通过统一的层次化反应表示，同时实现了竞争力强的条件预测精度和可检验的化学先例支撑，弥合了预测准确性与化学可解释性之间的鸿沟，为实用合成规划提供了兼具推荐能力和可解释性的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Reaction condition recommendation sits immediately after retrosynthetic disconnection selection, and in practice, chemists require both accurate predictions and the precedents that justify them. We present HiRes (Hierarchical Reaction Representations), a retrieval-augmented condition recommendation system whose learned reaction space serves as both a classifier feature and an inspectable precedent memory. The model combines a graph encoder, transformation-aware cross-attention, multi-stream reaction fusion, and a k-NN retrieval layer. HiRes achieves state-of-the-art performance among primary-slot USPTO-Condition models, reaching Catalyst, Solvent, and Reagent top-1 accuracies (Acc@1) of 0.929, 0.534, and 0.530 respectively. It ties the best reported baseline on Catalyst while outperforming models such as REACON on Solvent and Reagent. Furthermore, paired bootstrap analysis demonstrates that integrating retrieval with learned condition heads provides statistically significant gains for solvent and reagent selection over purely parametric approaches. Ultimately, HiRes bridges the gap between predictive accuracy and chemical interpretability, offering a single representation that supplies both competitive recommendations and the concrete chemical precedents necessary for practical synthesis planning.

中文摘要翻译: 反应条件推荐紧接在逆合成断键选择之后，在实际应用中，化学家既需要准确的预测，也需要支撑这些预测的具体先例。我们提出了HiRes（层次化反应表示），一种检索增强的条件推荐系统，其学到的反应空间既用作分类器特征，又用作可检验的先例记忆库。该模型结合了图编码器、变形感知交叉注意力机制、多流反应融合以及k-NN检索层。HiRes在主槽USPTC-Condition模型中达到了最先进的性能，催化剂、溶剂和试剂的top-1准确率（Acc@1）分别达到了0.929、0.534和0.530。它在催化剂指标上持平于最佳报道基线，同时在溶剂和试剂指标上超越了REACON等模型。此外，配对bootstrap分析表明，将检索机制与学习到的条件预测头相结合，在溶剂和试剂选择上相比纯参数化方法提供了统计显著的性能提升。最终，HiRes弥合了预测准确性与化学可解释性之间的差距，提供了一种统一的表示，既能给出具有竞争力的推荐，又能提供实际合成规划所必需的具体化学先例。

</details>


### [230] [Polynomial-Time Robust Multiclass Linear Classification under Gaussian Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21428)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Giannis Iakovidis,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯分布下多类线性分类器的不可知学习问题。对于k≥3的情况，现有算法在精度依赖上存在指数级障碍。本文通过新的结构结果，设计了完全多项式时间的鲁棒学习算法，实现了与维度无关的误差保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 二类线性分类器(k=2)已有成熟的算法理论，但k≥3时（即使k=3）现有鲁棒算法在复杂度和表示规模上对所需精度的倒数呈指数依赖。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，设计高效的多类线性分类器学习算法。

Method: 1) 分析了标准多类感知机算法的局限性，证明其在干净标签和高斯边际下仍需要超多项式样本和更新；2) 提出成对非恰当学习框架(pairwise improper-learning framework)，实现高效学习；3) 提出基于定位(localization-based)的更精确框架，针对k=3和几何正则的多类线性分类器进一步优化误差。

Result: 1) 标准多类感知机算法在k≥3时存在基本障碍（在二类情形下不存在）；2) 成对非恰当学习框架达到误差 $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$；3) 定位框架对k=3达到误差 $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$，对几何正则k类线性分类器达到误差 $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$。

Conclusion: 本文为多类线性分类器的不可知学习提供了首个完全多项式时间的鲁棒算法，揭示了多类(k≥3)与二类(k=2)情形之间的本质差异，并为后续研究奠定了理论基础。

Original Abstract: We study the task of agnostic learning of multiclass linear classifiers under the Gaussian distribution. Given labeled examples $(x, y)$ from a distribution over $\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$, with Gaussian $x$-marginal, the goal is to output a hypothesis whose error is comparable to that of the best $k$-class linear classifier. While the binary case $k=2$ has a well-developed algorithmic theory, much less is known for $k \ge 3$. Even for $k=3$, prior robust algorithms incur exponential dependence on the inverse of the desired accuracy in both complexity and representation size. In this work, we develop new structural results for multiclass linear classifiers and use them to design fully polynomial-time robust learners with dimension-independent error guarantees. Our first result shows that the standard multiclass perceptron algorithm requires super-polynomially many samples and updates, even with clean labels and Gaussian marginals, revealing a basic obstruction absent in the binary case. Our main positive result is a pairwise improper-learning framework which yields an efficient learner with error $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ for general $k$. Additionally, we develop a sharper localization-based framework which leads to error $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$ for $k=3$, and error $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$ for geometrically regular $k$-class linear classifiers.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了高斯分布下多类线性分类器的不可知学习任务。给定来自$\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$上某个分布、且$x$-边际为高斯分布的带标签样本$(x, y)$，目标是输出一个假设，其误差可与最佳$k$类线性分类器相当。虽然二类情形$k=2$已有成熟的算法理论，但$k \ge 3$的情况知之甚少。即使对于$k=3$，先前的鲁棒算法在复杂度和表示规模上对所需精度的倒数都呈指数依赖。在这项工作中，我们为多类线性分类器发展了新的结构结果，并利用它们设计了具有维度无关误差保证的完全多项式时间鲁棒学习器。我们的第一个结果表明，标准多类感知机算法即使使用干净标签和高斯边际，也需要超多项式数量的样本和更新，揭示了二类情形中不存在的基本障碍。我们的主要正面结果是一个成对非恰当学习框架，它为一般$k$提供了误差为$\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$的高效学习器。此外，我们开发了一个更精确的基于定位的框架，对于$k=3$可达到$O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$的误差，对于几何正则的$k$类线性分类器可达到$\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$的误差。

</details>


### [231] [Variance Reduction for Expectations with Diffusion Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21489)
*Jesse Bettencourt,Xindi Wu,Matan Atzmon,James Lucas,Jonathan Lorraine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARV框架，通过分层蒙特卡洛估计器（结合噪声重采样、时间步重要采样和分层逆CDF构造）降低扩散模型教师梯度估计的方差，在文本到3D、单步蒸馏等任务中实现2-3倍有效计算加速，并发现单步蒸馏中方差已非瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 预训练扩散模型作为冻结教师模型用于下游任务时，其梯度估计依赖蒙特卡洛期望，估计器方差大导致计算成本高（每次采样需渲染、仿真、编码等昂贵上游计算），因此需要一种能感知计算成本的方差缩减方法来提高计算效率。

Method: 提出CARV（Compute-Aware Variance-Accounting）框架，核心是分层蒙特卡洛（Hierarchical MC）估计器：将昂贵的上游计算结果在多个廉价扩散噪声重样本之间分摊使用，同时结合时间步重要采样（timestep importance sampling）和分层逆CDF（stratified-inverse-CDF）构造进一步降低方差。

Result: 在文本到3D蒸馏和归因实验中，CARV实现2-3倍有效计算倍增（主要来自重采样分摊，约25%来自重要采样+分层）；在单步蒸馏中，相同技术将梯度方差降低一个数量级，但下游FID未见改善，表明该场景下方差已不再是瓶颈。

Conclusion: CARV框架能有效降低扩散模型下游任务中的梯度估计方差并节省计算成本，但同时也揭示了方差缩减的有效性受任务场景限制——当MC方差不是主要瓶颈时，单纯降低方差无法带来下游性能提升。

Original Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models serve as frozen teachers feeding downstream pipelines such as text-to-3D, single-step distillation, and data attribution. The teacher gradients these pipelines consume are Monte Carlo (MC) expectations over noise levels and Gaussian noise samples; their estimator variance dominates compute cost because each draw requires expensive upstream work (rendering, simulation, encoding). We introduce CARV, a compute-aware variance-accounting framework that motivates a hierarchical MC estimator: amortize the expensive upstream computation over cheap diffusion-noise resamples, sharpened by timestep importance sampling and a stratified-inverse-CDF construction. In our text-to-3D distillation and attribution experiments, CARV delivers 2-3x effective compute multipliers (most from amortized reuse; ~25% additional from IS+stratification) without changing the objective; in single-step distillation, the same techniques cut gradient variance by an order of magnitude but do not improve downstream FID, marking the regime where MC variance is no longer the bottleneck.

中文摘要翻译: 预训练扩散模型作为冻结的教师模型，为文本到3D、单步蒸馏和数据归因等下游流程提供支持。这些流程使用的教师梯度是噪声水平和高斯噪声样本上的蒙特卡洛（MC）期望值；其估计器方差主导了计算成本，因为每次采样都需要昂贵的上游工作（渲染、仿真、编码）。我们提出了CARV，一个计算感知的方差核算框架，它启发了一种分层MC估计器：将昂贵的上游计算分摊到廉价的扩散噪声重采样上，并通过时间步重要采样和分层逆CDF构造进一步增强。在我们的文本到3D蒸馏和归因实验中，CARV在不改变目标函数的情况下实现了2-3倍的有效计算倍增（主要来自摊销重用；约25%来自重要采样+分层）；在单步蒸馏中，相同的技术将梯度方差降低了一个数量级，但并未改善下游FID，这表明在该场景下MC方差不再是瓶颈。

</details>


### [232] [torchtune: PyTorch native post-training library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21442)
*Mark Obozov,Maxime Griot,Joseph Cummings,Evan Smothers,Felipe Mello,Rafi Ayub,Philip John Bontrager,Salman Mohammadi,Ariel Kwiatkowski,Nathan Azrak,Mircea Mironenco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: torchtune 是一个 PyTorch 原生库，专注于 LLM 后训练阶段（微调、实验和部署），强调模块化、可扩展性和对底层 PyTorch 组件的直接访问。与 Axolotl 和 Unsloth 等框架相比，它在多种设置下提供了强大的性能和内存效率，同时保持了灵活性和可复现性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的微调框架（如 Axolotl、Unsloth）虽然在易用性、特定方案或硬件效率上有所优化，但往往牺牲了透明性和可扩展性。torchtune 旨在解决这一问题，提供一个更加模块化、易于定制且直接基于 PyTorch 的后训练框架。

Method: torchtune 的设计围绕三个核心组件展开：模型构建器（model builders）、训练方案（training recipes）和分布式训练栈（distributed training stack）。它强调模块化和可破解性（hackability），让用户能够直接访问和修改底层 PyTorch 组件。

Result: 与 Axolotl 和 Unsloth 等流行微调框架相比，torchtune 在多种后训练设置下表现出强大的性能和内存效率，同时保持了足够的灵活性以支持快速研究迭代。

Conclusion: torchtune 可以作为可复现的 LLM 后训练研究的实用基础框架。

Original Abstract: Modern LLMs typically require multistage training pipelines to achieve strong downstream performance, with post-training serving as the main interface for adapting open-weight models. We introduce torchtune, a PyTorch-native library designed to streamline the post-training lifecycle of LLMs, enabling efficient fine-tuning, experimentation, and deployment-oriented workflows. Unlike many existing fine-tuning frameworks, which often optimize for ease of use, specialized recipes, or hardware efficiency at the cost of transparency and extensibility, torchtune emphasizes modularity, hackability, and direct access to the underlying PyTorch components. In this paper, we present the design principles behind torchtune, describe how they are reflected in its model builders, training recipes, and distributed training stack, and evaluate the library across representative post-training settings. We compare against popular fine-tuning frameworks, including Axolotl and Unsloth, and show that torchtune provides strong performance and memory efficiency across many settings while remaining flexible enough for rapid research iteration. These results position torchtune as a practical foundation for reproducible LLMs post-training research.

中文摘要翻译: 现代大语言模型通常需要多阶段训练流程才能实现强大的下游性能，其中后训练是适配开放权重模型的主要接口。我们引入了 torchtune，一个 PyTorch 原生库，旨在简化大语言模型的后训练生命周期，支持高效的微调、实验和面向部署的工作流程。与许多现有的微调框架不同——这些框架通常为了易用性、专门的方案或硬件效率而牺牲了透明性和可扩展性——torchtune 强调模块化、可破解性以及对底层 PyTorch 组件的直接访问。在本文中，我们介绍了 torchtune 背后的设计原则，描述了这些原则如何体现在其模型构建器、训练方案和分布式训练栈中，并在具有代表性的后训练场景下对库进行了评估。我们将 torchtune 与流行的微调框架（包括 Axolotl 和 Unsloth）进行了比较，结果表明 torchtune 在多种设置下提供了强大的性能和内存效率，同时保持足够的灵活性以支持快速研究迭代。这些结果使 torchtune 成为可复现的大语言模型后训练研究的实用基础。

</details>


### [233] [A Machine Learning Framework for Weighted Least Squares GNSS Positioning based on Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21461)
*Pin-Hsun Lee,Harry Leib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习与激活函数的加权最小二乘（WLS）定位算法，通过信号质量指标和集成学习预测信号质量分数，并利用激活函数将分数转换为权重，显著提升了城市峡谷环境下的GNSS定位精度，并在单/多星座场景及跨区域迁移中表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 城市峡谷中高楼与窄巷导致GNSS信号阻塞、非视距（NLOS）接收和多径效应，给伪距测量引入误差。虽然多星座GNSS增加了可用卫星数量，但包含降质信号反而可能造成严重定位误差。因此需要一种有效识别并抑制低质量信号的方法以提高定位精度。

Method: 提出一种基于机器学习的加权最小二乘（WLS）定位框架。首先利用多个信号质量指标作为训练特征，通过集成学习算法为每颗卫星预测质量分数；然后采用激活函数将预测分数转换为适合WLS定位的权重；最后用这些权重进行WLS定位解算。实验中对比了不同激活函数、机器学习算法和GNSS星座配置的表现。

Result: 在香港和东京的真实城市数据集上，Sigmoid函数在不同机器学习算法和GNSS星座配置下均取得最佳效果；该算法在单星座和多星座场景中均显著降低了定位误差；算法展现出良好的地理迁移性，在相似城市化水平区域训练后仍能保持相近的性能水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于激活函数的机器学习加权最小二乘算法能有效提升城市峡谷环境下的GNSS定位精度，且具有良好的跨区域泛化能力，适用于不同城市化程度区域的定位任务。

Original Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) are widely used to provide position, velocity, and timing (PVT) information for various applications, including transportation, location-based communication services, and intelligent agriculture. In urban canyons, high-rise buildings and narrow streets can cause signal obstruction, non-line-of-sight (NLOS) reception, and multipath effects that introduce errors in GNSS pseudorange measurements. Although multi-constellations GNSS effectively increase the number of available satellites, the inclusion of degraded signals can lead to severe positioning errors. This study proposes a machine learning framework for the weighted least squares (WLS) algorithm incorporating activation functions to enhance positioning accuracy. Several signal quality indicators are employed as training features for ensemble learning algorithms to identify poor quality signals by providing quality scores. Then, activation functions are employed to transform the machine learning predicted scores to appropriate weights for WLS positioning. To evaluate the performance of our approach, experiments are conducted using real-world datasets from Hong Kong and Tokyo urban areas. Comparative analysis of activation functions reveals that sigmoid functions consistently yield the greatest improvements with different machine learning algorithms and GNSS constellation configurations. The proposed algorithm demonstrates substantial reductions in positioning errors for both single- and multiconstellation scenarios. Furthermore, our results indicate that the proposed algorithm exhibits strong geographical transferability. The proposed algorithm maintains comparable level of performance when trained on data from other regions with similar levels of urbanization.

中文摘要翻译: 全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）被广泛应用于交通、基于位置的信息通信服务以及智慧农业等各类场景，以提供位置、速度和时间（PVT）信息。在城市峡谷环境中，高层建筑和狭窄街道会导致信号遮挡、非视距（NLOS）接收以及多径效应，从而在GNSS伪距测量中引入误差。尽管多星座GNSS有效增加了可用卫星数量，但包含降质信号反而可能导致严重的定位误差。本研究提出一种融合激活函数的加权最小二乘（WLS）机器学习框架，以提升定位精度。研究采用多个信号质量指标作为集成学习算法的训练特征，通过提供质量分数来识别低质量信号；进而利用激活函数将机器学习预测的分数转换为适用于WLS定位的适当权重。为了评估该方法的性能，我们在香港和东京城区的真实数据集上进行了实验。对不同激活函数的对比分析表明，Sigmoid函数在不同机器学习算法和GNSS星座配置下均能取得最佳改善效果。所提出的算法在单星座和多星座场景中均显著降低了定位误差。此外，我们的结果表明该算法具有较强的地理迁移能力：当使用来自其他城市化水平相似区域的数据进行训练时，所提算法能够保持相当的性能水平。

</details>


### [234] [You Only Need Minimal RLVR Training: Extrapolating LLMs via Rank-1 Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21468)
*Zhepei Wei,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Chengsong Huang,Jiaxin Huang,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR训练的权重轨迹是极低秩且高度可预测的，并据此提出RELEX方法，通过观测少量训练步的秩1子空间并线性外推，即可匹配或超越完整RLVR训练的性能，所需训练步数仅为完整训练的15%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）训练过程中大语言模型参数轨迹的底层几何结构，即验证权重轨迹是否具有低秩性和可预测性。

Method: 提出RELEX方法：从短观测窗口中估计秩1子空间，然后通过线性回归对后续检查点进行外推，无需学习模型即可生成高质量检查点。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B、Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base三个模型上，RELEX仅需完整RLVR训练15%的步数即可匹配或超越其性能；能外推至观测窗口的10-20倍；秩1近似已足够，更高秩或非线性建模无额外收益。

Conclusion: RLVR权重轨迹的低秩性质表明优化噪声是影响外推性能的关键，RELEX通过投影到秩1子空间实现“去噪”效果，为计算高效的模型训练提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a dominant paradigm for improving reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying geometry of the resulting parameter trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that RLVR weight trajectories are extremely low-rank and highly predictable. Specifically, we find that the majority of downstream performance gains are captured by a rank-1 approximation of the parameter deltas, where the magnitude of this projection evolves near-linearly with training steps. Motivated by this, we propose a simple and compute-efficient method RELEX (REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation), which estimates the rank-1 subspace from a short observation window and extrapolates future checkpoints via linear regression, with no learned model required. Across three models (i.e., Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, Qwen3-4B-Base, and Qwen3-8B-Base), RELEX produces checkpoints that match or exceed RLVR performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, requiring as few as 15% steps of full RLVR training. Remarkably, RELEX is able to extrapolate far beyond the observation window at no training cost, predicting checkpoints up to 10-20$\times$ beyond the observed prefix with continued improvement (e.g., observe only the first 50 steps and extrapolate to 1000 steps). Our ablation analysis confirms the minimalist sufficiency of RELEX: neither increasing the subspace rank nor employing non-linear modeling yields further gains in extrapolation. Finally, we show that RELEX's success stems from a "denoising" effect: by projecting updates onto the rank-1 subspace, the model discards stochastic optimization noise that would otherwise degrade performance during extrapolation. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的主流范式，但其参数轨迹的底层几何结构尚未被充分探索。本文证明RLVR权重轨迹具有极低秩性和高度可预测性。具体而言，我们发现大多数下游性能提升被参数增量的秩1近似所捕获，且该投影的幅度随训练步数近似线性增长。受此启发，我们提出一种简单且计算高效的方法RELEX（强化学习外推），该方法从短观测窗口中估计秩1子空间，并通过线性回归外推未来检查点，无需任何学习模型。在三个模型（Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B、Qwen3-4B-Base和Qwen3-8B-Base）上的实验表明，RELEX生成的检查点在域内和域外基准测试中均可匹配或超越RLVR性能，且仅需完整RLVR训练15%的步数。值得注意的是，RELEX能够在零训练成本下远远超出观测窗口进行外推，预测超出观测前缀10-20倍的检查点且性能持续提升（例如，仅观测前50步即可外推至1000步）。我们的消融分析证实了RELEX的极简充分性：既不需要增加子空间秩，也无需采用非线性建模即可获得外推收益。最后，我们证明RELEX的成功源于一种"去噪"效应：通过将更新投影到秩1子空间，模型丢弃了原本会在外推过程中降低性能的随机优化噪声。我们的代码已在https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX上开源。

</details>


### [235] [Quantifying Hyperparameter Transfer and the Importance of Embedding Layer Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21486)
*Dayal Singh Kalra,Maissam Barkeshli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量化超参数迁移的三指标框架，并通过大量消融实验揭示了μP相比SP在AdamW训练中的优势主要源于嵌入层学习率的最大化，而非现有理论所声称的原因。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有超参数迁移理论（如μP）不足以解释其为何相对于标准参数化（SP）能提供高质量的学习率迁移，论文旨在通过系统实验揭示其真正原因。

Method: 首先提出三个量化指标（缩放律拟合质量、外推鲁棒性、参数化导致的新近损失惩罚）来评估超参数迁移效果；然后通过一系列消融实验对比μP与SP的差异，分析嵌入层学习率的作用以及权重衰减的影响。

Result: (1) μP相对于SP的优势主要源于嵌入层学习率被最大化（按宽度缩放以匹配μP即可消除不稳定）；(2) SP中嵌入层学习率是瓶颈，会引发训练不稳定；(3) 权重衰减改善缩放律拟合，但在固定token-per-parameter设置下损害外推鲁棒性。

Conclusion: μP的优势主要来自嵌入层学习率的合理设置而非深层理论原因；权重衰减对超参数迁移的影响具有双面性。

Original Abstract: Hyperparameter transfer allows extrapolating optimal optimization hyperparameters from small to large scales, making it critical for training large language models (LLMs). This is done either by fitting a scaling law to the hyperparameters or by a judicious choice of parameterization, such as Maximal Update ($μ$P), that renders optimal hyperparameters approximately scale invariant. In this paper, we first develop a framework to quantify hyperparameter transfer through three metrics: (1) the quality of the scaling law fit, (2) the robustness to extrapolation errors, and (3) the asymptotic loss penalty due to choice of parameterization. Next, we investigate through a comprehensive series of ablations why $μ$P appears to offer high-quality learning rate transfer relative to standard parameterization (SP), as existing theory is inadequate. We find that the overwhelming benefit of $μ$P relative to SP when training with AdamW arises simply from maximizing the learning rate of the embedding layer. In SP, the embedding layer learning rate acts as a bottleneck that induces training instabilities; increasing it by a factor of width to match $μ$P dramatically smooths out training while improving hyperparameter transfer. We also find that weight decay improves the scaling law fits, while, in the fixed token-per-parameter setting, it hurts the robustness of the extrapolation.

中文摘要翻译: 超参数迁移允许将最优优化超参数从小规模外推至大规模，这对于训练大型语言模型（LLMs）至关重要。实现方式可以是拟合超参数的缩放律，或者通过审慎选择参数化方法（如最大更新参数化μP），使得最优超参数近似尺度不变。本文首先开发了一个框架，通过三个指标来量化超参数迁移：（1）缩放律拟合的质量，（2）对外推误差的鲁棒性，以及（3）参数化选择导致的渐进损失惩罚。接着，通过一系列全面的消融实验，我们探究了为何μP相对于标准参数化（SP）能提供高质量的学习率迁移——因为现有理论尚不足以解释这一点。我们发现，在使用AdamW训练时，μP相对于SP的压倒性优势仅仅源于嵌入层学习率的最大化。在SP中，嵌入层学习率是一个瓶颈，会引发训练不稳定性；将其按宽度因子放大以匹配μP，可以显著平滑训练过程并改善超参数迁移。我们还发现，权重衰减改善了缩放律拟合，但在固定的token-per-parameter设置下，权重衰减会损害外推的鲁棒性。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Starlink Beacons for Passive LEO-Aided 9D Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20394)
*Nisanur Camuzcu,Tiep M. Hoang,Alireza Vahid*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端的LEO辅助混合框架，融合GPS、Starlink下行信标和IMU，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波实现9D（三维位置、速度、姿态）PNT估计，在GNSS退化或不可用时提供鲁棒的导航能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GNSS信号功率低，在对抗性环境（如海事场景）中易受干扰、欺骗或拒止，导致PNT可用性出现缺口。需要一种独立、普适的辅助信号，可被动跟踪并与惯性传感融合，无需专用或协作基础设施即可维持完整的导航状态估计。

Method: (i) 通过将测量值与卫星ID关联，从Starlink下行信标音中提取多普勒速率；(ii) 在统一处理/估计管道下，将信标多普勒速率与OFDM衍生距离观测量进行基准对比；(iii) 将所得观测量集成到惯性导航中。采用Fisher信息预测、蒙特卡洛仿真和硬件测量评估GPS/IMU、Starlink/IMU和GPS-Starlink-IMU三种融合方案。

Result: Starlink多普勒速率能提供有意义的互补PNT信息，在GNSS退化或间歇性不可用时能辅助9D估计。

Conclusion: 基于Starlink下行信标多普勒速率的辅助方案可以有效弥补GNSS的不足，在对抗性环境下维持完整的导航状态估计能力。

Original Abstract: Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) underpin positioning, navigation, and timing (PNT), yet their low-power signals are easily blocked or disrupted, leaving gaps in PNT availability in contested environments (e.g. maritime settings) where interference, spoofing, or denial can occur. A key practical need is an independent, ubiquitous aiding signal that can be tracked passively and fused with inertial sensing to sustain full navigation-state estimation without dedicated or cooperative infrastructure. This paper presents an end-to-end LEO-aided hybrid framework that fuses GPS, Starlink downlink beacons, and an inertial measurement unit (IMU) in a 9D (3D position, 3D velocity, and 3D attitude) PNT system using an extended Kalman filter (EKF). We (i) extract Doppler-rate from Starlink downlink beacon tones by associating measurements with satellite IDs, (ii) benchmark beacon Doppler-rate against OFDM-derived range observables under a common processing/estimation pipeline, and (iii) integrate the resulting observable into inertial navigation. We evaluate GPS/IMU, Starlink/IMU, and GPS-Starlink-IMU using Fisher-information predictions, Monte Carlo simulations, and hardware measurements. Results show that Starlink Doppler-rate provides meaningful complementary PNT information, and can aid 9D estimation when GNSS is degraded or intermittently unavailable.

中文摘要翻译: 全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）支撑着定位、导航与授时（PNT），但其低功率信号易被阻塞或干扰，在干扰、欺骗或拒止可能发生的对抗性环境（如海事场景）中导致PNT可用性出现缺口。一个关键的实际需求是拥有一种独立、普适的辅助信号，可被被动跟踪并与惯性传感融合，从而在无需专用或协作基础设施的情况下维持完整的导航状态估计。本文提出了一种端到端的低轨卫星辅助混合框架，该框架使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF），融合GPS、Starlink下行信标和惯性测量单元（IMU），构建了一个9D（三维位置、三维速度、三维姿态）PNT系统。我们(i) 通过将测量值与卫星ID关联，从Starlink下行信标音中提取多普勒速率；(ii) 在统一处理/估计管道下，将信标多普勒速率与OFDM衍生距离观测量进行基准对比；(iii) 将所得观测量集成到惯性导航中。我们使用Fisher信息预测、蒙特卡洛仿真和硬件测量对GPS/IMU、Starlink/IMU和GPS-Starlink-IMU三种方案进行评估。结果表明，Starlink多普勒速率能提供有意义的互补PNT信息，在GNSS退化或间歇性不可用时能够辅助9D估计。

</details>


### [237] [A New Approach for ARMA Pole Estimation Using Higher-Order Crossings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20483)
*Timothy I. Salsbury,Ashish Singhal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用高阶 crossings（穿越计数）来估计 ARMA 模型极点的新方法，该方法仅需存储穿越计数即可实现极点估计，适用于控制回路性能评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ARMA 模型的极点对应于特征方程的根，可用于评估控制性能，但传统估计方法通常需要存储大量原始数据；本文旨在利用更简洁的特征（穿越计数）来实现极点估计。

Method: 将数据中的 crossing（穿越）事件计数通过自相关域转换为 ARMA 模型的极点估计值，核心是利用高阶 crossings 这一特征进行变换。

Result: 穿越计数是唯一需要从原始数据中存储的特征，即可完成 ARMA 极点的估计。

Conclusion: 该方法具有数据存储需求低的优势，适用于控制回路性能评估场景。

Original Abstract: The paper describes a new method for estimating the poles of an ARMA model using higher-order crossings. The method involves transforming counts of crossing events into estimates of ARMA poles via the autocorrelation domain. An important advantage of the method is that the crossing counts are the only features that need to be stored from the original data. The poles of an ARMA model of a control loop correspond to the roots of the characteristic equation and are thus useful for evaluating control performance.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种利用高阶 crossings 估计 ARMA 模型极点的新方法。该方法将穿越事件的计数通过自相关域转换为 ARMA 极点的估计值。该方法的一个重要优势在于，穿越计数是从原始数据中唯一需要存储的特征。控制回路中 ARMA 模型的极点对应于特征方程的根，因此可用于评估控制性能。

</details>


### [238] [Microwave Linear Analog Computer (MiLAC)-Aided MIMO Radar Sensing: Transmit Beamforming Design and DoA Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21020)
*Ziang Liu,Zheyu Wu,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出将微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）应用于大规模MIMO雷达系统中，通过将线性操作从数字域迁移到模拟域，在保持与全数字架构相同克拉美罗界（CRB）和DOA估计性能的同时，显著降低硬件成本、功耗和数字处理复杂度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模MIMO雷达虽能提升感知性能，但会带来硬件成本、功耗和数字处理复杂度的剧增。现有的数字域处理方法需要高分辨率DAC、大量射频链和ADC，导致系统昂贵且低效。因此，需要探索一种在保持高性能的同时降低硬件开销的新架构。

Method: 1) 提出了MiLAC辅助的发射波束成形方法，将加权CRB最小化问题建模为无损互易MiLAC约束下的非凸优化问题，并采用基于罚对偶分解（PDD）的迭代算法求解。2) 提出了MiLAC辅助的接收端二维离散傅里叶变换（2D-DFT）的DOA估计方法，利用无损互易MiLAC在模拟域实现2D DFT，无需数字优化。

Result: 1) 理论证明MiLAC辅助波束成形与全数字波束成形能达到相同的CRB。2) MiLAC辅助方法在DOA估计性能上与全数字基准相同。3) 发射端仅需低分辨率DAC，接收端无需射频链和ADC，大幅降低硬件成本和功耗。4) 模拟域执行2D DFT消除了所有数字DFT运算。

Conclusion: MiLAC能够在保持与全数字架构相同感知性能（CRB和DOA估计精度）的前提下，有效降低大规模MIMO雷达的硬件成本、功耗和数字处理复杂度，是一种有前景的模拟-数字混合雷达架构。

Original Abstract: Multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) radar has waveform diversity and large spatial degrees of freedom (DoFs), making it attractive for high-resolution sensing. Scaling MIMO radar to massive arrays can further improve sensing performance, but it also increases hardware cost, power consumption, and digital processing complexity. The microwave linear analog computer (MiLAC) can tackle these challenges by moving linear operations from the digital domain to the analog domain. MiLAC has shown promising benefits for communications in recent studies and this paper identifies its potential for radar sensing. Specifically, we consider both MiLAC-aided transmit beamforming and receiver-side two-dimensional discrete Fourier transform (2D-DFT)-based direction-of-arrival (DoA) estimation. For transmit beamforming, we formulate a weighted Cramer Rao bound (CRB) minimization problem under lossless and reciprocal MiLAC constraints and propose a penalty dual decomposition (PDD)-based iterative algorithm to address the non-convex problem. We further prove that MiLAC-aided and fully-digital beamforming achieve the same CRB. For receiver processing, we show that the 2D DFT can be implemented by a lossless reciprocal MiLAC, which enables analog-domain DoA estimation without digital optimization. Numerical results confirm the theoretical finding and show that the MiLAC-aided approach achieves the same CRB and DoA estimation performance as the fully-digital benchmark. Meanwhile, hardware cost and power consumption are reduced because only low-resolution DACs are required at the transmitter, while RF chains and ADCs are eliminated at the receiver. Moreover, performing the 2D DFT in the analog domain eliminates all digital DFT operations for DoA estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 多输入多输出（MIMO）雷达具有波形多样性和大空间自由度（DoFs），使其在高分辨率感知中具有吸引力。将MIMO雷达扩展到大规模阵列可以进一步提升感知性能，但这也会增加硬件成本、功耗和数字处理复杂度。微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）可以通过将线性操作从数字域迁移到模拟域来应对这些挑战。最近的研究表明MiLAC在通信领域具有显著优势，而本文则识别了其在雷达感知中的潜力。具体而言，我们同时考虑了MiLAC辅助的发射波束成形和基于接收端二维离散傅里叶变换（2D-DFT）的波达方向（DoA）估计。对于发射波束成形，我们在无损互易MiLAC约束下构建了一个加权克拉美罗界（CRB）最小化问题，并提出了一种基于罚对偶分解（PDD）的迭代算法来解决该非凸问题。我们进一步证明了MiLAC辅助波束成形与全数字波束成形能达到相同的CRB。对于接收端处理，我们证明了2D DFT可以通过无损互易MiLAC实现，从而在模拟域进行DOA估计而无需数字优化。数值结果证实了理论发现，并表明MiLAC辅助方法在CRB和DOA估计性能上与全数字基准相同。同时，由于发射端仅需低分辨率DAC，接收端无需射频链和ADC，硬件成本和功耗得以降低。此外，在模拟域执行2D DFT消除了DOA估计中所有的数字DFT运算。

</details>


### [239] [Optimal Time Window and Frequency Bandwidth Parameter Combination for Subject-Specific Motor Imagery EEG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21319)
*Matthew A. McCartney,Liisa A. Kivioja,Sonal S. Baberwal,Shirley Coyle*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了运动想象脑电信号分类中，同时优化后提示时间窗口和频率带宽对分类性能的影响。通过对109名受试者进行参数组合的迭代训练与统计检验，发现(0,4)秒时间窗口与(4,12)Hz频率带宽的组合在所有受试者中表现最优，但其他组合也能取得相近精度，强调了个性化模型在参数选择中的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 运动想象脑电分类中，不同研究常采用不同的后提示时间窗口和频率带宽，导致模型性能差异较大。现有研究缺乏对这两个参数同时进行系统优化分析的探索。

Method: 对109名受试者，在不同频率带宽与时间窗口组合上迭代训练和测试个体专属模型，然后使用重复测量方差分析（repeated measures ANOVA）进行统计分析，以揭示不同带宽和时间窗口在分类精度上的显著差异。

Result: (0,4)秒时间窗口与(4,12)Hz频率带宽的组合在所有受试者中表现最优，且不同时间窗口和带宽之间存在统计显著的精度差异；但其他参数组合也能达到相近的分类精度。

Conclusion: 个性化模型能够有效检测出最优的时频参数组合，从而更好地分类受试者间固有差异较大的运动想象脑电信号。

Original Abstract: Motor-imagery (MI) EEG can be classified using supervised machine learning techniques such as Linear Discriminant Analysis applied to features extracted by Common Spatial Patterns. Performance of these models varies widely, possibly due to MI studies commonly utilising differing post-cue time windows and frequency bands to one another. This study aims to assess how the simultaneous optimisation of both these parameters impact MI classification performance. This is done by iteratively training and testing a series of subject-specific models on different combinations of frequency bandwidth and time window options across 109 subjects. This is followed by a statistical analysis using repeated measures ANOVA to uncover significant differences between different bandwidths and time windows in terms of accuracy across the patient cohort. The resulting visualisations and statistical tests show that there are, indeed, significant differences between both specific time windows and specific bandwidths in terms of accuracy. While the comparison of classification accuracies across 23 frequency bandwidths during five different time windows demonstrates an optimal temporal and spectral scale combination of (0, 4) s at the range of (4, 12) Hz across all subjects, the subjects demonstrate similar accuracies for other parameter combinations. These findings highlight the efficacy of personalised models to detect optimal temporal and spectral parameter combinations to best classify MI EEG signals that inherently vary across subjects.

中文摘要翻译: 运动想象（MI）脑电图可以通过监督式机器学习技术进行分类，例如将线性判别分析应用于由共空间模式提取的特征。这些模型的性能差异较大，可能原因是运动想象研究通常使用不同的后提示时间窗口和频率带宽。本研究旨在评估同时优化这两个参数对运动想象分类性能的影响。具体方法是，在109名受试者中，针对不同的频率带宽和时间窗口组合，迭代训练和测试一系列个体专属模型。随后使用重复测量方差分析进行统计分析，以揭示不同带宽和时间窗口在患者队列分类精度上的显著差异。最终的可视化结果和统计检验表明，不同时间窗口和不同带宽之间确实存在统计显著的精度差异。虽然对五种不同时间窗口下23种频率带宽的分类精度进行比较，显示所有受试者在(4,12)Hz频段、(0,4)秒时间窗口上存在最优的时频尺度组合，但受试者在其他参数组合下也表现出相近的分类精度。这些发现突显了个性化模型在检测最优时频参数组合方面的有效性，从而更好地分类受试者间固有差异较大的运动想象脑电信号。

</details>


### [240] [Advancements in Non-Invasive Neuroimaging: Exploring the Potential of Radar Technology for Brain Imaging and Tumour Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21350)
*Keniel Peart,Indu Bodala,Shelly Vishwakarma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用雷达技术进行无创脑成像和肿瘤检测，作为MRI和CT的替代方案。通过Ansys HFSS模拟比较Patch和Vivaldi天线，发现Patch天线更优用于肿瘤定位，Vivaldi天线适用于广域扫描。该技术有望实现更安全、更可及的医学成像，尤其在资源受限环境中具有潜力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统MRI和CT扫描成本高昂、设备庞大且存在辐射风险，在资源有限地区难以普及。本研究旨在探索雷达技术作为一种更安全、低成本、便携式的无创脑成像替代方案，用于脑肿瘤检测。

Method: 使用Ansys HFSS仿真工具模拟电磁波在脑组织中的相互作用，分别评估Patch天线和Vivaldi天线的穿透能力、信号强度及安全性，比较二者在脑肿瘤检测中的性能。

Result: Patch天线在肿瘤定位方面表现最优，Vivaldi天线则更适合宽范围扫描应用。两种天线均展现了雷达技术用于无创脑成像的可行性。

Conclusion: 雷达技术为无创脑成像和肿瘤检测提供了有前景的替代方案，尤其在资源受限环境中更具优势。但仍需进一步开展多样化模型和真实患者数据的实验研究，以推动该技术在无创医学诊断中的发展。

Original Abstract: This study investigates radar technology for non-invasive brain imaging and tumour detection, offering an alternative to MRI and CT scans. Using Ansys HFSS to simulate electromagnetic interactions in brain tissues, we evaluate the penetration, signal strength, and safety of Patch and Vivaldi antennas. Results show Patch antennas are optimal for tumour localization, while Vivaldi antennas suit broader scanning applications. Although promising for safer, more accessible imaging, especially in resource-limited environments, further research with diverse models and actual patient data is essential to advance this technology in non-invasive medical diagnostics.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究探讨了将雷达技术用于无创脑成像和肿瘤检测，作为核磁共振成像（MRI）和计算机断层扫描（CT）的替代方案。利用Ansys HFSS仿真软件模拟电磁波在脑组织中的相互作用，我们评估了Patch天线和Vivaldi天线的穿透能力、信号强度及安全性。结果表明，Patch天线在肿瘤定位方面表现最优，而Vivaldi天线更适用于宽范围扫描应用。尽管该技术在实现更安全、更可及的成像方面前景广阔，尤其是在资源有限的环境中，但仍需开展更多样化模型和真实患者数据的进一步研究，以推动该技术应用于无创医学诊断领域。

</details>

### [241] [Disentangling Sampling from Training Budget in Class-Imbalanced CT Body Composition Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20405)
*Iason Skylitsis,Dimitrios Karkalousos,Ivana Išgum*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出将小样本学习中的episodic采样应用于全监督医学图像分割，通过构建类别平衡的批次来缓解类别不平衡问题。在CT身体成分分割任务上，低数据场景下episodic采样优于随机和加权采样，并发现训练迭代次数是评估采样策略时被忽视的混杂因素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于损失重加权和采样策略的方法无法显式控制批次中出现的类别，导致稀有类别暴露不足。本文旨在通过引入episodic采样来实现类别平衡的批次构建，从而更有效地处理医学图像分割中的类别不平衡问题。

Method: 从小样本学习中借鉴episodic采样策略，将其与度量学习解耦，直接应用于全监督医学图像分割。在每个训练批次中，从每个类别均匀采样样本，构建类别平衡的批次，从而确保稀有类别获得充分训练曝光。

Result: 在210例CT扫描的9种肌肉和脂肪组织分割任务上：全数据训练下三种策略性能相当（episodic Dice 0.882，随机和加权0.878）；低数据训练下episodic采样优于随机（0.787 vs 0.758）和加权（0.762）；在匹配训练预算下，随机和加权更早过拟合，而episodic采样提升迭代次数约为其三倍后才趋于平稳。

Conclusion: Episodic采样通过隐式正则化效应提升小数据集上的分割性能，且无需修改模型架构；研究同时揭示训练迭代预算是评估采样策略的关键混杂因素，建议采用迭代感知的评估协议。

Original Abstract: Class imbalance is a fundamental challenge in medical image segmentation, where frequent classes typically dominate training at the expense of rare classes. Loss-based approaches mitigate imbalance by reweighting the per-pixel loss within the batch, while sampling strategies control which images enter the batch. Yet neither explicitly controls which classes appear within the batch, leaving rare-class exposure only partially rebalanced. In this work, we adopt episodic sampling from few-shot learning to promote class-balanced batch construction in a fully supervised setting. We decouple episodic sampling from its conventional metric-learning context and evaluate it in body composition segmentation in CT. We compare episodic sampling against random and weighted sampling on nine muscle and adipose tissues, derived from 210 scans of the public SAROS dataset. Training is performed under full- and low-data regimes, with additional comparisons under matched training iteration budgets. Under full-data training, all three strategies performed comparably (mean Dice 0.882 for episodic, 0.878 for random and weighted). Under low-data training, episodic sampling outperformed random and weighted (0.787 vs. 0.758 and 0.762), driven by a 12-fold difference in training iterations. Under matched training budgets, random and weighted overfit earlier, while episodic improved for approximately three times more iterations before plateauing. Our findings identify the training iteration budget as under-recognized confound in sampling strategies, motivating iteration-aware evaluation protocols for small datasets. Furthermore, the residual advantage of episodic sampling is consistent with an implicit regularization effect of class-balanced batches, offering a low-cost, model-agnostic strategy for class-imbalanced medical image segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/iasonsky/episodic-sampling.

中文摘要翻译: 类别不平衡是医学图像分割中的一个基本挑战，其中频繁出现的类别通常以牺牲稀有类别为代价主导训练过程。基于损失的方法通过批次内逐像素损失重加权来缓解不平衡，而采样策略则控制哪些图像进入批次。然而，这两种方法都无法显式控制批次中出现的类别，导致稀有类别的暴露仅能得到部分重新平衡。在本工作中，我们采用小样本学习中的episodic采样，在全监督环境下促进类别平衡的批次构建。我们将episodic采样与其传统的度量学习背景解耦，并在CT身体成分分割中对其进行评估。我们在来自公共SAROS数据集的210例扫描中，针对九种肌肉和脂肪组织，将episodic采样与随机采样和加权采样进行了比较。训练在全数据和低数据两种模式下进行，并在匹配训练迭代预算下进行了额外比较。在全数据训练下，三种策略性能相当（episodic采样平均Dice为0.882，随机采样和加权采样均为0.878）。在低数据训练下，episodic采样优于随机采样和加权采样（0.787对比0.758和0.762），这一优势由12倍的训练迭代次数差异驱动。在匹配训练预算下，随机采样和加权采样更早过拟合，而episodic采样在性能趋于平稳前持续提升了约三倍的迭代次数。我们的发现将训练迭代预算识别为采样策略中一个未被充分认识的混杂因素，从而激励针对小数据集的迭代感知评估协议。此外，episodic采样的剩余优势与类别平衡批次的隐式正则化效应一致，为类别不平衡的医学图像分割提供了一种低成本、模型无关的策略。代码可在 https://github.com/iasonsky/episodic-sampling 获取。

</details>


### [242] [Parallel Context Modeling for Sliding Window Attention in Neural Video Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20977)
*Alexander Kopte,André Kaup*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Most neural video codecs rely on temporal conditioning, which makes them susceptible to error propagation over long sequences. While Transformer-based architectures like the VCT offer a drift-free alternative, they suffer from high computational complexity and inferior RD performance. The recent SWA addresses these shortcomings by reducing complexity and enhancing RD performance, yet it restricts decoding to a strictly sequential raster-scan order, creating a critical bottleneck in decoding latency. To resolve this, we propose P-SWA, utilizing diagonal wavefronts to enable parallel decoding. By embedding a hyperprior and introducing an accumulator to fuse side information and local spatial context, our method increases decoding speed by 36% over the parallel VCT. Simultaneously, it achieves Bjøntegaard Delta-rate savings of up to 10.0% for I-frames and 7.1% for P-frames over the SWA baseline.

### [243] [Transcoding a 3D Gaussian Splatting Model from a Plenoptic Point Cloud or Mesh without the Original Multi-view Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21051)
*Maja Krivokuća,Riad Bendouro,Neus Sabater*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端转码管线，用于在缺少原始多视图图像的情况下，从现有三维全光点云或网格模型创建3D高斯泼溅模型，并设计了自定义初始化方法以加快收敛并提升表面表示质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际中常出现原始多视图图像不可用的情况，仅存在已生成的三维点云或网格模型，因此需要一种方法直接从这些现有三维表示出发构建高质量的3DGS模型。

Method: 提出一个端到端转码管线，包含自定义初始化策略，引导3DGS模型学习，并通过约束确保最终模型与输入点云或网格表面紧密对齐。

Result: 在高质量标准全光点云数据集上，该管线能以远少于原始稠密点云的泼溅数量生成高视觉质量的3DGS模型；自定义初始化相比默认SfM初始化收敛更快，表面表示更干净。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在缺乏原始多视图图像的条件下，有效从点云或网格转码生成高质量的3DGS模型，且自定义初始化带来的性能提升显著。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose an end-to-end transcoding pipeline, to create 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) models from existing 3D plenoptic point cloud or mesh models, when the original multi-view images of the captured 3D object or scene are not available. We also propose a custom initialisation to guide the 3DGS model learning, with constraints to ensure that the final 3DGS model aligns closely with the input point cloud or mesh surface. Tests on a high-quality, standard plenoptic point cloud dataset show that our pipeline produces 3DGS models of high visual quality, with many fewer splats than points in the original dense point clouds. Additionally, our custom initialisation leads to much faster convergence and cleaner surface representation than when starting from the default SfM-based initialisation that is typically used for 3DGS model learning.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种端到端的转码管线，用于在无法获取已捕获三维物体或场景的原始多视图图像时，从现有的三维全光点云或网格模型创建3D高斯泼溅模型。我们还提出了一种自定义初始化方法，以引导3DGS模型学习，并通过约束确保最终的3DGS模型与输入点云或网格表面紧密对齐。在一个高质量的标准全光点云数据集上的测试表明，我们的管线生成的3DGS模型视觉质量高，且泼溅数量远少于原始稠密点云中的点数。此外，与从3DGS模型学习通常使用的默认SfM初始化相比，我们的自定义初始化能够实现更快的收敛和更清晰的表面表示。

</details>


### [244] [Joint Alignment and Denoising for Event-Based Vision Sensors Using Regret-based Pareto Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21096)
*Shimpei Harada,Junya Hara,Hiroshi Higashi,Yuichi Tanaka*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文针对事件相机中事件对齐与去噪相互矛盾的难题，提出一种联合优化方法，通过双目标Pareto优化同时处理两者，实验表明该方法优于现有方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将事件对齐和事件去噪作为独立模块串行处理，但二者存在矛盾：不去噪时对齐受噪声偏置，不对齐时去噪难以区分信号与噪声。这种分离式处理导致性能受限，亟需一种联合优化方法。

Method: 基于对比度图（统计每个像素上的事件数量），将事件对齐建模为最大化对比度图的方差，将事件去噪建模为最小化对比度图的方差，形成两个相互冲突的目标。通过双目标Pareto优化框架，并采用遗憾策略（regret strategy）求解该问题。

Result: 在去噪和运动估计两项任务上的实验结果表明，所提方法相比现有方法取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过Pareto优化联合处理事件对齐与去噪是可行且有效的，能够克服二者分离处理时的矛盾困境。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes a joint alignment and denoising method for event-based vision sensors (EVSs). Existing signal processing methods for EVSs typically perform event alignment (EA) and event denoising (ED) as separate modules. However, this separation creates a dilemma: without ED, EA is biased by noise, whereas without EA, ED struggles to distinguish signal events from noise ones. To address this dilemma, we jointly optimize EA and ED by formulating a bi-objective Pareto optimization problem. Our formulation is built upon a contrast map that counts the number of events localized in each pixel. With a contrast map, we can formulate EA as maximizing its variance and ED as minimizing the variance. We cast these two conflicting problems as a Pareto optimization and use a regret strategy to obtain a solution. Experimental results on denoising and motion estimation demonstrate that our method achieves improvements against alternative ones.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种针对事件视觉传感器（EVS）的联合对齐与去噪方法。现有的事件信号处理方法通常将事件对齐（EA）和事件去噪（ED）作为独立模块分别执行。然而，这种分离方式带来了一个困境：没有去噪时，对齐会受到噪声的偏置；而没有对齐时，去噪难以区分信号事件与噪声事件。为解决这一困境，我们将事件对齐与去噪联合优化，构建了一个双目标Pareto优化问题。该公式基于对比度图——一种统计每个像素上事件数量的表示。借助对比度图，我们将事件对齐形式化为最大化其方差，将事件去噪形式化为最小化其方差。我们将这两个相互冲突的目标建模为Pareto优化问题，并采用遗憾策略来求解。在去噪和运动估计任务上的实验结果表明，所提方法相比现有方法取得了性能改进。

</details>


### [245] [GeoDiff-SAR II: 3D-Driven Foundation Diffusion Models for SAR Generation via Decoupled Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21116)
*Xuanting Wu,Fan Zhang,Fei Ma,Yingbing Liu,Lingxiao Peng,Qiang Yin,Yongsheng Zhou*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: Existing Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) image generation methods still lack reliable controllability over key imaging parameters, particularly azimuth angle, depression angle, and polarization mode. Our preliminary GeoDiff-SAR supported limited azimuth completion, but remained ineffective for large missing azimuth sectors and did not provide unified control over multiple imaging conditions. To address this problem, we propose GeoDiff-SAR II, a 3D model-guided decoupled framework for controllable SAR image generation. The proposed framework imposes controllability through physically grounded geometric-electromagnetic cues rather than image intensity alone. We introduce a Geometric-Electromagnetic Conditioning Map (GECM), a structured intermediate representation that encodes the target pose map and dominant scattering centers, thereby decoupling macroscopic geometry from microscopic scattering responses. During training, GECMs are derived from real sparse-azimuth SAR images. During inference, the same representation is rendered directly from a 3D CAD model under specified azimuth, depression angle, and polarization conditions, enabling physically consistent control across large viewpoint gaps. The imaging parameters are further converted into text conditions, while the GECM is injected through ControlNet to provide explicit spatial guidance. Combined with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on a FLUX backbone, the proposed framework unifies geometric-electromagnetic conditioning and parameter-aware generation within a single process. Experiments on simulated and real datasets demonstrate controllable generation over key SAR imaging parameters, stable generalization across large azimuth gaps, and consistent improvements in image fidelity, physical consistency, and downstream Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) performance.

### [246] [Local-sensitive connectivity filter (ls-cf): A post-processing unsupervised improvement of the frangi, hessian and vesselness filters for multimodal vessel segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21251)
*Erick O Rodrigues,Lucas O Rodrigues,João HP Machado,Dalcimar Casanova,Marcelo Teixeira,Jeferson T Oliva,Giovani Bernardes,Panos Liatsis*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Original Abstract: A retinal vessel analysis is a procedure that can be used as an assessment of risks to the eye. This work proposes an unsupervised multimodal approach that improves the response of the Frangi filter, enabling automatic vessel segmentation. We propose a filter that computes pixel-level vessel continuity while introducing a local tolerance heuristic to fill in vessel discontinuities produced by the Frangi response. This proposal, called the local-sensitive connectivity filter (LS-CF), is compared against a naive connectivity filter to the baseline thresholded Frangi filter response and to the naive connectivity filter response in combination with the morphological closing and to the current approaches in the literature. The proposal was able to achieve competitive results in a variety of multimodal datasets. It was robust enough to outperform all the state-of-the-art approaches in the literature for the OSIRIX angiographic dataset in terms of accuracy and 4 out of 5 works in the case of the IOSTAR dataset while also outperforming several works in the case of the DRIVE and STARE datasets and 6 out of 10 in the CHASE-DB dataset. For the CHASE-DB, it also outperformed all the state-of-the-art unsupervised methods.
